### [1] [Discovery of the Infrared Counterpart of the Rapid Burster with JWST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35956)
*Malina M. Desai,Kevin B. Burdge,Cristina Pallanca,Saul Rappaport,Francesco R. Ferraro,Barbara Lanzoni,Mason Ng,Paul Draghis,Emma T. Chickles,Ilaria Caiazzo,Deepto Chakrabarty*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用JWST/NIRCam首次确认了快速暴发天体（Rapid Burster）在Liller 1中的光学/红外对应体，并观测到其在X射线活跃态下强烈的红外光变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速暴发天体（MXB 1730−335）是唯一已知同时产生I型和II型X射线暴的中子星，是研究中子星吸积物理的关键目标。它位于Liller 1中，该星团核心密集且存在约11个星等的光学消光，致使其光学/红外对应体近五十年来一直未被发现。

Method: 使用JWST/NIRCam对Liller 1进行红外观测。

Result: 首次明确揭示了快速暴发天体的光学/红外对应体；在X射线活跃态下，该对应体表现出剧烈的红外光变，在F200W波段（1.755–2.227 μm）变暗约2.5个星等，并在分钟量级上存在强烈波动。

Conclusion: 该观测为快速暴发天体提供了红外定位基准，并为未来开展光谱观测以及红外与X射线同步监测、进而约束供体星光谱型和系统吸积物理开辟了道路。

Abstract: The Rapid Burster (MXB 1730$-$335) is the only known neutron star to produce both Type I and Type II X-ray bursts, making it a key system for understanding accretion physics onto neutron stars. It resides in Liller 1, a dense, metal-rich stellar system whose crowded core and $\sim$11 magnitudes of visual extinction have concealed the Rapid Burster's optical/infrared (OIR) counterpart for nearly five decades. Here, we present infrared observations of Liller 1 obtained with JWST/NIRCam that, for the first time, reveal the unambiguous OIR counterpart to the Rapid Burster. Caught in an X-ray active state, the counterpart exhibits dramatic infrared variability, fading by $\sim$ 2.5 magnitudes in JWST's F200W band (1.755$-$2.227 $μ$m) with strong fluctuations on minute timescales. These observations provide an infrared anchor for the Rapid Burster and open the way for future spectroscopy and simultaneous IR and X-ray monitoring to constrain the donor's spectral type and the system's accretion physics.

</details>


### [2] [Hierarchical Bayesian Inference on the intrinsic event rate of Type I gamma-ray bursts from the {\it Fermi}/GBM catalogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35994)
*Cao Tian-Yong,Yi Shu-Xu,Du Yun-Fei,Yorgancioglu S. Emre,Xiong Shao-Lin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用分层贝叶斯框架分析Fermi/GBM I型伽马射线暴目录,重建其随红移的本征事件率密度,并约束双中子星并合族群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I型伽马射线暴的本征事件率随红移的变化可用于探测双中子星并合族群,但仪器选择效应、多数暴缺乏红移测量以及喷流几何不确定性使这一推断变得复杂。

Method: 采用分层贝叶斯推断框架处理Fermi/GBM I型伽马暴目录,纳入选择效应并边缘化个体暴红移与喷流结构的有限信息后验;族群模型包含参数化光度函数、喷流结构参数分布、Madau-Dickinson恒星形成率及双中子星并合延迟时间分布;使用GPU加速的MCMC算法推断超参数后验。

Result: 将本征事件率密度约束至z=5,得到本地事件率密度R0=22.6^{+15.0}_{-10.6} Gpc^-3 yr^-1,与最新引力波测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 分层贝叶斯方法能有效处理选择效应和参数不确定性,所导出的I型伽马暴本地事件率与引力波测得的双中子星并合率相符,支持其作为并合族群的探针。

Abstract: The intrinsic event-rate of Type I gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) as a function of redshift, provides a probe for binary neutron star (BNS) mergers population. However, the above-mentioned inference is complicated by instrumental selection effects, the lack of redshift measurements for the majority of GRBs, and uncertainties in jet geometry. In this work, we applied a hierarchical Bayesian inference framework to the {\it Fermi}/GBM Type I GRB catalogue to reconstruct the underlying event-rate population. This framework accounts for the selection effects and marginalizes over the limited-information posteriors of individual GRB redshifts and jet structures. The population model uses parameterized luminosity function, distribution of jet structure parameters, the Madau-Dickinson star-formation rate and a delay-time distribution of BNS mergers. The posterior distribution of the hyperparameters in the model are to be inferred, using a GPU-accelerated MCMC algorithm. The intrinsic event rate density as function of redshift is constrained up to $z=5$, which a local rate density of \(R_0 = 22.6^{+15.0}_{-10.6}\,{\rm Gpc^{-3}\,yr^{-1}},\), which is consistent with the latest gravitational-wave measurements.

</details>


### [3] [Bayesian Evidence for Inspiraling Hotspot Motion in the Galactic Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35995)
*Pablo Ruales,Maciek Wielgus,Delilah E. A. Gates,Alejandro Cardenas-Avendano*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文构建贝叶斯框架，用偏振光变曲线推断Sgr A*热点随时间变化的运动学，发现其偏好非测地线、向内旋进的轨迹而非严格开普勒轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耀发期间Sgr A*的偏振观测在Q-U平面上呈现演化的环状轨迹（Q-U loops），此前常用瞬态加热的轨道热点开普勒模型进行拟合，但缺乏对热点运动学更一般、随时间变化形式的系统推断与模型比较。

Method: 将热点轨迹建模为K段连续分段运动学片段，每段由径向和角向速度参数描述；构建贝叶斯框架从偏振光变曲线推断时间相关运动学，采用K=1,2,3进行模型比较，并分析局域磁场几何、谱指数及轨迹运动学轮廓对偏振信号的影响，对照2017年4月11日ALMA毫米波观测（含两次相继Q-U loops）。

Result: 统计上显著地表明辐射区遵循非测地线的向内旋进轨迹；当建模更长的数据段时，该偏好明显增强。非开普勒旋进模型在贝叶斯对数证据比较和约化卡方统计上均优于纯开普勒模型。

Conclusion: 研究结果为银河系中心耀发事件及其伴随的间歇性热点性质提供了新认识，说明单一开普勒轨道不足以描述观测到的偏振演化。

Abstract: During flaring episodes, polarimetric observations of the Galactic Center black hole Sagittarius A* (Sgr A*) reveal evolving, loop-like trajectories in the Stokes Q-U plane, the so-called Q-$U loops. Keplerian models of transiently energized orbiting features, hotspots, have been employed to reproduce these polarimetric signatures. In this work, we develop a Bayesian framework to infer the time-dependent kinematics of the hotspot from polarized light curves, using a general prescription for its motion. We model the emitting region's trajectory as a continuous, piecewise sequence of K kinematic segments, each described by radial and azimuthal velocity parameters. We perform analyses with K=1,2,3 and compare these models to millimeter-wavelength ALMA observations of Sgr A* from 2017 April 11 that include two subsequent Q-U loops, extending the observational constraints on the kinematics. We examine the effects of the local magnetic field geometry, spectral index, and kinematic profile of the hotspot's trajectory on the polarized signal. We find statistically significant evidence that the emitting region follows a non-geodesic inspiraling trajectory, and this preference over a strictly Keplerian orbit strengthens sharply when a longer data segment is modeled. The inspiraling non-Keplerian models are preferred to a purely Keplerian model based on both the Bayesian log-evidence comparison and the reduced chi-square statistic. Our results offer new insight into the nature of flaring events in the Galactic Center and the episodically produced hotspots associated with them.

</details>


### [4] [First Detection of the Doppler Shift of X-ray Photons Due to a Telescope's Orbital Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36042)
*Stephen DiKerby,Shuo Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次探测到X射线望远镜绕地球轨道运动导致的X射线光子红移与蓝移调制，在XRISM/Resolve对银心分子云窄铁Kα线的观测中以95%置信度发现轨道红移调制证据，并呼吁重新审视高分辨率X射线光谱时代的光子数据封装方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前及未来的X射线望远镜（如XRISM/Resolve、NewAthena）能量分辨率已达R=E/ΔE≈1000，这要求对本地静止参考系、地球绕日轨道等物理效应进行光谱定标。然而，望远镜绕地球轨道运动引起的红移调制这一效应此前从未被研究，亟需建立探测方法并评估其在高分辨率光谱中的影响。

Method: 构建了一套先验（a priori）探测流程，用于检测X射线望远镜绕地球运动所导致的红移调制效应；将该流程应用于XRISM/Resolve对银河系中心分子云窄铁Kα发射线的多项观测。

Result: 在Kα发射线中以95%置信度探测到轨道红移调制的证据，首次实现了对望远镜绕地轨道运动引起X射线光子红移/蓝移的探测。

Conclusion: 轨道红移调制已存在于X射线数据中且可被探测，这促使人们重新审视高分辨率X射线光谱时代X射线光子数据的封装与处理方式。

Abstract: We have detected for the first time the redshifting and blueshifting of X-ray photons due to the orbital motion of an X-ray telescope around the Earth. Current and planned X-ray telescopes like XRISM/Resolve and NewAthena can achieve energy resolutions $R = E / ΔE \approx 1000$, requiring spectroscopic calibration of physical effects such as the local standard of rest and the orbit of the Earth around the Sun. We construct an a priori method for detecting a heretofore unexamined effect: the modulation of redshift due to the motion of an X-ray telescope around the Earth. We apply this pipeline to several XRISM/Resolve observations of narrow iron K$α$ emission lines from galactic center molecular clouds, and we detect evidence of orbital redshift modulation in the K$α$ emission at the 95% confidence level. Our findings suggest that orbital redshift modulation is already present and detectable in X-ray data and motivates reexamination of how X-ray photon data is packaged in the emerging era of high-resolution X-ray spectroscopy.

</details>


### [5] [Fast Radio Bursts from Induced Upscattering. I. Magnetar Polar Fireballs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36114)
*Rei Nishiura,Kunihito Ioka,Bing Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文提出磁星磁层内的相对论火球可通过诱导康普顿上散射将低频种子波放大并蓝移至GHz，解释FRB的极高亮温度，并给出可检验的亮度-带宽关系预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB的极端亮温度要求存在将磁星爆发能量转化为毫秒级射电辐射的相干机制，需从等离子体物理层面给出自洽的能量转换与辐射机制。

Method: 研究磁星极区电子-正电子火球中的诱导康普顿上散射：X射线暴辐射加速极区火球至洛伦兹因子Γ~10-10^3；低频种子波（快磁声波或阿尔芬波）从侧向或逆流进入火球，经诱导散射被相对论体运动蓝移，ν0~10 kHz→νobs~Γ²ν0~GHz；结合极区火球动力学与诱导散射不稳定的线性增长及非线性饱和，推导FRB各向同性等效光度与相对谱带宽。

Result: 在可能的对密度与温度范围内，该极区通道可解释银河系FRB 20200428的光度，并能达到最亮河外FRB的量级；若对加载区域窄于相对论束流角1/Γ，模型产生窄谱并预言L_iso∝(Δν/ν_c)^2，与重复FRB观测趋势一致。

Conclusion: 诱导上散射模型以等离子体物理中的相干辐射过程把磁星X射线暴与FRB联系起来，并给出可观测检验的预言。

Abstract: The extreme brightness temperatures of fast radio bursts (FRBs) demand a coherent process that converts magnetar burst energy into millisecond radio emission. We propose that FRBs can be produced within magnetar magnetospheres by induced (stimulated) upscattering in a relativistic fireball outflow. In this paper, we focus on induced Compton upscattering in an electron--positron fireball launched from the polar region of a magnetar. Radiation from the X-ray burst accelerates the polar fireball outflow to Lorentz factors of $Γ\sim10$--$10^3$. Low-frequency seed waves, such as fast magnetosonic or Alfvén waves, entering the outflow from the side or against its motion undergo induced scattering and are Lorentz-boosted by the relativistic bulk motion from $ν_0\sim10\mathrm{kHz}$ to $ν_{\rm obs}\simΓ^2ν_0\sim\mathrm{GHz}$. Combining the polar fireball dynamics with the linear growth and nonlinear saturation of the induced scattering instability, we derive the FRB luminosity $L_{\rm FRB}^{\rm iso}$ and fractional spectral bandwidth $Δν/ν_{\rm c}$. By considering the possible range of pair densities and temperatures, we find that this polar channel can account for the luminosity of Galactic FRB~20200428 and reach luminosities comparable to those of the brightest extragalactic FRBs. If the pair-loaded region is narrower than the relativistic beaming angle $1/Γ$, the model also produces a narrow spectrum and predicts $L_{\rm FRB}^{\rm iso}\propto(Δν/ν_{\rm c})^2$, a trend consistent with observations of repeating FRBs. The induced upscattering model thus connects magnetar X-ray bursts to FRBs through a coherent emission process grounded in plasma physics and makes observationally testable predictions.

</details>


### [6] [What We Have Learned from NICER About Neutron Star Radii](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36169)
*M. C. Miller*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了NICER对非吸积中子星半径的X射线测量及其对致密物质状态方程研究的推动作用，并指出目前已探讨的系统误差对半径推断结果影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星核心的密度、温度和质子-中子不对称性组合无法在实验室复现，也无法用第一性原理量子色动力学直接描述，因此必须借助中子星观测来理解该物理区域。其中，对中子星半径的精确测量能有效约束数倍核饱和密度下的冷催化物质性质。

Method: 本文采用综述形式，梳理NASA的NICER探测器对若干非吸积中子星所进行的X射线半径测量，并系统评估NICER分析中已探索的系统误差来源及其对半径推断的影响。

Result: NICER已给出若干非吸积中子星的半径测量结果，显著推进了对致密物质的理解与状态方程约束；同时分析表明，迄今所考察的系统误差对推断的中子星半径最多只有次要影响。

Conclusion: NICER的半径测量是研究致密物质的重要手段，其推断结果在现有系统误差评估下具有稳健性，可信度较高。

Abstract: The cores of neutron stars have a combination of density, temperature, and neutron-proton asymmetry that cannot be replicated in laboratories or understood using first-principles quantum chromodynamics. Thus, observations of neutron stars are necessary to understand this regime. In particular, precise and accurate measurements of neutron star radii are highly informative about cold, catalyzed matter at a few times nuclear saturation density. X-ray observations with NASA's Neutron star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER) have yielded radius measurements for a few nonaccreting neutron stars, which have advanced substantially our understanding of dense matter. In this review we discuss the NICER radius measurements and their implications and demonstrate that the systematic errors thus far explored in NICER analyses have at most a minor effect on the inferred radii of neutron stars.

</details>


### [7] [Assessing the detectability and identification of pulsar halos with H.E.S.S. and CTAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36229)
*Karim Sabri,Yves Gallant,Justine Devin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文构建基于模板的脉冲星晕模型并拟合模拟的H.E.S.S.与CTAO数据，评估新一代成像大气切伦科夫望远镜对银河系内区脉冲星晕的刻画能力。结果显示可同时约束多项脉冲星与扩散参数，CTAO较H.E.S.S.显著提升模型区分能力，且银河弥散辐射会降低探测显著性并导致参数估计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星晕是一类延展伽马射线源，由脉冲星风云中逃逸的电子/正电子对周围光子逆康普顿散射产生。自HAWC首次发现以来，候选源数量不断增加，但当前及未来成像大气切伦科夫望远镜能否刻画其物理性质尚不明确，尤其是内银河区弥散辐射的影响需要量化。

Method: 在Gammapy库中构建基于模板的脉冲星晕模型，采用各向同性扩散模型，针对不同物理参数的晕生成模拟H.E.S.S.与CTAO数据并进行拟合。用二维高斯成分对源进行谱-形态联合刻画，通过模拟数据评估晕模型与二维高斯假设之间的统计偏好，并量化银河弥散辐射的影响。

Result: 可同时约束多项晕参数，包括脉冲星真实年龄、自行、扩散系数、注入粒子谱指数及注入效率。部分情形下H.E.S.S.阵列能在统计上区分晕模型与含二维高斯成分的模型，而CTAO带来显著提升。随银河弥散辐射强度增加，源探测显著性急剧下降；若未采用数据生成模型对弥散辐射建模，晕参数估计会出现偏差。模拟晕的性质在仅需适度观测时间的当前一代望远镜巡天中即可能被发现。

Conclusion: 物理动机明确的脉冲星晕模型有助于在高层次数据分析中识别与刻画该类源，CTAO将显著增强区分与参数约束能力，而弥散辐射的正确建模是避免参数偏差的关键。

Abstract: Pulsar halos are a class of extended gamma-ray sources, resulting from inverse Compton scattering of ambient photons by electrons and positrons believed to have escaped confinement in a pulsar wind nebula. First identified by HAWC towards PSR J0633+1746 and PSR B0656+14, a growing number of pulsar-powered gamma-ray sources is proposed as candidate halos. Using a model of isotropic diffusion, we assessed the ability of current-and-future generation imaging atmospheric Cherenkov telescopes to characterize the physical properties of such sources in the inner Galaxy. We constructed template-based pulsar halo models in the Gammapy library and fitted them to simulated H.E.S.S. and CTAO data of halos with different physical parameters. The sources were characterized spectro-morphologically using 2D Gaussian components. We assessed the statistical preference between the halo and 2D Gaussian hypotheses by the simulated data and quantified the impact of the diffuse Galactic emission. A multitude of the halos' parameters can be constrained simultaneously, including the pulsar's true age, its proper motion, the diffusion coefficient, the index of the injected particle spectrum and the injection efficiency. In some cases, the H.E.S.S. array can statistically distinguish between the halo model and a model containing 2D Gaussian components, while a significant improvement is seen with CTAO. The source's detection significance is drastically reduced with increasing intensity of the Galactic diffuse emission. If the latter is not modeled using the data-generating model, the halo parameter estimation is biased. A physically-motivated model of pulsar halos can facilitate the identification and characterization of this class of sources in high-level data analyses. The simulated halos exhibit properties that may already be found in surveys of current-generation telescopes with only a modest observation time.

</details>


### [8] [JWST/MIRI Spectroscopy of the Quiescent Black Hole X-ray Binary XTE J1118+480: Constraints on Jet and Circumbinary Dust Contributions to the Mid-infrared Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36318)
*Zihao Zuo,Elena Gallo,Nuria Calvet,Richard M. Plotkin,David M. Russell,Gabriele Cugno,Rob Fender,Fraser Lewis,Joseph Michail,Jon M. Miller,James C. A. Miller-Jones,Fan Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 首次通过JWST中红外光谱观测到宁静态黑洞X射线双星XTE J1118+480的弯曲连续谱，倾向于用环双星尘埃而非稳态喷流解释其5–25 μm能谱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宁静态黑洞X射线双星在极低吸积率（亚爱丁顿九个量级）下，中红外辐射起源存在竞争性解释：紧凑喷流的同步辐射与环双星尘埃的热辐射。此前缺乏中红外光谱证据来区分二者，本文旨在通过JWST/MIRI高灵敏观测加以判别。

Method: 使用JWST/MIRI低分辨率光谱（5–12 μm）与成像（15–25 μm），同时辅以光学监测和6 GHz射电上限（4.5 μJy）。扣除供体星贡献后拟合连续谱，检验单幂律与黑体模型，并对比同步辐射自吸收模型与自洽辐照盘模型。

Conclusion: 这是首个（虽尚未定论）在宁静态黑洞X射线双星中支持环双星尘埃而非喷流的中红外光谱证据。

Abstract: We report on JWST/MIRI low-resolution spectroscopy ($5$-$12 μ$m) and imaging ($15$-$25 μ$m) of the black hole X-ray binary XTE~J1118+480 in quiescence, at an X-ray luminosity nine orders of magnitude sub-Eddington, together with contemporaneous optical monitoring and a radio upper limit of $4.5~μ$Jy at 6 GHz. With the donor subtracted, the $5$-$12 μ$m continuum is curved, turning over near $6~μ$m; a single power law is rejected in favor of a $764\pm 9$ K blackbody. Longward of the turnover the spectral index is $α= +1.21\pm0.36$, steeper at the $2σ$ level than the flat to slightly inverted spectrum of a compact jet. Interpreted as synchrotron, the turnover implies equipartition fields of $\sim$$10^{4}$ G on scales of tens of gravitational radii, but the MIRI photometry at $15$-$21~μ$m-taken $2.6$ hr before the spectrum-lies $30$-$90$ per cent above its extrapolation, suggesting a second self-absorbed zone. A single partially self-absorbed jet spectrum can reach the photometry only at the expense of the spectral curvature and the $25.5~μ$m upper limit, so a steady compact jet is disfavored, although not excluded. Representative self-consistent irradiated-disk models instead reproduce the principal features of the $5$-$25~μ$m spectral energy distribution, with a directly illuminated dust wall at $\simeq 5$ binary separations, outside the tidal-truncation radius, and a minimum dust mass of order $10^{22}$ g. This is the first spectroscopic evidence, though not yet conclusive, favoring circumbinary dust over a jet in a quiescent black hole X-ray binary.

Abstract: Experimental constraints on the diffuse neutrino flux above the PeV range have been obtained with Cherenkov neutrino telescopes, air-shower arrays, radio detectors in and above polar ice, and radio observations of the Moon. Published results use different flavor, energy-bin, and statistical conventions, so their flux limits cannot be combined directly. We reconstruct energy-dependent exposures of the published searches and place them in a common convention for the total all-flavor $ν+\barν$ flux over $10^{16}$-$10^{26}$ eV. Published event counts and expected backgrounds are combined with a Poisson likelihood, and 90% C.L. quasi-differential limits are obtained for one-decade $E_ν^{-1}$ test spectra using a one-sided profile-likelihood construction. Folding theoretical spectra of cosmogenic neutrinos and selected new-physics scenarios with the combined energy-dependent exposure yields constraints on their flux normalizations. This homogeneous analysis provides a reproducible observational benchmark across ten decades in neutrino energy and a common reference for current and projected searches.

Many astrophysical sources and environments exhibit emission processes detectable in both regimes; however, the synergies extend beyond shared emission mechanisms and include broader physical processes and observational connections linking millimetre, submillimetre and TeV energies.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: TADAF模型能够很好地解释GRS 1915+105的大部分观测现象；某些类型QSO的差异可用TADAF环面半径变化理解；喷流位置角偏移由Lense-Thirring效应导致，且喷流外射速度越慢，角度偏移越大。该效应同样适用于V404 Cygni与AGN M87，并可能适用于SS 433著名的"进动"喷流。

Conclusion: 变湍流ADAF模型为GRS 1915+105的复杂多变现象提供了统一解释，并将喷流位置角偏移机制推广至其他黑洞系统，具有较广的适用性。

Abstract: The Galactic microquasar GRS 1915+105 is one of the most interesting X-ray binary systems, exhibiting an exceptionally diverse range of observational phenomena. It displays the most complicated and variable X-ray light curves and carries superluminal radio jets. Its X-ray and radio fluxes change on very different timescales, from seconds to minutes to days and even longer. In recent years, its jet has also displayed large position angle shifts. We find that most of the observational phenomena can be well explained within our newly developed variable turbulent ADAF (TADAF) model. The accretion disk is composed of an outer thin disk and an unstable inner TADAF torus whose size is variable. The previously suggested 'disappearance' and regain of the inner disk is understood as the transition between the thick TADAF and the thin disk. Some types of QSOs are understood in terms of the radius change of the TADAF torus. The jet position angle shift is understood as the direct sideways frame-dragging on the jet by the Lense-Thirring effect from a tilted, spinning black hole. The slower the outshooting jet, the larger the angle shift. We note that such an effect also applies to other sources, such as V404 Cygni and the AGN M87. This mechanism may also be applicable to the famous 'precessing' jet in SS 433.

</details>

Abstract: We report on the constraints obtained from the multi-wavelength polarization degree and energy spectra measurements for the High Synchrotron Peaked Blazar (HBL) source Mrk 421 using different forms of the electron energy distribution. We find that the optical and X-ray data are consistent with the electron energy distribution being a broken power-law (BPL) or other distributions, with all electrons experiencing the same magnetic field configuration. This is in contrast to other HBL sources like the Mrk 501, where, for the BPL distribution, the X-ray producing higher energy electrons need to exist in a more ordered magnetic field environment than the optical producing lower energy ones. This is because the break energy of the spectrum produced by the BPL distribution for Mrk 421 lies below the IXPE energy band. We discuss the implications of the findings.

The analysis yielded no significant evidence for a neutrino excess over the expected background, and upper limits on the WIMP-proton interaction cross section have been obtained, for both spin-dependent and spin-independent processes. These limits are valid for dark matter masses ranging from 35 GeV/c$^2$ to 10 TeV/c$^2$ for the annihilation benchmark channels WIMP$+$WIMP $\longrightarrow$ $b\bar{b},τ^+τ^-, W^+W^-$, assuming a 100$\%$ branching ratio for each of these channels. Conclusion: NGC 3079 的弥散 X 射线辐射包含显著的电荷交换成分，其丰度比对前身星质量上限给出了较严格约束（约 13–15 M⊙，即使考虑至 10% 的 SNIa 贡献，1σ 上限仍小于 25 M⊙）。NSB 与 GSB 年龄相差约一个量级，暗示该星系存在间歇性（episodic）反馈过程。

Abstract: Outflows driven by feedback leave imprints on a galaxy's diffuse X-ray emission. Elemental abundances provide key diagnostics of feedback-driven chemical enrichment. We present a spectroscopic study of the diffuse X-ray emission in NGC 3079 using XMM-Newton RGS and EPIC, focusing on the nuclear region and the extended galactic-scale superbubble (GSB). We analyzed the high-resolution RGS spectrum of the central $\sim4~\rm kpc$ region, encompassing the $\sim1~\rm kpc$ nuclear superbubble (NSB), and the EPIC spectrum of the GSB out to $5'~(\sim25~\rm kpc)$. The nuclear spectrum is best described by a multi-temperature thermal-plasma model plus a charge-exchange (CX) component, which contributes approximately $13\%$ of the total observed energy flux in the $0.2-2~\rm keV$ band. The best-fit O/Fe and Ne/Fe ratios are $0.47$ and $0.77$ in solar units. Comparing these ratios with IMF-weighted core-collapse supernova (SNcc) yields gives an upper progenitor-mass cutoff of $M_{\rm u}\simeq13-15~M_{\odot}$ for pure SNcc enrichment. For the best-fit spectral model, including an SNIa contribution increases the inferred cutoff, but its $1σ$ upper limit remains below $25~M_\odot$ for SNIa fractions up to $10\%$ within the adopted yield models. The characteristic ages of the NSB and GSB are $0.92^{+0.39}_{-0.46}~\rm Myr$ and $29.6^{+1.6}_{-2.9}~\rm Myr$, respectively, suggesting episodic feedback in NGC 3079.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 高斯拟合通常低估HVFs速度，据此给出线性修正公式；HVFs的速度、强度、持续时间与光变率、光谱亚型等总体观测量相关：更亮、慢衰减的超新星具有更强、更高速度的HVFs，而较暗天体则表现为弱、低速HVFs；早型星系中的Ia型超新星比晚型星系中的事件表现出更弱、更慢的HVFs。

Conclusion: HVFs的整体物理图像相当复杂，可能由多种模型解释。双爆轰爆炸模型能自然解释产生HVFs的高速壳层，但非对称延迟爆轰模型或与星周物质相互作用也无法排除。

Abstract: High velocity absorption lines of Ca II and Si II (high-velocity features, HVFs) are common in the early spectra of Type Ia supernovae (SNe Ia), but their physical origin is still poorly understood. We used 181 optical spectra of 56 SNe Ia that show HVFs in at least the Ca II NIR triplet to better understand the nature of HVFs.
  To model the profiles of HVFs we applied Gaussian fitting as well as spectrum synthesis modeling with the SYNOW code. We found that Gaussian fitting usually underestimates the velocity of HVFs compared to SYNOW, and derived a linear formula for correction. In accord with many previous studies, the observed properties of HVFs (velocity, strength, duration) were found to correlate with the general observables of SNe Ia (decline rate, spectral subtype). Brighter, slow-declining SNe usually exhibit stronger, higher velocity HVFs, while fainter objects typically show weak, low velocity HVFs. SNe Ia in early-type galaxies showed weaker and slower HVFs compared to events in late-type galaxies in our sample.
  Comparing these observational findings with theoretical predictions, we conclude that the overall physical picture of HVFs is quite complex, and could be explained by multiple models. A double detonation explosion model is promising as it is inherently capable of explaining the creation of high-velocity shells that may produce HVFs, but an asymmetric delayed detonation model or interaction with circumstellar material cannot be ruled out.

</details>

Abstract: Gravitational wave (GW) astronomy offers unprecedented insights into binary black hole (BBH) coalescence. However, many of the key quantities involved remain inaccessible to direct observations. The BBH merger rate density is deeply linked to both the merger efficiency and the distribution of delay time between binary formation and merger, neither of which is directly constrained by observations. Disentangling their impact on the merger rate represents a highly non-trivial endeavour. Here we present a semi-parametric BBH population model, based on population synthesis simulations of both isolated and dynamically-formed BBHs, anchored on an observation-driven, metallicity-dependent star formation history. We parametrise the merger efficiency of these two formation channels, fitting the model to GW events from the Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog 5.0 within a hierarchical Bayesian framework. Our analysis suggests that the isolated BBH merger efficiency should be lowered by a factor $\mathcal{O}(10)$ relative to standard population synthesis results. The dynamical channel requires an efficiency more than an order of magnitude larger than the isolated one to reproduce current GW observations. Nevertheless, we find that the two channels provide comparable contributions to the observed number of events. Finally, we introduce a parametrisation for the delay time distribution of the isolated BBHs. We derive a distribution consistent with the observed local merger rate and show its degeneracy with the merger efficiency.

Abstract: X-ray pulse-profile modeling can measure neutron-star radii, but its physical interpretation remains limited by the largely phenomenological description of the surface hotspots. Here we connect the X-ray hotspots directly to the pulsar magnetospheric structure and jointly infer the mass, radius, and magnetic geometry of the massive millisecond pulsar PSR J0740+6620 from \emph{NICER} and \emph{XMM-Newton} observations. Using a GPU-accelerated implementation, we compare two low-dimensional magnetic configurations that can produce non-antipodal emission: a centered axisymmetric dipole--quadrupole and a shifted dipole. The shifted-dipole model is decisively favored under the adopted priors, with $Δ\ln Z=5.65$ (Bayes factor $\sim285$), and yields $M=2.059^{+0.068}_{-0.069}\,M_{\odot}$ and $R=13.28^{+1.47}_{-1.07}\,\mathrm{km}$, broadly consistent with previous phenomenological analyses, while requiring nearly symmetric heating of the two polar caps. Both the marginalized shifted-dipole posterior and the timing-compatible representative require an effective X-ray-emitting footprint much smaller than the nominal force-free polar cap calibrated for a centered dipole. This compact footprint is not reproduced by our exploratory photon-conversion estimates and instead points to missing physics in the mapping from magnetospheric currents to observable X-ray emission. Our results show that physically motivated hotspots can preserve the mass--radius inference while turning hotspot morphology into a probe of pulsar magnetospheric structure and emission physics.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 两颗脉冲星的散射指数与Kolmogorov湍流一致，多数脉冲星散射指数较平，提示存在以局域湍流为主的复杂散射环境。多数轮廓在200 MHz以下由散射主导。测得的固有幂律指数较小，绝对值介于0至0.3之间：五颗脉冲星固有宽度随频率减小，两三颗表现平坦，四颗随频率增大。在考虑偏差后，多数脉冲星的调制参数随频率下降，三颗表现平坦。

Conclusion: 脉冲宽度随频率的演化可由固有射电束变窄、轮廓成分出现以及二者之间的平衡来解释。调制分析表明，多数脉冲星在较低射电频率下单脉冲辐射更加不规则，与振幅调制增强一致。

Abstract: Aims. We aim to illuminate how the pulsars' integrated pulse profiles and single-pulse modulation evolve with radio frequency, while separating intrinsic and propagation effects. Methods. We present integrated pulse profiles and phase-resolved modulation indices for 12 radio pulsars at up to five frequency bands. Our wideband dataset was systematically acquired with the Nancay Radio Observatory telescopes and the uGMRT, covering the frequency range 10 MHz to 2.8 GHz, including NenuFAR, LOFAR FR606, uGMRT, and NRT observations. We carefully measured the pulse widths and scattering times using multi-component profile fitting. We employed a profile width scaling model to separate the profile evolution into scattering and intrinsic contributions. We quantified the pulsars' single-pulse variability across frequency. Results. We describe the pulse profile evolution. Two pulsars have scattering indices compatible with Kolmogorov turbulence, while the majority have flatter scattering indices. This suggests complex scattering environments, dominated by localised turbulence. Complex scattering behaviour is seen in some pulsars, and most profiles are scattering-dominated below 200 MHz. The measured intrinsic power law indices are small, with absolute values between 0 and 0.3. Five pulsars exhibit decreasing intrinsic widths, two or three pulsars show flat behaviour, and four pulsars have increasing profile widths with frequency. Accounting for biases, most pulsar modulation parameters decrease with frequency, while three pulsars show flat behaviour. Conclusions. The pulse width evolution is explained by intrinsic radio beam narrowing, the emergence of profile components, and the balance between the two. Our modulation analysis indicates that the single-pulse emission from most of our pulsars becomes more erratic towards lower radio frequencies, consistent with increasing amplitude modulation.

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于人工神经网络（ANN）的高效代理模型，用于模拟单区动力学同步辐射自康普顿（SSC）辐射代码，以加速伽马射线暴（GRB）余辉的贝叶斯参数推断，并将其应用于GRB 190114C的宽波段观测拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极高能（$E>100$ GeV）GRB余辉辐射可由同步辐射自康普顿（SSC）解释，但精确建模需要求解含Klein-Nishina抑制的时变耦合粒子-光子动力学方程的全数值代码。这类代码计算成本极高，难以用于MCMC等传统参数推断方法，因此需要构建高效的代理模型。

Method: 训练一个基于人工神经网络（ANN）的代理模型，用以模拟单区动力学SSC辐射代码。训练数据为100,800条光谱的参数网格样本，模型设定为在恒定密度星际介质（ISM）中传播的无限薄球形激波。随后在该代理框架下执行基于MCMC的贝叶斯后验参数估计，并拟合GRB 190114C从X射线到TeV γ射线的不同时期宽波段余辉观测。

Result: ANN能良好重现数值光谱，在整个频率范围内相对误差中位数一般低于2%。对GRB 190114C的拟合得到：各向同性能量当量动能 $E_{\rm k,iso}\simeq7.2\times10^{54}$ erg，滑行体洛伦兹因子 $Γ\gtrsim500$，ISM密度 $n\simeq0.1\,{\rm cm^{-3}}$。

Conclusion: 模型光曲线在长时间尺度上与观测的偏差表明ISM环境假设不一致，而径向分层的external medium（如Aguilar-Ruiz等人所指出的）更为可取。该工作展示了ANN代理模型在GRB高能余辉参数推断中的高效性与可行性。

Abstract: The very high-energy ($E > 100$ GeV) afterglow emission in gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) can be explained by synchrotron self-Compton (SSC). Modeling this emission accurately requires fully numerical codes that solve the time-dependent coupled particle--photon kinetic equations while accounting for Klein--Nishina suppression. However, their substantial computational cost makes them impractical for traditional parameter inference techniques such as Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling. In this work, we present a highly efficient surrogate model based on artificial neural networks (ANNs) that emulates a one-zone kinetic SSC radiation code. To train our ANN, we use a multi-dimensional model parameter grid that constitutes a sample of 100,800 spectra produced by an infinitely thin spherical blast wave propagating inside a constant density interstellar medium (ISM). The ANN reproduces the numerical spectra well, with the median relative error generally below 2 per cent across the full frequency domain. Using this surrogate framework, we perform Bayesian parameter posterior estimation via MCMC and fit the broadband GRB afterglow observations of GRB 190114C from X-rays to TeV $γ$-rays at different epochs. We find an energetic jet with isotropic-equivalent kinetic energy of $E_{\rm k,iso}\simeq7.2\times10^{54}$ erg moving with a coasting bulk Lorentz factor of $Γ\gtrsim500$ into an ISM with density $n\simeq0.1\,{\rm cm^{-3}}$. The deviation of our model light curve from observations on long timescales suggests that an ISM environment is inconsistent and a radially stratified external medium, as pointed out by \citet{Aguilar-Ruiz+26}, is preferred instead.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出受被动动态行走启发的框架：训练早期用倾斜重力场+课程奖励创造类斜坡条件以发现经济步态，随后移除全部PDW引导再优化，无需参考轨迹/步态相位/接触时序。29-DoF Unitree G1五种子实验在0.5–2.0 m/s下降低机械运输成本6.8–15.2%且不损速度跟踪；结合运动先验的全向运动速度匹配成本降18.7%，硬件上前向成本降16.3%（有先验）与4.5%（无先验，处于试验间波动内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 节能的类人行走需要发现机械上经济的步态协调，而非仅仅降低执行器出力。现有强化学习主要通过与做功相关的奖励惩罚间接促进效率，只能间接引导行走的步到步力学，难以直接搜索经济的步态协调方式。

Method: 受被动动态行走（PDW）启发的框架：早期训练采用倾斜重力场在平坦碰撞几何上辅助矢状面前进，并配合课程耦合奖励项，暂时构造等效斜坡条件以利于经济步态的发现；随后在标称动力学优化前移除所有PDW专用引导。框架不使用参考轨迹、步态相位或接触时序。另设奖励匹配对照以分离引导期更快的步态获取与倾斜本身对收敛经济性的额外贡献，并进行机械功分解。

Result: 在29自由度Unitree G1上五种子前向运动研究中，0.5–2.0 m/s指令速度范围内机械运输成本降低6.8–15.2%，速度跟踪无退化；机械功分解将该降低归因于正向执行器做功。框架可扩展至无辅助全向运动，在行走专用运动先验提供运动学协调后收益保持，组合使速度匹配运输成本降低18.7%。硬件上前向运输成本在有运动先验时降低16.3%，无先验时降低4.5%，后者处于试验间波动范围内。

Conclusion: 等效斜坡式的临时PDW引导可在不引入参考轨迹、相位或接触时序的前提下，帮助强化学习发现更经济的行走协调，并在移除引导后仍保留于收敛策略中；该收益来自正向执行器做功的减少，在仿真与硬件上均得到验证，且可与行走运动先验互补用于全向运动。

Abstract: Learning energy-efficient humanoid locomotion requires discovering mechanically economical gait coordination, not merely reducing actuator effort. Reinforcement learning promotes efficiency through effort-related reward penalties, which guide the step-to-step mechanics of walking only indirectly. This article proposes a framework inspired by passive dynamic walking (PDW) that temporarily creates slope-equivalent conditions favorable to economical gait discovery and removes all PDW-specific guidance before nominal-dynamics optimization. During early training, a tilted-gravity field assists sagittal progression on flat collision geometry, complemented by curriculum-coupled reward terms. The core framework requires no reference trajectories, gait phases, or contact schedules. In a five-seed forward-locomotion study on a 29-DoF Unitree G1, the framework reduces mechanical cost of transport by 6.8-15.2% over commanded speeds of 0.5-2.0m/s without degrading velocity tracking. Mechanical-work decomposition attributes the reduction to positive actuator work, and reward-matched comparisons separate the guided regime's faster gait acquisition from the tilt's additional benefit to converged economy. The framework extends to unassisted omnidirectional locomotion, where its benefit persists once a walking-specific motion prior supplies kinematic coordination, the combination reducing speed-matched cost of transport by 18.7%. On hardware, forward cost of transport falls by 16.3% with the motion prior and by 4.5% without it, the latter within the trial-to-trial spread.

</details>


### [21] [In-Context Learning for Robots: Methods and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36012)
*Haojian Huang,Zexi Li,Junhao Guo,Yehang Zhang,Wenxuan Peng,Bohan Zhou,Weilin Ruan,Leyi Wu,Chenxu Wang,Jianchong Su,Binghui Xie,Wosong Chen,Yingjie Xu,Tianhao Zhou,Suzeyu Chen,Pukun Zhao,Jiaqi He,Xinyi Li,Runze Li,Peiran Dong,Shaoxiang Dang,Jing Huang,Yingbing Chen,Yifan Chang,Tianyi Zhang,Shiyuan Deng,Haozhi Wang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Huaping Liu,James Cheng,Rui Shao,Donglin Wang,Yaochu Jin,Jianye Hao,Ying-Cong Chen,Yinchuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于机器人上下文学习（ICL）的综述，围绕"上下文证据→物理执行"的接口将文献划分为四类：上下文条件策略、几何示教迁移、世界模型控制、技能与智能体执行，并分析其迁移假设与评测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人需要从少量示教与交互中推断新任务要求并转化为恰当动作，而现有ICL研究分散、术语与假设各异。作者希望厘清不同方法如何把上下文证据接入执行过程，以及它们在对象、环境、执行条件变化时保留教学要求的条件，从而建立方法设计与评测实践之间的联系。

Method: 采用文献综述与分类学方法：以"上下文证据与执行的接口"为组织维度，区分四类机制——上下文条件策略（context-conditioned policies）、几何示教迁移（geometric demonstration transfer）、基于世界模型的控制（world-model-based control）、技能与智能体执行（skill- and agent-based execution）；对每类比较其迁移假设，以及训练、对应关系（correspondence）与记忆在使上下文有用中所起的作用；并覆盖操作与导航两类任务。

Result: 通过跨操作与导航领域的比较，揭示各类接口在迁移假设上的差异，并区分出三种评测维度：对教学的响应性、物理迁移能力、以及从保留经验中获益；同时给出将组合式任务获取、可靠迁移与"物理递归自我改进"相衔接的研究议程。摘要未报告定量实验数据。

Conclusion: 面向通用机器人的ICL研究应围绕接口层面统一理解迁移假设，并配套区分响应性、物理迁移与经验积累收益的评测；其长远目标是把组合式任务学习与忠实迁移结合，实现物理递归自我改进——即经验提升后续任务的学习能力。

Abstract: General-purpose robots must infer what a new task requires and translate that understanding into appropriate physical action. In-context learning (ICL) for robots supports this process by using demonstrations and interaction to direct existing competence with neural parameters held fixed during deployment. We organize this literature review around the interfaces connecting contextual evidence to execution, distinguishing four families: context-conditioned policies, geometric demonstration transfer, world-model-based control, and skill- and agent-based execution. Comparing these interfaces clarifies their transfer assumptions and the roles of training, correspondence, and memory in making context useful. Across manipulation and navigation, we examine how these mechanisms preserve taught requirements as objects, environments, and execution conditions change. This analysis links method design to evaluation practices that distinguish responsiveness to teaching, physical transfer, and benefits from retained experience. The resulting agenda connects compositional task acquisition and faithful transfer with physical recursive self-improvement, in which experience improves the ability to learn subsequent tasks.

</details>


### [22] [SAKI: Skill Assembly and Kinematic Imitation from Human Videos for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36031)
*Yijie Lu,James Zhao,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAKI框架，将人类视频中的操作技能获取、跨演示技能组装与闭环全身执行相连接，实现长时程移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频学习操作技能多局限于桌面场景；扩展到长时程移动操作，需在场景与机器人构型变化时迁移并组合已演示的交互。

Method: SAKI准备以物体为中心的可复用技能，保留任务关键交互并允许迁移路径自适应；依据给定目标与任务依赖选择、排序技能，将物体角色绑定到当前场景，并在技能间传递场景估计与机器人构型；全身运动学模仿生成基座、手臂与夹爪的协调运动，执行中用持续物体估计维持跨视角任务参考，并以视觉反馈更新剩余轨迹。

Result: 真实机器人实验证明技能可跨布局复用，且能将独立演示的交互组合为连续的移动任务（如整理、擦拭）；消融表明在全身优化与视觉反馈固定时，任务条件化的参考准备显著提升长时程任务完成率。

Conclusion: 任务条件化的技能准备与全身运动学模仿结合，可有效支持跨场景的长时程移动操作技能复用与组合，验证了所提框架的有效性。

Abstract: Learning from human videos offers a promising route to acquiring diverse manipulation skills. Extending this capability beyond tabletop settings to long-horizon mobile manipulation requires adapting and composing demonstrated interactions across changing scenes and robot configurations. We present Skill Assembly and Kinematic Imitation (SAKI), a framework connecting human-video skill acquisition, cross-demonstration assembly and closed-loop whole-body execution. SAKI prepares reusable object-centric skills that preserve task-critical interactions while allowing transfer paths to adapt. Given a goal and supplied task dependencies, it selects and orders skills, binds their object roles to the current scene, and carries scene estimates and robot configuration between successive skills. Whole-body kinematic imitation generates coordinated base, arm and gripper motion. During execution, persistent object estimates maintain task references across viewpoint changes, while visual feedback updates remaining trajectories. Real-robot experiments demonstrate skill reuse across layouts and the composition of independently demonstrated interactions into continuous mobile tasks, including tidying and wiping. Ablation results show that task-conditioned reference preparation substantially improves long-horizon task completion with whole-body optimisation and visual feedback held fixed. Check https://aus.bot/research/saki/ for video demos!

</details>


### [23] [MagNav: A Dual-Core Magnetic Track Guidance Framework for Lighting-Invariant Navigation in Two-Wheeled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36091)
*Arpita Kumari,Anupam Chatterjee,Richa Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于五通道模拟霍尔传感器阵列的磁轨循迹自平衡两轮倒立摆机器人，采用串级PID控制（内环IMU+互补滤波平衡，外环磁传感转向），配步进电机精确力矩控制，实现光照无关的稳定循迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两轮倒立摆(TWIP)机器人是研究欠驱动、天然不稳定系统控制的理想平台，但为其增加自主循迹功能较难，因为转向与平衡强耦合。现有方案多采用红外传感器，受光照（阳光、阴影）影响大，仅适合室内使用，缺乏光照无关的可靠循迹方案。

Method: 采用五通道模拟霍尔效应传感器阵列构建磁轨制导系统以规避光学干扰；控制上使用串级PID：内环基于IMU数据配合互补滤波维持平衡，外环依据磁传感器读数调整转向；执行机构采用步进电机提供精确力矩控制，无需额外旋转编码器；同时加入光学传感器模块用于对比。

Result: 在超过10,000 Lux的强光环境下，磁制导系统仍能保持精确循迹；而光学系统则丢失精度，有时甚至失效。对比实验验证了磁方案的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该设计为工厂、仓库、户外路径等场景提供了一种可靠、不受光照影响的自主导航方案，解决了TWIP机器人循迹中转向与平衡耦合及光学传感易受干扰的问题。

Abstract: Two-Wheeled Inverted Pendulum (TWIP) robots are useful for studying how to control systems that are naturally unstable and have fewer actuators than degrees of freedom. Adding autonomous line-following to these robots is challenging because steering and balancing are closely linked. Most existing systems use infrared sensors, which can be affected by changes in lighting, such as sunlight or shadows, making them reliable only indoors. This paper presents a self-balancing robot that can follow a line using a magnetic track guidance system. By using a five-channel analog Hall-effect sensor array, the robot is not affected by optical interference. The control system uses a cascaded PID structure: the inner loop keeps the robot balanced using data from an inertial measurement unit with a complementary filter, while the outer loop adjusts steering based on the magnetic sensor readings. Stepper motors provide precise torque control without needing extra rotary encoders. For comparison, an optical sensor module was also included. Tests show that the magnetic guidance system keeps accurate tracking even in very bright lighting, over 10,000 Lux, while the optical system loses accuracy and sometimes fails. This design provides a reliable, lighting-independent solution for autonomous navigation in places like factories, warehouses, and outdoor paths.

</details>


### [24] [Scouting the Dynamics Gap: Test-Time Policy Adaptation via Action-Outcome Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36107)
*Yishu Li,Liyuan Geng,Xinyi Mao,Amber Li,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT：一种动力学感知的元学习框架，通过共享信念隐空间让操作策略在部署时利用动作-结果预测误差在线更新内部信念，实现快速测试时自适应与sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略在受控环境中表现良好，但面对未观测的物理属性与动力学时需在部署中快速适应。现有测试时自适应方法多依赖稀疏标量奖励，未能利用物理交互中丰富的几何与动力学反馈。

Method: 提出SCOUT动力学感知元学习框架：动作预测策略与前向动力学模型通过共享的信念隐空间耦合。元训练中，内循环以动力学预测误差（针对观测到的动作结果）更新共享信念隐变量，外循环优化网络以改进动作选择。部署时，智能体依据动作-结果不匹配更新信念隐变量，从而在线推断并适应未知物理动力学。

Result: 在仿真操作基准上显著加速在线自适应，并在真实世界实现稳健的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 基于共享信念隐变量的动力学感知元学习可使策略在不产生灾难性遗忘的前提下在线自适应，是提升机器人操作策略部署适应性与现实迁移的有效途径。

Abstract: While pretrained robotic policies exhibit impressive capabilities in controlled environments, unobserved physical properties and dynamics require these policies to rapidly adapt during deployment. Existing test-time adaptation methods typically rely on sparse scalar rewards, failing to exploit the rich geometric and dynamic feedback from the environment during physical interaction. To address this challenge, we propose SCOUT, a dynamics-aware meta-learning framework that enables manipulation policies to rapidly adapt by continuously revising their internal beliefs about environment dynamics. Our approach couples an action-prediction policy with a forward dynamics model via a shared belief latent space. During meta-training, an inner loop updates this shared belief latent by minimizing the dynamics prediction error against the observed action outcome, while the outer loop optimizes the network for action selection. At deployment, this structure allows the agent to infer and adapt to unknown physical dynamics on the fly. By updating its latent belief based on action-outcome mismatches, the policy automatically adapts without risking catastrophic forgetting. We demonstrate that SCOUT significantly accelerates online adaptation across simulated manipulation benchmarks and achieves robust sim-to-real transfer in the real world. Project webiste can be found here: https://liy1shu.github.io/SCOUT/

</details>


### [25] [KPI: A Promptable Kernel for Physical Interaction on Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36151)
*Yikai Wang,Honghao Zhu,Xiao Hu,Hao Zhang,Zelin Wang,Yip Fun Yeung,Ding Zhao,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 KPI（可提示的物理交互内核），插入轨迹源与未修改的全身跟踪器之间，用"轨迹+契约"替代预置控制器，实现人形机器人通用接触交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身跟踪策略虽具备良好泛化性，但泛化性仅存在于轨迹中，轨迹本身无法充分表达接触时应产生的交互行为；单任务策略需在仿真中联合优化轨迹与控制器，而通用栈通常依赖预设或手工选择的控制器，二者难以兼得。

Method: KPI 位于轨迹源与未修改的全身跟踪器之间。轨迹源下发"契约"，按方向指定三种模式：跟踪、柔顺、保持力区间；内核依据跟踪误差与力/力矩估计，以接触频率自适应调节双臂的刚度、阻尼、参考轨迹与前馈力。并结合智能体框架：由视觉-语言智能体从单条指令同时生成参考轨迹与契约，无需任务专用代码。

Result: 成功演示了指令驱动的绞盘操作（转动曲柄将另一台机器人完全吊离地面）、开门与箱体搬运，并完成脚本化的表面交互实验。

Conclusion: 通过在接触点引入可提示的交互内核并解耦轨迹规划与接触控制，可在保持通用全身跟踪能力的同时获得任务级接触行为，且仅需自然语言指令即可部署。

Abstract: Humanoids now walk, balance and reach with remarkable generality: one whole-body tracking policy follows references from a human, or from an end-to-end policy. That generality travels in the trajectory, and a trajectory alone carries limited information about the interaction it should produce: at contact, the executing controller determines how the robot behaves. Single-task policies usually reach hard interactions by optimising trajectory and controller together in simulation; general stacks usually assume a preset or hand-chosen controller. We present KPI, a promptable kernel for physical interaction between the trajectory source and an unmodified whole-body tracker. Instead of a controller fixed before the task, the trajectory source sends a contract: per direction, track, comply, or hold a force range. From tracking error and a wrench estimate, the kernel adapts the arms' stiffness, damping, reference and feedforward toward it at contact rate. We demonstrate KPI through an agentic framework: from one instruction, a vision-language agent writes both the reference trajectory and the contract, with no task-specific code. We demonstrate instruction-driven winch operation, door opening, and box transport, alongside scripted surface-interaction experiments. In the winch demonstration, the humanoid is able to turn a crank to hoist a second robot fully off the ground.

</details>


### [26] [SkillWeaver: Agentic Exploration over Neural Interaction Skills for Scalable Robot Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36171)
*He Zhu,Lusen Zhao,Kwan Man Cheng,Su Li,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillWeaver：一个基于神经交互技能（NIS）的智能体框架，通过闭环技能调用与验证器引导的树搜索自主生成机器人经验，规模化产出39.1K示范并蒸馏为视觉运动策略，显著提升泛化与sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作采集机器人数据成本高、难以扩展到多样环境与长时程任务；仿真虽可扩展，但现有数据生成流程依赖开环控制器、脚本化技能序列或任务专用程序，缺乏自主性与通用性。

Method: 提出SkillWeaver智能体框架，将可复用、参数化、闭环的神经交互技能（NIS）暴露给推理智能体。VLM智能体根据任务与环境推理下一步动作，调用并参数化NIS与环境交互，观察结果并生成验证、反思与记忆以指导后续探索。NIS实例化为强化学习策略用于闭环接触丰富操作，探索组织为验证器引导的树搜索，无需预定执行流程。

Result: 自主扩展至14.1K场景、39.1K示范，并蒸馏为视觉运动策略。在仿真基准与真实操作任务上，基于SkillWeaver生成经验的训练显著提升对新颖物体、空间布局、任务与环境的泛化能力，并实现零样本与少样本sim-to-sim、sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于神经交互技能的智能体式探索可作为机器人数据生成的可扩展替代方案。

Abstract: Large-scale demonstrations have driven unprecedented progress in robot learning, yet collecting robot data through teleoperation is expensive and difficult to scale to diverse environments and long-horizon tasks. Simulation offers a scalable alternative, but existing data-generation pipelines often rely on open-loop controllers, scripted skill sequences, or task-specific programs. We introduce SkillWeaver, an agentic framework that autonomously generates robot experience by exploring over Neural Interaction Skills (NIS): reusable, parameterized, closed-loop policies that expose learned physical interaction capabilities to a reasoning agent. Given a task and a simulated environment, a VLM agent reasons about what to do next, invokes and parameterizes NIS to interact with the environment, observes their outcomes, and generates verification, reflection, and memory to guide subsequent exploration. We instantiate NIS as reinforcement-learned policies for closed-loop, contact-rich manipulation and organize exploration as verifier-guided tree search, enabling the agent to discover successful long-horizon behaviors without relying on predetermined execution pipelines. SkillWeaver scales autonomously to 39.1K demonstrations across 14.1K scenes, which we distill into visuomotor policies. Across simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation, training on SkillWeaver-generated experience substantially improves generalization to novel objects, spatial configurations, tasks, and environments, and enables zero- and few-shot sim-to-sim and sim-to-real transfer. Our results suggest agentic exploration over neural interaction skills as a scalable alternative for robot data generation.

</details>


### [27] [Test-Time Adaptation of Manipulation Policies Under Actuator Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36182)
*Som Sagar,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TeAR，一种策略无关方法，通过实时遥测（关节温度、电流、电压）校正冻结操作策略的输出动作，使其适应硬件退化，无需在线微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略通常假设指令动作与训练时的运动一致，但真实硬件在运行数小时后会因电机发热、电流饱和、电压跌落等导致相同动作产生更弱、延迟或噪声更大的运动。现有板载遥测信号仅用于日志记录或安全检查，未被用于策略自适应。

Method: TeAR在动作到达底层控制器前对其进行校正，将冻结策略转化为遥测条件策略。它训练一个轻量级Transformer，将拟输出动作与实时执行器遥测结合，对动作各分量进行放大、衰减或偏置调整。方法为策略无关。

Result: 在覆盖8个策略家族、5类操作任务的18组策略-任务对上进行评估。在退化模型失配的配对评估中，TeAR成功率为31.8%，基策略为25.6%，假定模型逆为30.6%。在物理机械臂上，TeAR在发热条件下将成功率提升10-15%，且无需机器人端微调。

Conclusion: 利用板载遥测对动作进行在线校正，可有效缓解硬件退化导致的操作策略性能下降，且该方法策略无关、轻量、无需在线微调，具有良好的通用性与实用性。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies are usually trained under the assumption that a commanded action produces the same motion as it did during training even after hours of operation. Real hardware violates this assumption as the motors gradually heat up, current saturates near contact, voltage sags under load, thus the same policy action can produce a weaker, delayed, or noisier motion. These conditions are already measured by onboard telemetry, such as joint temperature, motor current, and supply voltage, yet this signal is typically used only for logging or safety checks rather than policy adaptation. We introduce Telemetry-Aware Action Rectification (TeAR), a policy-agnostic method that turns a frozen manipulation policy into a telemetry-conditioned policy by rectifying its outgoing action before it reaches the low-level controller. TeAR learns a lightweight Transformer that combines the proposed action with live actuator telemetry and amplifies, damps, or biases individual action components. We evaluate TeAR across 18 policy-task pairs spanning 8 policy families and 5 manipulation tasks. In an additional paired evaluation with degradation-model mismatch, TeAR achieves 31.8% success, compared with 25.6% for the base policy and 30.6% for an assumed-model inverse. On a physical arm, TeAR improves success under heating by 10-15% without on-robot fine-tuning.

</details>


### [28] [Design and Validation of an Antagonistic Tendon-Driven Dexterous Robotic Hand with Bidirectional Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36241)
*Chunghyeon Lee,Hyukjun Kwon,Sungeon Kim,Saehyun Moon,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一款拟人化、人体尺度、轻量、3D打印的17自由度双向对拉腱驱动机械手，可在掌侧与背侧形成抓握闭合，并实现全部33种GRASP抓取类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统五指拟人机械手继承人类单向（掌侧）抓取工作空间，从对侧抓取需重新调整腕或臂姿态。现有可反转手多依赖非拟人、软体或任务专用指型，缺少基于常规五指拟人构型的双向抓取方案。

Method: 设计一款人体尺度（长200 mm）、轻量（220 g）、3D打印的17自由度拟人机械手，采用双向对拉腱走线机构：各关节屈伸（耦合关节除外）与内收外展均由主动驱动，无需被动回位弹簧；该走线使所有自由度可越过中立位，从而在掌侧或背侧形成抓握闭合。

Result: 实验测得平均电机到关节传动误差3.0%，平均关节传动带宽13.2 Hz，指尖最大力29 N，定位重复性达0.15 mm；Kapandji评分为8，成功完成全部33种GRASP分类抓取类型，并实现掌侧与背侧抓取任务。

Conclusion: 该研究在紧凑的人体尺度平台上验证了双向操作能力，表明拟人五指手可在不牺牲拟人构型的前提下实现掌侧与背侧双向抓取。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands typically reproduce human hand morphology but inherit its one-sided grasping workspace, requiring wrist or arm reorientation to grasp from the opposite side. Existing reversible hands generally rely on non-anthropomorphic, soft, or task-specific finger arrangements, whereas conventional five-digit anthropomorphic hands remain designed primarily for palmar-side grasping. This paper presents an anthropomorphic, human-scale (200 mm length), lightweight (220 g), 3D-printed, 17-DoF robotic hand built on a bidirectional antagonistic tendon-routing mechanism, in which flexion/extension (except the coupled joint) and abduction/adduction at joints are actively driven without passive return springs. The proposed routing mechanism allows all degrees of freedom to cross their neutral configuration and form grasp closures on either the palmar or dorsal side. Experimental evaluation demonstrates an average motor-to-joint transmission error of 3.0%, an average joint transmission bandwidth of 13.2 Hz, a maximum fingertip force of 29 N, and a positioning repeatability up to 0.15 mm. The hand further achieves a Kapandji score of 8, successfully performs all 33 GRASP Taxonomy grasp types, and performs palmar- and dorsal-side grasping tasks, validating bidirectional operation in a compact, human-scale platform.

</details>


### [29] [Bilinear World Models: Learning Representations with Structured Dynamics for Efficient Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36305)
*Antonio Pariente,Ignacio Boero,Nikolai Matni,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种JEPA风格的世界模型，将潜在动力学限制为双线性参数化形式，在构造上保证动作可恢复性并防止表征坍缩，兼顾高效规划与控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型学习任意的潜在动力学，缺乏结构约束，导致规划与控制效率低、易发生表征坍缩，且多局限于短时域离线规划。

Method: 提出JEPA风格世界模型，将潜在动力学约束为双线性参数化；该结构将建模负担转移至编码器，促使学习更丰富的表征并显式暴露系统的可控几何；同时结构性强制动作可恢复性，从构造上防止表征坍缩；并论证一大类非线性动力系统存在使其动力学变为双线性的变换。

Result: 在标准2D与3D控制任务上，可直接从高维观测中学习双线性参数化动力学的表征；规划时间缩短近三个数量级，同时保持甚至提升控制精度；在更长时域规划与实时控制等更严苛场景下同样成功。

Conclusion: 双线性动力学参数化虽看似受限，但适用面广且有效，使JEPA风格世界模型突破短时域离线规划的限制，迈向长时域规划与实时控制。

Abstract: World models jointly learn latent representations and dynamics that predict how high-dimensional observations evolve under actions. In this work, we propose a JEPA-style world model in which, rather than learning arbitrary latent dynamics, we restrict them to follow a bilinear parameterization. This structure enables efficient planning and control while shifting the modeling burden onto the encoder, encouraging richer representations that expose the controllable geometry of the system. In particular, this structured parameterization allows us to structurally enforce action recoverability, thereby preventing representation collapse by construction. Although prescribing a bilinear parametrization may appear restrictive, we show that a broad class of nonlinear dynamical systems admits a transformation under which the dynamics become bilinear. Empirically, we show across standard 2D and 3D control tasks that representations with bilinear-parameterized dynamics can be learned directly from high-dimensional observations, reducing planning time by nearly three orders of magnitude while retaining or even improving control accuracy. We also propose more demanding regimes of longer-horizon planning and real-time control, and demonstrate that our method succeeds in both, moving JEPA-style world models beyond short-horizon offline planning.

</details>


### [30] [ATLAS: Aligned Transport of Latent Structure for Reliable World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36333)
*Ke Fang,Yupu Yao,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出ATLAS训练目标，在潜在世界模型中显式保持关系几何并校准全局潜在分布，通过Wasserstein嵌入匹配提升规划稳定性与目标达成成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型依赖表示几何进行规划，但仅正则化潜在边缘分布无法决定用于动作选择的状态间关系，导致规划相关的新颖性结构在表示变换为规划器所用最终潜在时被削弱。

Method: ATLAS将信息丰富的编码器表示中的归一化成对结构迁移到规划潜在，并用Wasserstein嵌入匹配（WEMReg）通过一维Wasserstein-2传输校准其边缘分布；理论分析表明关系保持与边缘校准提供非冗余约束，并将有限候选规划稳定性与关系失真、潜在尺度失配及预测误差联系起来。方法在LeWM上实例化。

Result: 在PushT、TwoRoom和OGBench-Cube上，于低新颖性和高新颖性评估子集均提升平均目标达成成功率，其中高新颖性TwoRoom提升最大；表示与rollout诊断显示规划潜在中新颖性相关结构更强、边缘校准更好、多步预测误差更低。

Conclusion: 保持规划相关的潜在几何是可靠世界模型规划的重要要素，关系几何保持与边缘校准相结合可有效改善规划性能。代码见匿名链接。

Abstract: Latent world models rely on representation geometry for planning, yet regularizing the latent marginal alone does not determine the state-to-state relationships used for action selection. We show that this can cause planning-relevant novelty structure to be weakened as representations are transformed into the final latent used by the planner. We introduce Aligned Transport of Latent Structure (ATLAS), a training objective that explicitly preserves relational geometry while calibrating the global latent distribution. ATLAS transfers normalized pairwise structure from an informative encoder representation to the planning latent and uses Wasserstein embedding matching (WEMReg) to calibrate its marginal through one-dimensional Wasserstein-2 transport. Our analysis shows that relational preservation and marginal calibration impose non-redundant constraints, and connects finite-candidate planning stability to relational distortion, latent-scale mismatch, and prediction error. Instantiated in LeWM, ATLAS improves mean goal-reaching success across PushT, TwoRoom, and OGBench-Cube on both lower- and higher-novelty evaluation subsets, with the largest gain on higher-novelty TwoRoom episodes. Representation and rollout diagnostics further show stronger novelty-related structure in the planning latent, improved marginal calibration, and lower multi-step prediction error. Together, these results highlight preservation of planning-relevant latent geometry as an important ingredient for reliable world-model planning. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/atlas-world-model-72C4/.

</details>


### [31] [DORA: Divergence-Oriented Data-Relay Algorithm for Partially Connected Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36395)
*Jonathan Diller,Fernando Cladera,Camillo Jose Taylor,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出异构任务感知覆盖（HMAC）问题，并设计DORA算法，通过信息价值驱动通信，在多无人机间歇通信场景下显著提升任务相关目标发现效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机团队执行搜索与监测任务时常处于部分连接网络，机器人需在探索环境与向队友中继信息之间权衡。当机器人传感器能力异构时，对某机器人无用的观测可能对具备互补探测能力的队友至关重要，这一权衡更加突出。已有方法多基于时间调度通信，未充分考虑信息对团队的任务价值。

Method: 论文将问题形式化为异构任务感知覆盖（HMAC）问题，将区域完整多机器人覆盖与间歇通信下能力受限的任务相关目标（MRT）发现相耦合。提出DORA（面向分歧的数据中继算法），以信息对团队的价值驱动通信而非仅依据发现本身。DORA量化机器人当前信息状态与其对每个队友知识估计之间的任务相关分歧，综合了任务相关性、发现新颖性、传感器不确定性和信息年龄。

Result: 在四种不同物体密度与空间结构的仿真环境中评估DORA，并在实体无人机平台上进行了验证。结果显示，相比传统基于时间的通信调度方法，DORA将MRT解析延迟最多改善74.8%。

Conclusion: 以任务相关信息价值为导向的通信策略能有效应对异构多无人机在间歇通信下的探索-中继权衡，DORA在仿真与实体平台上均优于时间驱动调度方法。

Abstract: Teams of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) deployed for search and monitoring missions frequently operate as partially connected networks, forcing each robot to trade off exploring the environment against relaying information to teammates. This tradeoff is especially acute when robots are semantically heterogeneous: an observation that appears uninformative to the robot that made it may be critical to a teammate with complementary detection capabilities. In this work, we formalize this setting as the heterogeneous mission-aware coverage (HMAC) problem, which couples complete multi-robot coverage of an area with capability-constrained mission-relevant target (MRT) discovery under intermittent communication. We then present DORA, a divergence-oriented data-relay algorithm that drives communication by the value of information to the team rather than by discovery alone. DORA quantifies the mission-relevant divergence between a robot's current information state and its estimate of each teammate's knowledge, capturing mission relevance, discovery novelty, sensor uncertainty, and the age of information. We evaluate DORA in simulation across four environments with differing object densities and spatial structure, and validate it on a physical UAV platform. Our results show that DORA improves MRT resolution delay by up to 74.8% over traditional time-based communication scheduling methods.

</details>


### [32] [One from Infinity: Actualizing Futures from Pretrained World Models into Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36413)
*Bang Du,Yichen Xie,Shuqi Zhao,Yuxin Chen,Menglin Wu,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"actualization"任务范式：在冻结的视频世界模型之上，用仅60M参数的轻量actualizer实现任务条件下的未来选择与动作读出，无需微调重型世界模型主干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将世界模型转为可执行机器人策略时，需用大规模机器人数据和算力微调重型世界模型主干，成本高昂。作者认为昂贵的部分已在世界模型预训练中完成——其表征空间已涵盖多样的潜在未来，因此剩余工作只是选择能完成任务的未来并读出对应动作。

Method: 形式化"actualization"任务：在冻结世界模型提供的先验之上，学习任务条件下的选择与实现，由极小的actualizer模型完成。实现RoboActualizer，仅60M参数，基于冻结的世界模型编码器，由两个轻量DiT专家组成，通过flow matching联合预测未来latents与动作。训练可在单张32GB显存GPU上完成。

Result: 可训练参数量比现有WAM和VLA最多少100倍，在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0等仿真基准以及两个真实平台上的五个任务上取得优异性能，延迟仅39ms，支持实时控制。

Conclusion: 在世界模型已预训练的前提下，无需微调重型主干，仅需轻量actualizer即可高效地将世界模型转化为可执行机器人策略，兼顾性能、参数效率与实时性。

Abstract: A pretrained video world model admits many plausible futures for a scene, but a robot must realize the exact task-conditioned one. To turn world models into executable robot policies, existing methods fine-tune the heavy world model backbone using large-scale robot data and computational resources. Challenging this status quo, we argue that the expensive part has already been paid in the world model pretraining since the representation space of a video world model lays out the diverse potential futures. In this case, what remains is to select the future that accomplishes the task and to read out the actions that realize it. We formalize this task as actualization, which learns a task-conditioned selection and realization on top of a prior supplied by a frozen world model. This can be solved by a tiny actualizer model. We implement RoboActualizer with as few as 60M parameters on top of a frozen world model encoder. The actualizer is composed of two lightweight DiT experts that jointly predict future latents and actions by flow matching. The model can be trained entirely on a single GPU with 32 GB peak memory. With up to 100x fewer trainable parameters than existing WAMs and VLAs, RoboActualizer reaches great performance on simulation benchmarks including LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin 2.0 and five tasks on two real-world platforms, with a low latency of 39 ms that allows real-time control.

</details>


### [33] [FineART: Fine-grained Annotated Robotic Trajectory Dataset and Vision-Language-Action Model for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36416)
*Jade Choghari,Pepijn Kooijmans,Mansi Agarwal,Yusuf Umut Ciftci,Aseem Doriwala,Catherine Weaver,Mouli Sivapurapu,Kai Yang,Jackson Lee,Thomas Wolf,Pragna Mannam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出密集标注的双臂操作数据集FineART（40,543条轨迹、1,718小时、533,913个子任务、151个任务）及自预测下一子任务的视觉-语言-动作策略FineART-VLA，通过子任务预测式中期训练显著提升长时程复杂操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中的机器人需完成长时程、多步骤双臂任务，而非孤立单步动作。现有操作数据集难以支撑这一能力：单臂数据集虽达数十万轨迹，但通常每回合仅有一句高层指令；少数双臂数据集虽标注子任务，但覆盖时长比例极低，缺乏密集的子任务级监督。

Method: 构建FineART数据集，对151个任务下的40,543条双臂轨迹进行密集子任务级标注，形成533,913个子任务、总计1,718小时。提出FineART-VLA策略，让模型在中期训练阶段预测自身下一步子任务，从而获得细粒度时序与语义监督，并支持人类逐步子任务引导。

Result: 中期训练带来显著增益：空间消歧任务成功率由32.0%提升至100.0%；在未见长时程任务上，人工逐步子任务引导使成功率由16.0%提升至76.0%。在新机器人上仅需极少微调，数据量为未中期训练基线的一成，即可零样本泛化到新硬件上完全未见的任务。

Conclusion: 密集子任务标注与子任务自预测式中期训练是提升双臂长时程操作泛化性与数据效率的有效途径。作者开源全部数据集、模型权重与训练代码，为后续研究提供基础。

Abstract: Robots operating in real-world environments must execute complex, multi-step bimanual tasks over long horizons rather than single, isolated actions. Current manipulation datasets struggle to support this capability: although single-arm datasets reach hundreds of thousands of trajectories, they typically provide only one high-level instruction per episode while the rare bimanual effort that does label subtasks annotates only a fraction of its hours. We present FineART, a densely annotated bimanual manipulation dataset of 40,543 episodes, 1,718 hours, and 533,913 subtasks across 151 tasks. We also introduce FineART-VLA, a vision-language-action policy that predicts its own next subtask, and show that mid-training it this way yields substantial gains. Specifically, success on a spatial disambiguation task increases from 32.0% to 100.0%, and step-by-step human subtask guidance lifts success on an unseen long-horizon task from 16.0% to 76.0%. Furthermore, after minimal fine-tuning on a new robot, the policy requires one-tenth the data of baselines without mid-training and generalizes zero-shot to completely unseen tasks on the new hardware. We open-source the full dataset, model weights, and training code.

</details>


### [34] [Losing the name before the box: measuring and repairing what narrow fine-tuning costs a detector outside its deployment vocabulary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36426)
*Trung Minh Bui,Jongsul Moon,YoungOuk Kim,Jung-Hoon Hwang,Dongin Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出纵向评估协议，发现检测器微调到窄域后，域内精度上升但词汇未命名类别的top-K候选框覆盖度（C_τ）大幅下降，且该下降无法被域内指标或AP识别；失配具有结构一致性，并可通过无需训练的权重混合修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测器在大规模语料预训练后微调到窄域，域内精度提升就直接部署。但作者关注被忽视的风险：词汇表从未命名的物体（在障碍物检测与巡检中至关重要）覆盖能力是否在微调中退化。域内测试集不含此类样本，因此需要一种纵向协议来衡量预训练检查点与其微调后代之间的覆盖变化。

Method: 提出纵向协议：用同一预训练检查点与其微调后代对比，跟踪held-out top-K候选框覆盖度C_τ——在预训练覆盖但词表省略的类别上，检测器top-K区域仍覆盖的框比例。该指标源自开放世界候选框文献，但纵向解读是本文首创。在4种架构、3个域上评估，并考察检测AP、冻结阶梯（6个深度）、类别结构性一致性与修复方法（混入1/4预训练状态含归一化统计）。

Result: C_τ随域内精度上升而下降，在4种架构3个域上，对大于1024 px²的框下降5.12至63.35个百分点。域内指标和检测AP均无法识别该下降（AP对漏检和误命名框同等惩罚）。在同时评分两者的架构上，适应损失87%的AP却只损失1/5的覆盖；在冻结阶梯全部6个深度、每次运行中，命名均首先退化。三个无共享预训练的架构在哪些类别失去覆盖上保持一致，而模型从未学过的类别无损失。

Conclusion: 微调会结构化地损害对词表未命名类别的候选框覆盖，这一风险不被现有指标暴露。无需训练即可修复：混入1/4预训练状态（含归一化统计），在扫描的每个单元格上都提升覆盖，域内精度最多损失2.47个百分点，额外代价仅一次评估前向。

Abstract: A detector pretrained on a broad corpus is fine-tuned on a narrow domain, its in-domain accuracy improves, and it ships. We ask what happens meanwhile to its coverage of objects the vocabulary never names, which in obstacle detection and inspection carry the risk. No in-domain test set holds an example of one. We give a longitudinal protocol: one pretrained checkpoint against its own fine-tuned descendants. It tracks held-out top-$K$ proposal coverage $C_τ$: of categories pretraining covered and the vocabulary omits, the share of boxes a detector's top $K$ regions still cover. The quantity is the open-world proposal literature's; the longitudinal reading is not. $C_τ$ falls while in-domain accuracy rises, on four architectures and three domains, by $5.12$ to $63.35$ points on boxes above $1024$ px$^2$. No in-domain number identifies the fall, and neither does detection average precision, which charges a missed and a misnamed box alike. On the one architecture scoring both, adaptation costs $87\%$ of the AP against a fifth of the coverage, and the naming goes first at all six depths of its freeze ladder, every run. What breaks is structured: three architectures sharing no pretraining run agree on which categories lose coverage, and those a model never learned do not lose any. A repair follows and needs no training: mixing a quarter of the pretrained state back, normalisation statistics included, raises coverage on every cell swept for at most $2.47$ points of in-domain accuracy. Seeing it costs one extra evaluation pass.

</details>


### [35] [World4Scorer: Outcome-Grounded World Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36438)
*Jieyuan Pei,Meiyi Lu,Sining Ang,Yubo Zhao,Zhangyi Hu,Mingwei Xu,Haokai Ding,Wei Li,Zihan You,Jianwei Zheng,Li Yu,Yifeng Pan,Ji Tao,Rongjunchen Zhang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 World4Scorer，将生成-选择式规划器的打分器建模为轨迹条件化的 JEPA 式世界模型预测器：为每个候选轨迹预测一个状态并从中读出得分，用仿真结果标签监督所有候选、用已执行轨迹的真实未来锚定预测器，并结合场景匹配候选库与惯性重排序。在 NAVSIM-v2 达 SOTA，在闭环 Bench2Drive 表现优异，并在 OGBench-Cube 上提升操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成-选择式规划（generate-and-select）在 NAVSIM 上优于直接预测基线，但其打分器必须评估从未被执行过的候选轨迹。驾驶日志只记录已执行轨迹的未来，因此以"匹配日志未来"为监督会使其对备选候选的预测缺乏约束；仿真器则能为每个候选给出结果标签。因此需要一个既能利用仿真全候选标签、又能被真实场景演化锚定的打分机制。

Method: 1) 将打分器构建为轨迹条件化的 JEPA 式预测器：对每个候选轨迹预测一个状态，并从该状态读出该候选的分数。2) 用仿真器结果标签监督所有候选的状态，并用已执行轨迹的观测未来锚定预测器至真实场景演化；由于单一预测器产生所有候选状态，锚定可约束用于评估未执行计划的共享参数，而真实未来仅在训练时需要。3) 针对生成候选大多得分偏高的问题，引入场景匹配候选库补充低分计划以增强结果监督；针对逐帧选择可能冲突的问题，采用惯性重排序保持相邻选择的一致性。

Result: 在 NAVSIM-v2 上取得 state-of-the-art 性能；在闭环 Bench2Drive 上获得较强的适配系统结果；在固定 LeWM 世界模型与规划预算的前提下，基于结果的打分同样提升了 OGBench-Cube 基准上的操作规划性能。

Conclusion: 通过将打分器转化为轨迹条件化的 JEPA 式状态预测，并辅以仿真全候选结果监督与真实未来锚定，可在不依赖执行期真实未来的情况下对未执行计划做出可靠评估；该方法在开环与闭环自动驾驶基准及跨域操作规划任务上均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires choosing a safe and efficient plan as surrounding traffic evolves. Generate-and-select planners propose multiple trajectories and score them for execution, and they have outperformed representative direct-prediction baselines on NAVSIM. Their scorer must compare plans that were never executed. Driving logs record the future of only the executed trajectory, so matching the logged future can leave predictions for the alternatives unconstrained; a simulator, in contrast, can label the outcome of every candidate. We introduce World4Scorer, which builds the scorer as a trajectory-conditioned JEPA-style predictor: it predicts a state for each candidate and reads the candidate's scores from that state. Simulator outcome labels supervise the states of all candidates, and the observed future of the executed trajectory anchors the predictor to real scene evolution. Because one predictor produces every candidate's state, the anchor can constrain shared parameters used to score unexecuted plans, while the future itself is needed only during training. Generated candidates mostly score well, so a scene-matched bank adds low-scoring plans to the outcome supervision; framewise choices can conflict, so inertial re-ranking keeps consecutive selections consistent. World4Scorer achieves state-of-the-art NAVSIM-v2 performance and a strong adapted-system result on closed-loop Bench2Drive. With the LeWM world model and planning budget fixed, outcome-based scoring also improves manipulation planning on the OGBench-Cube benchmark.

</details>


### [36] [Staircase Policy: Streaming Inference for World-Action Models with Large Action Chunks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36471)
*Guoheng Sun,Chen Chen,Jin Wang,Ang Li,Teresa Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 STAIRCASE POLICY（S-WAM），将流匹配 VLA 转为 JEPA 式世界-动作模型，通过错位去噪与子块流式推理/训练，实现长时程执行、低首动作延迟与高吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAM）通过在未来观测条件下生成动作来提升机器人操作性能，但未来预测会加剧迭代式动作生成的高昂推理开销。动作分块（action chunking）虽可摊薄该成本，却在长执行时程中因后续动作仍依赖陈旧观测而导致性能退化。

Method: STAIRCASE POLICY 将流匹配 VLA 改造为 JEPA 风格的 WAM，把一个大动作块划分为处于错位去噪阶段的多个子块：近期动作生成后立即执行，后续动作继续精化；在每个子块边界处，基于最新观测重新预测未来潜变量并更新所有未执行动作，从而无需重复进行完整策略推理即可支持长时程执行。未来预测误差还可作为自适应分块的信号。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上达到 97.7%、LIBERO-Plus 上达到 87.9%，并在多种策略骨干与真实机器人任务上均有提升。实现每秒 292.7 个执行动作，在精度相当的情况下吞吐量为常规执行方式的 3.62 倍，同时将首动作延迟从 123.6 ms 降至 73.3 ms；配合额外推理优化，吞吐量进一步提升至每秒 642.9 个动作。

Conclusion: 该流式推理与训练框架通过错位去噪子块与潜变量重预测，缓解了动作分块在长时程下的陈旧观测问题，兼具高精度、低首动作延迟与高吞吐，并可推广至多种策略骨干及真实机器人场景。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) improve robotic manipulation by conditioning action generation on predicted future observations, but future prediction adds further inference overhead to already expensive iterative action generation. Action chunking can amortize this cost over multiple actions, yet performance degrades over long execution horizons because later actions remain conditioned on stale observations. We introduce STAIRCASE POLICY, a streaming inference and training framework that turns a flow-matching VLA into a JEPA-style WAM and partitions a large action chunk into sub-chunks at staggered denoising stages. Near-term actions are executed as soon as they become available, while later actions continue to be refined. At each sub-chunk boundary, the future latent is re-predicted from the latest observation and used to update all unexecuted actions, enabling long-horizon execution without repeated full policy inference. The resulting future-prediction error can further serve as a signal for adaptive chunking. S-WAM achieves 97.7% on LIBERO and 87.9% on LIBERO-Plus, and improves performance across multiple policy backbones and real-robot tasks. It reaches 292.7 executed actions per second, $3.62\times$ the throughput of conventional execution at comparable accuracy, while reducing time-to-first-action from 123.6 to 73.3 ms. With additional inference optimizations, throughput further increases to 642.9 actions per second.

</details>

*Bryan S. Guevara,Luis F. Recalde,Guanrui Li,Tiago Nascimento*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向四旋翼竞速的对偶四元数模型预测轮廓控制（DQ-MPCC），将位姿统一为单位对偶四元数并在其流形切空间投影轮廓误差；仿真与实机表明其门间穿越精度与圈速均优于基线MPCC。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼竞速要求激进姿态机动并精准穿越每一道门。传统MPCC在惯性系建立预测模型、在机体系定义姿态误差，坐标不统一，导致位姿—推力耦合下的轮廓误差刻画与实机迁移性受限。

Method: 以单位对偶四元数表示位姿，将轮廓误差投影到对偶四元数流形的切空间，并在期望机体系下表达；采用与常规模型相同的刚体动力学，在机体系内以统一的位姿—旋量（pose-twist）坐标建模，构造DQ-MPCC。通过蒙特卡洛软件在环仿真与11×4.5×3.65 m八门赛道的实机竞速，与基线MPCC在相同增益下对比，机载运行频率100 Hz。

Result: 相同增益下，DQ-MPCC所有完成飞行的门间穿越均保持在规定几何裕度内，而基线超出裕度；基线最差门偏差中位数从仿真到实机增大71.5%，DQ-MPCC反而降低10.1%。在所有仿真门穿越均满足几何裕度的配置中，DQ-MPCC最小圈速降低6.7%，实机飞行降低10.5%。

Conclusion: 在位姿—旋量统一坐标下以对偶四元数建模轮廓误差，可显著改善四旋翼竞速的门间穿越鲁棒性与仿真到实机的迁移性，并在保证几何裕度的同时提升圈速，具备100 Hz机载实时性。

Abstract: Quadrotor racing demands aggressive attitude and progress control while passing through every gate, and conventional quadrotor MPCC formulations state the prediction model in inertial coordinates and the attitude error in the body frame. We present a Dual-Quaternion Model Predictive Contouring Control (DQ-MPCC) for quadrotor racing in which the pose is a unit dual quaternion and the contouring errors are projected onto the tangent space of the dual quaternion manifold, expressed in the desired body frame: the same rigid-body dynamics as the conventional model, in unified pose-twist coordinates in the body frame. We compare DQ-MPCC against a baseline MPCC through Monte Carlo software-in-the-loop simulations and real-world racing on an eight-gate circuit of 11x4.5x3.65 m. With the same gains in simulation and hardware, DQ-MPCC keeps every crossing of its completed flights within the prescribed geometric margin, whereas the baseline exceeds it, its median worst-gate offset growing by 71.5% sim-to-real against a 10.1% decrease for DQ-MPCC. Among the configurations that keep every simulated gate crossing within the geometric margin, DQ-MPCC also reduces the minimum lap time by 6.7%, and by 10.5% in the real-world flights, while running onboard at 100 Hz.

</details>


### [38] [Closed-Form Cartesian Forward Kinetostatics for Spatial Multi-Segment Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36495)
*Ke Wu,Fangju Yang,Xiaohui Zhang,Junda He,Guanjun Bao,Jingang Yi,Jian S. Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于空间多段肌腱驱动连续体机器人的力—笛卡尔构型模型，具有求积形式闭式解，无需迭代平衡求解，计算效率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间肌腱驱动连续体机器人的正运动静力学通常需对每个驱动输入进行非线性平衡求解，计算成本高，缺乏高效的力—构型显式映射。

Method: 以笛卡尔骨架中心线与累积材料扭转作为广义坐标，推导应变度量与肌腱几何；通过变分平衡得到显式轴向与弯曲关系，并证明在所提模型下对于容许的纵向非螺旋布线存在零平衡材料扭转；解按段递推传播，避免迭代平衡求解，同时保留轴向变形、空间变化的轴向与弯曲刚度、肌腱布线直径及分段相关肌腱参与。

Result: 与全应变几何变应变模型（GVS）数值对比，单段与三段机器人最大长度归一化末端位置偏差分别为8.91×10^-6和1.01×10^-5；平均求解时间分别为1.52 μs和2.94 μs，相对基线加速约1864倍与3348倍。

Conclusion: 所提显式力—构型映射在保持与全应变模型一致精度的同时，显著降低计算量，验证了其在空间多段肌腱驱动连续体机器人正运动静力学中的计算优势。

Abstract: Forward kinetostatics of spatial tendon-driven continuum robots typically requires a nonlinear equilibrium solve for each actuation input. This paper develops a force-to-Cartesian-configuration model with a closed-form solution in quadratures for spatial multi-segment robots under tendon actuation. The Cartesian backbone centerline and accumulated material twist serve as generalized coordinates, from which the strain measures and tendon geometry are derived. Variational equilibrium yields explicit axial and bending relations and establishes zero equilibrium material twist within the proposed model for admissible longitudinal non-helical routing. The solution is propagated segment by segment without an iterative equilibrium solve, while retaining axial deformation, spatially varying axial and bending stiffnesses and tendon-routing diameter, and segment-dependent tendon participation. Numerical comparisons with a full-strain geometric variable-strain model (GVS) yield maximum length-normalized tip-position discrepancies of 8.91 x 10^-6 and 1.01 x 10^-5 for the single- and three-segment robots, respectively. Mean evaluation times of 1.52 μs and 2.94 μs, with corresponding speedups of approximately 1864x and 3348x over the baseline, demonstrate the computational advantage of the explicit force-to-configuration mapping in the reported benchmark.

</details>


### [39] [LIBERO-MAX: Do Robot Policies Adapt When the World Changes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36518)
*Yunbei Zhang,Zijian Jin,Yuanzhe Liu,Janet Wang,Xilun Zhang,Yuyou Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Daoan Zhang,Shuaicheng Niu,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang,Jihun Hamm,Ismini Lourentzou,Weirui Ye,Bo Liu,Peter Stone,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-MAX 基准，用 8000 组配对案例系统评估机器人在任务执行中途遭遇环境变化时的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人须在目标移动、视角变化或障碍出现后继续任务，但已有仿真鲁棒性基准多在重置时固定外部条件，忽视了这种时序性挑战，导致中途变化下的失败未被充分研究。

Method: 构建 LIBERO-MAX，包含 8,000 组配对案例，覆盖几何、观测、外观、杂物与路径等八类变化。每组在同一任务、初始状态、策略随机种子及事件前动作序列固定的条件下，对比有/无中途事件的任务执行，从而将事件相关退化与原本即存在的失败区分开；并评估十四种 VLA、混合及 world-action 策略，辅以相机控制与查询频率实验。

Result: 中途事件使十四种策略成功率下降 11.0–25.7 个百分点；事件画像显示各策略对几何与观测变化存在共性脆弱性，而不同策略家族的排名出现交错。相机控制实验表明鲁棒性既取决于变化条件下的能力，也取决于遭遇变化时所处轨迹；改变查询频率无法消除差距。

Conclusion: 配对协议与时序诊断使 LIBERO-MAX 成为可复现的测试平台，用于诊断执行中途变化引发的失败，并衡量机器人策略在世界变化下保持有效性的进展。

Abstract: Robots must often continue a task after a target moves, the viewpoint shifts, or an obstacle appears, even though their earlier observations and committed actions reflect the previous scene. Many simulation robustness benchmarks fix external conditions at reset, leaving this temporal challenge underexamined. We introduce LIBERO-MAX, a benchmark of 8,000 paired cases spanning eight types of changes to geometry, observations, appearance, clutter, and paths. Each pair compares task execution with and without a mid-task event, holding the task, initial state, policy seed, and pre-event action sequence fixed. This controlled comparison distinguishes event-associated regressions from failures already present without the change. Across fourteen current VLA, hybrid, and world-action policies, events reduce success by 11.0-25.7 percentage points. Event profiles reveal shared vulnerabilities to geometry and observation changes, while policy-family rankings interleave. Camera controls show that robustness reflects both competence under the changed conditions and the trajectory from which they are encountered; varying query cadence does not eliminate the gap. Together, the paired protocol and temporal diagnostics establish LIBERO-MAX as a reproducible testbed for diagnosing failures under mid-execution changes and measuring progress toward robot policies that remain effective as the world changes.

</details>


### [40] [Distilling Privileged Control Barrier Functions into RGB-Only Safety Filters for Dynamic Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36520)
*Seungyeon Yoo,Gawon Lee,Seungwoo Jung,Inkyu Jang,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出教师-学生视觉蒸馏框架，将特权CBF教师的安全行为迁移到仅用RGB的学生安全过滤器，无需在线渲染或显式3D重建，提升动态环境导航安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅RGB端到端视觉导航策略在真实动态环境中仍易发生碰撞，需要专门的安全层。现有视觉CBF方法依赖实时渲染或显式场景重建，且主要面向静态场景，难以在机载部署。

Method: 采用教师-学生视觉蒸馏：特权教师使用真值机器人与障碍物状态，在real-to-sim动态高斯溅射环境中构建CBF约束；学生仅输入短RGB历史、机器人速度和名义控制动作，直接输出安全动作。为缩小信息差，教师仅依据学生RGB历史中可观测到的障碍物构建约束，并考虑障碍物速度不确定性以增强对运动变化的鲁棒性；通过动作增强让学生接触多样化的安全与不安全名义动作，更好地学习安全边界。

Result: 实验表明，该方法优于视觉CBF基线，并在动态障碍物运动下提升了基于RGB的导航策略的安全性。部署时学生仅需RGB观测和机器人速度，无需显式3D重建或在线渲染。
