Conclusion: 该框架实现了可机载部署的RGB-only动态安全过滤，通过缩小教师-学生信息差与不确定性建模，有效提升动态环境下的导航安全。项目页面：https://syeon-yoo.github.io/distill-cbf-site/。

Abstract: RGB-only end-to-end visual navigation policies remain vulnerable to collisions in real-world dynamic environments, motivating a dedicated safety layer. Existing visual Control Barrier Function (CBF) approaches seek to provide safety from RGB observations, but often rely on real-time rendering or explicit scene reconstruction and are primarily designed for static scenes, limiting their practicality for onboard deployment. We propose a teacher-student visual distillation framework that transfers the safety behavior of a privileged CBF teacher to an RGB-only student filter for dynamic environments. The student maps a short RGB history, robot velocity, and a nominal control action directly to a safe action, while the teacher uses ground-truth robot and obstacle states in a real-to-sim dynamic Gaussian Splatting environment. To reduce the teacher-student information gap, the teacher constructs safety constraints only from obstacles observable within the student's RGB history. It also accounts for obstacle-velocity uncertainty to improve robustness to motion variations, while action augmentation exposes the student to diverse safe and unsafe nominal actions to better capture the safety boundary. At deployment, the student requires only RGB observations and robot velocity, without explicit 3D reconstruction or online rendering. Experiments show that the proposed method outperforms visual CBF baselines and improves the safety of RGB-based navigation policies under dynamic obstacle motion. Project page: https://syeon-yoo.github.io/distill-cbf-site/.

</details>


### [41] [Trajectory-Level Mode Guidance for Controllable Diffusion-Based Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36530)
*Tianyou Yu,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在干净轨迹空间中进行引导的扩散规划框架，通过时间步相关引导强度渐进注入轨迹先验，在保持多模态性的同时实现可控轨迹生成，并自然扩展到多机器人协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动规划通常存在多个可行解，多模态生成对灵活的多机器人协调尤为重要。扩散模型能自然学习轨迹分布，但如何在不限制生成的前提下融入粗糙、部分轨迹先验仍是难题。这类先验指示的是解空间中的期望区域而非单一解，因此需要既引入先验又保留多模态性的条件生成方法。

Method: 在干净轨迹空间引导生成，并采用时间步相关的引导强度渐进融入轨迹先验。在每个反向扩散步，重建的干净轨迹提供统一空间以整合规划代价与部分轨迹先验：规划代价通过基于梯度的精修引入；部分先验在对应噪声水平上以递减的引导强度渐进注入。早期强引导趋向先验，随后逐步释放约束以保留扩散模型固有的多模态性。通过加入机器人间碰撞代价，框架自然扩展到多机器人规划。

Result: 在单机器人与多机器人规划任务上的实验表明：可实现可控轨迹合成、多样化的可行解，以及安全的多智能体协调。

Conclusion: 所提方法在干净轨迹空间中以时间步相关的引导强度渐进融入轨迹先验，兼顾规划代价优化与多模态保留，并能拓展至多机器人协调，验证了可控性、多样性与安全性。

Abstract: Motion planning often admits multiple feasible solutions, making multimodal generation valuable, particularly for flexible multi-robot coordination. Diffusion models naturally learn such trajectory distributions, yet incorporating coarse and partial trajectory priors without restricting generation remains challenging. Such priors indicate a desirable region of the solution space rather than a single solution, motivating conditioned generation that preserves multimodality. In this paper, we guide trajectory generation in the clean trajectory space and progressively incorporate trajectory priors with a timestep-dependent guidance strength. At each reverse diffusion step, the reconstructed clean trajectory provides a unified space for integrating planning costs and partial trajectory priors. Planning costs are incorporated through gradient-based refinement, while the partial prior is progressively injected at the corresponding noise levels with decreasing guidance strength. This guides generation toward the prior in early stages while gradually releasing the constraint to preserve the inherent multimodality of the diffusion model. The framework naturally extends to multi-robot planning by incorporating inter-robot collision costs. Experiments on single- and multi-robot planning tasks demonstrate controllable trajectory synthesis, diverse feasible solutions, and safe multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [42] [Asymmetric Scout-Worker Reconnaissance for Route Validation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36537)
*Kashif Khurshid Noori,Jaskrit Singh,Athanasios Ch. Kapoutsis,Jing Xiao,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"侦察者-工作者"共生侦察框架，利用小型高机动侦察机器人只为较大工作机器人探索其可通过的路径段，从而在未知环境中为按序访问目标点规划可行路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侦察者与工作者的通过性模型不同：侦察者体型更小、机动性更强，其可通行的路线对工作者未必可行，因此工作者的可行性必须由侦察者的观测推断。现有探索与重规划方法通常假设单一通过性模型，并为同一机器人寻找最优路径，无法直接处理该非对称设定。

Method: 提出一种基于侦察者的共生（symbiotic）框架：利用侦察者的高机动性，仅探索未知环境中识别连接有序目标点的"工作者可行路径段"所必需的区域，并由侦察者观测推断工作者的通过性，进而在受阻路段上用经验证的工作者可行绕行进行修复。

Result: 在仿真与真实环境中的评估表明：该方法能够验证可行路线、用经验证的工作者可行绕行修复受阻路段，并相比基线探索与规划方法显著减少侦察者的行驶距离；真实室内部署进一步展示了侦察者穿行狭窄走廊以识别工作者可行路线。

Conclusion: 非对称侦察-工作者侦察问题需显式建模通过性差异；所提共生框架通过定向探索与可行性推断，在保证工作者路线可行的同时大幅降低侦察开销，并在真实场景中验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies asymmetric scout-worker reconnaissance in unknown environments, where a small, agile autonomous scout explores routes for a larger worker robot that must visit an ordered sequence of goal locations. Because the scout has a smaller footprint and greater mobility, a scout-traversable route may be infeasible for the worker; worker feasibility must therefore be inferred from scout observations. This setting is not explicitly addressed by existing exploration and replanning methods, which typically assume a single traversability model and seek optimal paths for the same robot performing the exploration. We introduce a symbiotic scout-based framework that exploits the scout's superior mobility to explore only the portions of the unknown environment needed to identify worker-feasible path segments connecting the ordered goals. Evaluations in simulated and real-world settings demonstrate that the proposed approach validates feasible routes, repairs blocked segments with validated worker-feasible detours, and substantially reduces scout travel compared to baseline exploration and planning methods. A real-world indoor deployment further demonstrates the scout navigating narrow corridors to identify a worker-feasible route.

</details>


### [43] [Reactive Real-Time Flow Policies via Asynchronous Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36540)
*Moritz Zoellner,Reece O'Mahoney,Ioannis Havoutis,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 异步执行会改变VLA的动作分布并限制响应性；作者提出递归流场蒸馏与先提议后解析两种机制来对齐分布，在LIBERO上追平原VLA，在RoboMimic上保留约80%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略（如VLA）泛化能力强，但推理延迟与实时控制需求相冲突；异步执行虽能消除动作块之间的停顿，但尚不清楚它是否保持原VLA的动作分布，而非马尔可夫演示可能导致分布偏移、削弱策略响应性。

Method: 提出两种互补机制以对齐异步策略与原VLA的动作分布：(1) 递归流场蒸馏（Recursive Flow-Field Distillation），利用VLA的动作生成流训练异步策略，并对其学到的分布进行理论刻画；(2) 先提议后解析（Propose-Resolve），异步生成多条动作序列，再用最新观测基于其在VLA动作分布下似然的轻量近似进行选择。

Result: 理论上刻画了所学分布；实验表明异步策略可生成原VLA几乎全部动作范围，而现有异步方法仅恢复其中一小部分。最终方法在LIBERO上达到与原VLA相当的成功率，在RoboMimic上保留约80%成功率，比现有异步方法高约30个百分点。

Conclusion: 异步执行在非马尔可夫演示下会产生与原VLA本质不同的动作分布并损害响应性；通过流场蒸馏与提议-解析的分布对齐，可在保持异步高效执行的同时恢复响应性，显著优于现有异步方法。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) have achieved remarkable generalization, but their inference delays can conflict with the demands of real-time control. Asynchronous execution avoids pauses between action chunks by predicting the next sequence of actions while the robot carries out the previous one. In this paper, we study whether asynchronous execution produces the same action distribution as the original VLA. We find that, for non-Markovian demonstrations, asynchronous execution can produce a fundamentally different action distribution, which can limit the policy's reactivity. In our method, we seek to restore this reactivity by aligning the asynchronously produced action distribution with that of the original VLA through two complementary mechanisms. First, Recursive Flow-Field Distillation trains the asynchronous policy using the VLA's action-generation flow. We characterize the learned distribution theoretically and show experimentally that our asynchronous policy can generate nearly the full range of actions the original VLA would produce, while existing asynchronous methods recover only a fraction of that range. Second, Propose-Resolve prepares multiple action sequences asynchronously and uses the latest observation to select among them based on a lightweight approximation of their likelihood under the VLA's action distribution. Our resulting method matches the original VLA's success on LIBERO and retains about 80% of its success on RoboMimic, about 30 percentage points more than existing asynchronous methods.

</details>


### [44] [Planning Oriented 3D Scene Completion via Coupled TUDF Occupancy Representation Learning from Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36543)
*Tianyou Yu,Pengfei Zhao,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向路径规划的3D场景补全框架，联合预测TUDF连续几何表示与体素占据图，并采用双向耦合学习，在未见环境中同时提升几何重建质量与规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航中部分可观测性问题突出：传感器覆盖有限与遮挡导致环境大面积未被观测。现有场景补全方法多聚焦于改进不完整建图或重建部分观测的3D结构，很少探讨如何设计场景补全以服务于路径规划等下游任务。

Method: 以部分LiDAR观测为输入，框架联合预测补全后的截断无符号距离场（TUDF）连续几何表示与体素级占据图，超越纯占据建模，采用耦合几何公式。引入双向耦合学习机制：TUDF特征为占据重建提供稠密几何引导，占据特征则提供互补结构约束以精化距离场估计。网络直接预测完整占据与TUDF表示，无需后处理即可将TUDF无缝接入轨迹规划。

Result: 在未见环境上的大量实验表明，该方法在几何重建质量与下游规划性能上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 面向路径规划的耦合场景补全设计可行且有效：TUDF与占据的双向耦合能增强对障碍边界与自由空间几何的推理，并支持无需后处理的规划集成，为下游任务导向的场景补全提供了新思路。

Abstract: Partial observability remains a fundamental challenge in robotic navigation, where limited sensor coverage and occlusions leave large portions of the environment unobserved. Existing scene completion methods primarily focus on improving incomplete mapping or reconstructing partially observed 3D structures, but rarely investigate how scene completion can be designed to benefit downstream tasks such as path planning. In this work, we propose a path-planning-oriented 3D scene completion framework that moves beyond pure occupancy modeling toward a coupled geometric formulation. Specifically, given partial LiDAR observations as input, the proposed framework jointly predicts completed Truncated Unsigned Distance Field (TUDF)-based continuous geometric representations and voxel-wise occupancy maps. This coupled representation allows the network to better reason about obstacle boundaries and free-space geometry. To fully exploit the synergy between the two representations, we introduce a bidirectionally coupled learning scheme, where TUDF features provide dense geometric guidance to improve occupancy reconstruction, while occupancy features in turn offer complementary structural constraints that refine distance-field estimation. Consequently, the proposed network directly predicts complete occupancy and TUDF representations, allowing seamless integration of TUDF into trajectory planning without post-processing. Extensive experiments on unseen environments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently improves both geometric reconstruction quality and downstream planning performance.

</details>


### [45] [Learning to Explore Hidden Kinematics for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36553)
*Ruiyao Liu,Boshu Lei,Zhuoyang Pan,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将主动感知中的动作选择"摊销"到训练阶段：用贝叶斯滤波维护关节类型与参数的信念分布，将其渲染为逐点"关节流场"作为策略条件，并用强化学习奖励每次交互从后验中消除的熵，从而学到信息性探索行为而非逐步贪心搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭视觉推断铰接物体的运动学往往存在歧义，交互可以消解歧义。已有主动感知方法在每一步贪心地寻找最能锐化运动学参数信念的单一动作，但这种贪心搜索无法扩展到多步视野，因为缺少接触与惯性动力学的前向模型，而这些模型本身也是未知的。

Method: 1) 维护关于关节类型与参数的信念分布，由生成式先验初始化，并依据观测到的部件运动通过贝叶斯滤波更新；2) 将该信念渲染为逐点的"关节流场"（当前后验对物体上每个点预测的运动），作为策略的条件输入，这一表征带有铰接运动的归纳偏置，比信念的隐编码或从观测跟踪得到的流泛化更好；3) 用强化学习训练策略，奖励每次交互从后验中移除的熵，使信息性探索成为习得行为而非每步搜索。

Result: 在PartManip基准的门与抽屉操作任务上优于此前方法；在ArticuRiddle（外观暗示错误铰接方式的新数据集）上达到61.7%成功率，而此前最佳方法为44.4%。

Conclusion: 通过将动作选择摊销进训练，并用关节流场这一具归纳偏置的信念表征条件化策略，可在无需未知动力学前向模型的情况下实现长时程的信息性交互，显著提升铰接物体操作与视觉歧义场景下的性能。

Abstract: The kinematics of an articulated object is often ambiguous from vision alone. Interaction resolves the ambiguity, and active perception methods exploit this by searching for the single action that most sharpens a belief over the kinematic parameters at each step. Such greedy search cannot be extended over a horizon without forward models of the contact and inertial dynamics, which are themselves unknown. We instead amortize action selection into training. We maintain a belief distribution over joint type and parameters, initialized from a generative prior and updated by Bayesian filtering on the observed part motion. To condition the policy on this belief, we render it as a per-point articulation flow field, the motion that the current posterior predicts for every point on the object. Carrying the inductive bias of articulated motion, this representation generalizes better than a latent encoding of the belief or flow tracked from observation. We train the policy with reinforcement learning, rewarding the entropy that each interaction removes from the posterior, so that informative exploration becomes learned behavior rather than a search at every step. Our method outperforms previous approaches across door and drawer manipulation on the PartManip benchmark, and reaches 61.7% success on ArticuRiddle, a new dataset of objects whose appearance implies the wrong articulation, against 44.4% for the best previous method. Project Website: https://hiddenkinematics.github.io/

</details>


### [46] [A robust single-sensing-element tactile sensor for concurrent pressure and tackiness detection with real-time signal decoupling capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36558)
*Ying Yang,Mingwei Gu,Jia-Sen Xie,Xingyu Ma,Yan-Na Lu,Lin Zheng,Jinhui Gu,Junshuai Chen,Yunjie Lu,Denys Makarov,Jin Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种表面柔性触觉传感器，将霍尔效应传感器与软磁复合材料集成于坚固弹性框架中，通过凸起/凹陷动态改变霍尔-磁体距离，实现压力与拉脱力的双模态实时区分，具备无串扰、高稳定性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将黏性（tackiness）感知集成到仿人机器人人工皮肤中可显著提升其认知与操作能力。但现有触觉传感器在多模态信号解耦与稳定性方面存在挑战。

Method: 采用单敏感单元设计：霍尔效应传感器与软磁复合材料嵌入稳健弹性框架；传感器表面受压时凹陷、从黏性表面脱离时显著凸起，动态改变霍尔传感器与磁体间距离。

Result: 产生全过程可追溯、基线分离的信号，实现压力与拉脱力的实时区分；同一接触点双模态感知，消除应力串扰，提升精度与灵敏度；坚固框架与磁-机械机制带来优异可靠性和基线稳定性。

Conclusion: 该触觉传感器在评估黏附特性、监测橡胶老化、精确操作轻质物体以及认知自然物体表面特性等机器人能力提升方面具有较大潜力。

Abstract: Integrating tackiness sensation into the artificial skin of humanoid robots significantly enhances their cognitive and operational capabilities. However existing tactile sensors face challenges in decoupling of the multimodal signal and stability. Here we present a surface-soft tactile sensor that incorporates a Hall effect sensor and a soft magnetic composite within a robust elastic framework. The sensor surface indents under pressure and bulges prominently when retracted from sticky surfaces dynamically altering the Hall sensor-magnet distance. This generates whole-process-traceable and baseline-separated signals enabling real-time differentiation between pressure and pull-off force. This single-sensing-element design facilitates bimodal sensing at the same contact spot while eliminate stress cross-talk enhancing both accuracy and sensitivity. The fusion of a robust framework and magneto-mechanical sensing mechanism equips the sensor with exceptional reliability and excellent signal baseline stability. This tactile sensor holds substantial potential for advancing robotic capabilities in evaluating adhesive properties monitoring rubber aging precisely handling lightweight objects and cognizing natural objects surface characteristics.

</details>


### [47] [Riemannian Splat Regression Models for Learning Time Fields on Arbitrary Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36561)
*Anastasios Manganaris,Rohit Banerjee,Gokul Prabhakaran,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需坐标投影的流形学习模型，将splat回归与缠绕高斯分布定义的splat结合，用于在黎曼流形上学习到达时间场，并验证其精度和模型规模优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意黎曼流形上进行运动规划困难，欧氏空间的方法难以直接适用；尤其以神经网络逼近最优time-to-go函数的方法（如NTFields）需借助ad-hoc坐标投影到更高维空间，费时且增加模型容量，因此期望无需投影即可直接在流形上学习。

Method: 结合splat回归模型与由缠绕高斯分布（wrapped Gaussian distributions）定义的splat，构建可在黎曼流形上学习任意函数的模型，无需坐标投影；并将其用于学习多个黎曼流形上的到达时间场。

Result: 在多个黎曼流形上成功学习到达时间场；与针对各流形分别适配的多层感知机（MLP）相比，评估了该方法的精度和模型规模。

Conclusion: 该splat回归方法无需坐标投影即可在黎曼流形上学习到达时间场，为高维流形上的最优规划提供了运行时间更低、模型容量更小的方案。

Abstract: Motion planning on arbitrary Riemannian manifolds is an important and difficult problem that frustrates typical planning methods for Euclidean spaces. In particular, motion planning methods that approximate optimal time-to-go functions with neural networks, e.g., Neural Time Fields (NTFields), cannot be directly applied without using ad-hoc coordinate projections into higher dimensions. Using these methods directly without such projections is desirable, as it promises to provide the lowest-possible-runtime method for obtaining optimal plans on high-dimensional manifolds while using minimal model capacity. In this work, we develop a model that requires no coordinate projection and can learn arbitrary functions on Riemannian manifolds by combining splat regression models with splats defined by wrapped Gaussian distributions. We successfully apply this model for learning arrival time fields on several Riemannian manifolds, and we compare the accuracy and model size of this approach with multi-layer perceptrons adapted to work on each manifold individually.

</details>


### [48] [EquivDP3: A SIM(3)-Invariant Point-Cloud Encoder for Data-Efficient Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36575)
*Abu Hanif Muhammad Syarubany,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将 SIM(3)-等变 VNN 编码器引入分层扩散策略，提出 EquivDP3，在 43 自由度人形机器人上实现了几何对称性感知，在低数据量（5-10 条演示）下显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人 loco-manipulation 的视觉运动策略需在少量演示下泛化到不同物体位姿与光照。DP3 用点云条件化扩散动作生成器，但其 PointNet 式编码器不具备对操作任务所遵循的旋转、平移与缩放（SIM(3)）的内置等变性；EquiBot 已在轮式机械臂上通过 SIM(3)-等变 VNN 编码器弥补这一缺口，但尚未扩展到更复杂的高自由度人形本体。

Method: 提出 EquivDP3，两阶段策略：高层扩散规划器配备 SIM(3)-等变 VNN 编码器，输出 6 Hz 全身命令块；由冻结的预训练 RL 运动策略以 50 Hz 执行，机械臂由差分逆运动学模块控制；整体通过行为克隆端到端训练。在 IsaacLab 两个仿真基准、四种非等变基线、5-100 条演示及分布内外条件下评估。

Result: 优势集中在低数据量区间：5-10 条演示时成功率达 67.1%，基线为 38.2-52.4%；50-100 条时所有编码器收敛（74.3-85.2%），排序失去意义。仅本体感觉的对照实验证实差距源于感知：去除点云后，5 条演示成功率降至 31%（EquivDP3 为 60%），10 条时 78% vs 99%，50-100 条时差距消失，说明高数据平台期是基准上限而非各编码器学到相同不变性。编码器每动作块仅增加 0.8 ms 延迟。

Conclusion: 在分层扩散策略的感知骨干中嵌入几何对称性，是提升人形 loco-manipulation 在演示稀缺时数据效率的一种实用且几乎零成本的方法。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for humanoid loco-manipulation must generalize across object poses and lighting from only a handful of demonstrations. 3D Diffusion Policy (DP3) conditions a diffusion-based action generator on point-cloud features, but its PointNet-style encoder has no built-in equivariance to the rotations, translations, and scalings (SIM(3)) that manipulation tasks respect. EquiBot closed this gap for wheeled manipulators with a SIM(3)-equivariant Vector Neuron Network (VNN) encoder. We extend this to a substantially more complex embodiment, the 43-joint Unitree G1 humanoid, and propose EquivDP3: a two-stage policy where a high-level diffusion planner with a SIM(3)-equivariant VNN encoder emits 6 Hz whole-body command chunks, executed at 50 Hz by a frozen, pre-trained RL locomotion policy and a differential inverse-kinematics module for the arms, trained end-to-end by behavior cloning. Across two simulated IsaacLab benchmarks and four non-equivariant baselines (5-100 demonstrations, in- and out-of-distribution), EquivDP3's advantage concentrates in the low-data regime: at 5-10 demonstrations it reaches 67.1% success versus 38.2-52.4% for the baselines, while by 50-100 all encoders converge (74.3-85.2%) and the ordering is no longer meaningful. A proprioception-only control confirms this gap is genuinely perceptual: with the point cloud removed, success drops to 31% vs. 60% (EquivDP3) at 5 demonstrations and 78% vs. 99% at 10, but vanishes by 50-100, showing the high-data plateau reflects a benchmark ceiling, not five encoders learning the same invariance. The encoder costs only 0.8 ms of extra latency per action chunk over the PointNet encoder it replaces. Baking geometric symmetry into a hierarchical diffusion policy's perception backbone is a practical, nearly free way to improve data efficiency for humanoid loco-manipulation when demonstrations are scarce.

</details>


### [49] [Inferring Soil Friction Angle from Robot Foot-Ground Force Histories: A Bayesian Inverse Approach to Proprioceptive Soil Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36582)
*Dawei Xu,Zhijie Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用四足机器人旋转腿的力信号，通过MPM正向模型与高斯过程代理的贝叶斯反演，可准确识别无黏性土的内摩擦角φ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人足-地交互信号有望实现空间分布式的原位土壤强度表征；本文作为第一步，检验能否从简化旋转腿的力历史中识别无黏性土的内摩擦角φ。

Method: 采用基于物质点法(MPM)的二维连续体模型，并以实测旋转腿力历史进行基准验证以生成训练数据；构建两个高斯过程代理模型，对完整力历史进行贝叶斯反演。

Result: 在匹配模型实验中，框架成功恢复14个非网格摩擦角，中位绝对误差约0.1°（最大约0.7°），且所报告的可信区间在所有情形下均包含真值。

Conclusion: 当正向模型设定正确时，φ是可识别的；这支持进一步发展本体感知土壤传感以应对空间变异地形，应用涵盖机器人训练的物理基础世界模型到野火后边坡评估。

Abstract: Foot-ground interaction signals recorded by quadruped robots may enable spatially distributed, in situ characterization of soil strength. As a first step, we test whether the internal friction angle $φ$ of cohesionless soil can be identified from the force history of a simplified rotating leg. A two-dimensional continuum model implemented with the material point method, benchmarked against measured rotating-leg force histories, generates the training data, and two Gaussian-process surrogates support Bayesian inversion of the full histories. In matched-model experiments, the framework recovers 14 off-grid friction angles with a median absolute error of approximately $0.1^\circ$ (maximum $\sim 0.7^\circ$); the reported credible intervals contain the true value in every case. These results establish that $φ$ is identifiable when the forward model is correctly specified, and support further development of proprioceptive soil sensing for spatially variable terrain, with applications from physics-grounded world models for robot training to post-wildfire slope assessment.

</details>


### [50] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Vision-Language-Action Models via Reinforced Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36588)
*Ruixiao Xu,Wong Lik Hang Kenny,Zhiqian Liu,Jianing Guo,Hanxiao Li,Kejian Shi,Shuning Zhang,Pu Feng,Yongjia Ma,Yuqing Ma,Kai Chen,Qi Dou,Yaodong Yang,Xianglong Liu,Simin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向多智能体VLA模型的强化学习框架，通过三阶段"强化微调（RFT）"流水线，在初始化感知数据采集、离线信用过滤微调与在线潜空间微调上协同设计，显著提升协作操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在大规模单智能体数据上预训练，缺乏多机器人协作所需的细粒度协调能力；多机器人示教上的SFT受数据上限约束，难以从自身经验中改进。

Method: 三阶段RFT流水线：（1）初始化感知数据采集：遍历初始构型，仅在预训练VLA反复失败时调用人类示教，降低人力成本并增强对初始分布偏移的鲁棒性；（2）离线信用过滤微调：为各智能体分配信用，仅在单智能体正优势轨迹上微调，而非整段联合轨迹；（3）在线潜空间微调：冻结VLA，在其潜噪声空间中进行RL，以缓解多智能体协同探索噪声大与更新不稳定问题。

Result: 在RoboTwin、RoboFactory及两台Franka真机操作共11项任务上，基于π0与π0.5骨干验证，平均成功率分别提升+23.1%、+16.4%和+44%。

Conclusion: 针对多智能体VLA，SFT存在性能上限，标准在线RL在困难协作任务上因协同探索噪声与更新不稳定而低效；所提三阶段RFT可在保持低人力成本的同时显著提升多机器人协作性能。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) methods for cooperative multi-agent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. This problem is challenging because VLAs are pretrained on large-scale single-agent data and therefore lack the fine-grained coordination skills required for inter-robot collaboration. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on multi-robot demonstrations partially bridges this gap, but its performance is bounded by the demonstration data and cannot improve from its own experience. We present a three-stage reinforced fine-tuning (RFT) pipeline for multi-agent VLAs. First, initialization-aware data collection sweeps over initial configurations and invokes human demonstrations only when the pretrained VLA repeatedly fails, yielding robustness to initialization shift with reduced human cost. Second, offline credit-filtered tuning assigns credit to individual agents and fine-tunes on per-agent trajectories with positive advantage rather than on entire joint rollouts. Third, we find existing online RL for VLAs are less effective for hard multi-agent tasks, which we attribute to noisy co-exploration and unstable updates. We instead use online latent-space fine tuning, which freeze the VLA and perform RL in its latent noise space. We evaluate our multi-agent VLA with both $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$ backbones across 11 tasks in RoboTwin, RoboFactory and real-world manipulation with two Franka robots. Our multi-agent VLA improves the average success rate by $+23.1\%$, $+16.4\%$, and $+44\%$ on RoboTwin, RoboFactory, and real-world tasks, respectively. Code available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/mavla_rft-2BC0/.

</details>


### [51] [Simple Agentic Memory for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36595)
*Yuyou Zhang,Yunbei Zhang,Miao Li,Janet Wang,Zijian Jin,Shilong Liu,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SimpleARM——一种面向冻结的通用机器人策略的免训练记忆层，通过从任务指令中确定监控内容、维护紧凑的类型化状态、按需结构化检索以及当前视图落地，将"交互历史派生的状态"而非"保留的视觉历史"作为机器人控制记忆的核心，在RoboMME基准16项任务上取得67.17%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉记忆系统多以保留或压缩过去的观测为核心，但机器人控制所需的许多状态（持久身份关系、累积进度、有序流程）并不显式体现在任何单帧中。当前观测往往已不再包含必要的历史信息，仅靠视觉历史保留难以支撑控制决策，因此需要一种以任务相关状态为中心的记忆机制。

Method: SimpleARM为免训练记忆层，可加装于冻结的通用机器人策略之上，包含四个环节：(1) 依据任务指令指定需要监控的内容；(2) 由冻结的感知工具在线维护紧凑的类型化状态（关系、引用、进度、路线等）；(3) 结构化访问机制，仅当候选子目标依赖历史时才检索相应状态；(4) 当前视图落地，在执行前对召回实体进行指代消解与校验。

Result: 在RoboMME（需依赖当前观测中已不可得的历史信息的机器人操作记忆基准）上，SimpleARM在全部16项任务、3个策略随机种子下取得67.17%的平均成功率，而最强的非oracle基线为44.51%。匹配的消融实验显示机制具有特异性：分别移除关系、引用、进度或路线状态时，在需要该状态支持控制的任务上性能大幅下降，而对其他任务影响甚微。

Conclusion: 结果支持机器人记忆的"状态观"：有效的控制记忆并非简单保留的视觉历史，而是从交互历史中提炼出的、紧凑且与任务相关的状态。

Abstract: Visual-memory systems commonly retain or compress past observations. Robot control additionally requires interaction-derived state that no individual frame may explicitly represent, such as persistent identity relations, accumulated progress, or ordered procedures. We introduce Simple Agentic Robot Memory (SimpleARM), a training-free memory layer for frozen generalist robot policies. From the task instruction, SimpleARM specifies what to monitor; frozen perceptual tools maintain compact typed state online; structured access retrieves that state only when a proposed subgoal depends on history; and current-view grounding resolves recalled entities before execution. We evaluate SimpleARM on RoboMME, a benchmark of memory-dependent robot manipulation tasks that require history information no longer available in the current observation. Across all 16 tasks and three policy seeds, SimpleARM achieves 67.17% mean success, compared with 44.51% for the strongest non-oracle baseline. Matched ablations show mechanism specificity: removing relation, reference, progress, or route state produces large losses where the affected state is retrieved for control, while largely sparing other tasks. These results support a state-based view of robot memory: effective memory for control is not simply retained visual history, but compact task-relevant state derived from the interaction history.

</details>


### [52] [HACo: Learning Haptic Active Compliance for Force-Aware Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36596)
*Naisheng Ye,Yinzhe Zhou,Junkai Zhao,Yuhang Lu,Checheng Yu,Zhenjie Yang,Pengwei Wang,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HACo，一种从触觉反馈中直接学习力调节动作的触觉主动柔顺策略，通过指尖触觉与关节力矩反馈、门控触觉交叉注意力及柔顺意图辅助监督，实现闭环力调节，在真实基准上平均成功率达83%，远超最强基线35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的灵巧操作需要策略将物理反馈转化为运动指令，同时在不断演变的多接触交互中调节交互载荷。这既需要接触状态的触觉观测，也需要指示命令如何适应的动作监督。现有策略常忽视指尖触觉与关节力矩反馈的互补性，且常用动作目标要么编码过大载荷，要么忽略受物体约束的运动。

Method: 提出HACo（Haptic Active Compliance）策略，直接从触觉反馈学习力调节动作。采用柔顺调节遥操作将操作者输入转换为控制器可执行的柔顺动作，在保持运动意图的同时调节载荷；HACo直接学习这些动作，以命令-状态差异作为辅助柔顺意图监督。融合局部指尖触觉响应与关节力矩反馈（后者捕捉经关节手传递、含触觉覆盖范围之外的接触载荷）。引入柔顺接地模块（Compliance Grounding Module），通过门控触觉交叉注意力将动作生成锚定于演变的触觉状态，实现无需显式在线接触建模的闭环力调节。

Result: 在覆盖多接触摩擦、切向交互、易损曲面接触、旋转力矩和可变形物体操作的真实世界基准上评估。每项任务20次试验中，HACo平均成功率达83%，而最强基线仅35%。

Conclusion: 结果表明HACo在多样化的力敏感灵巧操作任务中实现了主动柔顺控制。

Abstract: Contact-rich dexterous manipulation requires policies that translate physical feedback into motion commands while regulating interaction loads across evolving multi-contact interactions. This requires haptic observations of contact state and action supervision showing how commands should adapt. Existing policies often overlook complementary fingertip tactile and joint-torque feedback, while common action targets either encode excessive loading or omit motion constrained by the object. We introduce HACo, a Haptic Active Compliance policy that learns force-regulating actions directly from haptic feedback. Compliance-regulated teleoperation converts operator inputs into controller-executable compliant actions that preserve motion intent while regulating loads. HACo learns these actions directly, using command-state discrepancy as auxiliary compliant-intent supervision. It combines local fingertip tactile responses with joint-torque feedback capturing load transmission through the articulated hand, including contacts beyond tactile coverage. A Compliance Grounding Module uses gated haptic cross-attention to ground action generation in the evolving haptic state, enabling closed-loop force regulation without explicit online contact modeling. We evaluate HACo on a real-world benchmark covering multi-contact friction, tangential interaction, fragile curved-surface contact, rotational torque, and deformable-object manipulation. Across 20 trials per task, HACo achieves an 83% mean success rate, compared with 35% for the strongest evaluated baseline. These results demonstrate active compliance across diverse force-sensitive dexterous manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [53] [Executor-aware Candidate Selection via a Feasibility Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36597)
*Sooin Choi,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于证书的候选选择框架,在执行器硬约束下构造命令见证并验证,以避免几何可行但执行器不可行的候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化机器人系统中运动规划与执行器分离,导致几何有效的候选可能与执行器可用命令集不兼容。

Method: 在预测rollout状态上从执行器硬约束集构造命令见证,并与原始约束进行验证;不改变候选生成、排序或执行器。

Result: 5,085次几何有效数值评估中795次无执行器可行命令;证书充分但保守,795例均未认证,参考可行案例中7.09%未认证;FR3与固定基座RB-Y1仿真中认证准入常改变候选选择,事后精确LP准入基线显示平台相关保守性;相比几何选择,认证准入伴随更低规划器-命令覆盖和更高标称跟踪误差,且无一致的到达状态交互裕度优势。

Conclusion: 证书式准入可保证执行器可行性但偏保守,其效果依赖平台;后续用MoveIt/OMPL研究在外部生成候选池上评估同一准入规则。

Abstract: Modular robotic systems often separate motion planning from a downstream executor that enforces state-dependent hard constraints. A candidate that is geometrically valid may therefore be incompatible with the executor's available command set. We present a certificate-based candidate-selection framework that constructs a command witness from the executor hard set at predicted rollout states and verifies it against the original constraints, without changing candidate generation, ranking, or the executor.
  Across 5,085 geometry-valid numerical evaluations on two robot models, 795 admitted no executor-feasible command. The certificate is sufficient but conservative: none of the 795 was certified, while 7.09\% of reference-feasible cases remained uncertified. In controlled FR3 and fixed-base RB-Y1 simulations, certificate admission frequently changed candidate selection, and a post-hoc exact linear-programming (LP) admission baseline revealed platform-dependent conservatism. Relative to geometry-based selection, certificate admission was associated with lower planner-command coverage and higher nominal tracking error, without a consistent advantage in reached-state interaction reserve. A planner-generated MoveIt/OMPL study further evaluates the same admission rule on externally generated candidate pools.

</details>


### [54] [OTRetarget: Joint Robot and Object Motion Retargeting via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36602)
*Guillaume Besset,Erwann Carn,Timothée Carecchio,Valentin Tordjman-Levavasseur,Fabian Schramm,Yann de Mont-Marin,Justin Carpentier,Ajay Suresha Sathya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OTR ETARGET，一种联合重定向机器人与多物体运动的统一方法，通过熵最优传输与约束逆运动学，在保持环境交互的同时将人类演示迁移至人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类运动迁移至人形机器人时，需适配机器人形态并保持与环境的交互；在移动操作任务中，地面与操作物体的接触须在身体比例差异下保持一致。仅靠骨骼运动无法完整描述这些交互，而固定物体轨迹会限制对新本体的适配。

Method: 用带符号距离、最近表面点和相对方向表示表面交互，并借助熵最优传输在人体、机器人与物体几何之间迁移这些量；将所得交互目标嵌入约束逆运动学公式，在保持接触与运动风格间平衡，并逐帧联合优化机器人与物体位姿；该公式支持机器人-物体及物体-物体交互，且无需对场景或演示进行缩放。

Result: 在 OMOMO 上验证：机器人-物体交互 Jaccard 分数 87%，深度误差 8.7 mm，优于 OmniRetarget 的 28% 与 29.3 mm；并在实体 G1 人形机器人上，用基于重定向参考经强化学习训练的全身策略完成迁移，涵盖双手搬箱放置到桌面的等动作。

Conclusion: OTR ETARGET 通过联合重定向机器人与多物体运动，在不缩放场景的前提下有效保持人-物与环境交互，并在仿真指标与实体人形机器人任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Transferring human motion to humanoid robots requires adapting the demonstrated motion to the robot morphology while preserving interactions with the environment. This is particularly challenging for loco-manipulation tasks, where contacts with the ground and manipulated objects must remain consistent despite differences in body proportions. Yet, skeletal motion alone does not fully describe these interactions, and fixing object trajectories limits the adaptation to a new embodiment. In this paper, we introduce OTR ETARGET, a unified approach to jointly retarget robot and multi-object motion from human demonstrations. Our approach represents surface interactions through signed distances, closest surface points, and relative directions, and uses entropic optimal transport to transfer these quantities across human, robot, and object geometries. We incorporate the resulting interaction targets into a constrained inverse kinematics formulation that balances contact preservation with motion style and jointly optimizes robot and object poses at each frame. This formulation accommodates robot-object and object-object interactions without rescaling the scene or the demonstration. We validate the proposed approach on OMOMO, where it achieves a robot- object interaction Jaccard score of 87% and a depth error of 8.7 mm, compared with 28% and 29.3 mm for OmniRetarget. Finally, we demonstrate transfer to a physical G1 humanoid using whole-body policies trained with reinforcement learning on the retargeted references, across motions including two-handed box pick-and-place onto a table.

</details>


### [55] [RoboChrono: A Real Robot Benchmark for Streaming Task Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36605)
*Yuzhou Wu,Longteng Fan,Zimeng Li,Yu Wanchan,Ting Zhang,Yiyang Ma,Shihao Li,Wei Ying,Jianbin Qin,Jiajian Jing,Fangwen Chen,Yifan Wu,Zichen Zhang,Ruiqi Yang,Weibin Kong,Yihang Xu,Haoran Liu,Zonghang He,Xuyang Liu,YiFan Xiong,Siteng Huang,Tao Xu,Zhuo Xu,Long Chen,Ruoxiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboChrono 基准，用于评测机器人操作中的流式任务理解，包含 39 个场景与 34,713 个评测实例，覆盖七类任务。对 18 个视觉语言模型的零样本评测显示，视觉匹配能力强并不代表时间顺序推理能力强，且下一动作预测可在缺乏视觉证据时依赖任务与动作先验，凸显按能力细分评测的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解机器人操作过程需要模型结合交互历史与任务进度来解释视觉观测，但现有评测多依赖聚合分数，难以区分模型在视觉匹配、时间推理等不同能力上的差异，因此需要一个诊断性基准来细致考察流式任务理解的各项能力。

Method: 构建 RoboChrono 流式任务理解基准：由真实机器人执行与配套的裸手人类录制数据构成，含 39 个场景、34,713 个评测实例；将七项任务划分为识别、对齐、时间定位三类，涵盖动作理解与预测、视觉对应、时间排序、动作定位。对 18 个视觉语言模型进行零样本评测，并对五个开源权重模型在匹配问题上做输入消融，以考察对视觉证据的依赖。

Result: 不同任务间模型表现差异显著：GPT-6-Astra 在 Frame Matching 上达 98.3%，而 Frame Ordering 仅 68.3%；RynnBrain1.1-122B-A10B 差距更大，分别为 95.4% 与 32.9%。消融实验显示，移除视觉观测使 Current Action Recognition 准确率下降 22.1 个百分点，而 Next Action Prediction 仅下降 0.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 强视觉匹配能力并不总伴随强时间排序能力，下一动作预测可借助任务与动作先验在缺乏视觉证据时得到支持。RoboChrono 为诊断这些能力差异提供了评测环境，表明在评估机器人操作任务理解时，需要超越聚合分数、开展按具体能力区分的评测。

Abstract: Understanding ongoing robot manipulation requires models to interpret visual observations in relation to interaction history and task progress. We introduce RoboChrono, a benchmark for streaming task understanding comprising 39 scenarios and 34,713 evaluation instances, constructed from real robot executions and complementary bare-hand human recordings. The benchmark evaluates seven tasks grouped into recognition, alignment, and temporal grounding, covering action understanding and anticipation, visual correspondence, temporal ordering, and action localization. Zero-shot evaluation of 18 vision-language models reveals substantial differences across tasks. GPT-6-Astra achieves 98.3% accuracy on Frame Matching but 68.3% on Frame Ordering, while RynnBrain1.1-122B-A10B exhibits a larger gap, reaching 95.4% and 32.9%, respectively. Input ablations on matched questions with five open-weight models further reveal distinct dependencies on visual evidence: removing visual observations reduces Current Action Recognition accuracy by 22.1 percentage points, whereas Next Action Prediction decreases by only 0.7 points. These findings show that strong visual matching does not consistently coincide with strong temporal ordering, and suggest that next-action prediction can be supported by task and action priors even when visual evidence is unavailable. RoboChrono provides a diagnostic setting for examining these differences, highlighting the need for capability-specific evaluation beyond aggregate scores when assessing task understanding in robot manipulation.

</details>


### [56] [Foundation-Model-Guided Topology-Aware Semantic Risk Fields for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36640)
*Giung Lee,Weihang Guo,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种由基础模型引导、拓扑感知的语义风险场，将语义风险先验与体素化3D几何结合，作为模块化代价用于机械臂运动规划，在保持几何约束的同时降低语义暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常环境中的机器人运动规划不仅需满足硬几何约束（避碰），还需考虑依赖上下文的语义风险；传统仅避碰的规划无法刻画此类风险，且语义先验缺乏几何与拓扑一致的空间表达。

Method: 对每个"操作物体—场景物体"对，用基础模型输出六个方向风险权重与配对专属的空间衰减尺度；将这些先验与体素化3D场景几何结合，采用拓扑感知屏蔽（shielding）与测地空间衰减；使用GPU并行后端批量计算物体级距离与风险，构建稠密3D语义风险场，作为下游运动规划的模块化代价。

Result: 在完整与部分屏障条件下评估该场的屏蔽行为；与逐像素语义先验基线对比3D工作空间表示；在三个家庭仿真场景中，相同几何约束下，所提场优化的轨迹语义暴露低于仅避碰轨迹；并评估了支撑流程的计算实用性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 结果表明该语义风险场是一种实用的、拓扑感知的语义代价表示，可将操作规划的安全考量从单纯避碰拓展到语义风险层面。

Abstract: Robot motion planning in everyday environments must satisfy hard geometric constraints while accounting for context-dependent semantic risk. We present a foundation-model-guided, topology-aware semantic risk field that extends manipulation safety beyond collision avoidance. For each manipulated-object/scene-object pair, a foundation model provides six directional risk weights and a pair-specific spatial decay scale. The method combines these priors with voxelized 3D scene geometry using topology-aware shielding and geodesic spatial decay. A GPU-parallel backend batches object-level distance and risk computations to construct a dense 3D field that serves as a modular cost for downstream motion planning. We evaluate the field's shielding behavior under full and partial barriers and compare its 3D workspace representation with a pixel-wise semantic-prior baseline. Across three household simulation scenarios, trajectories optimized with the proposed field have lower semantic exposure than collision-only trajectories under the same geometric constraints. We also evaluate the computational practicality and reliability of the supporting pipeline. Together, these results support the proposed field as a practical topology-aware semantic cost representation for manipulation planning beyond collision avoidance.

</details>


### [57] [Where Predictive Supervision Goes Shapes What VLA Policies Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36645)
*Hanseul Kim,Jewon Yeom,Youngjoon Jeong,Minsoo Jo,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究未来预测监督如何影响VLA策略的视觉表征：预测质量本身不代表表征更利于控制；不同预测接口产生的预测与表征差异显著，监督能否作用于策略行动所依赖的表征才是提升分布偏移下鲁棒性的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来预测被广泛用于改进VLA策略，其假设是"预见场景演化能促使学习到有利于控制的表征"。但预测质量好并不等于策略学到了更好的动作表征，这一区别在分布偏移下尤为关键——成功控制依赖于在陌生配置之外保持空间状态与可能的场景变化。作者因此想要厘清：究竟是什么决定了预测监督能否改善VLA策略所使用的视觉表征。

Method: 采用受控对比实验：在目标构造（target construction）、预测视界（prediction horizon）和训练条件相匹配的前提下，比较不同预测接口（prediction interfaces）对预测结果与视觉表征的影响；并从预测误差如何塑造策略视觉流的角度，追溯这些差异的来源。最后在模拟与物理分布偏移下验证鲁棒性。

Result: 不同的预测接口会产生显著不同的预测结果与视觉表征，包括在空间、动力学与动作信息上能否迁移到陌生场景的差异；这些差异可追溯到预测误差对策略视觉流的作用方式。与受控发现一致，使用更直接、与场景匹配的未来监督所训练的VLA策略，在模拟和物理分布偏移下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 未来预测应被视为一个表征学习的设计问题：其对控制的价值取决于其监督信号是否能到达策略据以行动的那些表征。仅优化预测质量不足以保证控制鲁棒性，需让监督与场景匹配并直接作用于行动相关的表征。

Abstract: Future prediction is increasingly used to improve vision-language-action (VLA) policies, based on the premise that anticipating scene evolution encourages representations useful for control. However, forecast quality alone does not establish that a policy has learned a better representation for action. This distinction matters under distribution shift, where successful control depends on preserving spatial state and likely scene change beyond familiar configurations. We study what determines whether predictive supervision improves the visual representation used by a VLA policy. Through controlled comparisons with matched target constructions, prediction horizons, and training conditions, we find that different prediction interfaces produce markedly different forecasts and visual representations, including in the spatial, dynamics, and action information that transfers beyond familiar scenes. We trace these differences to how predictive errors shape the policy's visual stream. Consistent with this controlled finding, VLA policies trained with more direct, scene-matched future supervision show stronger robustness under simulated and physical distribution shifts. Together, our results frame future prediction as a representation-learning design problem whose value for control depends on whether its supervision reaches the representations through which the policy acts.

</details>


### [58] [Kinematic Nonlinear Spatio-Temporal Trajectory Warping for Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36676)
*Hyojae Park,Arjun S. Lakshmipathy,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于接触分布的简单有效方法，复用现有富接触灵巧操作演示，输出高质量非线性轨迹变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有富接触灵巧操作演示数据稀缺，复用这些演示以适应新场景（中途路径点、环境障碍、时间偏移、起止构型变化）存在困难。

Method: 以手和物体轨迹为输入，利用接触分布（contact distributions）作为核心，采用以物体为中心的变形（object-centric warp）规范化流程，计算复杂高维灵巧手轨迹变形。

Result: 在公开人手运动数据集上，基于4个演示生成12种变体进行评估，与基线方法对比，并验证了方法对不同机械臂/操作器的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法能可靠、高效地生成适应多种约束的高维灵巧手轨迹，具备跨操作器泛化性。

Abstract: We present a straightforward but effective method for repurposing existing contact-rich dexterous manipulation demonstrations. Starting from inputs of hand and object trajectories, our method outputs high-quality nonlinear trajectory warps that account for intermediate waypoints, environmental barriers, temporal shifts, and varied start/end configurations. Foundational to our method is the utilization of contact distributions, which we show allows us to reliably compute complex and high-dimensional dexterous hand trajectories following a simple object-centric warp specification pipeline. We evaluate our method across 12 variations sourced from 4 demonstrations in a publicly available dataset of human hand motion data, perform baseline comparisons, and demonstrate generalization of our approach to different manipulators. Results and code will be made available on publication.

</details>


### [59] [T$^2$Mem: Learning Test-Time Memory for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36720)
*Yize Liu,Huang Huang,Yining Hong,Zijian Du,Zhi Cao,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出T²Mem框架，让无记忆的预训练视觉-语言-动作策略通过测试时训练将观测历史编码为快速权重，在无需外部记忆模块或专门标注的情况下获得依赖记忆的机器人操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖记忆的机器人操作要求策略利用当前观测中已不存在的信息。仅保留历史并不充分，记忆必须保留能支持未来动作的信息。核心挑战在于：无记忆的基础模型能否仅凭动作演示，在无外部记忆支持的条件下学会保留并使用历史信息。

Method: T²Mem在预训练VLA策略内部构建该能力，不使用外部推理模型或记忆专用标注。方法采用测试时训练，通过在线自监督更新将观测历史编码为紧凑的快速权重，避免重复处理完整历史；采用观测锚定接口提取视觉-语言信息用于记忆形成，并向动作专家提供检索到的上下文；由动作监督塑造记忆应保留和使用的内容；并采用交替的记忆-策略学习，使各组件在优化时拥有固定的对应部分。

Result: 在16项RoboMME任务上，T²Mem将平均成功率从无记忆基础策略的17.93%提升至56.83%，优于基准中报告的循环记忆方法；受控性能分析显示，相比显式方法至少有3倍推理加速。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无记忆的基础策略可仅通过动作演示与测试时自监督获得有效的记忆能力；T²Mem在精度与效率之间取得较好平衡，为依赖记忆的机器人操作提供了一种无需外部记忆模块的可行方案。

Abstract: Memory-dependent robotic manipulation requires policies to use information that is no longer available in the current observation. Retaining history alone is insufficient: memory must preserve information that supports future actions. One challenge is whether a memory-free foundation model can learn to retain and use historical information from action demonstrations alone, without external memory support. We introduce T$^2$Mem, a framework that develops this capability within a pretrained vision-language-action policy, without external reasoning models or memory-specific annotations. T$^2$Mem uses test-time training to encode observation history into compact fast weights through online self-supervised updates, avoiding repeated processing of the full history. An observation-grounded interface extracts vision-language information for memory formation and supplies retrieved context to the action expert. Action supervision shapes what the memory learns to retain and use, while alternating memory-policy learning gives each component a fixed counterpart during optimization. Across 16 RoboMME tasks, T$^2$Mem improves average success from 17.93% to 56.83% over the memory-free base policy and outperforms the recurrent-memory methods reported in the benchmark, while controlled profiling indicates at least 3x inference speedup over explicit methods. Project website: https://yzliu84.github.io/T2MEM-project/

</details>


### [60] [Degeneracy-Orthogonal Geometric Constraints for LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36753)
*Minseo Kim,Yina Kim,Jinhwa Hwang,Alex Junho Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeCOD几何描述子，利用横截面地标（如管缝、结构环）约束LiDAR里程计在轴向退化走廊中的纵向漂移，在公开基准与现场实验中实现稳健地标检索并稳定轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人导航依赖SLAM估计运动与位姿，但在长隧道、管道等轴向均匀走廊中，LiDAR里程计沿特征稀疏的行进方向存在无约束漂移。这种结构性退化无法仅靠局部扫描匹配解决，需要沿退化轴提供度量约束。

Method: 提出退化正交轮廓偏移描述子（DeCOD）：面向横截面地标的结构对齐几何描述子。横截面边界（管道接头、结构环）沿退化轴提供度量约束；描述子对估计边界轮廓的有符号法向偏差进行参数化，匹配时显式消解航向歧义，并通过畸变估计解耦一阶轮廓误差；匹配地标生成几何因子，在位姿图优化中约束横截面位置一致性与走廊轴向对齐，修正纵向漂移，同时保留绕公共轴的旋转自由度。

Result: 在公开基准与现场实验中，DeCOD相比标准3D描述子实现更稳健的地标检索，并可跨不同里程计前端稳定轨迹，在几何退化条件下可靠约束纵向漂移。

Conclusion: DeCOD通过结构对齐的横截面几何描述与匹配，为轴向退化环境提供沿退化轴的度量约束，有效抑制LiDAR里程计纵向漂移，且与前端里程计解耦，具有较好的通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation relies on simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) to estimate motion and maintain an accurate pose within an environment. However, in axially uniform corridors such as long tunnels and pipelines, LiDAR odometry is fundamentally limited by unconstrained drift along the feature-weak travel direction. This structural degeneracy cannot be resolved by local scan matching alone. To address this challenge, we propose the Degeneracy-orthogonal Contour Offset Descriptor (DeCOD), a structure-aligned geometric descriptor for cross-sectional landmarks. Cross-sectional boundaries, such as pipe joints and structural rings, provide metric constraints along this degenerate axis, but distinguishing individual landmarks requires capturing subtle surface variations across nearly identical profiles. The descriptor parameterizes signed normal deviation from estimated boundary contours, and matching explicitly resolves heading ambiguity and decouples first-order contour errors by distortion estimation. Matched landmarks yield geometric factors that enforce agreement in cross-section position and corridor axis alignment during pose-graph optimization, correcting longitudinal drift while leaving rotation about the common axis unconstrained. On a public benchmark and in field experiments, DeCOD achieves robust landmark retrieval over standard 3D descriptors and successfully stabilizes trajectories across different odometry frontends, reliably constraining longitudinal drift under geometric degeneracy.

</details>


### [61] [LexiconVLA: Learning Reusable Atomic Action Codebooks for Unseen Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36774)
*Zeming Wei,Jianheng Ye,Xinshuai Song,Sirui Chen,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LexiconVLA，一个可检索的原子动作词典，通过全局/细节双码本与视觉-原子动作对齐实现跨任务复用，在RLBench与真机上提升未见任务成功率且无需技能专用专家或部署时参数更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型难以在未见任务中复用重复出现的交互模式；诊断研究表明，任务可靠完成并不意味着其组成原子动作在不同任务语境下能被一致执行，说明缺乏对可复用原子动作的结构化表征。

Method: 构建可检索的原子动作词典LexiconVLA：全局码本捕捉共享交互结构，细节码本刻画细粒度执行变化；采用视觉-原子动作对齐，将基于视觉状态变化的轨迹重建与基于动作码的视觉结果预测耦合，使词典同时扎根于运动及其效果。在自建AtomAction数据集（69个任务、57,803个片段）上以轨迹重建与视觉对齐学习码本。推理时由规划器与场景感知适配器将新目标转化为码条件子任务，交由共享策略执行。

Result: 在5种策略骨干、26个RLBench任务上，18个已见任务性能基本保持，8个策略训练中留出任务成功率提升；以BridgeVLA为骨干，未见任务成功率由16.67%升至34.17%（+17.50个百分点），总体成功率达71.08%，为已有报告结果方法中最高。真机实验展示了分步执行与失败恢复能力。

Conclusion: 通过原子动作词典的可检索复用与视觉对齐，可在不引入技能专用专家、不做部署时参数更新的前提下提升VLA跨任务泛化与执行一致性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models struggle to reuse recurring interactions in unseen tasks. Our diagnostic study reveals that reliable task completion does not imply consistent execution of constituent atomic actions across task contexts. We present LexiconVLA, a retrievable atomic-action lexicon for cross-task reuse. Global and detail codebooks capture shared interaction structure and fine-grained execution variation, respectively, preserving both reusable patterns and execution details. Visual-Atomic Action Alignment couples trajectory reconstruction from visual state changes with visual outcome prediction from action codes, grounding the lexicon in motion and its effects. We learn these codebooks with trajectory reconstruction and visual alignment on our AtomAction Dataset of 57,803 segments from 69 tasks. A planner and scene-aware adapter translate new goals into code-conditioned subtasks for a shared policy, without skill-specific experts or deployment-time parameter updates. Across five policy backbones on 26 RLBench tasks, LexiconVLA largely maintains performance on 18 seen tasks while improving success on 8 tasks held out from policy training. With BridgeVLA, unseen-task success rises from 16.67% to 34.17% (+17.50 percentage points), and overall success reaches 71.08%, the highest among methods with reported results. Real-robot experiments demonstrate stepwise execution and failure recovery.

</details>


### [62] [DRHeC: Differentiable Rendering for Hand-Eye Calibration with RGB-Based Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36779)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Yusheng Wang,Naoya Kagawa,Noritaka Takamura,Keiji Okuhara,Hiroyasu Baba,Jun Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于RGB的可微分渲染手眼标定框架，引入颜色与掩码几何特征，并设计掩码引导的图像到图像翻译方法，提升精度与优化稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于标记的手眼标定精度依赖标记精度与可观测性；无标记学习方法需单张图像但缺乏可解释性；现有基于可微分渲染的方法虽无需物理标记且可解释，但使用二值掩码会丢失内部轮廓细节，精度受限，且优化不稳定、易陷入局部极小。

Method: 提出基于RGB的可微分渲染框架，融合颜色与掩码几何特征以提供更丰富的几何与外观线索；并提出掩码引导的图像到图像翻译方法，在翻译过程中显式保持颜色与几何一致性。

Result: 在仿真与真实实验中验证，精度与鲁棒性均有明显提升，优于现有可微分渲染方法；在UR5e真机实验中抓取成功率达88.9%、插入成功率达57.4%，分别比SOTA方法EasyHeC高出46.3与48.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 该RGB可微分渲染手眼标定框架通过引入颜色与几何特征及掩码引导翻译，有效提升标定精度与优化稳定性，在真机抓取与插入任务中显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Accurate hand-eye calibration is crucial for precision manipulation. Traditional methods rely on markers, with their precision dependent on marker accuracy and observability. In contrast, markerless methods, such as learning-based approaches, use deep neural networks to directly extract keypoints or features from images, enabling the computation of hand-eye transformation with a single image and without the need for physical markers. Recently, differentiable rendering-based methods for hand-eye calibration have leveraged physical models to render binary masks and compare them with observations, enabling hand-eye calibration without fiducial markers in the calibration stage and providing interpretable optimization. While the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering methods achieve remarkable accuracy, the use of binary masks can result in the loss of internal profile details, reducing precision. Additionally, these methods can also suffer from unstable optimization and local minima.
  In this study, we propose a novel RGB-based differentiable rendering framework that provides richer geometric and appearance cues by incorporating color and mask geometric features, thereby improving calibration accuracy and optimization stability. Additionally, we propose a mask-guided image-to-image translation method to ensure explicit preservation of color and geometric consistency throughout the translation. Our approach is validated through both simulation and real-world experiments, with results demonstrating strong accuracy and robustness and clear improvements over existing differentiable rendering methods. Our method achieves a grasping success rate of 88.9% and insertion success rate of 57.4% on the UR5e real-world experiment, outperforming the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering hand-eye calibration method EasyHeC by 46.3 and 48.1 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [63] [Scale-Invariant Manipulability Shape Tracking Across Heterogeneous Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36784)
*Geunwoo Kwon,Dong-gyu Lee,Kai Li,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种尺度不变的机械臂可操作度形状跟踪方法，将仅差正标量因子的矩阵视为等价，在AIRM度量下推导单位行列式形状代表的微分与切空间正交坐标表示，并集成到约束型关节速度二次规划中。四台异构机器人仿真表明：无需尺度调参即可实现高精度形状跟踪，人-机迁移中位置误差仅为参考臂长的2.4-5.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨不同尺寸和运动学结构的系统迁移可操作度时，若目标仅为复现椭球的方向与半轴长度比，则无需匹配绝对椭球尺度。而全矩阵跟踪同时惩罚形状差异与绝对尺度差异，即便只需形状匹配也会产生不合理代价，因此需要一种尺度不变的可操作度形状跟踪方法。

Method: 将仅相差正标量因子的矩阵视为等价类，采用其单位行列式代表元；推导单位行列式形状代表元的微分，并在仿射不变黎曼度量（AIRM）下给出切空间跟踪残差的正交坐标表示；将所得尺度不变目标与位置任务及末端方向任务一起，集成到带约束的关节速度二次规划（QP）中。

Result: 在四台异构机器人上进行机器人-机器人及人-机器人迁移仿真。在三台跟随者上，所提方法无需尺度调参即达到9.30×10^-5的末端形状距离；在KR500与UR20上，若在运动过程中整定机器人特定目标尺度，Full方法仍保留0.19-0.31的末端轴比误差。在包含并发任务的人类伸手动作中，所提方法在四台机器人上均得到与人类目标一致的沿X方向拉长的双力形状，末端位置误差为参考臂长的2.4-5.6%，而Full方法跟踪原始人类椭球时误差最高达75%。

Conclusion: 以单位行列式代表元与AIRM几何为基础的尺度不变形状跟踪，可在不进行绝对尺度匹配的情况下有效实现异构系统间的可操作度形状迁移，并与位置、方向任务协同优化；相比全矩阵跟踪，在多机器人及人-机迁移任务中显著降低形状与位置误差，验证了形状匹配与尺度匹配解耦的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: When transferring manipulability across systems with different sizes and kinematic structures, matching absolute ellipsoid scale may be unnecessary when the goal is to reproduce orientation and semi-axis length ratios. Full-matrix tracking, however, penalizes both shape and absolute-scale differences, even when only shape matching is required. We therefore propose a scale-invariant manipulability shape-tracking method that treats matrices differing only by a positive scalar factor as equivalent and uses their unit-determinant representatives. We derive the differential of the unit-determinant shape representative and an orthonormal coordinate representation of the tangent tracking residual under the affine-invariant Riemannian metric (AIRM). The resulting scale-invariant objective is integrated with position and end-effector direction tasks in a constrained joint-velocity quadratic program. Simulations with four heterogeneous robots evaluate robot-to-robot and human-to-robot transfer. On three followers, the proposed method achieves endpoint shape distances of 9.30 x 10^-5 without scale tuning. With robot-specific target scales tuned during motion, the Full method retains endpoint axis-ratio errors of 0.19-0.31 on KR500 and UR20. For human reaching with concurrent tasks, the proposed method yields dual force shapes elongated along X like the human target on all four robots, with endpoint position errors of 2.4-5.6% of reference arm length versus up to 75% for the Full method tracking the original human ellipsoid.

</details>


### [64] [TaRL: Learning General and Physical Rewards from Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36785)
*Po-Yi Wu,Dao-Jan Chang,Shang-Ya Hsiao,Hong-Ming Chen,Yu-Cheng Su,Tsung-Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TaRL，一种从触觉示范中学习奖励的框架，将触觉形变图序列映射为任务完成进度，显著提升接触密集型操作任务的样本效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型操作需要机器人有序编排精确接触、维持稳定抓取并施加方向正确的力。RL 可自动习得此类行为，但性能依赖奖励设计：稀疏奖励学习效率低，密集奖励难以手工指定。视觉奖励学习虽能从无动作示范中推断奖励，却仅以视觉观测为条件，无法捕捉视觉目标之外的奖励（如抓取稳固性、施力方向）。

Method: TaRL 以一段触觉形变图（tactile deformation maps）序列为输入，同时利用成功与失败示范，回归任务完成进度作为奖励。由于触觉刻画的是机器人—物体的局部交互，该奖励能对稳固抓取与方向正确的力提供有效反馈，且对物体位置等场景布局变化具有鲁棒性。TaRL 可作为塑形奖励使用，也可与视觉奖励结合。

Result: 在仿真四个任务与真实世界两个任务上评估。作为塑形奖励时，样本效率和最终成功率均大幅提升：仿真中 Nut threading 成功率由 34% 提升至 56%，真实世界中 cube pickup 由 37% 提升至 97%。触觉与视觉奖励结合可进一步提升性能。TaRL 还具备跨物体实例泛化能力：在 box placement 上训练后直接部署到 can placement，仍显著改善新任务上的策略学习。

Conclusion: 触觉示范中蕴含视觉无法覆盖的交互信息，从中学习奖励可有效弥补稀疏/密集奖励与纯视觉奖励的不足；TaRL 在仿真与真实任务上均验证了其有效性、可组合性与泛化性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to sequence precise contacts, maintain stable grasps, and apply directed forces. Reinforcement learning (RL) can acquire such behaviors automatically, but its performance hinges on reward design: sparse rewards reduce the learning efficiency, while dense rewards are hard to specify. Visual reward learning addresses this by inferring rewards from action-free demonstrations. Because it conditions only on visual observations, it fails to capture rewards beyond visual goals. We propose Tactile Reward Learning (TaRL), a framework that learns rewards from tactile demonstrations. TaRL takes a sequence of tactile deformation maps as input, and regresses task-completion progress from both successful and failed demonstrations. Because TaRL captures local robot-object interaction, it provides informative feedback to learn firm grasps and correctly directed forces; meanwhile, it is robust to changes in scene layout such as object position. We evaluate TaRL on four manipulation tasks in simulation and two in the real world. Used as a shaping reward, it substantially improves both sample efficiency and final success rate, raising success on Nut threading from 34% to 56% in simulation and on cube pickup from 37% to 97% in the real world. Combining tactile with visual rewards improves performance further. TaRL also generalizes across object instances: trained on box placement and directly deployed to can placement, it significantly improves policy learning on the new task. Project page is available at https://embodiedai-ntu.github.io/tarl.

</details>


### [65] [Spotter: Let the Embodied Model Lead, and the VLM Reflect for It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36808)
*Long Li,Qichao Zhao,Yue Yang,Fan Xu,Zhe Wang,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Chao Qu,Heng Tao Shen,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 现有具身模型无法从自身失败中反思修复，本文提出 Spotter：让具身模型主导执行，VLM 并行监控、仅在检测到错误时介入反思纠错，在 RoboCasa、RoboTwin 2.0 及真实机器人上显著提升成功率且时间开销小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身模型仅用成功演示训练，输入信息过窄，无法根据失败产生自我纠正，也难以从语言描述错误或随机重试中修复，best-of-N 选择也无法在失败后挑出成功样本。因此反思能力需借助信息更丰富、更通用的视觉语言模型（VLM）。但已有 VLM 主导方案让 VLM 规划每一步、把具身模型当工具，使 VLM 处于关键路径上，效率低下。

Method: 提出 Spotter，反转二者角色：具身模型主导并连续执行；VLM 并行运行，通过轻量级本地筛查器（screener）监控，仅在检测到错误时介入，对错误进行反思与纠正，然后交回控制权。

Result: 用 Qwen 和 GPT 分别作为 VLM 均提升具身模型表现。以 GPT 为例，Spotter 在 RoboCasa 上将 Cosmos Policy 与 π0.5 分别提升 5.6 和 7.5 个百分点；在 RoboTwin 2.0 的 Hard 设置上将 π0.5 从 47.2% 提升至 57.0%；在真实机器人上从 53% 提升至 83%。由于 VLM 仅在确认错误时介入，Qwen 版成功回合仅比纯具身模型多耗时 13–16 秒，且比同模型的 VLM 主导基线快约 70%。

Conclusion: 反思应来自 VLM 而非具身模型自身；在错误触发下让 VLM 并行、稀疏介入的 Spotter 架构，能在保持执行效率的同时有效修复具身模型失败，兼具性能提升与低时间开销。

Abstract: Current embodied models do not respond to their own failures, although what just went wrong could inform a small adjustment on the next attempt, the kind of reflection behind the gains of thinking in language models. We test whether they can repair a known error, which requires producing a correction and judging whether it is right. Stopped at a failure and allowed to retry, they seldom repair it through their own randomness or from a language description of the error, and best-of-N selection cannot pick the successful candidate after a failure. We attribute this to training only on successful demonstrations and to inputs too narrow to show what went wrong, and conclude that reflection must come from a vision-language model (VLM), which takes in far more information, such as the episode history and text, and is more general. Prior VLM-led work has the VLM plan every step and invoke the embodied model as a tool, placing the VLM on the critical path. We propose Spotter, which reverses the roles: the embodied model leads and executes continuously, while the VLM runs in parallel, monitors through a lightweight local screener, intervenes only when an error is detected, reflects on and corrects it, and returns control. We run Spotter with Qwen and with GPT as the VLM, and both improve the embodied models; with GPT, Spotter improves Cosmos Policy and $π_{0.5}$ by 5.6 and 7.5 percentage points on RoboCasa, and raises $π_{0.5}$ from 47.2% to 57.0% on the Hard setting of RoboTwin 2.0 and from 53% to 83% on a real robot. Because the VLM steps in only when an error is confirmed, a successful episode with Qwen takes only 13 to 16 s longer than with the embodied model alone and about 70% less time than with a VLM-led baseline using the same model. Our code is available at https://github.com/zqc3117/Spotter.

</details>


### [66] [VidAct: Learning Manipulation from In-the-Wild Videos with Object-Centric 3D Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36870)
*Hang Li,Mingxin Zhang,Zihan Wu,Yang Tian,Dong Chen,Fengyi Shen,Yuan Meng,Xiangtong Yao,Heng Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Zhenshan Bing,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VidAct是一个从单目视频学习物体中心、3D感知操作策略的框架，实现零样本真机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重建的视频模仿学习方法受限于固定相机视角或人到手重定向，重建轨迹难以适应新物体配置且易扭曲形状；同时策略缺乏物体级3D几何感知，制约物体接地与形状感知。

Method: VidAct包含三部分：(1)从任意演示视频重建物体网格与运动，并在静态物体坐标系中规范化运动，避免本体相关重定向并适应多样视角；(2)采用残差轨迹迁移，将重建运动适配到新物体配置同时保持运动形状；(3)作为策略学习核心，在每帧预测仿真提供的特权完整物体点云作为辅助任务，部署时仅用RGB，从而提供对物体位姿与3D几何的密集物体中心监督。

Result: 在人类、机器人、生成及互联网视频上的实验表明其广泛的视频适用性与零样本部署能力；逐帧完整物体3D监督提升了策略泛化与sim-to-real成功率，残差轨迹迁移实现可靠轨迹适配并更好保持形状。

Conclusion: VidAct通过物体中心3D感知监督与残差轨迹迁移，有效利用单目视频实现可泛化的零样本真机操作。

Abstract: Video demonstrations offer a scalable alternative to costly robot data for learning manipulation, yet existing reconstruction-based approaches often rely on constrained camera viewpoints or human-to-robot retargeting, while the reconstructed trajectories are difficult to adapt to new objects configurations without distorting the trajectory shape. Another key limitation is that the resulting policies often lack precise object-level 3D geometry awareness, limiting object grounding and object shape awareness critical for precise manipulation. To bridge these gaps, we propose VidAct, an efficient video-to-robot framework that learns object-centric, 3D-aware manipulation policies from a single monocular video per task and enables zero-shot real-world deployment. VidAct consists of three key components. First, VidAct reconstructs object meshes and motion from arbitrary demo videos and canonicalizes the motion in the static object frame, avoiding embodiment-specific retargeting and accommodating diverse camera viewpoints. Second, VidAct employ residual trajectory transfer for adapting the reconstructed motion to novel object configurations while preserving its motion shape. Finally, as the key policy-learning component, VidAct predicts simulation-provided privileged complete-object point clouds at each frame as an auxiliary task while retaining RGB-only deployment, providing dense object-centric supervision over both object pose and 3D geometry. Experiments on human, robot, generated, and internet videos demonstrate broad video applicability and zero-shot deployment. Per-frame complete-object 3D supervision improves policy generalization and sim-to-real success, while residual trajectory transfer enables reliable trajectory adaptation with better shape preservation.

</details>


### [67] [PreferenceFlow: Test-Time Guidance of Flow-Matching Robot Policies from Human Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36872)
*Yiqi Tang,Diyuan Shi,Runze Li,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PreferenceFlow：无需环境奖励、不更新基座策略，利用人类干预片段构建偏好模型，并在推理时通过改进的QGF采样实现测试时策略提升，在真实精密插入任务中将成功率从69%提升至90.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略能表征复杂机器人行为，但在部署时面对分布偏移易出现局部误差。现有强化学习策略改进方法依赖难以指定或获取的奖励信号，尤其在真实操作场景中难以应用。

Method: 将人类干预片段与同一初始条件状态下机器人生成的片段配对，训练偏好模型。推理阶段采用QGF采样更新，用估计干净动作处评估的偏好梯度替代其价值梯度。同时引入梯度上限损失惩罚干预对上的过大梯度，以及零梯度损失抑制在专家示范动作附近的引导。

Result: 在Franka机器人上四项真实精密插入任务中，PreferenceFlow平均成功率达90.5%，而冻结策略为69%。消融实验支持梯度正则化与偏好标签正确排序在评测设定中的作用。

Conclusion: 人类干预可作为局部偏好监督，用于引导冻结的生成式机器人策略，实现测试时策略改进。

Abstract: Flow-matching policies can represent complex robot behaviors but remain susceptible to local errors under distribution shift at deployment. Many reinforcement learning approaches to policy improvement require reward signals that are difficult to specify or obtain in real-world manipulation. We present PreferenceFlow, a framework for improving a pretrained flow policy at test time without environment rewards or updates to the base policy. Human intervention chunks are paired with robot chunks generated from the same initial conditioning state to train a preference model. During infer- ence, we adopt the QGF sampling update, replacing its value gradient with the preference gradient evaluated at an estimated clean action. A gradient-cap loss penalizes excessive gradients on intervention pairs, while a zero-gradient loss discourages guidance near actions from expert demonstrations. On four real-world precision insertion tasks with a Franka robot, Pref- erenceFlow achieves a mean success rate of 90.5%, compared with 69% for the frozen policy. Ablations support the roles of gradient regularization and correctly ordered preference labels in the evaluated settings. These results demonstrate the utility of human interventions as local preference supervision for guiding frozen generative robot policies.

</details>


### [68] [NIDAR: NIR-Guided Intrinsic Decomposition for Scalable Scene-Agnostic LiDAR Intensity Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36878)
*Junjie Zhang,Jie Yin,Kefei Qian,Jie Li,Mengpei Jia,Yajuan Dun,Wenbo Chu,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NIDAR 是一个前馈框架，用 RGB 外观与仿真几何合成稠密类强度观测，无需目标场景强度标签或逐场景拟合，并在 Waymo/nuScenes 上取得有竞争力的像素精度与更优的结构、感知保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 回波强度为机器人感知与状态估计提供互补的表面响应线索，但许多仿真管线要么省略该信号，要么依赖需要真实强度监督和逐场景优化的重建方法；这类要求提高了数据采集与拟合成本，并限制了跨仿真场景的复用。

Method: NIDAR 从 RGB 外观和仿真器几何出发，组合预训练伪近红外（pseudo-NIR）转换、层级本征分解、几何感知调制与源域分布校准，将反射率相关图像线索迁移至仿真点云。其学习组件用 Waymo 数据离线训练，评估时权重与校准保持固定；部署无需目标场景强度标签，也无需目标场景梯度拟合。

Result: 与所评估的重建基线相比，NIDAR 报告了有竞争力的逐像素精度以及更优的结构与感知保真度。受控的伪近红外对 RGB 诊断表明，伪近红外先验经文中所用的“反射率—重映射”路径使用时收益最大，而非作为简单的直接强度回归器。作者进一步将 NIDAR 与 Unreal Engine 5、Isaac Sim 及生成式 LiDAR 管线集成；在两个仿真室内场景中评估的两个强度感知 SLAM 系统提示了潜在的下游可用性，但不构成真实机器人验证。

Conclusion: NIDAR 在所评估的设定下提供了一种可扩展的强度合成接口；跨波长、相机配置、嵌入式部署与真实传感器验证仍属未来工作。

Abstract: LiDAR return intensity provides complementary surface-response cues for robotic perception and state estimation, yet many simulation pipelines omit it or reproduce it using reconstruction methods that require real intensity supervision and per-scene optimization. These requirements increase data-collection and fitting costs and limit reuse across simulated scenes. We present NIDAR, a feed-forward framework that synthesizes dense intensity-like observations from RGB appearance and simulator geometry. NIDAR combines pretrained pseudo-NIR translation, hierarchical intrinsic decomposition, geometry-aware modulation, and source-domain distribution calibration to transfer reflectance-related image cues to simulated point clouds. Its learned components are trained offline using Waymo data; their weights and calibration remain fixed during evaluation on Waymo and nuScenes. Deployment therefore requires neither target-scene intensity labels nor target-scene gradient-based fitting. The reported comparisons show competitive pixel-wise accuracy and favorable structural and perceptual fidelity against the evaluated reconstruction baselines. A controlled pseudo-NIR-versus-RGB diagnostic further shows that the pseudo-NIR prior is most beneficial when used through the paper-aligned reflectance-and-remapping route, rather than as a simple direct intensity regressor. We further integrate NIDAR with Unreal Engine 5, Isaac Sim, and a generative LiDAR pipeline. Two intensity-aware SLAM systems evaluated in two simulated indoor scenes suggest potential downstream utility, but do not constitute real-robot validation. NIDAR therefore offers a scalable intensity-synthesis interface for the evaluated settings; cross-wavelength, camera-configuration, embedded, and real-sensor validation remain future work.

</details>


### [69] [All Roads Lead to Rome: Flow-driven Multi-Anchor Exploration for Open-Environment Active 3D Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36889)
*Yang Li,Aming Wu,Zihao Zhang,Ziju Han,Sijia Zhang,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对开放环境主动3D建图中长程探索的泛化性问题，指出单点目标预测在部分可观测下易产生脆弱决策，提出用条件流匹配进行条件多模态锚点生成，并配合障碍感知规划、探索模式聚类与分层选择模块，提升开放环境下的泛化性与重建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环境主动3D建图需要长程且最短轨迹探索以重建未知场景，但仅有有限的未知环境信息，基于闭集假设的方法泛化能力不足。现有方法将长程探索建模为"预测粗粒度长程目标再转为可执行路径"，而该阶段被表述为单点预测；在部分可观测条件下，同一局部观测可能对应多个合理的探索方向，确定性预测易导致脆弱决策，实验也验证这是泛化能力弱的关键因素。

Method: 将长程目标预测重新表述为基于条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）的条件多模态锚点生成：从当前建图状态学习粗粒度探索锚点的条件分布，而非预测单一目标。锚点先经障碍感知规划转换为可执行候选路径；再用探索模式聚类压缩几何相似轨迹、减少候选冗余；最后由分层选择模块选出最优模式，并在该模式内对路径重排序，输出最终可执行轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在开放环境下提升了泛化能力与重建效率。

Conclusion: 通过将长程探索目标预测从确定性单点预测转为条件多模态锚点生成，并结合障碍感知规划、模式聚类与分层选择，可有效缓解部分可观测下的脆弱决策问题，改善开放环境主动3D建图的泛化性与探索效率。

Abstract: To advance the development of embodied intelligence, Open-Environment Active 3D Mapping has attracted increasing attention, aiming to perform a long-horizon and shortest trajectory exploration for reconstructing unseen scenarios. Since only limited information about unseen environments is available, methods built on the closed-set assumption, i.e., assuming that the test environments are similar to those seen during training, cannot generalize satisfactorily. In existing active mapping methods, long-horizon exploration is often guided by predicting a coarse long-range goal and then converting it into an executable path. However, this stage is usually formulated as single-point prediction. Under partial observability, the same local observation may correspond to multiple plausible exploration directions, making such deterministic prediction prone to brittle decisions and degraded performance in unseen scenarios. Our experiments further verify that this is a key factor underlying their weak generalization. To address this issue, we reformulate long-horizon target prediction as conditional multimodal anchor generation using Conditional Flow Matching.Instead of predicting a single goal, our method learns a conditional distribution over coarse exploration anchors from the current mapping state. These anchors are first converted into executable candidate paths through obstacle-aware planning. We then apply exploration-mode clustering to compress geometrically similar trajectories and reduce candidate redundancy. Finally, a hierarchical selection module selects the most promising mode and reranks paths within it to produce the final executable trajectory. Experiments show that our method improves generalization and reconstruction efficiency in open environments.

</details>


### [70] [AeroManip-VLA: Scalable Vision-Language-Action Learning for Aerial Manipulation with RL-Generated Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36915)
*Rui Huang,Yanlin Mu,Lidong Li,Yucong Wang,Zichen Yan,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AeroManip-VLA，一个面向空中视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的可扩展基准，用于数据生成与策略评估，支持在仿真中自动生成演示并系统评估 VLA 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂将操作能力拓展至地面机器人难以到达的三维空间，但将 VLA 模型迁移到空中机器人面临操作与飞行紧耦合、观测持续变化、安全攸关交互等挑战。这些挑战需要多样化训练数据与系统化策略评估，而在真实空中平台上采集演示和评估策略成本高、难扩展、难在受控条件下复现。

Method: AeroManip-VLA 提供 GPU 加速的仿真框架，在大规模并行环境中实现底层载荷感知的飞行与操作控制；结合可复用强化学习策略与专家任务规则，在多样物体、环境和随机初始条件下自动生成演示，无需人工遥操作；数据涵盖抓取、放置等基础技能以及需导航与操作的长时程任务；并引入自动事件标注与轨迹分类以过滤演示，实现任务进度、行为结果与安全相关失败的细粒度分析。

Result: 作者在多种任务设置下评估了多类模仿学习与 VLA 基线，揭示了它们的性能特征与失败模式。

Conclusion: AeroManip-VLA 支持可扩展的空中操作数据生成、结构化轨迹分析以及部署前的系统化 VLA 仿真评估。

Abstract: Aerial manipulators extend robotic manipulation into 3D workspaces that are difficult for ground-based robots to access, creating new opportunities for general-purpose manipulation. However, extending Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models to aerial robots introduces distinct challenges due to the tight coupling between manipulation and flight, continuously changing observations, and safety-critical physical interactions. These challenges demand diverse training data and systematic policy evaluation, yet collecting demonstrations and evaluating policies directly on physical aerial platforms are costly, difficult to scale, and hard to repeat under controlled conditions. We present AeroManip-VLA, a scalable benchmark for aerial VLA data generation and policy evaluation. AeroManip-VLA provides a GPU-accelerated simulation framework with low-level payload-aware flight and manipulation control in massively parallel environments. Building on this framework, we combine reusable reinforcement learning policies with expert task rules to automatically generate demonstrations without human teleoperation across diverse objects, environments, and randomized initial conditions. The generated data include basic skills such as grasping and placing, as well as long-horizon tasks that require both navigation and manipulation. We further introduce automated event labeling and trajectory categorization to filter demonstrations. These mechanisms enable fine-grained analysis of task progress, behavioral outcomes, and safety-related failures. Finally, we evaluate a range of imitation learning and VLA baselines across different task settings, revealing their performance characteristics and failure modes. Together, AeroManip-VLA enables scalable aerial manipulation data generation, structured trajectory analysis, and systematic VLA evaluation in simulation prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [71] [Track-and-Complete: Learning Humanoid Skills from a Single Failed Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36924)
*Sarmad Idrees,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRACC流程，从失败的人类演示视频中学习人形机器人技能：先模仿失败前的有效轨迹前缀，再依据推断的任务结果补全任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人从视频学习技能通常依赖成功的人类演示，往往需要专门定制采集数据。失败视频传统上仅被当作负样本，但其中蕴含失败前的可用轨迹前缀以及任务意图结果等可复用信息。

Method: TRACC将失败视频的模仿分为两阶段：失败发生前，以可用运动前缀作为先验知识进行轨迹模仿；失败发生后，利用任务完成奖励引导策略学习推断出的目标结果，从而无需成功轨迹即可完成任务。

Result: 在Oops!数据集的六个真实场景失败人类任务上进行评估，实验结果表明，在无成功演示可用时，该方法能有效利用失败视频学习技能。

Conclusion: 失败的人类视频可作为人形技能学习的可行监督来源，拓展了机器人从视频模仿学习的数据边界。

Abstract: Learning humanoid skills from videos typically requires a successful human demonstration, which often demands custom data collection. Although failures have traditionally been treated only as negative examples in robot learning, they can still reveal a usable trajectory prefix before the task fails, as well as the intended outcome. To leverage this information from a failed-attempt video, we propose TRACC, a pipeline that imitates the useful portion of the motion trajectory and then completes the task based on the inferred task outcome. The usable motion prefix serves as prior knowledge until the failure occurs, after which the task-completion reward guides the policy to learn the intended task goal without requiring a successful task trajectory. We evaluate our method on six in-the-wild failed human tasks from the Oops! dataset. Our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach for learning from failed attempts when no successful demonstration is available. Thus, these findings establish failed human videos as a viable source of supervision for humanoid skill learning.

</details>


### [72] [ComManip: Overfitting Manipulation Policies to Comfortable Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36928)
*Yidan Lai,Xinyi Chen,Shuquan Man,Huiping Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ComManip：在有限示教预算下，将操作策略专门化到“舒适操作区域”，推理时通过移动底座把目标中心移入该区域，从而提升跨大工作空间的成功率约20个百分点以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略训练依赖昂贵的机器人示教，大规模数据采集不现实。为提升泛化性，现有方法在采集时刻意增加视觉观测多样性（变化物体摆放、视角、机器人构型），但在示教预算有限时，这迫使策略去建模多样的视觉观测，导致相似局部条件下的观测-动作对应监督不足，最终任务成功率反而低于在紧凑、视觉与运动学稳定的区域内训练的策略。

Method: 作者将上述稳定区域称为“舒适操作区域”（comfortable manipulation regions），并提出ComManip学习范式：训练阶段将操作策略专门化到舒适操作区域；推理阶段，ComManip先移动移动底座，直到检测到的目标中心进入由舒适区域示教估计出的熟悉图像空间范围，然后执行同一个操作策略，从而在多样目标位置下完成有效操作。方法不改变策略本身，属于即插即用的范式。

Result: 在多种操作任务、示教预算和策略族（ACT、π_{0.5}、RDT、OpenVLA-OFT、SmolVLA）上开展了大量实验。结果表明，在有限示教预算下的大工作空间中，ComManip在不同策略架构上把任务成功率提升约20个百分点甚至更多。

Conclusion: 将操作策略专门化到舒适区域是一种更具数据效率的学习范式，说明在有限示教预算下追求视觉多样性并非最优，聚焦稳定区域并结合底座重定位可获得更高的操作成功率。

Abstract: Training robot manipulation policies relies on costly robot demonstrations, making large-scale data collection impractical. Meanwhile, to improve policy generalization, existing approaches seek greater diversity in visual observations by varying object placements, viewpoints, and robot configurations during data collection. However, under a limited demonstration budget, this strategy forces the policy to model diverse visual observations, providing insufficient supervision to learn reliable observation-action correspondences under similar local conditions. Our study reveals that policies trained under this strategy achieve lower task success rates than those trained within a compact, visually and kinematically stable region. We refer to these stable regions as comfortable manipulation regions. To exploit this finding, we propose ComManip, a learning paradigm that specializes manipulation policies to comfortable manipulation regions. During inference, ComManip repositions the mobile base until the detected target center enters the familiar image-space range estimated from comfortable-region demonstrations. It then executes the same manipulation policy, enabling effective manipulation across diverse target locations. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple manipulation tasks, demonstration budgets, and policy families including ACT, $π_{0.5}$, RDT, OpenVLA-OFT, and SmolVLA. The results demonstrate that ComManip improves task success by roughly 20 percentage points or more across different policy architectures in large workspaces under limited demonstration budgets, suggesting that specializing manipulation policies to comfortable regions provides a more data-efficient learning paradigm for manipulation.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Token Importance: Preserving Spatial Scaffolds for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36967)
*Jiayu Chen,Shuyong Gao,Jingkai Jia,Xiaosheng Bu,Jiyuan Fu,Lingyi Hong,Kaixun Jiang,Yipan Xu,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出VLA剪枝忽视空间信息，提出训练无关的GeoScaffold方法，仅保留20%视觉token即保持93.2%成功率并实现1.78倍预填充加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA视觉token剪枝主要按任务级语义相关性选择单个token，忽略了机器人操作所需的空间信息，导致在剪枝比例稍增时性能崩溃。

Method: 构建Stride基线（沿一维展平视觉序列均匀采样，纯几何策略）进行对照；用"空间覆盖半径"刻画剪枝后保留token集的最大空间盲区；据此提出GeoScaffold：将图像划分为空间区域，按任务相关性权重分配区域间token预算，并用最远点采样选择区域内"支架"token以降低局部覆盖半径，全程无需训练。

Result: Stride在部分剪枝比例下优于语义与随机剪枝，但token预算略降即崩溃；分析显示保留token的空间结构与任务成功强相关。在pi 0.5与LIBERO上，GeoScaffold仅保留20%视觉token，保持93.2%平均成功率，并获得1.78倍预填充加速。

Conclusion: 可靠的VLA剪枝不仅要保留任务相关token，还须维持场景的空间支架；兼顾语义相关性与空间覆盖是有效方向。

Abstract: Existing VLA pruning strategies primarily select individual visual tokens according to task-level semantic relevance, while overlooking the spatial information required for robotic manipulation. To examine this limitation, we construct a simple Stride baseline that uniformly samples tokens along the flattened one-dimensional visual sequence, representing a purely geometric pruning strategy. Surprisingly, Stride outperforms semantic pruning and random pruning at certain pruning ratios, but collapses when the token budget is only slightly reduced. We characterize this phenomenon through the spatial coverage radius, defined as the largest spatial blind spot induced by the retained token set after pruning. Our analysis reveals a strong correlation between the spatial structure of retained tokens and task success, suggesting that reliable VLA pruning requires preserving not only task-relevant tokens but also the spatial scaffold of the scene.
