Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose GeoScaffold, a training-free visual token pruning method that partitions each image into spatial regions, allocates inter-region token budgets using task-relevance weights, and selects intra-region scaffold tokens via farthest point sampling to reduce the local coverage radius. On pi 0.5 and LIBERO, GeoScaffold retains only 20% of visual tokens while preserving a 93.2% average success rate, and achieves a 1.78 times prefill speedup over the unpruned baseline.

</details>


### [74] [All You Need Is Low Fidelity: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real of Learned Robotic Fish Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36993)
*Liam Maloney,Simon Ramchandani,Mike Y. Michelis,Ronan Hinchet,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一个刻意低保真度（无尾流、无附加质量历史的无状态准稳态流体模型）仿真器足以学习通用的闭环控制器，无需调参即可零样本迁移到软体欠驱动机器鱼硬件上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人的复杂任务受限于控制器能力。为软体、欠驱动机器鱼学习更好的控制器时，仿真的保真度与计算成本之间存在权衡。作者质疑高保真度仿真的必要性。

Method: 平台为软体、单电机、腱驱动的机器鱼，策略仅观测硬件可测的量。采用分阶段流程：通过两项独立辨识将仿真器锚定，分别确定尾部动力学与无状态流体模型；策略经由带限节律轨迹生成器执行动作，而非直接控制尾部。

Result: 在户外泳池中不加修改地部署，单一策略实现了闭环目标到达、扰动抑制以及分布外目标的获取与跟踪。

Conclusion: 迁移的成功依赖于约束而非保真度：生成器无法超出流体被辨识的频带范围。这引出问题——多少物理规律可以置于控制器中，而非仿真器中。

Abstract: Complex tasks for underwater robots remain limited by the capabilities of their controllers. Learning a better one for a soft, underactuated robotic fish trades simulator cost against fidelity. We show that an intentionally low-fidelity simulator is enough: a stateless, quasi-steady fluid model with no wake and no added-mass history suffices to learn a \emph{general}, closed-loop controller that transfers to hardware without tuning. Our platform is a soft, single-motor, tendon-driven fish whose policy observes only what the hardware can measure. A staged pipeline grounds the simulator in two independent identifications, fixing the tail dynamics and a stateless fluid model; the policy then acts through a band-limited rhythmic trajectory generator rather than commanding the tail directly. Deployed unchanged in an outdoor pool, a single policy performs closed-loop target reaching, disturbance rejection, and out-of-distribution target acquisition and tracking. The transfer rests on the constraint rather than the fidelity: the generator cannot leave the band over which the fluid was identified. This raises the question of how much of the physics can reside in the controller rather than in the simulator.

</details>


### [75] [FACT: Fidelity-Aware Construction of Articulated Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37067)
*Kuixiang Shao,Chuansen Nie,Yinuo Bai,Jiayuan Gu,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 FACT——一个智能体框架，通过“证据—诊断—修正”闭环逐步构建铰接孪生体，在几何、接触与动态三方面提升保真度，并在重建、交互与物理响应复现上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉上合理的铰接资产在接触交互中仍可能失败，或表现出不准确的运动。现有方法难以同时保证几何、接触与动态三种保真度。

Method: FACT 在共享的可编辑表示上驱动“证据—诊断—修正”闭环，由智能体依据定量反馈选择测量方式与模型编辑，数值工具负责执行与验证更新。具体流程为：1）通过特征规划、针对性测量与诊断式细化，从图像重建可编辑的铰接几何；2）在该参考基础上，通过任务感知的局部重新划分修复碰撞代理，再进行保真度约束下的压缩；3）从被动响应视频构建响应模型，以仿真残差指导模型修正，并进行带约束的物理参数拟合。

Result: 实验表明，FACT 相较基线提升了几何重建质量，能在更简单的碰撞代理下实现更可靠的交互，并且比直接参数推断更好地复现留出的物理响应。

Conclusion: FACT 通过统一的可编辑表示与智能体闭环，将几何、接触与动态保真度协同优化，显著提升了铰接孪生体的构建质量与交互可靠性。

Abstract: Visually plausible articulated assets may still fail during contact interactions or exhibit inaccurate motion. We present FACT (Fidelity-Aware Construction of Articulated Twins), an agentic framework that progressively constructs articulated twins to improve geometry, contact, and dynamic fidelity. The agent drives an evidence--diagnosis--revision loop on a shared editable representation, selecting measurements and model edits using quantitative feedback, while numerical tools execute and validate the updates. It reconstructs editable articulated geometry from images through feature planning, targeted measurements, and diagnostic refinement. On this reference, it repairs collision proxies through task-aware local repartitioning before fidelity-constrained compression. Finally, it constructs response models from passive-response videos, using simulation residuals to guide model revision and constrained physical parameter fitting. Experiments show that FACT improves geometric reconstruction over baselines, enables more reliable interaction with simpler collision proxies, and better reproduces held-out physical responses than direct parameter inference.

</details>


### [76] [Predictive Safety Curricula for Robust Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37070)
*Ivan Ovinnikov,Pascal Sutter,Christian Gehring,Jordis Herrmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出预测安全课程（PSC），利用学习到的未来安全代价预测来分配足式机器人运动训练经验，优先训练地形上下文和随机事件，从而在不改变任务奖励与策略优化损失的前提下显著降低稀有失效（如小腿碰撞）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使平均任务性能很高，学习到的足式机器人运动策略仍会残留稀有但后果严重的失效。标准课程主要调整任务难度，而非调整安全关键经验的分布，因此难以针对性地覆盖那些安全关键但罕见的训练情景。

Method: PSC 从策略 rollout 中训练一个分布式的安全评论家（distributional safety critic），用其对未来安全代价的预测同时为地形上下文和先前遇到的随机事件分配优先级，从而形成一种只改变训练分布、而不改动任务奖励和策略优化损失的课程分配机制。

Result: 在受控的崎岖地形运动环境和生产运动系统中评估：相比标准地形递进、基于优势的回放和学习进度课程，PSC 提升了可靠性，在困难地形和观测退化条件下增益最大。在 ANYmal-D 硬件上，跨三个匹配训练种子，PSC 相比学习进度课程将小腿碰撞发生率降低 63%，且每个种子均有下降；在生产爬楼梯平台上，PSC 在评估的硬件试验中消除了观察到的小腿碰撞。

Conclusion: 学习到的未来安全代价预测可为训练经验分配提供有效信号，将训练导向稀有失效模式，从而提升足式机器人运动的可靠性，并且该分配原则可迁移到不同的生产运动系统。

Abstract: Rare but consequential failures can persist in learned locomotion policies for legged robots even when average task performance is high, in part because standard curricula primarily adapt task difficulty rather than the distribution of safety-critical experience. We introduce Predictive Safety Curricula (PSC), a framework for allocating locomotion training experience using learned predictions of future safety cost. PSC trains a distributional safety critic from policy rollouts and uses its predictions to prioritize both terrain contexts and previously encountered randomized events. The resulting curriculum modifies the training distribution while leaving the task reward and policy-optimization loss unchanged. We evaluate PSC in controlled rough-terrain locomotion and in production locomotion systems. PSC improves reliability relative to standard terrain progression, advantage-based replay, and learning-progress curricula, with the largest gains on difficult terrain and under degraded observations. The same allocation principle transfers to two production locomotion stacks. On ANYmal-D hardware, PSC reduces shank-collision incidence by $63\%$ relative to the learning-progress curriculum across three matched training seeds, with a reduction in every seed. On a production stair-climbing platform, PSC eliminates observed shank collisions in the evaluated hardware trials. These results show that learned predictions of future safety cost can provide an effective signal for allocating training experience toward rare failure modes and improving locomotion reliability.

</details>


### [77] [From Sky to Soil: A Morphing Aerial-Ground Robot for Seed Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37072)
*Namai Chandra,Lining Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种几何辅助的三功能变形机器人，采用"飞行-行驶-播种"架构，结合空中接入、地面移动与可控深度播种，通过复用推进电机驱动钻头实现穴播，硬件样机验证了重构与播种能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中播撒种子虽能到达偏远修复场地，但难以控制种子在土壤中的落位（埋深与位置）。因此需要一种兼具空中到达能力与地面精准播种能力的平台。

Method: 设计几何辅助的三功能变形机器人，构建"飞行-行驶-播种"一体化架构。着陆后变形为四轮播种构型：电子耦合双电机驱动使后臂外展形成地面轮，同时下降的前托盘将推进电机与钻齿轮系接合；复用推进电机进行钻孔，省去专用钻具驱动。播种流程为成穴—投种—机体重新定位或恢复飞行构型。配套控制器发出重构、托盘与种门指令，飞行控制由专用自驾仪负责。

Result: 通过硬件样机验证了变形与播种机构，演示了地面重新定位与种子埋入功能。

Conclusion: 该概念验证为通过协同重构与执行器复用实现空中-地面种子埋入建立了硬件基础。

Abstract: Aerial seed broadcasting can reach remote restoration sites, but provides limited control over seed placement within the soil. This paper presents a geometry-assisted, tri-functional morphing robot that combines aerial access, ground locomotion, and controlled-depth seed embedding in a fly-drive-plant architecture. After landing, the platform reconfigures into a four-wheeled planting configuration: an electronically coupled dual-motor drive folds the rear arms outward to form ground wheels, while a descending front tray engages the propulsion motors with a drill gear train. Reusing the propulsion motors for drilling eliminates a dedicated drill drive. The planting sequence forms a hole, dispenses a seed, and allows the vehicle to reposition on the ground or return to its flight configuration. Ground mobility supports repeated planting without requiring a separate flight between adjacent sites. A companion controller issues reconfiguration, tray, and seed-gate commands, while a dedicated autopilot handles flight control. The morphing and planting mechanisms are validated using a hardware prototype that demonstrates ground repositioning and seed embedding. This proof of concept establishes a hardware basis for aerial-ground seed embedding through coordinated reconfiguration and actuator reuse.

</details>


### [78] [BCNav: Bearing-Conditioned Depth Policies for Sound Source Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37084)
*Yaozhong Kang,Jiang Wang,Takeshi Ashizawa,Benjamin Yen,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BCNav，一种解耦的声源导航框架，通过DOA估计将声学模块与导航策略分离，使策略仅处理深度图像与方位角，从而规避声学仿真域差与离散动作的运动学域差，在仿真与真实机器人上实现无需声学微调、先验建图及真实音频采集的未知环境导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声源导航方法依赖声学仿真器进行联合音视觉学习，但声学仿真保真度低、成本高，导致仿真到真实的域差，阻碍了实际部署；同时，网格化仿真器继承的离散动作空间在物理机器人上引入了额外的运动学域差。作者希望缓解这两类问题。

Method: 提出BCNav解耦框架，将声学模块与学习到的导航策略分离，利用到达方向（DOA）估计：估计器提供声源的标量方位角，导航策略仅接收深度图像和方位角两种域差已被充分刻画的输入。采集最短路径演示并注入标定后的方位噪声，通过模仿学习训练策略，直接输出可在地面机器人上执行的连续速度指令。

Result: 在仿真和物理机器人上进行了验证，能够在未知环境中导航，且无需任何声学微调、先验建图或真实世界音频数据采集。

Conclusion: 通过解耦声学估计与导航策略，并采用连续动作空间与噪声注入的模仿学习，BCNav有效缓解了声学仿真域差与运动学域差，实现了向真实机器人的可靠迁移。代码已开源。

Abstract: The ability to navigate toward sound sources extends a robot's reach beyond its visual field, enabling response to auditory events in unknown environments. To equip robots with this capability, existing methods couple acoustic and visual information through joint audio-visual learning in acoustic simulators. However, acoustic simulation is both low-fidelity and expensive, producing a domain gap that prevents reliable real-world deployment, while the discrete action spaces inherited from grid-based simulators introduce an additional kinematic gap on physical robots. To alleviate these issues, we propose BCNav, a decoupled framework that separates the acoustic module from the learned navigation policy using direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation: an estimator provides a scalar bearing to the sound source, so the navigation policy only processes depth images and a bearing angle, two inputs whose domain gaps are well characterized. We collect shortest-path demonstrations with calibrated bearing noise injection and train the policy via imitation learning to output continuous velocity commands directly executable on ground robots. We demonstrate the method in simulation and on a physical robot, navigating unknown environments without any acoustic fine-tuning, prior mapping, or real-world audio data collection. Code is available at https://github.com/york1to/bcnav.

</details>


### [79] [Multifunctional Locomotion Control of Multi-Jointed BURs with Swimming and gait Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37086)
*Takumi Asada,Hideo Furuhashi,Kenta Tabata,Renato Miyagusuku,Koichi Ozaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多关节腿鳍机构与势函数的多模态仿生水下机器人运动控制机制，实现同一结构下多行为表达与切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态仿生水下机器人需兼顾游动与足式运动以适应不同水下任务，但现有运动控制多依赖基于规则的行为选择与设计者经验，导致机器人只能执行预定行为，难以获得新的行为技能。

Method: 设计一种同一多关节结构即可表达多模态运动能力的机构与控制系统：机构包含四条腿鳍，每条腿鳍具有四个轴；控制器基于传感器模态实现非线性行为，而非依赖预定义的条件规则式运动函数；结合多类传感器数据与行为模式进行实物与仿真验证，并在多模态运动控制中引入势函数。

Result: 验证表明，所提系统在实物与仿真测试中均可行，利用势函数可在多模态运动控制中实现两种或三种行为之间的切换。

Conclusion: 将所提控制方法实现为多模态控制器，有望增强其在水下探索中的应用能力；项目页面：https://tasada038.github.io/multi-jointed-bur/。

Abstract: Multimodal biomimetic underwater robots (BURs) can conduct underwater tasks suitable for the environment. Combining the characteristics of aquatic organisms enables the swimming and leggedlocomotion required for underwater exploration. Locomotion control mechanism relies on rule-based behavior selection and the designer's discretion. This limits the robot's ability to acquire new behavioral capabilities to the predetermined range of behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose a mechanism and control system that enables the expression of multimodal locomotion capabilities from the same multi-jointed structure. A mechanism equipped with four leg-fins each having four axes is used. This controller achieves nonlinear behavior based on sensor modalities, rather than relying on predefined conditional rule-based on locomotion functions. This system was validated through both physical and simulation testing based on multiple sensor data and behavioral patterns. Utilizing a potential function in multimodal locomotion control was verified to enable transitions between two or three behaviors. Implementing the control method as a multimodal controller is expected to enhance its application in underwater exploration. Our project page is at https://tasada038.github.io/multi-jointed-bur/.

</details>


### [80] [V2X-WAM: A Cooperative World Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37098)
*Junwei You,Weizhe Tang,Can Wang,Yan Zhao,Jun Hua,Haotian Shi,Wei Zhang,Lin Wang,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出V2X-WAM协同世界动作模型，紧密耦合协同场景理解、动作生成与未来世界推演，通过可靠性感知时空表征与量化消息压缩，实现规划轨迹与未来占位/动态流预测的双向闭环交互，在真实协同驾驶数据集上提升规划精度、安全性并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同驾驶方法主要利用路侧信息增强当前场景表征，而很少显式建模未来驾驶动作的后果，导致规划器难以预判决策与动态交通环境的交互。车路协同虽能提供更广、更有信息量的观测以支撑端到端自动驾驶，但缺乏对动作-未来演化的前瞻推理能力。

Method: V2X-WAM采用三项核心设计：1）从车辆侧与路侧观测构建可靠性感知的时空表征，并将路侧信息压缩为紧凑的量化消息以实现高效通信；2）基于协同表征，多模态规划器生成前瞻轨迹，并显式以该轨迹为条件预测未来占位与动态流；3）将预测的世界后果反馈以精炼规划轨迹，形成动作与未来世界演化之间的闭环交互。

Result: 在大规模真实协同驾驶数据集上，V2X-WAM相较代表性端到端协同驾驶方法持续提升规划精度与安全性，同时获得更强的未来世界预测能力与显著更低的通信开销；消融实验进一步验证了各设计模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模驾驶动作的未来后果并将其反馈至规划，可有效增强协同驾驶的预见性与决策质量；可靠性感知表征与量化通信压缩在保证性能的同时显著降低通信负担，为高效协同端到端自动驾驶提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Vehicle-infrastructure cooperation can complement onboard sensing with broader and more informative observations of the traffic environment, providing valuable support for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing cooperative driving methods mainly exploit roadside information to enhance the representation of the current scene, while the future consequences of prospective driving actions are rarely modeled explicitly. This limits the ability of the planner to anticipate how its decisions may interact with the evolving traffic environment. To address this issue, we propose V2X-WAM, a cooperative world action model that tightly couples cooperative scene understanding, action generation, and future-world reasoning. V2X-WAM constructs a reliability-aware spatiotemporal representation from vehicle- and infrastructure-side observations, while compressing infrastructure information into a compact quantized message for efficient communication. Based on the resulting cooperative representation, a multimodal planner generates prospective trajectories, which explicitly condition future occupancy and dynamic-flow prediction. The predicted world consequences are then fed back to refine the planned trajectory, forming a closed interaction between action and future-world evolution. Experiments on a large-scale real-world cooperative driving dataset demonstrate that V2X-WAM consistently improves planning accuracy and safety over representative end-to-end cooperative driving methods, while achieving stronger future-world prediction and substantially lower communication overhead. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed design.

</details>


### [81] [Adversarially Robust Geometric Safety Certificates for Nonholonomic Robots Against Maneuvering Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37126)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Bazeela Banday,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗鲁棒几何证书（AR-DPCBF），通过对视距型CBF证书参数做闭式收缩，将障碍机动的最坏影响直接嵌入安全集几何，实现有界机动障碍下的前向不变性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对具备有界主动机动能力的障碍，传统鲁棒控制障碍函数方法把障碍动作当作一般扰动处理，保守且非最坏情形精确；微分博弈方法虽能刻画对抗性，却在在线导航中计算代价过高。

Method: 利用视距（LoS）型证书的结构性质：机器人与障碍动作通过共同的、状态相关的几何增益进入证书，该增益在最坏情形比较中相消，从而把微分博弈约化为障碍机动能力与机器人纵向/转向权限中较弱者的直接比较。基于抛物线证书实例化为AR-DPCBF，给出在有界输入的运动学自行车动力学下、对所有允许障碍机动的前向不变性充分条件。障碍能力未知时，用滑窗估计器给出高概率上界，保持相应覆盖概率下的保证；并构造软约束与缓冲变体以在密集环境中恢复可行性。

Result: 在多种障碍能力、密度与能力失配情形下的仿真显示，屏障违反与碰撞显著减少；并表明对屏障导数做逐点鲁棒化无法替代对证书几何的收缩。

Conclusion: 该对抗鲁棒几何证书以闭式收缩取代昂贵的微分博弈求解，在保持实时性的同时提供最坏情形安全保证，并揭示了"几何收缩"相对于"导数鲁棒化"的必要性。

Abstract: Safe navigation against obstacles that can actively maneuver within bounded capabilities remains challenging: robust control barrier function methods typically treat obstacle actions as generic disturbances, while differential-game approaches are computationally expensive for online navigation. We propose an adversarially robust geometric certificate that accounts for the worst-case effect of admissible obstacle maneuvers directly in the safe-set geometry through a closed-form contraction of the certificate parameters. The construction exploits a structural property of line-of-sight (LoS) certificates: the robot and obstacle actions enter the certificate through a common state-dependent geometric gain. This gain cancels in the worst-case comparison, reducing the differential game to a direct comparison between obstacle maneuvering capability and the weaker of the robot's longitudinal and steering authorities. Instantiated on the parabolic certificate, the construction yields Adversarially Robust Dynamic Parabolic Control Barrier Functions (AR-DPCBF), for which we establish sufficient conditions for forward invariance of the contracted safe set against all admissible obstacle maneuvers under kinematic bicycle dynamics with bounded inputs. When the obstacle capability is unknown, a sliding-window estimator supplies a high-probability upper bound, allowing the guarantee to be retained with the corresponding coverage probability. We further formulate soft and buffered variants to recover feasibility in dense environments. Simulations across obstacle capabilities, densities, and capability mismatch show substantial reductions in barrier violations and collisions and demonstrate that pointwise robustification of the barrier derivative cannot substitute for contraction of its geometry.

</details>


### [82] [ReF-HIL: Shaping the Critic around Human Action Neighborhoods for Efficient Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37131)
*Shaoyin Luo,Song Wang,Shibo Xia,Tianle Zhang,Zhaowei Liang,Guanghui Shen,Bin Wang,Dan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReF-HIL 框架，通过人类参考引导的价值塑造与人类动作围栏机制，高效利用人类成功经验并消除模仿惩罚的负面影响，在真实机器人操作任务中显著提升学习效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HIL-RL 方法存在两个不足：(1) 对成功人类经验在价值学习中的利用不充分，导致昂贵的真实世界训练周期被拉长；(2) 持续的模仿惩罚会限制价值驱动的策略改进。

Method: 提出 ReF-HIL 框架，包含两个核心组件：Human-Reference-Guided Value Shaping，从成功人类经验中学习独立的价值参考以引导在线价值学习，并融入局部纠错反馈；Human Action Fence，定义学习到的人类动作邻域，在邻域内允许价值驱动优化以避免模仿惩罚，在邻域外则约束策略与价值更新。

Result: 在五个多样化且具有挑战性的真实世界操作任务上，ReF-HIL 相比基线方法整体学习效率更高、成功率更高；仅需 18–63 分钟主动训练即可达到 90% 自主成功率，最终成功率达 91.7%–100%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，人类引导的强化学习有潜力在真实世界中高效习得可靠的机器人操作技能。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) offers a promising route to efficient training of robotic manipulation policies by combining autonomous learning with human demonstrations and online corrections. However, insufficient use of successful human experience in value learning prolongs costly real-world training, while persistent imitation penalties can limit value-driven policy improvement. To address these limitations, we propose ReF-HIL, an efficient HIL-RL framework that uses human guidance to accelerate the learning process. Human-Reference-Guided Value Shaping learns an independent value reference from successful human experience to guide online value learning, while incorporating local corrective feedback. A Human Action Fence defines a learned human-action neighborhood, allowing value-driven optimization for better performance without imitation penalties inside while constraining policy and value updates outside. Experiments on five diverse and challenging real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved overall learning efficiency and higher success rates compared with the evaluated baselines. Specifically, ReF-HIL reaches 90% autonomous success in only 18-63 minutes of active training and achieves final success rates of 91.7-100%. These results highlight the potential of human-guided reinforcement learning to acquire reliable manipulation skills efficiently in the real world. Project website: https://anonymous.4open.science/w/ReF-HIL-7762/

</details>


### [83] [CoRe-VLA: Preserving Cross-View Coordination in VLAs under Camera Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37150)
*Tianhang Pan,Xuanhao Wang,Yiwen Pang,Bo Zhou,Jun Yang,Min-Ling Zhang,Shimin Di*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRe-VLA，一种即插即用框架，通过重建场景点云并从VLA训练视角渲染观测，修复相机位移下的跨视角协调崩溃，无需额外多视角数据采集或VLA微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在相机位移下任务成功率显著下降，限制其可靠部署；现有方法依赖额外多视角配对数据采集或微调视觉适配模块，成本高昂。作者进一步识别出外部相机位移导致的"跨视角协调崩溃"：机器人过度依赖腕部视角线索，丢失全局视角时按错误顺序执行子任务。

Method: 提出CoRe-VLA即插即用框架，兼容现有VLA，无需额外多视角数据与微调。通过重建场景点云并将观测渲染至VLA训练视角以恢复跨视角协调；其中Render-to-Camera（R2C）恢复模块降低渲染导致的视觉退化，Execution-Trajectory-Conditioned Alignment（ETCA）模块减少机器人空闲时间并缓解异步执行中的运动冲突。

Result: 在5个真实机器人任务、LIBERO-100和LIBERO-Plus上验证，CoRe-VLA在相机位移下显著提升主流VLA的任务成功率。例如在真实环境中1.6m相机位移下，将PI0.5的成功率从13.3%提升至83.3%。

Conclusion: CoRe-VLA以即插即用方式有效缓解VLA在相机位移下的性能退化，无需额外数据与微调，具备较强的通用性与实用部署价值。

Abstract: VLAs combine pretrained vision-language representations with action generation to enable language-guided control across diverse tasks, becoming a mainstream paradigm in embodied intelligence. However, multiple studies have reported VLA's substantial declines in task success under camera shifts, revealing a key vulnerability that limits reliable deployment. To address this vulnerability, existing methods collect paired observations of the same scene from different viewpoints to fine-tune the VLA or train visual adaptation modules. Unfortunately, they require additional data collection and VLA training costs. In this paper, we first identify \emph{cross-view coordination breakdown} under external camera shifts: the robot may rely too heavily on wrist-view cues and consequently execute subtasks in the wrong order when losing global view. Motivated by this, we propose CoRe-VLA, a plug-and-play framework requiring neither additional multi-view data collection nor VLA fine-tuning, which can incorporate with exsiting VLAs. It reconstructs a scene point cloud and renders the observation from the VLA's training viewpoint to restore cross-view coordination. In CoRe-VLA, Render-to-Camera (R2C) Restoration reduces rendering-induced visual degradation, while Execution-Trajectory-Conditioned Alignment (ETCA) reduces robot idle time and mitigates motion conflicts during asynchronous execution. Experiments on 5 real-robot tasks, LIBERO-100 and LIBERO-Plus demonstrate CoRe-VLA substantially improves task success across mainstream VLAs under camera shifts. For example, CoRe-VLA raises PI0.5's success rate from 13.3% to 83.3% at a 1.6m camera shift in real-robot environment.

</details>


### [84] [Disentangling Spurious Correlations in Vision-Language-Action Models via Predicting Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37165)
*Junghyun Kim,Ngseo Kim,ChungWoo Lee,Seoyeon Lee,Woo-Jeong Baek,Adam Zhou,Chip Huyen,Jun-Ki Lee,Gi-Cheon Kang,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DILL 表示学习框架，通过域不变未来隐变量监督 VLA 策略，缓解视觉分布偏移下的捷径学习，LIBERO-Plus 平均成功率 69.1%，超越最强基线 11.4 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在视觉分布偏移下表现脆弱，往往依赖与特定域因素绑定的虚假相关，而非任务相关结构，即存在捷径学习问题，导致跨域泛化能力不足。

Method: 训练 Task-Domain Encoder，采用对比目标与高斯解耦正则，将任务相关结构与域特定视觉变化分离；用其提供域不变的未来隐变量，通过前瞻预测与域解耦监督 VLA 策略学习，使策略关注任务相关结构而非偶然视觉因素。

Result: 反事实任务视角评估显示 DILL 降低对捷径的依赖；LIBERO-Plus 上平均成功率 69.1%，较最强基线提升 11.4 个百分点；真实机器人操作实验验证其超出仿真环境的适用性；隐空间诊断表明表征更好地保留任务一致结构并抑制域特定变化。

Conclusion: 域不变未来隐变量监督可有效缓解 VLA 策略的捷径学习，同时提升视觉鲁棒性与跨域表征质量，并在仿真与真实场景中均具适用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remain brittle under visual distribution shifts, often relying on spurious correlations tied to domain-specific factors rather than task-relevant structure. We propose Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead (DILL), a representation-learning framework that mitigates shortcut learning in VLA policies. Our key idea is to supervise policies with domain-invariant future latents learned from domain-transformed trajectory data. A Task-Domain Encoder is trained with contrastive objectives and Gaussian disentanglement regularization to separate task-relevant structure from domain-specific visual variation. The learned encoder then provides future latents for VLA policy learning through lookahead prediction and domain disentanglement, encouraging the policy to focus on task-relevant structure rather than incidental visual factors. Counterfactual task-view evaluations show that DILL reduces shortcut reliance, while LIBERO-Plus evaluations demonstrate improved visual robustness, with 69.1% average success, 11.4 percentage points above the strongest baseline. Real-world manipulation experiments further support DILL's applicability beyond controlled simulation. Complementary latent-space diagnostics show that these behavioral gains are accompanied by representations that better preserve task-consistent structure while suppressing domain-specific variation. Our project page is available at https://dill-vla.github.io/.

</details>


### [85] [EgoHumanoid-V2: Human-to-Humanoid Transfer of Coordinated Whole-Body Skills for Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37181)
*Jin Chen,Yiming Jiang,Chongyang Xu,Modi Shi,Shijia Peng,Li Chen,Tianyu Li,Mu Xu,Yilun Chen,Steven Hoi,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoHumanoid-V2，首个面向协调全身移动操作的以自我为中心人类到人形机器人技能迁移框架，通过粗到细动作对齐与视觉域缩小实现零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示可提供多样场景与丰富全身技能，且无需机器人遥操作。已有以自我为中心的迁移研究多聚焦于解耦控制下的移动操作场景泛化，对协调全身技能的直接迁移探索不足。

Method: 核心为粗到细动作对齐：结合运动学参考校正与动力学感知精修，在保持全身协调的同时提升末端执行器位姿精度；并采用机械臂渲染与训练时图像增强，缩小视觉具身差异、增强视角鲁棒性。

Result: 在四项真实世界任务上，基于对齐后人类数据训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，在无目标任务机器人演示的情况下实现零样本技能迁移，任务得分与基于遥操作数据训练的策略相当，且采集成本更低。

Conclusion: 结果支持将人类数据作为直接技能监督信号，用于人形机器人的协调全身移动操作技能学习。

Abstract: Human demonstrations capture diverse scenes and rich whole-body skills without requiring robot teleoperation. Prior work on egocentric transfer has emphasized scene generalization in loco-manipulation under decoupled control, leaving direct transfer of coordinated whole-body skills less explored. We present EgoHumanoid-V2, the first egocentric human-to-humanoid skill transfer framework for coordinated whole-body loco-manipulation. At its core, coarse-to-fine action alignment combines kinematic reference correction with dynamics-aware refinement. It improves end-effector pose accuracy while preserving whole-body coordination. We also use robot-arm rendering and training-time image augmentation to reduce the visual embodiment gap and improve viewpoint robustness. On four real-world tasks, vision-language-action (VLA) policies trained on aligned human data show zero-shot skill transfer without target-task robot demonstrations. Task scores are comparable to those of policies trained on teleoperation data at a lower collection cost. These results support human data as direct skill supervision.

</details>


### [86] [Recovering the View: Benchmarking Physical Active Vision for Occlusion Recovery in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37292)
*Kaijun Luo,Yudi Huang,Qijun Zhong,Xinshuai Song,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双臂主动视觉基准 BAVO-Bench 及主动视觉策略 A-FAR，用于在执行过程中应对遮挡并实现视角与操作的联合控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作基准对策略在执行中如何从遮挡中恢复的支持有限，缺乏系统评估主动视觉恢复能力的设置。

Method: BAVO-Bench 通过 Clean、Stage Occlusion、Random-time Occlusion 三种条件系统控制外部可见性；A-FAR 将移动相机观测统一到以机器人为中心的 3D 坐标系，并从预训练 4D 模型中蒸馏关系结构及其未来演化，从而在部署时不需未来观测即可获得未来感知的几何引导。

Result: 在多个操作任务上，A-FAR 提升了对结构化遮挡和时间偏移遮挡的鲁棒性，同时在清晰观测下保持较强性能。

Conclusion: BAVO-Bench 与 A-FAR 为双臂主动视觉下的遮挡恢复研究提供了基准与方法基础。

Abstract: Physical active vision allows robots to change their viewpoint when task-relevant observations become unreliable, yet existing manipulation benchmarks provide limited support for studying how policies recover from occlusion during execution. We introduce BAVO-Bench (Bimanual Active Vision under Occlusion), a bimanual active-vision benchmark that systematically controls external visibility through Clean, Stage Occlusion, and Random-time Occlusion conditions, enabling evaluation of both manipulation performance and active visual recovery. Building on this setting, we present A-FAR (Active Future-Aware Recovery), an active-vision policy for joint viewpoint and manipulation control. A-FAR represents moving-camera observations in a unified robot-centric 3D frame and distills relational structure together with its future evolution from a pretrained 4D model, providing the policy with future-aware geometric guidance without requiring future observations at deployment. Experiments across multiple manipulation tasks show that A-FAR improves robustness to both structured and temporally shifted occlusions while maintaining strong performance under clean observations.

</details>


### [87] [Remember What You Did: Action-History Memory with Dual-Expert Denoising for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37307)
*Yaxin Zhao,Dianye Huang,Chenwei Wang,Chenguang Yang,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActMem-VLA 通过双专家交接架构，为冻结的 VLA 基础策略外接 Mamba 记忆模块与轻量 PreAction Expert，以低成本缓解由交互历史缺失引发的感知混淆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型缺乏对交互历史的显式访问，导致感知混淆：不同任务阶段出现相似的当前观测与机器人状态时，会产生动作歧义并降低成功率。已有引入时序或进度线索的方法多依赖特征条件化、动作先验修改或采样引导，但若联合微调记忆模块与基础 VLA，会带来额外的策略训练开销，因此需要将可训练的历史条件化引导与冻结的基础策略精炼相分离。

Method: 提出 ActMem-VLA 双专家交接架构：在冻结且已微调的 VLA 上外接由 Mamba 记忆模块与轻量 PreAction Expert（PAE）组成的记忆插件。Mamba 将已执行动作历史编码为记忆，与当前上下文共同条件化 PAE。PAE 在早期高噪声去噪阶段引导任务推进，随后将部分去噪的动作交给冻结的 Action Expert（AE），在剩余低噪声步骤中精炼动作细节。训练全程冻结微调后的基础 VLA，仅联合优化 Mamba 模块与 PAE。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Mem 上，ActMem-VLA 在全部十个任务上取得 80.8% 的平均成功率，优于 π_{0.5} 的 65.2% 与 MemoryVLA 的 49.5%，且仅增加 3.45% 的参数量。在四个真实世界任务上，相较 π_{0.5} 平均成功率提升 28.8%。

Conclusion: 将可训练的历史条件化引导与冻结的基础策略精炼相分离，是一种参数高效且有效的方案；ActMem-VLA 在不微调基础 VLA 的前提下显著缓解感知混淆，并在仿真与真实任务上取得一致提升。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have driven rapid progress in robotic manipulation, demonstrating strong fine-grained control and promising performance on long-horizon tasks. However, many existing VLAs lack explicit access to interaction history, making them vulnerable to perceptual aliasing: similar current observations and robot states at different task stages may induce action ambiguity and lower success rate. Existing methods incorporate temporal or progress cues through feature conditioning, action-prior modification, or sampling guidance. However, methods that jointly fine-tune memory modules and the base VLA incur additional policy-training costs, motivating the separation of trainable history-conditioned steering from frozen base-policy refinement. We propose ActMem-VLA, a dual-expert handover architecture that augments a frozen, fine-tuned VLA with a memory plugin comprising a Mamba-based memory module and a lightweight PreAction Expert (PAE). Specifically, Mamba encodes executed-action history into memory that conditions PAE alongside current context. With these inputs, PAE steers task progression during early, high-noise denoising, then passes the partially denoised action to the frozen Action Expert (AE) to refine action details during the remaining low-noise steps. The fine-tuned base VLA remains frozen throughout training, while only the Mamba module and PAE are jointly optimized. On LIBERO-Mem, ActMem-VLA achieves 80.8\% average success across all ten tasks, compared with 65.2\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 49.5\% for MemoryVLA, while introducing only 3.45\% additional parameters. Across four real-world tasks, it improves the average success rate over $π_{0.5}$ by 28.8\%.

</details>


### [88] [Battery-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Aggressive Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37316)
*Alejandro Sanchez Roncero,Olov Andersson,Petter Ogren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将辨识得到的负载瞬态电池模型与旋翼动力学及固件饱和耦合，训练带滤波电压输入的前馈策略，在保持固件电压补偿与角速率控制的前提下利用电池放电带来的额外推力。38 g Crazyflie Brushless实机上，圆轨迹跟踪误差较原权限RL降低49%（3.84 m/s），20圈平均竞速时间从106.22 s降至95.24 s；相比同权限但无电压输入的策略，硬件误差与圈时分别降低15.3%与4.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷飞行（如竞速、追逃）需要大加速度与精确转向，但电池放电及负载下电压跌落会改变可用推力。为容忍这种变化，常用保守指令限幅，代价是性能未被充分利用。作者希望在学习控制器中安全利用这部分额外推力，同时不破坏现有飞控的电压补偿与角速率控制。

Method: 训练仿真将辨识的负载瞬态电池模型与旋翼动力学、固件饱和耦合；前馈策略在训练与部署阶段均接收滤波后的电压；通过受控消融，区分扩大推力指令范围与引入电压信息各自的收益。硬件平台为38 g Crazyflie Brushless，并辅以仿真实验（用不同电池工况的录制作电压输入）。

Result: 实机上，策略在3.84 m/s下将圆轨迹跟踪误差相较原权限RL降低49%，并保持简易任务精度；20圈平均竞速时间由106.22 s降至95.24 s。与同等扩大权限但电压盲的策略相比，硬件误差和圈时分别低15.3%和4.5%。仿真中，将策略电压输入替换为不同电池工况的录制会使高难度圆轨迹跟踪变差，竞速中影响较小且依赖电压条件。

Conclusion: 简单电压输入可在激进学习飞行中补充现有执行器补偿，且其收益与扩大推力权限的收益可区分；该结果表明电压信息在高机动飞行控制中具有实际价值。

Abstract: Agile flight tasks such as drone racing and pursuit-evasion require strong acceleration and precise turns, but the available thrust changes as the battery discharges and voltage drops under load. Conservative command limits make this variation easier to tolerate, at the cost of unused performance. We investigate how learned controllers can use that additional thrust while retaining the flight controller's voltage compensation and rate control. Our training simulator couples an identified load-transient battery model to rotor dynamics and firmware saturation. The feedforward policy receives filtered voltage during both training and deployment. Controlled ablations distinguish the benefit of a larger thrust-command range from that of voltage information. On a 38 g Crazyflie Brushless, the resulting policy reduces circle tracking error by 49% relative to stock-authority RL at 3.84 m/s, while preserving easy-task precision. Mean 20-lap race time decreases from 106.22 s to 95.24 s. Compared with a voltage-blind policy with the same increased authority, hardware error and race time are lower by 15.3% and 4.5%, respectively. In simulation, replacing the policy's voltage input with a recording from a different battery condition worsens hard-circle tracking, with a smaller, voltage-dependent effect in racing. Together, these results show where a simple voltage input complements existing actuator compensation in aggressive learned flight.

</details>


### [89] [Taming VLAs under Robot Execution Errors: Self-Compensation and Stress Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37334)
*Sohyun Lee,Yoonjae Baek,Jaesang Won,Jinnyeong Kim,Kang Hyunwoo,Seung-Hwan Baek,Ivan Laptev,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出自补偿VLA（self-compensating VLA），一种部署时自适应方法，利用VLA指令动作与机器人实际执行动作之间的残差在线更新策略，使策略在生成指令时预补偿执行误差；并构建RoboStress仿真基准测试VLA鲁棒性。实验表明其平均任务成功率优于基线及训练期鲁棒性方法，在两个真实机械臂上成功率提升超过30个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在机器人实际执行动作偏离其指令动作时往往失败，这类执行误差源于机器人机械结构与运行条件（如磨损、负载变化）。现有方法难以应对，且仅靠物理机器人难以覆盖各类执行条件。

Method: 提出自补偿VLA：一种部署时自适应方法，无需任务奖励或标签，利用VLA指令动作与机器人实际执行动作之间的残差在线更新策略，从而在生成指令时预补偿执行误差。同时提出RoboStress仿真基准，将摩擦、齿隙、柔顺性、重力补偿误差等关节级模型组合为七种部署场景，其执行误差依赖机器人状态与运动历史。

Result: 在RoboStress上，自补偿VLA的平均任务成功率高于基础策略及训练期构建鲁棒性的方法。在两个具有不同使用历史的真实机械臂上，每个机械臂的平均任务成功率提升超过30个百分点，且增益可泛化到任务演示中未出现的物体。

Conclusion: 自补偿VLA通过部署时利用执行残差在线自适应，有效补偿机器人执行误差，提升VLA策略在多种执行条件下的鲁棒性与任务成功率，并具备向新物体泛化的能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies often fail when a robot's executed motion deviates from their commanded action. Such execution errors arise from the robot's mechanics and operating conditions, such as wear and payload changes. We propose self-compensating VLA, a deployment-time adaptation method that enables a VLA policy to pre-compensate for the robot's execution errors when generating commands. Without task rewards or labels, it updates the policy online using the residual between the action commanded by a VLA and the motion executed by the robot. To stress-test VLA robustness across execution conditions that are impractical to cover with physical robots alone, we introduce RoboStress, a controlled simulation benchmark. It combines established joint-level models of friction, backlash, compliance, and gravity-compensation error into seven deployment scenarios whose execution errors depend on the robot's state and motion history. On RoboStress, self-compensating VLA achieves higher average task success than both the base policies and methods that build in robustness during training. On two physical robot arms with different usage histories, it raises the average task success rate by more than 30 percentage points on each arm, and the gains extend to objects not seen in the task demonstrations.

</details>


### [90] [DROM: A Language-Guided Diffusion Framework for Multi-Skill Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37348)
*Vincenzo Pomponi,Rocco Felici,Paolo Franceschi,Stefano Baraldo,Oliver Avram,Loris Roveda,Luca Maria Gambardella,Anna Valente*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DROM是一个语言引导的扩散框架，让机器人在单一生成策略中学习、表示并组合多种操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限示教中学习多样、长时程操作策略仍是机器人学的根本挑战，且传统运动规划或硬编码控制器难以设计方向敏感行为。

Method: 用DMPs将少量专家示教扩增为多技能数据集；基于Motion Planning Diffusion，通过交叉注意力扩展为语言条件多技能轨迹生成；并用大语言模型将高层指令分解为可执行技能序列。

Result: 在Franka Emika Panda、FANUC CRX25ia及MuJoCo仿真上验证，性能优于Motion Planning Diffusion与Behavior Cloning基线，实现稳健多技能泛化并能按自然语言指令可靠执行长时程任务。

Conclusion: DROM仅用少量人类示教即可实现多技能泛化与自然语言驱动的长时程操作，兼具数据效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning robust manipulation policies for diverse, long-horizon tasks from limited demonstrations remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. We present DROM, a language-guided diffusion framework that enables robots to learn, represent, and compose multiple manipulation skills within a single generative policy. DROM leverages Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) to augment a small set of expert demonstrations into expressive multi-skill datasets, substantially reducing data collection while improving spatial generalization beyond the demonstrated workspace. Building upon Motion Planning Diffusion (MPD), we extend the diffusion architecture to support language-conditioned multi-skill trajectory generation through cross-attention, allowing a single model to generate skill-consistent motions for a diverse set of manipulation primitives, including orientation-sensitive behaviors that are difficult to design using conventional motion planning or hard-coded controllers. For long-horizon manipulation, a large language model decomposes high-level operator requests into executable sequences of skills, enabling natural language interaction and autonomous task execution. We validate DROM on a Franka Emika Panda robot, a FANUC CRX25ia robot, and in MuJoCo simulation across a wide range of manipulation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that DROM outperforms Motion Planning Diffusion and Behavior Cloning baselines, achieves robust multi-skill generalization, and composes learned skills to reliably execute long-horizon manipulation tasks from natural language instructions using only a limited number of human demonstrations. Datasets, simulation environments, and more at https://github.com/automation-robotics-machines/drom.

</details>


### [91] [Encore: Few-Shot Agentic Discovery of Manipulation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37359)
*Yifan Kang,Zihan Wang,Zhiwen Fan,Bangya Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ENCORE让编码智能体将少量人类演示当作"可读证据"而非训练数据，通过确定性构建的演示信息包生成策略程序，在LIBERO-PRO上以96.3%成功率超越最强基线，并成功迁移到真实双臂机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务通常仅以一句话描述，未说明抓取方式、接触顺序与目标结果，智能体只能靠试错补充细节。现有做法多把演示当作训练数据，成本高且难以在评测时利用。本文希望让智能体把少量演示作为可读证据，从而快速、准确地写出可执行策略。

Method: 提出ENCORE框架：确定性构建器把每条演示蒸馏成信息包（多视角关键帧、夹爪事件、帧条带、完整轨迹）；编码智能体研读信息包，针对固定的感知与动作API编写策略程序，在少量开发性 rollout 上迭代精化，随后冻结程序进入封闭评测——评测过程不暴露成功信号。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO上：有演示时智能体首个程序即在半数扰动任务中成功，无演示时仅1个任务成功；冻结程序达到96.3%，超过使用同一语言模型的最强先前智能体系统（89.3%）。在指令未明示目标的RoboDojo任务上，无演示时无任何程序成功。此外，ENCORE在真实双臂机器人上运行，每项任务用5条演示学会方块交接与倒杯。

Conclusion: 将演示作为可读证据而非训练数据，可显著提升编码智能体在机器人任务上的程序化策略生成能力与泛化性，并有效迁移至真实双臂操作场景。

Abstract: Coding agents can now write, run, and debug programs with little human help. Robot tasks, however, are usually specified by a sentence that leaves out how to grasp, in what order to make contact, and what the result should look like, and an agent given only the sentence must find these details by trial and error. We introduce ENCORE, which gives the agent a few demonstrations as evidence to read rather than as training data. A deterministic builder distills each demonstration into a pack of multi-view keyframes, gripper events, frame strips, and the full trajectory. A coding agent studies the pack, writes a policy program against a fixed perception and action API, refines it iteratively over a few development rollouts, and freezes it before a sealed evaluation that never reveals the success signal. On LIBERO-PRO, the agent's first program already succeeds in half of the perturbed tasks with demonstrations and in one task without them, and the frozen programs outperform the strongest prior agentic system run with the same language model (96.3% against 89.3%). On RoboDojo tasks whose instructions leave the goal unstated, no program succeeds without demonstrations. ENCORE also runs on a real bimanual robot, learning cube handover and cup inversion from five demonstrations each.

</details>


### [92] [Towards Spatial Perception for Heterogeneous Robot Collaboration in Subterranean Mining Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37419)
*Mario Alberto Valdes Saucedo,Akash Patel,Christoforos Kanellakis,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PERSEPHONE自主采矿任务中的机载感知流水线，通过轻量级Explorer机器人与重型Inspector机器人的异构协作，实现废弃地下矿井深部矿体的自主探测与近距离勘测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 废弃地下矿井深部矿体自主开采本质上是多智能体集成问题：单一平台无法同时具备穿越数公里退化巷道所需的机动性与刻画矿体所需的传感载荷，因此需要异构智能体分工协作。

Method: Explorer机器人运行零样本视觉-语言语义分割栈，直接从自然语言提示中检测矿藏，构建未知矿井地图与巡检目标3D场景图；地图与场景图移交给搭载高级传感载荷的Inspector机器人用于规划近距离勘测视角。流水线核心为几何抽象：逐视角边界框生成、跨视角框合并、平面拟合与多边形提取，将原始检测转化为可执行的巡检目标。

Result: 在地下场测试设施及实际菱镁矿中开展了广泛现场验证，覆盖铁脉与菱镁矿化，且在感知退化的真实条件下完成。

Conclusion: 异构多机器人感知流水线可在退化地下环境中实现矿藏的自主检测与巡检目标生成，并具备实际矿区部署的可行性。

Abstract: The autonomous extraction of deep mineral deposits in abandoned underground mines is fundamentally a multi-agent integration problem. No single platform simultaneously offers the mobility to traverse kilometers of degraded drifts and the sensing payload required to characterize an ore body. This article presents the onboard perception pipeline that bridges two heterogeneous agents within the PERSEPHONE autonomous mining mission. Which consist of a lightweight Explorer robot that maps an unknown mine and generates a 3D scene graph of inspection targets, by running a zero-shot, vision-language semantic segmentation stack that detects mineral deposits directly from natural-language prompts. The map and the graph are then handed to a second Inspector robot, which carries an advanced sensing payload and uses them to plan close-range inspection viewpoints. We detail the complete pipeline, with emphasis on the geometric abstraction that turns raw detections into actionable inspection targets, spanning per-view bounding-box generation, cross-view box merging, plane fitting, and polygon extraction, and we report an extensive field validation in a subterranean test facility and in an active magnesite mine, covering both iron-vein and magnesite mineralization under realistic, perceptually degraded conditions.

</details>


### [93] [FP2: Equipping Robotic Foundation Models with Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37433)
*Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Junjian Hu,Shidong Zhang,Derek Zhang,Linhao Chen,Dehai Li,Mingyu Mei,Wanxi Liu,Cewu Lu,Shiquan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FP2——一种轻量级下游接口，为任务适配的机器人基础模型（RFM）引入显式力控，同时保留其动作生成能力，在四类真实接触密集任务上稳定提升性能与力调节质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型虽具备通用操作能力，但在接触密集场景中可靠物理交互仍困难，缺乏显式力控能力。

Method: 采用"动作调节分解"：任务适配RFM作为基础策略生成任务级动作，高频力控策略专注交互调节；压缩基础策略上下文表示，与力/力矩及本体感受历史结合，预测结构化力控参数。

Result: 在四种RFM主干、四类真实接触密集任务上评估，FP2持续优于对应基础策略及代表性力感知/力控基线。消融显示基础策略上下文与物理反馈互补，保留基础策略动作生成可提升效率与新物体泛化。

Conclusion: FP2以轻量接口方式将显式力控融入RFM，兼顾任务性能、力调节质量与泛化能力，验证了分层分解的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models (RFMs) are increasingly capable of general-purpose manipulation, yet reliable physical interaction remains challenging in contact-rich settings. We present FP2, a lightweight downstream interface that equips task-adapted RFMs with explicit force control while preserving their action-generation capability. FP2 adopts an action-regulation decomposition: the task-adapted RFM serves as a foundation policy responsible for task-level action generation, while a high-frequency force control policy focuses solely on interaction regulation. To condition force regulation on the ongoing manipulation, FP2 compresses foundation-policy contextual representations and combines them with wrench and proprioceptive histories to predict structured force-control parameters. We evaluate FP2 with four RFM backbones across four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks. FP2 consistently improves task performance and force regulation quality over the corresponding foundation policies, while comparing favorably with representative force-aware and force-control baselines. Ablations further show that foundation-policy context and physical feedback are complementary for effective force regulation, while preserving foundation-policy action generation improves both efficiency and novel-object generalization. Project website: http://force-policy.github.io/fp2

</details>


### [94] [Anisotropic Representations Improve Planning in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37441)
*Mingu Kang,Yoori Oh,Sookyung Kim,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 AnisoWM 与 ΛReg，用可学习的对角协方差替代固定各向同性高斯正则，在固定迹与各向异性约束下重塑潜空间几何，使其与任务代价对齐，从而提升潜世界模型规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型通常在表示空间学习动作条件动态，并以到目标表示的欧氏距离对候选动作打分。联合训练常用正则化防止表示坍缩，但由此形成的表示几何会决定规划时终端误差的加权方式。论文指出：预测准确且表示不坍缩，并不能保证潜空间规划代价与任务代价对齐——各向同性高斯正则可能诱导出一种与任务代价排序不一致的几何，造成规划偏差。

Method: 提出 AnisoWM 与 ΛReg：将固定的各向同性高斯目标替换为可学习的对角协方差，并施加固定迹（fixed-trace）与各向异性（anisotropy）约束。预测目标、预测器结构与欧氏规划器保持不变，该目标仅用于训练阶段。论文分析了预测驱动的目标方差分配机制、其对训练分布的依赖，以及诱导度量降低规划遗憾（planning regret）的条件。

Result: 在四个视觉控制环境中，AnisoWM 的规划成功率均优于 LeWorldModel；其潜空间规划代价与任务实际结果的一致性也更好。

Conclusion: 表示不坍缩与预测精度不足以确保任务对齐的潜规划代价；通过在训练时引入带约束的各向异性对角协方差目标来调控潜空间几何，可有效改善潜空间度量与任务代价的一致性，进而提升规划性能。

Abstract: Latent world models learn action-conditioned dynamics in representation space and often score candidate actions by Euclidean distance to a goal representation. Joint training typically regularizes the representation to prevent collapse, but the resulting representation geometry also determines how terminal errors are weighted during planning. We show that accurate prediction and noncollapsed representations do not guarantee a task-aligned latent planning cost: isotropic Gaussian regularization can induce a geometry that ranks feasible outcomes differently from the task cost. To address this mismatch, we introduce AnisoWM with $Λ$Reg, which replaces the fixed isotropic Gaussian target with a learnable diagonal covariance under fixed-trace and anisotropy constraints. The prediction objective, predictor architecture, and Euclidean planner remain unchanged; the target is used only during training. Our analysis characterizes the prediction-driven allocation of target variance, its dependence on the training distribution, and the conditions under which the induced metric reduces planning regret. Across four visual control environments, AnisoWM improves planning success over LeWorldModel in all four. Its latent planning cost also shows better agreement with task outcomes. Project website: https://rkdrn79.github.io/AnisoWM-page/

</details>


### [95] [Surgical Master Console Using General-Purpose Robot Arms and a Separable Articulated Distal Interface: Porcine In-Vivo Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37455)
*Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Dongho Yee,Younghoon Noh,Juahn Oh,Yechan Seo,Jinseok Lee,Jiyul Lee,Seong Jeong,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种低成本腹腔镜手术主手：以通用机械臂为可编程基座，把术式专用交互集中于可分离远端适配器，实现2自由度直驱腕、连续抓持感知与离合式工作空间管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能手术主操作台虽具备直观的关节式操作，但价格昂贵且难以复现；而市售易得触觉设备缺乏手术腕关节与连续抓持的接口。因此需要一种兼具低成本、易复现及术式适配交互的手术主手方案。

Method: 每条6自由度机械臂搭载定制2自由度直驱腕、连续抓持感知与离合式工作空间管理；双手机硬件总成本约5500美元。对电流缩放、重力与起动摩擦进行表征，并据此实现姿态相关重力补偿与运动门控辅助；统一主手状态可驱动Isaac Sim或RCM约束的双FR3平台。

Result: 主手状态发布至FR3目标更新至多增加8 ms；末端跟踪的平均时延对齐残差为0.31–0.66 mm。猪胆囊切除实验中，五个操作者侧指标及手/末端速度分布与来自120份临床Versius记录（99例胆囊切除）的人体在体遥测进行描述性比较；主操作台未引发任何中断，术后7天观察期内无异常临床体征。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可采用通用机械臂构建手术主手，并将术式专用交互集中于可分离的远端接口，在显著降低成本的同时保持可用的操作性能与安全性。

Abstract: High-performance surgical master consoles offer intuitive articulated manipulation but are expensive and difficult to reproduce, while accessible commercial haptic devices lack built-in interfaces for surgical wrist articulation and continuous grasp. We present a laparoscopic master in which general-purpose robot manipulators provide the programmable base and surgical-specific interaction is concentrated in a separable distal adapter. Each 6-DoF arm carries a custom 2-DoF direct-drive wrist, continuous grasp sensing, and clutch-based workspace management; the complete bimanual hardware costs approximately US$5,500, below common bimanual commercial haptic references. To support sustained hand-guided use, we characterize current scaling, gravity, and break-away friction and use them for posture-dependent gravity compensation and motion-gated assistance, reducing continuous actuation and thermal loading. A common master state drives either Isaac Sim or an RCM-constrained dual-FR3 platform. Master-state publication to FR3 target update adds at most 8 ms, and tip tracking shows 0.31-0.66 mm mean delay-aligned residuals. In a porcine cholecystectomy experiment, five common operator-side metrics and hand/tip-speed distributions were compared descriptively with human in-vivo telemetry from 120 clinical Versius recordings of 99 cholecystectomy procedures; no interruption originated from the master console, and no abnormal clinical signs were recorded during the 7-day postoperative observation period. These results demonstrate a surgical master built from general-purpose robot arms with surgical-specific interaction concentrated in a separable distal interface.

</details>


### [96] [Contact-Adaptive Robotic Ultrasound Probe Control for Tissue Exploration and Continuous Task-Relevant Visualization Using Robot-Free Image-Motion Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37456)
*Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Dongho Yee,Juahn Oh,Yechan Seo,Jiyul Lee,Jinseok Lee,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人辅助超声监测任务：术者主操作，机器人维持所选切面以观测组织变化。Sonologger 用六轴惯性传感器夹持在手动探头上，免机器人/相机/跟踪器录制同步超声与探头相对旋转；据此提取探头轴与符号参数化非学习的 DIMC 控制器，配合束轴力调节与 5 N 强制退让安全监督。膀胱体模 7/7 成功对 1/7 静态保持（McNemar p=0.031），并在注水扩张中保持视野、力受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声多面向标准化切面、预设扫描协议或专家操作复现，缺乏"术者主导、机器人辅助维持切面"的角色定位。本文旨在让机器人助手在临床医师执行主操作时持续保持医师选定的视野，使变化的组织保持可观测，实现接触自适应术中监测。同时避免依赖机器人、相机或跟踪器的复杂示教采集。

Method: 提出 Sonologger：将六轴惯性传感器夹持于医师操作的探头上，无需机器人、相机或跟踪器即可同步记录超声图像与探头坐标系相对旋转。不模仿轨迹，而是从记录中测量医师用于纠正图像偏移的探头轴与符号；先去除测得的图像延迟，再经"静止-移动-静止"分割得到相对旋转标签；因惯性漂移，平移不纳入控制。以这些测量参数化 DIMC——一种可解释、非学习的控制器，结合束轴方向力调节与独立安全监督器（5 N 强制退让）。

Result: 在动态膀胱体模上，DIMC 在 7 次盲法、位姿配对试验中 7/7 成功获取并保持任务相关视野，而静态保持仅 1/7（McNemar p = 0.031）；在注射器驱动的水扩张过程中保持视野，同时安全监督器将力偏移限定在范围内。

Conclusion: 该框架为接触自适应术中监测提供了可操作的超声上下文表示，展示了从免机器人示教到机器人辅助视野维持的可行路径；临床验证仍为未来工作。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound commonly targets standardized views, predefined scanning protocols, or expert-scan reproduction. We target a different role: while a clinician performs the primary procedure, a robotic assistant maintains a clinician-selected view so that changing tissue remains observable. We present contact-adaptive robotic ultrasound monitoring derived from robot-free demonstrations. Sonologger clamps a six-axis inertial sensor to the clinician-operated probe and records synchronized ultrasound and probe-frame relative rotations without a robot, camera, or tracker. Rather than imitating trajectories, the recordings measure which probe axis and sign the clinician uses to correct an image offset. A measured image latency is removed before still-move-still segmentation yields relative-rotation labels; translation is excluded from control because of inertial drift. These measurements parameterize the Demonstration-Informed Image-Motion Controller (DIMC), an interpretable, non-learned controller combined with beam-axis force regulation and an independent safety supervisor with 5 N forced retreat. On a dynamic bladder phantom, DIMC acquired and held a task-relevant view in 7 of 7 blind, pose-paired trials versus 1 of 7 for a static hold (McNemar p = 0.031), and retained the view during syringe-driven hydro-distension while the supervisor bounded the force excursion. The framework offers an operational representation of ultrasound context for contact-adaptive intraoperative monitoring; clinical validation remains future work.

</details>


### [97] [Waggle-Dance-Inspired Multi-UAV Recruitment with RGB and Synthetic Event Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37464)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在MuJoCo中构建了受蜜蜂摇摆舞启发的空中机器人通信机制，对比RGB与合成事件视觉两种接收实现在"观测运动→完整消息→本地任务选择→执行"链路中的功能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂摇摆舞启发了一种由一个体的运动引导其他个体后续工作的通信机制；将其迁移到空中机器人需要打通从观测运动到完整消息、再到本地任务选择与执行的完整链路，而RGB与事件视觉在该链路中的适用性尚缺乏对比验证。

Method: 构建MuJoCo仿真系统：一名执行者向1至3名观测者广播六比特任务提议，观测者各自独立解码两项提议后再选择任务。RGB接收端用稀疏光流提取轨迹；事件接收端仅依赖局部事件支持与数据包时序。两者共用同一运动字母表与显式空值定界符；由于事件静默不能证明目标可见，事件版本加入物理探测运动与连续性合格的空值确认。

Result: 标称三观测者情形下，两种实现各产生12次完整消息接收、6个完成的工作单元，并在耗竭任务撤回后完成重分配。6组配对功能用例得到相同的消息与工作计数；额外的事件流丢包用例保持受影响观测者的非活动状态。回放42,060,633个记录的合成事件复现了全部12个事件消息载荷与完成时间戳。标称任务总时长为RGB 493.80 s、事件517.40 s，差异主要来自事件探测调度。

Conclusion: 固定仿真结果确立了两种接收实现的功能可行性，而非传感器性能优势或硬件验证。

Abstract: The honeybee waggle dance motivates a communication mechanism in which one agent's motion guides the subsequent work of other agents. For aerial robots, realizing this mechanism requires a link from observed movement to a complete message, local task selection, and execution. This paper presents a MuJoCo system for comparing RGB and synthetic event vision within that link. One performer broadcasts six-bit task offers to one to three observers, each of which independently decodes both offers before selecting a task. The RGB receiver extracts trajectories using sparse optical flow; the event receiver uses only local event support and packet timing. Both use a common motion alphabet and explicit null delimiters. Because event silence does not establish target visibility, the event version includes a physical acquisition motion and continuity-qualified null confirmation. In the nominal three-observer case, each version produced 12 complete message receptions, six completed work units, and reassignment after withdrawal of a depleted task. Six paired functional cases produced the same message and work counts; an additional event-stream dropout case preserved inactivity of the affected observer. Replaying 42,060,633 recorded synthetic events reproduced all 12 event-message payloads and completion timestamps. Total nominal mission time was 493.80 s for RGB and 517.40 s for events, with the difference dominated by the event acquisition schedule. These fixed simulation results establish functional feasibility across two receiver implementations, rather than a sensor-performance advantage or hardware validation.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Social Navigation from Internet Videos in the Policy State Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37476)
*Jiaming Wang,Duc Thang Nguyen,Jizhuo Chen,Volodymyr Shcherbyna,Diwen Liu,Zhengcheng Shen,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将单目行走视频直接转换为闭环社交导航训练环境的流程，在策略状态空间中表示场景与行人，无需重建或渲染逼真观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练鲁棒的社交导航策略需要多样化的场景布局、地形与行人运动模拟器，但构建此类环境并指定行人行为成本高昂。

Method: 将静态场景表示为度量可通行性地图，可在反事实机器人运动下刚性变换；同时按时间直接回放从视频中恢复的行人轨迹。该抽象允许直接在策略状态空间中定义前向动力学，并高效模拟反事实机器人状态。

Result: 所得策略在独立Arena基准上成功率81.2%，最强基线为75.0%；在20次真实机器人试验中成功19次，且无需策略微调。

Conclusion: 将普通单目行走视频高效转化为闭环社交导航训练环境是可行的，能提升策略性能并具备良好的真机迁移能力。

Abstract: Training robust social-navigation policies requires simulators with diverse scene layouts, terrain, and human motion, but constructing such environments and specifying pedestrian behavior is costly. We propose an efficient pipeline that converts ordinary monocular walking videos directly into closed-loop social-navigation training environments in the policy's state space. Our key observation is that local social navigation primarily depends on two types of information: where the robot can traverse and how nearby pedestrians move. We therefore represent the static scene as a metric traversability map, which can be rigidly transformed under counterfactual robot motion, while directly replaying the pedestrian trajectories recovered from the video over time. This abstraction allows us to define the forward dynamics directly in the policy's state space and efficiently simulate counterfactual robot states without reconstructing or rendering photorealistic observations. The resulting policy achieves 81.2% success in the independent Arena benchmark, compared with 75.0% for the strongest baseline, and succeeds in 19/20 real-robot trials without policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [99] [Video2STL: Grounding VLM-Generated Temporal Specifications for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37519)
*Merve Atasever,Keyan Azbijari,Cagan Bakirci,Bo-Ruei Huang,Tolga Izdas,Zahra Shahrooei,Richard Yang,Erdem Biyik,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Video2STL 将纯观测视频转化为参数化信号时序逻辑（STL）规范，用形式化表示驱动机器人策略学习，实现跨本体迁移并在操作与运动任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频策略学习的核心难题是"从视频中应当迁移什么信息"。现有方法把视觉观测压缩为标量相似度/价值信号，或让基础模型直接生成奖励代码，导致任务的时间结构难以被检视、落地（ground）与复用。

Method: 1）用视觉-语言模型从观测视频中提取与本体无关的语义事件轨迹，构建符号化时序规范库，由模型确定任务结构；2）数值谓词阈值与时间边界则由成功机器人轨迹落地标定；3）策略学习时分离短/长时尺度：短时规范通过滚动窗口的定量鲁棒度提供稠密奖励，长时规范经因果监视器为合法时序前缀提供一次性进度奖励；4）该表示支持从人类或动物视频到机器人控制的跨本体迁移。

Result: 四项操作任务中，Video2STL 达到平均 85.8% success-once、67.0% success-at-end，对比原生稠密 PPO 的 81.5%/59.5% 与 Text2Reward 的 65.0%/42.3%；四足运动任务中，基于 Qwen-3.8 与 GPT-5.6 的 Video2STL 策略在 0.3–2.1 m/s 速度范围内成功率 100%，且高速能效具竞争力。

Conclusion: 以参数化 STL 作为中间形式表示，可让视频中的任务时序结构显式可检视、可落地、可复用，并在操作与运动任务上同时提升成功率与跨本体迁移能力。

Abstract: Video-based policy learning is particularly promising, as it illustrates target behaviors without requiring action annotations or embodiment-matched demonstrations. A central challenge is deciding what information should be transferred from the video to the robot. Existing approaches commonly convert visual observations into scalar similarity or value signals, or ask foundation models to directly generate reward code. These approaches can make the temporal structure of a task difficult to inspect, ground, and reuse. We present Video2STL, a framework that converts observation-only videos into parametric Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications and uses the resulting formal representation for robot learning. A vision-language model extracts an embodiment-independent semantic event trace and constructs a bank of symbolic temporal specifications. The model determines the task structure, while numerical predicate thresholds and temporal bounds are grounded from successful robot trajectories. For policy learning, we separate short- and long-timescale temporal information: short-horizon specifications provide dense rewards through rolling-window quantitative robustness, while a causal monitor over a retained long-horizon specification provides one-time progress rewards for valid temporal prefixes. The same representation supports cross-embodiment transfer from human or animal videos to robot control. Across four manipulation tasks, Video2STL achieves $85.8\%$ average success-once and $67.0\%$ success-at-end, compared with $81.5\%/59.5\%$ for native dense PPO and $65.0\%/42.3\%$ for Text2Reward; in quadruped locomotion, Qwen-3.8 and GPT-5.6-based Video2STL policies achieve $100\%$ success across velocities from $0.3$ to $2.1\,\mathrm{m/s}$ while remaining competitive in high-speed energy efficiency. Project webpage: \href{https://video2stl.github.io/}{video2stl}.

</details>


### [100] [RawVLA: Embodied Neural Image Signal Processor For Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37530)
*Shuhong Liu,Heng Zhou,Lingfeng Qian,Yuhao Fang,Xianbao Hou,Qianyu Zhou,Lin Gu,Wei Sui,Jianfei Yang,Ziteng Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文揭示VLA模型依赖固定ISP的RGB图像这一被忽视的设计选择的影响，并提出自适应RAW处理的神经ISP（RawVLA）及RAW域操作基准（RawVLA-Bench），证明其可提升退化成像下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常直接使用固定相机ISP输出的RGB图像，使成像流水线被排除在学习与评估循环之外，这一被忽视的设计选择对动作预测与操作成功率的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 系统分析ISP五个基本维度（增益、传感器噪声、色度响应、色调响应、位深）对动作预测与操作成功率的影响；提出RawVLA——一种流式神经ISP，为冻结的VLA策略自适应渲染RAW观测，并将容量集中于与具身行为相关的成像因素；构建RawVLA-Bench，在干净与恶劣采集条件下将图像处理作为显式评估变量。

Result: RAW到RGB处理显著影响动作预测与操作成功率，不同ISP维度的影响差异显著；在RawVLA-Bench上，RawVLA在标准条件下保持性能，同时在成像退化条件下大幅提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应RAW处理可作为物理相机与具身策略之间的有效接口，成像流水线应作为VLA学习与评估中的显式变量。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically operate on RGB images produced by a fixed camera image signal processor (ISP), leaving the imaging pipeline outside the learning and evaluation loop. We systematically examine the consequences of this overlooked design choice across five fundamental ISP dimensions: gain, sensor noise, chromatic response, tonal response, and bit depth. Our analysis reveals that RAW-to-RGB processing materially shapes both action prediction and manipulation success, with different ISP dimensions exerting substantially different effects. Guided by these findings, we introduce RawVLA, a streaming neural ISP that adaptively renders RAW observations for frozen VLA policies while concentrating its capacity on the imaging factors relevant to embodied behavior. We further present RawVLA-Bench, a RAW-domain manipulation benchmark to expose image processing as an explicit evaluation variable across clean and adverse acquisition conditions. Experiments on RawVLA-Bench show that RawVLA preserves performance under standard conditions while substantially improving robustness under degraded imaging, establishing adaptive RAW processing as an effective interface between physical cameras and embodied policies.

</details>


### [101] [Wrench-ACT: Enhancing Robot Policies for Contact Rich Behavior Using Direct Wrench Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37552)
*Johannes Hechtl,Yannik Blei,Simon Ball,Reihaneh Mirjalili,Michael Krawez,Seongjin Bien,Philipp Schmitt,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以力旋量(wrench)为唯一动作输出的模仿学习策略，直接驱动力控制器，在五个接触丰富操作任务上匹配或超越基于位置的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作本质上需要主动调节交互力，但主流机器人操作学习多将动作表示为目标位置/位姿；引入力传感的方法也仅把力作为观测，或把力预测与混合力控制器结合，未能让策略直接以力为动作输出。

Method: 以 Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) 为基座架构，在双边力反馈遥操作采集的力旋量演示数据上训练单任务模型；策略以wrench为唯一动作输出，由纯力控制器直接执行。

Result: 在五个接触丰富操作任务上，wrench策略全部匹配或优于基于位置的基线，增益大小随任务对刻意力调节的需求程度而变化；跨条件消融表明，双边数据采集接口与wrench动作空间各自独立贡献性能。

Conclusion: 力域模仿学习的关键在于数据采集方式，力反馈遥操作能捕捉操作者的刻意力调节，从而提升策略性能；纯力控制器的wrench动作表示是可行且有效的范式。

Abstract: While contact-rich manipulation requires deliberate regulation of interaction forces, recent approaches to robot manipulation learning predominantly represent actions as target positions or poses. Even methods that incorporate force sensing either use it solely as an observation or, when predicting forces as part of the output, rely on a hybrid force controller. In this paper, we propose an imitation learning policy that predicts wrenches as its sole action output for direct use by a pure force controller. Our studies suggest that force-domain imitation learning depends critically on data collection, with force-feedback teleoperation improving policy performance by capturing the operator's deliberate force regulation. Using Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) as the base architecture, we train single-task models on bilateral wrench demonstrations and evaluate them on five contact-rich manipulation tasks. The wrench policy matches or outperforms position-based baselines across all tasks, with gains varying according to the degree of deliberate force regulation each task requires. Cross-condition ablations show that the bilateral data collection interface and the wrench action space each contribute independently to performance. To support further research, we will release over 1000 wrench-action demonstrations spanning these tasks on a companion website upon publication.

</details>


### [102] [Risk-Aware Semantic Grounding for Trustworthy LLM-Based Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37554)
*Łukasz Sobczak,Nur Keleşoğlu,Sławomir Piotr Nowak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种风险感知语义接地框架，将LLM机器人规划的语义接地可靠性建模为多维风险估计问题（模糊、幻觉、语义冲突），据此决定执行、澄清或拒绝指令，并构建TRUST-NAV基准验证其在模糊检测与语义冲突拒绝上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用作机器人导航的高层规划器，但当指令存在歧义、环境不支持或语义不一致时，其输出可能不可靠。现有基于LLM的规划器主要优化规划生成，忽视了"何时不应执行"的可靠性判断问题。

Method: 作者将语义接地可靠性形式化为多维风险估计问题，提出风险感知语义接地框架，在规划之前显式建模接地不确定性，包括歧义风险、幻觉风险和语义冲突风险，从而使系统能够决定是执行指令、请求澄清还是拒绝指令。同时构建了TRUST-NAV基准，包含标准导航任务与诱发风险的指令场景。

Result: 实验显示，传统LLM规划器在有效导航任务上表现强劲，而所提框架在模糊检测和语义冲突拒绝方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: 可信的机器人规划不应仅以任务完成度来评价，还应评估系统识别"何时不应执行"的能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as high-level planners in robot navigation, but their outputs may become unreliable when instructions are ambiguous, unsupported by the environment, or semantically inconsistent. This paper presents a Risk-Aware Semantic Grounding framework for trustworthy LLM-based robot planning. Unlike existing LLM-based planners that primarily optimize plan generation, we formulate semantic grounding reliability as a multi-dimensional risk estimation problem. The proposed architecture explicitly models grounding uncertainty through ambiguity, hallucination and semantic-conflict risks before planning occurs, enabling the system to decide whether to execute the instruction, request clarification, or reject it. To evaluate the approach, we introduce TRUST-NAV, a benchmark containing both standard navigation tasks and risk-inducing instruction scenarios. Experimental results show that while conventional LLM planners achieve strong performance on valid navigation tasks, the proposed framework substantially improves ambiguity detection and semantic conflict rejection. These findings suggest that trustworthy robot planning should be evaluated not only by task completion, but also by the ability to recognize when execution should not occur.

</details>


### [103] [RoboFin3D: A Sim-to-Real Platform for Robotic Surface Finishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37560)
*Haowei Wen,Shangtao Li,Vaibhav Sanjay,Philip Huang,Jiaoyang Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboFin3D，一个基于Isaac Sim与Newton物理引擎的sim-to-real打磨/砂磨仿真平台，利用SDF建模工件几何变化并支持实时接触计算与渲染，另设表面场刻画砂磨外观演变；并提出WeldGen在8,918个真实工件网格上生成焊道。参数经真实实验标定后，仿真可提升感知模型性能：仅用仿真数据微调SAM2将未打磨区域分割IoU从77.15%提升至84.47%，合成与真实数据联合训练达97.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人表面精加工中的打磨与砂磨研究依赖物理试验，成本高且消耗工件，导致实验难以复现。因此需要一个廉价、可重复的物理仿真平台来支撑相关实验与数据生成。

Method: 构建RoboFin3D平台，基于Isaac Sim与Newton物理引擎实现物理驱动的打磨与砂磨仿真。采用有符号距离场（SDF）表示工件变化几何，无需中间网格即可完成接触计算、实时更新与渲染；另用独立表面场建模砂磨过程中表面外观的渐进变化。提出WeldGen焊道采样模块，在8,918个真实工件网格上生成焊道以提供多样化仿真资产。仿真参数基于真实实验结果标定。

Result: 评估表明仿真与真实世界具有较高保真度；仿真生成数据可用于提升感知模型性能。仅使用仿真数据微调SAM2，未打磨区域分割IoU由77.15%提升至84.47%；合成与真实数据联合训练进一步提升至97.41%。

Conclusion: RoboFin3D提供了一个可复现、低成本的打磨与砂磨物理仿真平台，兼具几何与外观建模能力及多样化资产生成能力，并经真实实验标定验证其保真度；同时证明仿真数据能有效增强真实场景下的感知模型表现。

Abstract: Grinding and sanding are fundamental processes in industrial robotic surface finishing. However, physical trials are expensive and consume workpieces, making reproducible experiments difficult. We present RoboFin3D, a sim-to-real platform built on Isaac Sim and the Newton physics engine, that provides physics-based grinding and sanding simulation for cheap and repeatable robotic surface finishing experiments. RoboFin3D utilizes a signed distance field (SDF) to model the changing geometry of the workpiece, enabling contact computation, live updates and rendering without an intermediate mesh. It additionally uses a separate surface field to model progressive surface appearance change during sanding. We also introduce WeldGen, a weld sampling module, to generate weld beads on 8,918 real-world workpiece meshes for providing diverse simulation assets. The simulation parameters are calibrated on real experimental results and our evaluation demonstrates our simulation's fidelity against the real world. We also demonstrate that simulation-generated data can be used to improve the performance of perception models. Simulation-only fine-tuning of SAM2 improves IoU for segmentation of unsanded regions from 77.15% to 84.47%, while combined synthetic and real training reaches 97.41%.

</details>


### [104] [RoboHarn-Evo: Evolving Hierarchical Physical Knowledge for Self-Improving Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37583)
*Shifeng Bao,Fanding Huang,Yihan Lin,Youhe Feng,Guanlin Li,Chen Zhao,Yang Li,Jiawei He,Cheng Chi,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboHarn-Evo 双环框架，从物理交互经验中演化分层物理知识（HPK），在不更新基座模型的情况下提升视觉语言模型的机器人长程操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型虽能协调长程机器人操作，但其任务推理成功与否仍取决于局部物理交互是否产生预期效果；本文关注如何通过重复交互改进这一能力，且不更新基座模型。

Method: RoboHarn-Evo 双环框架从物理经验中演化分层物理知识（HPK）。HPK 耦合两级可复用知识：任务知识（Task Knowledge）刻画应执行哪个子任务及何时完成，动作知识（Action Knowledge）刻画物体相对几何策略及其物理效果。执行时，智能体在对应决策层级检索知识，并在任务目标下将其落地到当前场景；跨回合利用物理反馈修正历史知识、更新其适用性并组织可复用条目以供后续检索。

Result: 在 RMBench 上，HPK 使不同智能体模型的平均成功率最高提升 24.2 个百分点；经 80 次交互 rollout，GPT-5.5 的留出成功率从 48.3% 升至 75.0%，GPT-6 从 70.0% 升至 88.3%。RoboHarn-Evo 可修正超过 83% 的历史知识错误，同时保留 95.8% 的有效知识，并从 RMBench 零样本迁移至 RoboDojo，分别取得 35.0 和 25.0 个百分点的增益。

Conclusion: 物理交互可被积累为可复用知识，从而持续改善后续机器人操作表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models can coordinate long-horizon robot manipulation, yet successful task reasoning still depends on whether local physical interactions produce the intended effects. We study how repeated interaction can improve this capability without updating the base model. We introduce RoboHarn-Evo, a dual-loop harness that evolves Hierarchical Physical Knowledge (HPK) from physical experience. HPK couples two levels of reusable knowledge: Task Knowledge captures which subtask should be executed and when it is complete, while Action Knowledge captures object-relative geometric strategies and their physical effects. During execution, the agent retrieves knowledge at the corresponding decision level and grounds it in the current scene under the task goal. Across episodes, physical feedback is used to revise historical knowledge, update its applicability, and organize reusable entries for subsequent retrieval. Experiments on RMBench show that HPK improves average success by up to 24.2 percentage points across different agent models. With 80 interaction rollouts, held-out success rises from 48.3% to 75.0% for GPT-5.5 and from 70.0% to 88.3% for GPT-6. RoboHarn-Evo also resolves over 83% of historical knowledge errors while retaining 95.8% of valid knowledge, and transfers zero-shot from RMBench to RoboDojo with gains of 35.0 and 25.0 percentage points. These results demonstrate that physical interaction can be accumulated into reusable knowledge for improving subsequent manipulation.

</details>


### [105] [Credit-Guided Policy Improvement for Test-time Adaptive Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37591)
*Yang Li,Sijia Zhang,Yihan Li,Aming WU,Zihao Zhang,Ziju Han,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Credit-Guided Policy Improvement (CGPI)，通过动作引发的观测转移恢复有符号、参照相对的决策信用，在冻结预训练导航策略的前提下提出轻量适应更新，并依据历史信用支持交互进行验证，仅在支持时保留更新，否则回滚，在多个VLN基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA-VLN方法依赖预测不确定性、轨迹级反馈或累积适应经验来纠正分布偏移导致的偏离航向决策，但这些信号无法直接揭示某次执行动作是否支持指令导向的目标进展，且看似合理的纠正信号也不保证策略更新的可靠性。核心挑战在于：识别支持目标导向改进的交互，并判断由此产生的更新是否值得保留。

Method: 作者观察到每个执行动作都会引发即时观测转移，从而提供其局部后果的证据。基于此，CGPI从动作引发的观测转移中恢复有符号、参照相对的决策信用，无需外部结果反馈。预训练导航策略保持冻结，CGPI利用该信用提出轻量适应更新，并与先前信用支持的交互进行验证；更新仅在获得支持时保留，否则回滚。

Result: CGPI在所评估的VLN基准和导航主干网络上取得一致增益；定性机器人试验进一步展示了零样本仿真到现实迁移的可行性。

Conclusion: CGPI通过动作引发的观测转移提取决策信用，并对轻量更新进行信用验证与回滚，为TTA-VLN提供了一种不依赖外部反馈的可靠策略改进方案，兼顾性能提升与sim-to-real迁移潜力。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation for vision-language navigation (TTA-VLN) enables pretrained policies to adapt online to unseen environments using only test-time observations and interaction history. However, distribution shifts can distort local action preferences and lead to off-course decisions. Existing methods rely on predictive uncertainty, trajectory-level feedback, or accumulated adaptation experience to correct such deviations. These signals, however, do not directly reveal whether an executed action supports instruction-guided progress toward the goal. Moreover, a plausible corrective signal does not guarantee a reliable policy update. The key challenge is thus twofold: identifying interactions that support goal-directed improvement and determining whether the resulting updates are worth retaining. We observe that each executed action induces an immediate observation transition, providing evidence of its local consequences. Based on this insight, we propose Credit-Guided Policy Improvement (CGPI), which recovers signed, reference-relative decision credit from action-induced observation transitions without external outcome feedback. With the pretrained navigation policy frozen, CGPI uses this credit to propose lightweight adaptation updates and verifies them against prior credit-supported interactions. Updates are retained only when supported and rolled back otherwise. CGPI achieves consistent gains across the evaluated VLN benchmarks and navigation backbones, while qualitative robot trials further illustrate the feasibility of zero-shot sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [106] [BlenDAgger: Blended Shared Control for Interactive Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37599)
*Cailyn Smith,Geoffrey Sun,Henny Admoni,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Blended DAgger (BlenDAgger)，在干预过程中用共享控制混合策略与演示者的动作来采集数据，从而提升模仿学习策略的自主性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略常依赖人类纠正数据训练，但遥操作提供纠正负担重，且人类演示者并非总是最优。需要一种既能减轻演示负担、又能提升策略自主性能的数据采集方式。

Method: BlenDAgger：在人机共享控制框架下，于干预期间将策略动作与演示者动作进行混合（blend），而非典型的人为门控纠正（HG-DAgger）中完全由人类接管。在五个操作任务上验证（2 个真实世界、3 个仿真）。

Result: 在两个真实任务上，自主性能比 HG-DAgger 提升 30 个百分点以上；控制切换更平滑（提升 57%），轨迹与训练数据相似度更高（提升 14%）；用户研究（n=14，两个真实任务）显示数据采集更快（BF=13.32），主观感受无显著差异。

Conclusion: 混合共享控制相较传统完全遥操作干预方法，能带来更高的自主性能，是微调机器人策略的有效数据采集范式。

Abstract: Robot policies are frequently trained from human corrections, yet teleoperating a robot to provide corrections is burdensome, and human demonstrators are not always optimal. We propose Blended DAgger (BlenDAgger), an approach for collecting data to train imitation learning policies by using shared control to blend the policy's and demonstrator's actions during interventions. By blending human and policy actions, we aim to improve the autonomous performance of manipulation policies. We validate our approach across five manipulation tasks, two in the real world and three in simulation. Our approach achieves higher autonomous performance by 30 or more percentage points on two real-world tasks compared to a typical human-gated correction approach (HG-DAgger). We also investigate the advantages of BlenDAgger that allow for higher autonomous performance, finding that BlenDAgger results in 57% smoother transitions between policy control and human interventions, and 14% higher trajectory similarity to the training data. In a user study (n=14) on two real-world tasks, we find that BlenDAgger results in faster data collection (BF=13.32), and we do not find a difference in subjective perceptions. These results show that blended shared control leads to higher autonomous performance compared to typical methods for fine-tuning robot policies from fully teleoperated interventions.

</details>


### [107] [When to Adapt: Multi-Signal Domain Shift Detection for Efficient Training-Free Adaptation in Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37602)
*Michele Antonazzi,Alejandra C. Hernandez,José Araujo,Olov Andersson,Patric Jensfelt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多信号域偏移检测方法，用于开放词汇分割的无训练持续测试时自适应，仅在需要时触发适配，在保持精度的同时大幅减少适配次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在长期任务中面临环境变化导致的域偏移，视觉基础模型虽提升开放词汇语义分割，但未经适配会显著性能下降；现有无训练域适配逐帧执行，在资源受限的机器人硬件上不实用。

Method: 针对开放词汇分割的无训练持续测试时自适应（TF-CTTA），利用相邻帧的时间一致性，监测并融合三个互补的域偏移方面——视觉变化、适配器失配与语义漂移，构建多信号域偏移检测，仅在必要时触发适配。

Result: 在包含室内外环境的基准及真实机器人数据上验证，方法在保持分割精度的同时显著减少适配次数，使无训练适配对长期真实机器人部署变得切实可行。

Conclusion: 多信号域偏移检测可有效降低TF-CTTA的适配冗余，兼顾精度与效率，为长期真实环境下资源受限机器人的开放词汇分割部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robust and reliable perception is essential for autonomous robots operating in real-world environments, particularly in long-term missions where environmental conditions may change significantly over time. Although recent advances in Visual Foundation Models (VFMs) have improved open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, these models can still suffer from domain shift, which can significantly degrade performance if they are not adapted to the current environment. Training-free domain adaptation is a relevant paradigm for adaptation, consisting of adjusting the model online using lightweight adapters. Recent approaches apply this on a per-frame basis, which is impractical for deployments on resource-constrained robotic hardware. To tackle this, we propose a multi-signal domain shift detection method for training-free continual test-time adaptation (TF-CTTA) in open-vocabulary segmentation. Our method leverages temporal coherence across consecutive frames by monitoring and combining complementary aspects of domain shift (visual change, adapter mismatch, and semantic drift) to trigger adaptation only when needed. We validate our approach on a benchmark including indoor and outdoor environments and using real robotic data. We demonstrate that our approach maintains segmentation accuracy while substantially reducing adaptations, making training-free adaptation practical and feasible for long-term, real-world robotic deployments.

</details>


### [108] [Cascaded consensus splitting for multi-branch contingency games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37612)
*Bastien Lechardoy,Pau de las Heras Molins,Thibault Lahire,Laurent Pautet,David Filliat,David Fridovich-Keil,Georgios Bakirtzis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出多分支应急架构，使意图不确定性可在不同分支时刻逐步揭示，并给出基于ADMM的场景级并行求解器，在三智能体紧耦合交互中优于传统单分支且滚动时域求解更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有应急博弈采用单一分支时刻，简化了不同智能体意图在不同时间揭示的交互；同时计算量随智能体与意图数量快速增长，因为所有场景相关的最优反应需联合求解。

Method: 引入多分支应急架构，允许意图不确定性来源在不同分支时刻逐步解决，使规划轨迹适应意图的异步揭示；并开发基于ADMM的求解器，利用场景级并行性。

Result: 在三智能体紧耦合交互实验中，所提架构优于传统单分支，并实现了更低的平均滚动时域求解时间。

Conclusion: 多分支应急架构结合ADMM并行求解可有效处理异步意图揭示，提升交互规划性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Contingency games enable agents to anticipate and plan for other agents' hypothetical intents by constructing trajectories with a shared prefix and intent-dependent branches. While contingency games capture intent uncertainty, existing formulations rely on a single branching time, oversimplifying interactions in which different agents' intentions are revealed at different times. Moreover, the computational cost of such problems grows rapidly with the number of agents and intents, as all scenario-dependent best responses must be solved jointly.
