This study develops a provider-county performance assessment framework by linking national Centers for Medicare & Medicaid Services (CMS) hospice data and Consumer Assessment of Healthcare Providers and Systems (CAHPS) Hospice Survey outcomes with county measures of rurality, social vulnerability, health burden, and workforce and health-resource context. The adjusted analysis includes 2,928 providers in 1,078 counties and combines geographic mapping, blockwise regression, six secondary CAHPS outcomes, and eight sensitivity analyses. Adding county context increased adjusted R-squared from 0.077 to 0.202. After full adjustment, for-profit hospices had overall caregiver ratings 3.832 percentage points lower than nonprofit hospices. This negative association appeared across all six secondary CAHPS domains and remained significant in every sensitivity specification. Higher county social vulnerability was also associated with poorer caregiver experience, although its magnitude depended partly on the specification of community health burden. These findings show that county context materially improves hospice performance assessment but does not eliminate the ownership difference. The framework supports context-aware monitoring, peer comparison, and targeted quality improvement.

</details>


### [223] [Feeling Left Behind? Territorial Disadvantage, Well-Being and Cohesion across European Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37866)
*Stefano Maria Iacus,Giuseppe Porro,Haodong Qi,Devika Jain*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究结合十年期区域指标与地理定位社交媒体主观测度，覆盖28个欧洲国家1200多个NUTS-3区域，揭示"落后地区"的经济人口识别与其福祉、社会凝聚力之间存在不均衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上"落后地区"主要依据经济、人口和可达性等客观指标识别，但这些指标可能与居民福祉和社会凝聚力不一致，可能掩盖区域劣势的不同形态与演化轨迹。

Method: 整合十年（2013-2023）领土指标与基于地理定位社交媒体数据的主观测度，覆盖28个欧洲国家1200多个NUTS-3区域；采用区域画像分析与组内-组间面板模型。

Result: 区域间关系不均衡：劣势区域可呈现截然不同的福祉与凝聚力水平；国家背景会改变跨国关联；区域间差异与区域内变化趋势相背离。

Conclusion: 总体性聚合指标掩盖了区域劣势的不同形态与轨迹，需结合主观测度以更全面识别落后地区。

Abstract: Left-behind places are usually identified using economic, demographic and accessibility indicators, but these may not align with well-being and social cohesion. We combine ten years of territorial indicators with subjective measures derived from georeferenced social-media data for over 1,200 NUTS-3 regions in 28 European countries (2013-2023). Regional profiles and within-between panel models reveal uneven relationships: disadvantaged regions can display contrasting levels of well-being and cohesion, national context alters cross-country associations, and between-region differences diverge from within-region change. The findings show that aggregate measures conceal distinct forms and trajectories of territorial disadvantage.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [Advancing Fuzzy-Sphere CFTs: $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-Rotation-Resolving Exact Diagonalization, 3D Ising Heavy Primaries, and Quasi-Hole-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35960)
*Zheng Zhou,Yin-Chen He*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用对称性与希尔伯特空间结构改进模糊球正则化框架，实现对三维Ising CFT的高效精确对角化并揭示大量新算子及新物理结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊球正则化已成为研究三维共形场论（CFT）的有力框架，但以往计算成本高、对称性利用不足，且存在模糊球结果与bootstrap结果之间的分歧，亟需更高效且更精确的方法来推进该框架。

Method: 发展了一种解析完整SO(3)旋转对称性的精确对角化算法，将计算成本降低数百倍；并对三维Ising CFT进行数值求解，识别主算子并分析其波函数结构；同时构造由准空穴态张成的约化希尔伯特空间以研究涉及分数量子霍尔态的临界点。

Result: 在三维Ising CFT中识别出约100个主算子，包括大量此前未知的重标量主算子，标度维数最高约达Δ≈16.5；解决了关于奇宇称标量主算子的模糊球与bootstrap之间的分歧；发现一族与φ^4拉氏描述中φ^n算子相关的标量主算子（始于identity、σ、ε、ε′、σ′），其波函数具半经典结构，与球面保面积微分同胚不变态密切相关；并表明涉及分数量子霍尔态的临界点可在由Laughlin态等准空穴态张成的显著约化希尔伯特空间中研究。

Conclusion: 该工作通过对称性与希尔伯特空间结构的巧妙利用，大幅提升了模糊球框架的效率与解析能力，不仅澄清了三维Ising CFT的算子谱与既有分歧，还揭示了与保面积微分同胚及分数量子霍尔态相关的新结构，为研究CFT与拓扑有序相耦合提供了通用策略。

Abstract: Fuzzy-sphere regularization has recently emerged as a powerful framework for studying three-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs). In this work, we exploit symmetry and Hilbert-space structures to substantially advance this framework. We develop an exact-diagonalization algorithm that resolves the full $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ rotational symmetry, reducing computational costs by factors of several hundred. For the three-dimensional Ising CFT, we identify around 100 primary operators, including a large number of previously unknown heavy scalar primaries up to scaling dimension $Δ\approx16.5$. We also resolve the tension between previous fuzzy-sphere and bootstrap results regarding the parity-odd scalar primary. Furthermore, we identify a branch of scalar primaries associated with $φ^n$ operators in the $φ^4$ Lagrangian description, beginning with the identity, $σ$, $ε$, $ε'$, and $σ'$. Their wave functions exhibit a semi-classical structure closely related to states invariant under area-preserving diffeomorphisms of the sphere. Finally, we show that critical points involving fractional quantum Hall states can be studied within a substantially reduced Hilbert space spanned by quasi-hole states of the corresponding model quantum Hall wave functions like the Laughlin state. This suggests a general strategy for exploring CFTs intertwined with topologically ordered phases.

</details>


### [225] [Spectroscopy of phonon-coupled integer and fractional Chern insulators: emergence of polarons and chirality deficit of graviton mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36027)
*Min Long,Yuzhu Wang,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用引入局域基优化的密度矩阵重整化群方法，研究平带Haldane模型与动态Holstein声子耦合体系在整数与分数填充下的行为，发现声子主要通过"缀饰"电中性集体模式对低能物理施加影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子-声子耦合可使Chern绝缘体（CI）与分数Chern绝缘体（FCI）失稳并转变为电荷有序相，但CI与FCI失稳是否具有共同的微观机制尚不清楚。作者旨在澄清这一问题。

Method: 在平带Haldane模型中引入动态Holstein声子，于整数填充与分数填充下，采用带局域基优化的密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG-LBO）进行计算，考察电中性集体模式的低能行为。

Result: 声子将激子与磁转子分别缀饰为"激子极化子"与"磁转子极化子"；这些复合模式在逼近相变时显著软化，其能量最小值对应的动量预示了电荷有序相的调制波矢。声子谱因此继承激子与磁转子的色散特征，成为这些中性激发的晶格探针。在FCI相中，电子-声子耦合还增强引力子响应中相反手性的谱权重，产生手性缺失。

Conclusion: 结果给出了CI与FCI中声子驱动失稳的统一激发图像，并确立晶格动力学作为其电中性集体模式的探测手段。

Abstract: Strong electron-phonon coupling can destabilize both Chern insulators (CIs) and fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) in favor of charge order, but whether the instabilities in CI and FCI share a common microscopic mechanism remains unclear. In this paper, we address this question by studying a flat-band Haldane model coupled to dynamical Holstein phonons at integer and fractional fillings using density-matrix renormalization group with local basis optimization. We find that the low-energy effects of phonons are dominated by their dressing of charge-neutral collective modes. In the CI and FCI, phonons dress excitons and magnetorotons into composite modes that we identify as exciton polarons and magnetoroton polarons, respectively. These modes soften strongly upon approaching the transition, while the momenta of their energy minima anticipate the ordering wave vectors of the charge-ordered phases. The phonon spectrum, in turn, acquires dispersive features inherited from the exciton and magnetoroton modes, providing direct lattice signatures of these neutral excitations. Within the FCI phase, electron-phonon coupling also enhances the opposite-chirality spectral weight of the graviton response, thereby resulting in a chirality deficit. Our results provide a unified excitation-based picture of phonon-driven instabilities in CIs and FCIs and establish lattice dynamics as a probe of their neutral collective modes.

</details>


### [226] [Excitonic Stripe Order in the Two-Orbital Hubbard-Kanamori Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36051)
*Rafael D. Soares,Luke Staszewski,Chunhan Feng,Alexander Wietek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在双轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型中，利用无约束实空间Hartree-Fock结合RPA不稳定性分析，揭示了激子条纹这一新型对称破缺态及其稳定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子凝聚与条纹形成是电子关联的两种不同表现：激子序天然出现在多轨道系统中，条纹序则是掺杂关联电子模型的显著特征。本文旨在探究多轨道体系中激子序与条纹序的结合，即"激子条纹"这一类比态。

Method: 在方格子双轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型上，采用无约束实空间Hartree-Fock计算，并辅以随机相近似（RPA）不稳定性分析，考察Kanamori相互作用如何选择不同激子通道。激子条纹由轨道间粒子-空穴相干性的空间调制表征，在轨道宇称对称极限下伴随相对轨道Z₂对称性的自发破缺。

Result: Hund交换的符号决定凝聚体的自旋特征：铁磁Hund耦合倾向于三重态激子序，反铁磁Hund耦合稳定单态激子序。掺杂后，公度激子密度波演化为非公度织构，包括激子条纹，并在三重态区出现螺旋激子密度波。晶体场劈裂通过改变轨道间嵌套强烈重组激子不稳定性，即便无对跳跃也可稳定三重态激子序。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了激子条纹作为多轨道关联系统一种独立对称破缺态的地位，并指出了其稳定化的微观途径。

Abstract: Excitonic condensation and stripe formation are two distinct manifestations of electronic correlations. While excitonic order naturally arises in multi-orbital systems, stripe order is a prominent feature of doped correlated-electron models. Here, we investigate an excitonic analogue of stripe order in the two--orbital Hubbard-Kanamori model on the square lattice, characterized by a spatial modulation of inter-orbital particle-hole coherence and, in the orbital-parity-symmetric limit, spontaneous breaking of a relative orbital $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry. Using unrestricted real-space Hartree-Fock calculations complemented by random-phase-approximation instability analysis, we determine how the Kanamori interactions select different excitonic channels. The sign of Hund's exchange controls the spin character of the condensate: ferromagnetic Hund coupling favors triplet excitonic order, whereas antiferromagnetic Hund coupling stabilizes singlet excitonic order. Upon doping, commensurate excitonic density waves develop incommensurate textures, including excitonic stripes and, in the triplet sector, spiral excitonic density waves. We further show that crystal-field splitting strongly reorganizes the excitonic instability by modifying inter-orbital nesting and can stabilize triplet excitonic order even in the absence of pair hopping. Our results establish excitonic stripes as a distinct symmetry-broken state of multi-orbital correlated systems and identify microscopic routes for their stabilization.

</details>


### [227] [Magnetic excitations of the strongly-anisotropic triangular XXZ model: a projected spin-product state approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36140)
*Achille Mauri,Frédéric Mila*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“投影”相干态近似方法，用于研究强Ising型各向异性下的自旋-1/2三角晶格XXZ模型，并与钴酸盐KCSO的实验结果进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验激发了对强Ising型各向异性自旋-1/2三角晶格XXZ模型的基态性质与激发谱的研究兴趣，需要更有效的理论近似方法来解释实验现象。

Method: 构建“投影”相干态近似：将自旋乘积态投影到三角Ising反铁磁体的低能构型上；在此近似下讨论基态有序结构与纵向磁场下的磁化曲线；通过含时变分原理导出运动方程并线性化，以获得激发谱，从而在强约束Hilbert空间内描述类自旋波激发。

Result: 零场下，变分近似给出非常平坦的准简并态能landscape，导致激发谱中出现低能赝Goldstone模。与钴酸盐KCSO的实验测量对比，该近似相比线性自旋波理论有显著改进，但在赝Goldstone能隙值以及低场下M点附近的色散上仍存在偏差。

Conclusion: 该“投影”相干态近似能显著优于线性自旋波理论，为强Ising型各向异性三角XXZ模型的激发谱提供了自然描述，但仍需进一步改进以精确匹配实验中赝Goldstone能隙与M点附近色散。

Abstract: Recent experiments have sparked interest on the ground-state properties and on the excitation spectrum of the spin-$1/2$ triangular XXZ model with strong Ising-like anisotropy. In this work, we introduce and analyze a ``projected'' coherent-state approximation to the model, constructed by projecting spin-product states onto the low-energy configurations of the triangular Ising antiferromagnet. Within this approximation, we discuss the structure of the ground-state ordering and the magnetization curve under a longitudinal field. We then discuss the excitation spectrum by linearizing equations of motion deriving from a time-dependent variational principle. This leads to a natural description of spin-wave-like excitations within the strongly constrained Hilbert space. At zero field, the variational approximation leads to a very flat landscape of quasi-degenerate states, which results in a low-energy pseudo-Goldstone mode in the excitation spectrum. We compare our results to experimental measurements on the cobaltite K$_{2}$Co(SeO$_{3}$)$_{2}$ (KCSO). Our approximation leads to a dramatic improvement as compared to linear spin-wave theory. However, there remain deviations in the value of the pseudo-Goldstone gap and in the dispersion near $M$ points at low field.

</details>


### [228] [Magnetic properties and Fermi-surface topology of kagome magnet HoV$_6$Sn$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36258)
*Tucker Beekmann,Ganesh Pokharel,Wyatt Edward Ackerman,Stephen D. Wilson,David E. Graf,Keshav Shrestha*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 综合磁化测量、TDO量子振荡实验与第一性原理计算，研究了kagome磁体HoV6Sn6的磁各向异性、多带费米面拓扑及电子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究kagome磁体HoV6Sn6的磁性与费米面拓扑，理解其磁各向异性来源及低能电子性质，并与理论计算相互验证。

Method: 采用磁化测量和隧穿二极管振荡器（TDO）测量（磁场至41.5 T、温度低至0.35 K）研究磁性与量子振荡；通过角度和温度依赖测量确定费米面几何与回旋有效质量；结合第一性原理计算能带、态密度与费米面。

Result: 高温呈Curie-Weiss行为，Tc≈2.4 K以下出现长程磁序；低温场依赖磁化显示强各向异性，面外/面内饱和磁矩约9.5和7 μB/Ho。TDO显示多频量子振荡，频率最高约9 kT。计算得到平带、van Hove奇点和费米能级附近Dirac型交叉，费米能级态密度以V-d轨道为主；计算支持Ho磁矩沿c轴的铁磁基态，计算量子振荡频率与实验总体吻合。

Conclusion: 实验与理论结合揭示了HoV6Sn6的磁各向异性、低能电子结构（由V kagome层主导）及其多带费米面拓扑。

Abstract: We report a study of the magnetic properties and Fermi-surface topology of the kagome magnet HoV$_6$Sn$_6$ using magnetization and tunnel-diode-oscillator (TDO) measurements. The temperature-dependent magnetization exhibits Curie-Weiss behavior at high temperatures and reveals long-range magnetic order below $T_c$ $\approx$ 2.4 K. Below Tc, the field-dependent magnetization reveals pronounced magnetic anisotropy, with saturation moments of $\approx$ 9.5 and 7 $μ_B$ /Ho for fields applied along the out-of-plane and in-plane directions, respectively. To probe the Fermi surface, we perform TDO measurements in magnetic fields up to 41.5 T and at temperatures down to 0.35 K. The TDO signal exhibits well-defined quantum oscillations with multiple frequencies extending up to $\sim$ 9 kT. Angular- and temperature-dependent measurements are used to determine the Fermi-surface geometry and cyclotron effective masses, respectively. To elucidate the electronic structure, we perform first-principles calculations of the electronic bands, density of states (DOS), and Fermi surface. The calculated band structure exhibits flat-band features, van Hove singularities, and Dirac-like crossings near the Fermi level. The DOS near the Fermi level is dominated by the V-d orbitals, indicating that the V kagome layers play a major role in determining the low-energy electronic properties of HoV6Sn6. Our calculations further support a ferromagnetic ground state with the Ho moments oriented along the c axis, consistent with neutron-scattering measurements. The calculated quantum-oscillation frequencies from the Fermi-surface pockets of HoV6Sn6 are in overall good agreement with the experimentally observed frequencies. Our combined experimental and theoretical results provide insight into the magnetic anisotropy and electronic structure of HoV$_6$Sn$_6$ and reveal its multiband Fermi-surface topology.

</details>


### [229] [Quantum metric induced nonlinear transport in the hidden loop-current phase of kagome metal RbV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36353)
*Jia-Chen Shi,Shupeng Xu,Utkarsh Khandelwal,Nashra Pistawala,Luminita Harnagea,Steven J. May,Ritesh Agarwal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用三阶非线性输运探测RbV₃Sb₅中~35 K的隐藏低温相，发现纵向立方响应出现拐点和各向异性、横向立方响应出现奇磁组分，二者同温起始，标志时间反演对称破缺的电子重构，并揭示量子度规多极的直接贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome金属AV₃Sb₅中被提出的隐藏低温相（可能具有环流序）缺乏明确的实验证据，其信号微弱且间接，亟需更敏感的探测手段。

Method: 对RbV₃Sb₅开展三阶非线性输运测量，分析纵向与横向立方响应随温度和角度的变化，并结合环流电荷密度波模型进行理论解释。

Result: 在约35 K处，纵向立方响应出现强拐点及显著方向各向异性，明显偏离常规弛豫时间标度；横向立方响应同时获得奇磁（磁场奇）组分。二者的同步起始表明三阶谐波响应是时间反演对称破缺电子重构的敏感标记；量子度规多极直接贡献于纵向三阶响应，环流电荷密度波模型中其方向选择性增强可再现观测到的角度重构。

Conclusion: 高阶非线性输运可将电子对称性与量子度规的细微变化转化为可测量的电学信号，为研究量子材料中的复杂电子相提供有效手段。

Abstract: A hidden low-temperature phase with possible loop-current order has been proposed in the kagome metals AV$_3$Sb$_5$ (A=K, Rb or Cs), but its experimental signatures remain subtle and indirect. Here, we use third-order nonlinear transport to probe this hidden phase in RbV$_3$Sb$_5$. At ~35 K, the longitudinal cubic response develops a strong kink and pronounced directional anisotropy with a strong departure from common relaxation time scaling, while the transverse cubic response acquires a magnetic field-odd component at the same temperature. Their coincident onset identifies the third-harmonic response as a sensitive marker of the low-temperature electronic reconstruction with time-reversal broken symmetry. Crucially, we find that the quantum metric quadrupole contributes directly to the longitudinal third-order response. In a loop-current charge-density-wave model, its direction selective enhancement captures the observed angular reconstruction. Our work shows how higher-order nonlinear transport translates subtle changes in electronic symmetry and quantum metric into measurable electrical signatures in quantum materials that is important for studying complex electronic phases of matter.

</details>


### [230] [Artificial versus Natural Atoms: The uncanny capability of the many-body Schrödinger equation to produce emergent behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36356)
*Constantine Yannouleas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文回顾了25年来“人造原子与分子”物理的理论与实验进展，核心是由局域化费米子或玻色子形成的维格纳分子（WM），即体维格纳晶体的有限量子类比物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限量子体系（如半导体与莫尔过渡金属硫族化物量子点、阱中囚禁的冷原子或离子）中强关联效应的物理机制尚未被系统总结，且其在量子信息与量子计算中的潜力需要统一的理论框架来解释。因此有必要综述维格纳分子物理的进展。

Method: 从多体薛定谔方程（MBSE）出发，在势能主导动能（强关联）、高磁场及快速旋转等条件下求解，分析局域化费米子/玻色子形成的维格纳分子，并综合理论与实验（单阱或多阱、可变构型的人工器件）结果。

Result: 维格纳分子表现出显著量子效应：谱能隙强烈淬灭、出现类似天然分子的转动-振动谱、纠缠以及由外部微扰导致的钉扎效应；在高磁场或快速旋转下，WM可作为分数量子霍尔效应的替代理论。

Conclusion: 维格纳分子物理源于强关联区多体薛定谔方程的解，是有限量子体系（人造原子与分子）中普遍而重要的现象，对基础多体物理和量子信息技术均有重要意义。

Abstract: The paper reviews the theoretical and experimental progress achieved in the last 25 years in understanding the novel physics of artificial atoms and molecules as arising from the formation of Wigner molecules (WMs) of localized (to a stronger or lesser extent) fermionic or bosonic particles, which are finite quantum analogs of the more familiar bulk Wigner crystal. The term artificial atoms, as used here, encompasses a broad range of recently fabricated quantum nanodevices and experimental apparatuses consisting of a finite number of mutually repelling confined particles, including two-dimensional semiconductor and moiré transition metal dichalcogenide quantum dots, as well as trapped ultracold neutral atoms or ions. These nano-sized or micro-sized artificial devices and apparatuses (in single well or multi-well of variable-shape arrangements) hold a great promise for technological applications in the field of quantum information and quantum computers, as well as for advances in fundamental many-body physics. Prominent quantum effects of Wigner molecularization are the strong quenching of the spectral energy gaps, the appearance of rovibational spectra (in analogy with natural molecules), entanglement, and pinning due to an external perturbation. In high magnetic fields or at rapid rotation, WMs provide an alternative theory to the fractional quantum Hall effect. The physics of Wigner molecules is shown to derive from the solutions of the many-body Schrödinger equation (MBSE) in the regime of strong interparticle correlations arising from the dominance of the potential over the kinetic energy, or from a high magnetic field, as well as from a rapid rotation... (continues in the paper).

</details>


### [231] [Probing the electronic structure of $\mathrm{UTe}_2$ with ARPES and high-energy spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36706)
*Shin-ichi Fujimori*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了UTe₂的最新谱学研究，结合动量分辨ARPES及多种X射线谱学手段，指出其电子结构推断具有显著的实验技术依赖性，并支持存在5f²与5f³组态显著混合的中间价态基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTe₂因其非常规超导及可能的自旋三重态、拓扑非平庸配对态而成为研究热点。其核心未解问题是U 5f电子的性质及其如何参与低能准粒子态，需通过谱学手段加以厘清。

Method: 综述动量分辨角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）以及元素与组态敏感的X射线探针，包括X射线吸收谱（XAS）、X射线吸收近边结构（XANES）、X射线磁圆二色（XMCD）、共振X射线发射谱（RXES）和共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS），并将结果与DFT+DMFT等现代电子结构计算相结合。

Result: 发现推断出的电子结构具有显著的实验技术依赖性；综合各谱学结果与DFT+DMFT计算，支持UTe₂中存在5f²与5f³组态显著混合的中间价态基态。

Conclusion: 研究勾勒出将正常态电子结构与超导机理相联系所需的关键实验与理论基准，并强调多技术互补对于理解UTe₂电子结构的重要性。

Abstract: $\mathrm{UTe}_2$ has emerged as one of the most intensively studied strongly correlated materials in recent years owing to its unconventional superconductivity and the possible realization of a spin-triplet, topologically nontrivial pairing state. A central open issue concerns the nature of the $\mathrm{U}\,5f$ electrons and their participation in low-energy quasiparticle states. In this review, we summarize recent spectroscopic studies of $\mathrm{UTe}_2$ using momentum-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and element- and configuration-sensitive X-ray probes, including X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS), X-ray absorption near-edge structure (XANES), X-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD), resonant X-ray emission spectroscopy (RXES), and resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS). A key finding is the pronounced technique dependence of the inferred electronic structure. We synthesize the present spectroscopic picture by integrating these results with modern electronic-structure calculations such as density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT). These findings support an intermediate-valence ground state with significant admixture of $5f^2$ and $5f^3$ configurations in $\mathrm{UTe}_2$, and they delineate key experimental and theoretical benchmarks needed to connect the normal-state electronic structure to the superconducting mechanism.

</details>


### [232] [Quantized Hyperfine Field at an Implanted $μ^+$ Site in PrPb$_3$: Interplay between Localized $f$ Electrons and an Interstitial Charged Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36868)
*T. U. Ito,W. Higemoto,K. Ohishi,N. Nishida,R. H. Heffner,Y. Aoki,A. Amato,T. Onimaru,H. S. Suzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过μSR实验在PrPb₃中首次于f电子化合物观察到自发μ⁺自旋进动，揭示其源于μ⁺与近邻¹⁴¹Pr超精细增强核自旋的耦合，并表明间隙带电粒子改变了局部f电子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究间隙氢样粒子（μ⁺）对局域f电子的影响，理解间隙带电粒子如何改变f电子态及近邻核自旋动力学。

Method: 在PrPb₃中采用μ⁺自旋旋转与弛豫（μSR）技术，观测自发μ⁺自旋进动及其谐波频率，并分析μ⁺自旋与具有Ising型各向异性的近邻¹⁴¹Pr超精细增强核自旋之间的耦合。

Result: 首次在f电子化合物中观测到具有谐波频率的自发μ⁺自旋进动；信号源于μ⁺与近邻¹⁴¹Pr超精细增强核自旋的耦合；近邻¹⁴¹Pr的自旋动力学相比体态¹⁴¹Pr被显著抑制。

Conclusion: 结果表明间隙带电粒子导致PrPb₃中局域f电子态发生改变，表现为近邻Pr核自旋动力学的显著抑制。

Abstract: The local effect of an interstitial hydrogen-like particle on localized $f$ electrons was studied in PrPb$_3$ by means of $μ^+$ spin rotation and relaxation. Spontaneous $μ^+$ spin precession with harmonic frequencies was observed for the first time in $f$ electron compounds. We demonstrate that the signal is derived from a coupling between the $μ^+$ spin and the hyperfine-enhanced nuclear spin of nearest neighbor (nn) $^{141}$Pr with Ising-like anisotropy. The signal also suggests a marked suppression of spin dynamics of the nn $^{141}$Pr in comparison with that of the bulk $^{141}$Pr. These facts strongly indicate modification of the $f$ electronic state due to the interstitial charged particle.

</details>


### [233] [Ergodicity in a hole-dopped Anderson spin insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36936)
*M. S. Bahovadinov,A. A. Markov,G. V. Shlyapnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在一维t-J_XX模型中加入单个空穴，可热化弱无序下的自旋链并导致空穴离域；强磁场下空穴局域化，自旋Anderson绝缘体恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用多体系统在无序中局域化后可能无法自充当热库，而电子系统中电荷与自旋自由度可互为热库。作者因此想探究：对自旋扇区已Anderson局域化的绝缘体进行电荷掺杂（引入空穴）后，其局域化是否稳定。

Method: 采用一维双组分费米子晶格模型，取强在位排斥极限（t-J_XX模型），并引入单个空穴与随机磁场。数值分析弱无序与强无序两种情形下系统的遍历性与空穴局域性质。

Result: 无空穴时自旋扇区在任意弱随机磁场下均局域；加入单个空穴后，在弱无序极限下自旋链被热化，数值结果显示整个系统呈现遍历性且空穴离域；在较强磁场下空穴最终以有限局域长度局域化，Anderson自旋绝缘体状态恢复。

Conclusion: 单空穴可充当热库破坏自旋扇区的局域化，说明电荷掺杂能显著改变Anderson绝缘体的稳定性，并揭示存在由无序强度控制的离域—局域转变。

Abstract: An interacting many-body system being localized in the presence of disorder can fail to serve as its own bath. In an electronic system, the charge and spin degrees of freedom can act as thermal baths for each other. In this paper, we study the stability of an Anderson insulator in the spin sector with respect to charge doping. We consider one-dimensional two-component fermions in a lattice with large onsite repulsion ($t-J_{XX}$ model) and with a single hole in a random magnetic field. In the absence of the hole the spin sector is localized in arbitrarily weak random magnetic fields. We argue that a single hole thermalizes the spin chain in the weak disorder limit. In this regime our numerical results indicate ergodicity of the system as a whole and delocalization of the hole. At larger magnetic fields the hole eventually localizes with a finite localization length, and the Anderson spin insulator is restored.

</details>


### [234] [Evolution of Magnetism in Ce$_4$Ge$_7$ under Magnetic Field and Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37129)
*Kaixin Ye,Qihe Yu,Yongjian Li,Yanan Zhang,Ye Chen,Rui Li,Lin Jiao,M. Smidman,Yongjun Zhang,Yu Liu,Huiqiu Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Ce4Ge7单晶呈非中心对称正交结构，在7.3 K发生反铁磁转变，b轴磁场下1.3 T出现变磁转变并伴随以本征Karplus-Luttinger机制为主导的异常霍尔效应；静水压下反铁磁序在约10.2 GPa被抑制，出现反铁磁量子临界点特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非中心对称反铁磁体Ce4Ge7的磁性与输运性质，并探索通过静水压调控其反铁磁序、寻找可能的反铁磁量子临界点。

Method: 对C2221空间群（非中心对称正交结构）的Ce4Ge7单晶进行磁性与输运测量，沿b轴施加磁场，并进行静水压实验。

Result: 在TN = 7.3 K发生反铁磁转变；2 K时b轴磁场1.3 T处出现变磁转变，并伴随由本征Karplus-Luttinger机制主导的异常霍尔效应。随静水压增大，TN先略微升高随后逐渐被抑制；高于Pc = 10.2 GPa时磁性有序消失。在Pc附近观察到A系数发散、剩余电阻率极大值以及非费米液体行为。

Conclusion: Ce4Ge7中TN可被静水压连续抑制，Pc附近的反常输运特征表明体系可能存在反铁磁量子临界点。

Abstract: We report the magnetic and transport properties of single-crystalline Ce$_4$Ge$_7$, which crystallizes in a non-centrosymmetric orthorhombic structure (space group C222$_1$). It undergoes an antiferromagnetic transition at $T_N$ = 7.3 K. When a magnetic field is applied along the $b$-axis, a metamagnetic transition occurs at 1.3 T (at 2 K). Correspondingly, this transition gives rise to an anomalous Hall effect, which is dominated by the intrinsic Karplus-Luttinger mechanism. Under hydrostatic pressure, $T_N$ of Ce$_4$Ge$_7$ initially increases slightly and is then gradually suppressed. For pressures above $P_c$ = 10.2 GPa, no magnetic order is observed. The divergence of the $A$ coefficient, the maximum of the residual resistivity, and the non-Fermi liquid behavior around $P_c$ indicate the possible existence of an antiferromagnetic quantum critical point in Ce$_4$Ge$_7$.

</details>


### [235] [Uniform-Charge-Density $d$-Wave Superconductivity in the Pure $t$-$J$ Model at $1/8$ Doping on an Infinite Cylinder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37191)
*Guangyu Yu,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用VUMPS方法在热力学极限下研究正方晶格t-J模型1/8掺杂基态，发现均匀电荷密度的d波超导基态，无条纹或磁序，为解决条纹-超导长期争议提供新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正方晶格t-J模型在1/8空穴掺杂下的基态长期存在争议，数值研究对条纹序与超导性给出相互矛盾的报道，需借助方法上独立、可消除边界效应的手段加以澄清。

Method: 采用变分均匀矩阵乘积态（VUMPS）方法，沿柱轴在热力学极限下直接优化波函数，从而消除有限柱计算中可能出现的边界钉扎效应；在周长L_y=8的无限柱上计算，并辅以扭曲边界条件（twisted-boundary-condition）计算探测超导相位刚度。

Result: 在L_y=8无限柱上得到均匀电荷密度的d波超导基态，具有准长程配对关联；主导配对出现在零质心动量，未探测到明显的有限动量配对成分；扭曲边界条件下显示有限超导相位刚度，序参量相位随外加磁通平滑缠绕；电荷与自旋关联快速衰减，无长程条纹或磁序。作为对照，在L_y=6、1/6掺杂的柱上同一方法能识别出条纹序。

Conclusion: 结果为纯t-J模型中长期的条纹-超导争议提供了方法学上独立的证据，表明在掺杂Mott绝缘体的最小模型中存在均匀d波超导。

Abstract: The ground state of the square-lattice $t$-$J$ model at $1/8$ hole doping has been the subject of a long-standing controversy, with conflicting numerical reports of stripe order versus superconductivity. Here, we address this debate using the variational uniform matrix product state (VUMPS) method, which optimizes the wave function directly in the thermodynamic limit along the cylinder axis and eliminates the boundary pinning effects that can arise in finite-cylinder calculations. On an infinite cylinder of circumference $L_y = 8$, we find a uniform-charge-density $d$-wave superconducting ground state with quasi-long-range pairing correlations. The dominant pairing occurs at zero center-of-mass momentum, while no sizable finite-momentum pairing component is detected. Twisted-boundary-condition calculations reveal a finite superconducting phase stiffness, with the order-parameter phase winding smoothly to follow the applied flux, providing an independent probe of the robustness of the superconductivity. Moreover, both charge and spin correlations decay rapidly, with no signatures of long-range stripe or magnetic order, in contrast to an $L_y=6$ cylinder at $1/6$ doping where the same method readily identifies stripe order. Our results provide methodologically distinct evidence in the long-standing stripe--superconductivity debate in the pure $t$-$J$ model and reveal uniform $d$-wave superconductivity in a minimal model of doped Mott insulators.

</details>


### [236] [Correlation-assisted topological and metamagnetic transitions in Rashba-coupled superconductors: $t$-$J$-$U$ model study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37812)
*Tushar Dey,Maciej Fidrysiak,Józef Spałek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在含Rashba自旋轨道耦合的方格子t-J-U模型中，用SGA方法建立相图，发现相关效应可驱动单纯BdG方案无法给出的C=±2拓扑超导态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物中存在与Rashba型自旋轨道耦合相符的自旋-动量锁定，提示需研究电子关联与RSOC对配对及超导相图的联合效应，而不仅是弱关联BdG图像。

Method: 采用统计一致变分近似（SGA），构建含RSOC的方格子t-J-U模型相图；引入Zeeman场破坏时间反演对称性；与弱耦合BdG方案对比，用Chern数表征拓扑超导态。

Result: 靠近半填充的宽泛在位库仑排斥区间出现C=±4的TSC，该态不受关联主导，弱耦合BdG亦可给出；识别出由电子关联驱动、经无体能隙闭合的拓扑转变产生的C=±2 TSC，并伴随不连续的变磁转变和Lifshitz转变；金属-绝缘体转变之上，混合宇称SC序参量的d波与p波分量随掺杂呈现定性不同的演化。

Conclusion: 关联与RSOC的联合效应超出BdG方案，对RSOC耦合超导体的相图具有重要影响，解释拓扑超导态需纳入关联效应。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity commonly emerges in systems characterized by strong electronic correlations, with layered copper-oxides serving as a canonical example. Observation of spin-momentum locking within Bi-family of the cuprates, compatible with the presence of non-negligible Rashba-type spin-orbit coupling (RSOC), calls for an investigation of the joint effects of electronic correlations and RSOC on pairing in copper-oxide and related superconductors. Employing statistically-consistent variational approximation (SGA), we carry out such an analysis by constructing the phase diagram for the case of square-lattice \textit{t-J-U} model incorporating RSOC. We also investigate the effects of time-reversal-symmetry breaking by Zeeman field, as well as characterize emergent topological superconducting (TSC) states. Chern number $C = \pm 4$ TSC is found in a broad regime of on-site Coulomb repulsion close to half-filling. The latter is not governed by correlations and emerges also within the weak-coupling Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) scheme. Yet, we identify a distinct $C = \pm 2$ TSC state that is driven specifically by electronic correlations via a topological transition occurring with no bulk quasiparticle gap closure, and is accompanied by discontinuous metamagnetic and Lifshitz transitions. Moreover, a qualitatively distinct doping evolution of the $d$- and $p$-wave components of the underlying mixed-parity SC order parameter above the metal-to-insulator transition is demonstrated. Our work points toward the relevance of joint correlation and RSOC effects beyond BdG scheme to phase diagrams of RSOC-coupled superconductors.

</details>


### [237] [Strong-to-Weak Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking of Dephased Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37965)
*Abhijat Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究无限去相位下具有U(1)对称性的费米子混合态的强弱自发对称性破缺（SWSSB），通过将Renyi关联函数映射为紧致XY模型并发展图展开与复制场论，确定了金属与绝缘体母态在不同维度下的SWSSB行为及其转变普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合态中的强弱自发对称性破缺（SWSSB）是一种与全局电荷信息丢失相关的新型相变，但其在费米子系统、尤其是无限密度去相位条件下的具体性质尚不清楚，需要建立可计算的映射与判据来刻画金属、绝缘体等母态的SWSSB行为。

Method: 在无限密度去相位下，将每个Renyi关联函数精确映射为紧致XY模型的spin-spin关联函数，其Boltzmann权重为母态所构造经典系综的全计数统计生成函数的平方；对Renyi-1关联函数发展了适用于任意对角混合态的新型图展开以计算XY模型耦合；并构建偶数Renyi指数的复制场论以处理有限去相位情形。

Result: 完全去相位的金属在d≥2呈现长程SWSSB（d=2经数值与实验证实），在d=1为准长程SWSSB。绝缘母态在无限去相位下可能处于SWSSB或平庸相，取决于UV细节。d≥3长程SWSSB与d=2准长程SWSSB在非普适、依赖Renyi指数的阈值以上存活，当母态深入绝缘相时消失，但量子霍尔绝缘体例外，通常具有准长程SWSSB。对偶数Renyi指数还确定了SWSSB相偏离无限去相位时的稳定性。

Conclusion: 无限去相位为费米子混合态的SWSSB提供了精确的XY模型映射框架，金属普遍支持长程或准长程SWSSB，绝缘体则依UV细节而定，量子霍尔绝缘体例外；对偶数Renyi指数给出了SWSSB相在有限去相位下的稳定性及其转变普适类。

Abstract: Strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) is a novel phase transition in mixed states associated with the loss of global charge information. In this work we study fermions with $U(1)$ symmetry under infinite density dephasing and derive the SWSSB properties of the resulting mixed states. Our central observation is that at infinite dephasing every Renyi correlator is exactly the spin-spin correlator of a compact XY model, whose Boltzmann weight is the squared full-counting-statistics generating function of a classical ensemble built from the parent state. For the Renyi-2 correlator it is the full counting statistics of the parent state itself. We complement the infinite dephasing mapping with a novel diagrammatic expansion for the Renyi-1 correlator, applicable to any diagonal mixed state, which computes the couplings of the Renyi-1 XY model. We also study a replica field theory for even Renyi index quantities, applicable at finite dephasing. Our analysis indicates that fully dephased metals display long-range SWSSB in $d\geq 2$, as confirmed numerically and experimentally in $d=2$, and quasi-long-range SWSSB in $d=1$. For insulating parent states both an SWSSB and a trivial phase are possible at infinite dephasing depending on UV details. Long-range SWSSB in $d\geq 3$, or quasi-long-range SWSSB in $d=2$, survives above a nonuniversal, Renyi-index-dependent threshold and is lost when the parent state is deep in the insulating phase, with the exception of quantum Hall insulators which generically have quasi-long-range SWSSB. For even Renyi indices we also determine the stability of the SWSSB phase away from infinite dephasing and derive the universality class of the SWSSB transition.

</details>


### [238] [Variational Monte Carlo for the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38015)
*Luca F. Tocchio*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本讲义系统介绍了用于Hubbard模型的变分蒙特卡洛方法，涵盖模型引入、变分波函数构造、蒙特卡洛计算、随机重构优化及相关关联函数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hubbard模型是研究强关联电子系统的核心模型，其超导与磁性等基态性质难以通过解析方法精确求解，需要发展高效的数值方法来进行研究。

Method: 首先简要介绍单轨道Hubbard模型及其多轨道推广（仅关注电子模型）；随后阐述变分蒙特卡洛的一般框架；给出能正确描述单轨道与多轨道Hubbard模型中超导态和磁态的变分波函数；详述针对给定波函数的蒙特卡洛计算流程；并说明如何用随机重构（stochastic reconfiguration）方法优化变分波函数。

Result: 展示了若干可在变分蒙特卡洛中计算的关联函数结果，用于判定电荷序、自旋序以及超导性的存在。

Conclusion: 本讲义完整呈现了将变分蒙特卡洛方法应用于Hubbard模型的理论框架与实施步骤，为研究强关联电子系统中的超导与磁有序等物相提供了实用工具。

Abstract: In these lecture notes, we introduce the variational Monte Carlo method for the Hubbard model. First, we briefly introduce the single-orbital Hubbard model and its multi-orbital extension, focusing only on electronic models. Then, we introduce the general scheme under which variational Monte Carlo works and we present the variational wave functions that can properly describe superconductive and magnetic states in single-orbital and multi-orbital Hubbard models. We also detail how to perform Monte Carlo calculations for the given wave functions. We also describe how to optimize a variational wave function with the stochastic reconfiguration method. Finally, we present a few results for correlation functions that can be computed in variational Monte Carlo, to determine, for instance, the presence of charge or spin order and superconductivity.

</details>


### [239] [Boundary Criticality in (2+1)-dimensional U(1) Dirac Quantum Spin Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38088)
*Huan Jiang,Zhiming Pan,Xue-Jia Yu,Shao-Kai Jian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究 (2+1) 维 U(1) Dirac 自旋液体中涌现规范场对边界临界行为的调控，发现 Neumann 与 Dirichlet 边界条件导致不同的边界普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涌现规范场可以在不改变体态量子自旋液体的前提下控制边界临界行为，但其边界临界性机制与分类尚不清楚。

Method: 在 (2+1) 维 U(1) Dirac 自旋液体中，采用半空间微扰重整化群分析，对比涌现规范场的 Neumann 与 Dirichlet 边界条件，计算基本场与规范不变算符的边界标度维度，并提出可调边界相互作用的费米子-规范格点模型。

Result: Neumann 与 Dirichlet 边界条件对应不同的边界普适类，并确定了可观测的基本场与规范不变算符的边界标度维度。

Conclusion: 涌现规范场的边界条件可分类边界普适性，所提出的格点模型为微观实现与探测这些边界普适类提供了可行方案。

Abstract: An emergent gauge field can control boundary critical behavior without changing the bulk quantum spin liquid. We investigate the boundary criticality in $(2+1)$-dimensional $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquid, whose low-energy physics is described by massless quantum electrodynamics. Using a perturbative renormalization-group analysis in a half-space, we show that Neumann and Dirichlet boundary conditions for the emergent gauge field lead to distinct boundary universality classes. We determine the boundary scaling dimensions of the fundamental fields and gauge-invariant operators that provide observable signatures. We further propose a fermion-gauge lattice model with tunable boundary interactions as a microscopic setting for realizing and probing these boundary universality classes.

</details>


### [240] [Neural-quantum-state based downfolding of the three-band Emery model for cuprates and nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38150)
*Hannah Lange,Julius F. A. Tirpitz,Annabelle Bohrdt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将神经量子态（HFDS）用于铜氧化物和镍氧化物层的三带Emery模型，成功模拟了最大10×10（300格点）的全二维系统，并揭示其有效单带描述显著偏离传统Fermi-Hubbard模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温超导（铜氧化物及无限层镍氧化物）的微观物理机制仍是凝聚态物理的核心难题，需要可扩展的变分方法来处理三带Emery模型这类强关联多带系统，并厘清其多带起源对有效单带描述的影响。

Method: 采用神经量子态（NQS），具体为隐藏费米子行列式态（HFDS），作为三带Emery模型的可扩展变分方法；先在宽度为2的几何上与矩阵乘积态（MPS）做基准对比，再研究最大10×10（300格点）的二维基态；通过构造相互作用Wannier函数将三带模型下折叠为有效单带描述，并系统扫描从铜氧化物电荷转移区到镍氧化物Hubbard-Mott区的参数及电荷转移能隙。

Result: 发现掺杂的动量空间分布呈现与铜氧化物实验类似的显著电子-空穴二象性；有效单带模型在所有区域都显著偏离常规Fermi-Hubbard模型：典型U/t被增强，部分参数出现明显掺杂依赖，且存在超出常规Hubbard模型的显著项，尤以密度辅助跳跃t_n最为突出；t_n对粒子-空穴不对称性的贡献大于次近邻跳跃；有效参数敏感依赖于电荷转移能、掺杂和相互作用比。

Conclusion: HFDS被确立为研究二维Emery模型的高效工具；单带描述可能需要包含由底层多带模型产生的新相互作用项，这对理解高温超导的有效低能物理具有重要意义。

Abstract: Understanding the physics underlying high-temperature superconductivity in cuprates and, more recently, infinite-layer nickelates has remained a central challenge in condensed-matter physics. We establish neural quantum states (NQS), specifically Hidden Fermion Determinant States (HFDS), as a scalable variational approach to the three-band Emery model of the copper- and nickel-oxide layers in these materials. After benchmarking HFDS against matrix product states on width-two geometries, we study ground states of fully two-dimensional (2D) systems of up to $10\times10$ unit cells ($300$ sites). We characterize the momentum-space distribution of dopants and find a pronounced electron-hole dichotomy similar to cuprate experiments. We further downfold the three-band model to effective single-band descriptions by constructing interacting Wannier functions. We consider a wide range of parameters -- from the charge-transfer regime relevant to cuprates to the Hubbard-Mott regime of nickelates, as well as systematic scans of the charge-transfer gap that has been demonstrated to impact the critical superconducting temperatures. Across all regimes, the effective model significantly deviates from the usual Fermi-Hubbard model: The typical ratio $U/t$ is enhanced, some parameters experience a significant doping dependence, and sizable terms beyond the conventional Hubbard model are present, most notably a density-assisted hopping $t_n$. Notably, in all effective models, $t_n$ has the largest contribution to particle-hole asymmetry, rather than next-nearest-neighbor hopping contributions. The effective parameters sensitively depend on the charge-transfer energy, doping, and interaction ratios. Our results establish HFDS as an efficient tool for studying the 2D Emery model and demonstrate that single-band descriptions can require interaction terms generated by the underlying multi-band models.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Counterexamples and gap phenomena for derived delooping levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35849)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文完整回答了 Guo–Igusa 关于导出（sub-derived）delooping level 的若干问题：证明 Artin 代数上 ddell ≤ sub-ddell；给出八维代数使 k-sub-ddell=0<1=k-ddell (k≥2)；对 Barrios–Lanzilotta–Mata 单项式族算出全部 k-阶 level，其满足 k-ddell = Findim(op)，故非反例；并构造十一维单项式代数 Λ 使 Findim(Λ^op)=1<2=ddell(Λ)=dell(Λ)，及一族单项式代数 B_r 使 Findim(B_r^op)=1 而 ddell(B_r)=2r+1，从而证明 ddell(A)-Findim(A^op) 无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guo 与 Igusa 提出 derived delooping level 与 sub-derived delooping level 作为 delooping level 的细化，并留下三个问题：两个不变量如何比较；derived delooping level 是否总等于 opposite big finitistic dimension；若不等，差距能有多大。本文旨在系统回答这三个问题，并检验文献中关于反例的猜测。

Method: 采用 Artin 代数与有限维单项式代数的同调/表示论方法：比较 ddell 与 sub-ddell 的阶（k-阶）定义与不等式；对 Barrios--Lanzilotta--Mata 单项式族逐阶计算 k-derived 与 k-sub-derived delooping level；通过构造显式有限维代数（八维代数 C、十一维单项式代数 Λ、十维非单项式变体、族 B_r）来分离 Findim(A^op) 与 ddell(A)、dell(A)。

Result: (1) 对任意 Artin 代数 A，有 ddell(A) ≤ sub-ddell(A)，正面回答 ordinary-degree 比较问题；但存在八维代数 C，使对每个 k≥2 有 k-sub-ddell(C)=0<1=k-ddell(C)。(2) 对 Barrios--Lanzilotta--Mata 单项式族 A_{n,s}，对一切 k≥1 计算了所有 k-derived 与 k-sub-derived delooping level，且满足 k-ddell(A_{n,s})=Findim(A_{n,s}^op)，故该族不构成文献暗示的反例。(3) 构造十一维单项式代数 Λ（任意域上），使 Findim(Λ^op)=1<2=ddell(Λ)=dell(Λ)；并给出十维非单项式变体。(4) 构造有限维单项式代数族 B_r 满足 Findim(B_r^op)=1 而 ddell(B_r)=2r+1。

Conclusion: derived 与 sub-derived delooping level 在普通阶满足 ddell ≤ sub-ddell，但高阶可以严格分离；derived delooping level 不必等于 opposite big finitistic dimension。差距 ddell(A)-Findim(A^op) 即使在 opposite big finitistic dimension 固定的单项式代数中也是无界的。文献中基于 Barrios--Lanzilotta--Mata 族的反例猜测不成立，真正的反例需另行构造。

Abstract: Guo and Igusa introduced the derived and sub-derived delooping levels as refinements of the delooping level and asked how these two invariants compare, whether the derived delooping level always coincides with the opposite big finitistic dimension, and, if not, how large their difference can be. We answer these questions. First, we prove that every Artin algebra $A$ satisfies $\mathrm{ddell}(A)\leq\mathrm{sub\mbox{-}ddell}(A)$. This gives a positive answer to the ordinary-degree comparison problem, whereas a single explicit eight-dimensional algebra $C$ satisfies $k\mbox{-}\mathrm{sub\mbox{-}ddell}(C)=0<1=k\mbox{-}\mathrm{ddell}(C)$ for every $k\geq 2$. We then compute, for every $k\geq 1$, all $k$-derived and $k$-sub-derived delooping levels of the monomial family of Barrios--Lanzilotta--Mata. In particular, that family satisfies $k\mbox{-}\mathrm{ddell}(A_{n,s})=\mathrm{Findim}(A_{n,s}^{\mathrm{op}})$ for every $k\geq 1$, so it does not provide the counterexample suggested in the later literature. By contrast, we construct an eleven-dimensional monomial algebra $Λ$ over an arbitrary field such that $\mathrm{Findim}(Λ^{\mathrm{op}})=1<2=\mathrm{ddell}(Λ)=\mathrm{dell}(Λ)$. A ten-dimensional non-monomial variant is also given. Finally, we construct a family of finite-dimensional monomial algebras $B_r$ satisfying $\mathrm{Findim}(B_r^{\mathrm{op}})=1$ and $\mathrm{ddell}(B_r)=2r+1$. Thus the difference $\mathrm{ddell}(A)-\mathrm{Findim}(A^{\mathrm{op}})$ is unbounded even among monomial algebras with fixed opposite big finitistic dimension.

</details>


### [242] [Relative Suspension and Higher Delooping for Support $τ$-Tilting Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36444)
*Mingfei Xu,Xiaojin Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文构建了由支撑τ-tilting模生成的精确范畴上的相对悬垂（relative suspension），用伴随的单位刻画高阶delooping，推广了Gélinas (Adv. Math. 2022)的结果，并给出在循环Nakayama代数上的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gélinas (Adv. Math. 2022) 已建立delooping理论与相对同调的相关结果，但尚缺乏在由支撑τ-tilting模生成的精确范畴框架下的相对悬垂构造及其与普通高阶delooping的精确比较，作者希望填补这一空白并将其应用于具体代数类。

Method: 在由支撑τ-tilting模生成的精确范畴上构造相对悬垂，并用所得伴随的单位来刻画高阶delooping；建立相对构造与普通高阶delooping之间的比较（需指标平移一）；证明张量induction保持相对构造与诱导模上的高阶delooping level；最后应用于带任意局部系数与自同构twist的循环Nakayama代数。

Result: 与普通高阶delooping的比较需要指标平移一：对任意k,d≥1，存在模其普通k-delooping level为零而相对level为d；张量induction保持相对构造及诱导模上的高阶delooping level；循环Nakayama代数（任意局部系数与自同构twist）的左、右little与big finitistic dimension以及普通与derived delooping level均等于底层Nakayama代数的finitistic dimension。

Conclusion: 该工作将相对悬垂与delooping理论推广到支撑τ-tilting模生成的精确范畴，明确了相对与普通高阶delooping之间相差一个指标平移，并给出循环Nakayama代数各类同调维数的一致刻画。

Abstract: We construct relative suspension for the exact category generated by a support $τ$-tilting module and characterize higher delooping by the units of the resulting adjunction, which are the generalizations of Gélinas's results in Adv. Math.(2022). We prove that comparison with ordinary higher delooping requires a shift of one in the index. More precisely, for every $k,d\ge1$, there is a module whose ordinary $k$-delooping level is zero and whose relative level is $d$. We also prove that tensor induction preserves the relative constructions and higher delooping levels on induced modules. As an application, we study cyclic Nakayama algebras with arbitrary local coefficients and automorphism twists. Their left and right little and big finitistic dimensions and ordinary and derived delooping levels all equal the finitistic dimension of the underlying Nakayama algebra.

</details>


### [243] [Complexes of modules over hierarchical groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36823)
*Ioannis Emmanouil,Olympia Talelli*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究由Kropholler算子LH与Talelli算子Φ迭代生成的群类$\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$，刻画其群代数上投射模无环复形的余核结构，并给出投射/平坦/内射复形的可缩性（或纯无环性）结论及若干应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画比有限群更广的群类上群代数的同调性质，推广有限群及已知广义群类的无环复形可缩性结果，并统一处理Gorenstein同调维数与普通同调维数的关系等问题。

Method: 以有限群类$\mathfrak{F}$为起点，反复施加Kropholler算子LH与Talelli算子Φ得到群类$\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$；对系数取自交换环的$\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-群群代数，分析投射模无环复形的余核结构，并对其投射、平坦与内射模复形分别讨论可缩性、纯无环性，辅以完全分解、周期上同调等工具。

Result: 若G为$\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-群，则任意投射（或平坦）$\mathbb{Q}G$-模无环复形分别可缩（或纯无环）；若G无挠，则对$\mathbb{Z}G$上的投射或平坦模无环复形结论同样成立；内射模无环复形亦有类似结果。

Conclusion: $\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-群在有理系数及无挠整数系数情形下具有良好的同调刚性；相关结论可应用于具完全分解的模、若干步后周期上同调的群，以及群代数上Gorenstein维数与普通同调维数之间关系的刻画。

Abstract: Let $\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$ be the group class obtained from the class $\mathfrak{F}$ of finite groups by iterated applications of Kropholler's operation ${\scriptstyle{\bf LH}}$ and Talelli's operation $Φ$. We describe the structure of the cokenels of acyclic complexes of projective modules over the group algebra of $\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-groups with coefficients in a commutative ring. If $G$ is an $\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-group, we show that any acyclic complex of projective (respectively, flat) $\mathbb{Q}G$-modules is contractible (respectively, pure acyclic). If $G$ is torsion-free, the same conclusions hold for acyclic complexes of projective or flat $\mathbb{Z}G$-modules. Analogous results are valid for acyclic complexes of injective modules. We present some applications regarding modules that admit complete resolutions, groups with periodic cohomology after some steps and the relation between Gorenstein and ordinary homological dimensions for modules over group algebras.

</details>


### [244] [Dirac operators for Cartan motion groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37245)
*Spyridon Afentoulidis-Almpanis,Eyal Subag*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究实约化李群对应 Cartan motion group 的代数 Dirac 算子，建立其基本性质（含涉及自然 Casimir 型元素的平方公式），定义 Dirac 上同调，并证明强形式的 Vogan 猜想：Dirac series 中的模由其 Dirac 上同调完全决定；同时显式确定 Cartan motion group 的 Dirac series，并说明其 Dirac 理论可作为相应实约化群代数形变族的 Dirac 理论的零纤维得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Vogan 猜想及 Dirac 上同调理论从实约化李群推广到其对应的 Cartan motion group，并揭示二者通过代数形变族的零纤维联系。

Method: 构造 Cartan motion group 的代数 Dirac 算子；建立平方公式（涉及自然 Casimir 型元素）；定义 Dirac 上同调；利用实约化群代数形变族 Dirac 理论的零纤维进行刻画。

Result: 证明了强形式 Vogan 猜想：Dirac series 中的模由 Dirac 上同调完全决定；显式确定了 Cartan motion group 的 Dirac series；将 Cartan motion group 的 Dirac 理论实现为相应实约化群代数形变族 Dirac 理论的零纤维。

Conclusion: Cartan motion group 上存在与实约化群平行的 Dirac 理论，且可通过形变族的零纤维获得，强化了 Vogan 猜想并给出 Dirac series 的显式描述。

Abstract: We introduce and study the algebraic Dirac operator for the Cartan motion group associated with a real reductive Lie group. We establish its fundamental properties, including a square formula involving a natural Casimir-type element, define the corresponding notion of Dirac cohomology, and prove a strong form of Vogan's conjecture: a module in the Dirac series is completely determined by its Dirac cohomology. We explicitly determine the Dirac series of a Cartan motion group.
  We explain how the Dirac theory of a Cartan motion group can be obtained as the zero fiber of the recently introduced Dirac theory for the algebraic deformation family of the corresponding real reductive group.

</details>


### [245] [Absolutely irreducible modules via James triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37430)
*Nora Krauß*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将James关于对称群绝对不可约模的构造推广到一般有限群，通过识别适当子群数据构造模，使其关联的商模绝对不可约或为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造域$K$上有限群$G$的绝对不可约$KG$-模是表示论的基本问题。Gordon James利用$\\mu$-tableau $t$（$\\mu\\vdash n$）关联的子群数据，为对称群$\\operatorname{S}_n$给出了显式构造。本文旨在探究James方法能否推广到对称群之外的一般有限群。

Method: 从有限群中识别合适的子群数据，仿照James对对称群的处理方式，利用该子群数据构造一个$KG$-模，并考察其关联商模的性质。

Result: 证明了所构造模的关联商模是绝对不可约的，或者为零，从而给出James方法在一般有限群情形下的一种推广框架。

Conclusion: James关于对称群绝对不可约模的构造可部分推广至一般有限群，为更广泛群类上绝对不可约模的显式构造提供了途径。

Abstract: The construction of absolutely irreducible $KG$-modules for a field $K$ and a finite group $G$ is a basic problem in representation theory. For the symmetric group $\operatorname{S}_n$, Gordon James gave an explicit solution using subgroup data associated to a $μ$-tableau $t$ for partitions $μ\vdash n$. Here, we want to investigate in which way James's method can be extended beyond symmetric groups. We identify suitable subgroup data from a finite group and use it to construct a module such that an associated factor module is absolutely irreducible or zero.

</details>


### [246] [A Galois-equivariant Dade-Glauberman-Nagao correspondence with central defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37558)
*Shi Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为具有中心亏损的块构造了广义Dade-Glauberman-Nagao对应之上高度为零的常不可约特征之间的对应，并给出其等变性、块关系及射影表示相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模表示论中，推广Dade-Glauberman-Nagao对应并刻画高度为零特征之间的联系是重要课题，尤其需要处理包含非平凡中心特征的中心亏损块情形。

Method: 利用中心亏损子群群代数上的重数代数、剩余半线性实现的规范化整提升，以及分次角同构来构造该对应。

Result: 所构造的对应在群自同构与Galois自同构下等变，满足H-三元组之间的块关系；对每对对应特征，相关射影表示实现匹配的因子集、相等的中心标量、同步混合比较函数及每个中间群的相容块对应。

Conclusion: 该构造统一处理所有中心特征纤维（含非平凡中心特征情形），并得到适用于Alperin-McKay-Navarro约化中正规p-截断的形式。

Abstract: We construct a correspondence between ordinary irreducible characters of height zero above the generalized Dade-Glauberman-Nagao correspondence for blocks with central defect. The correspondence is equivariant under group and Galois automorphisms and satisfies a block relation between $\mathcal{H}$-triples. For each pair of corresponding characters, one pair of associated projective representations realizes matching factor sets, equal central scalars, synchronized mixed comparison functions and compatible block correspondences for every intermediate group. The construction uses the multiplicity algebra over the group algebra of the central defect subgroup, a normalized integral lift of a residual semilinear realization, and a graded corner isomorphism. It treats all central-character fibers, including those with nontrivial central character. We also obtain a form suited to normal $p$-sections in an Alperin-McKay-Navarro reduction.

</details>


### [247] [Asymptotics for number of indecomposable components of tensor powers of the natural $\mathrm{SL}_2$-module in odd characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37706)
*Aranya Lahiri,Nai-Heng Sheu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究了 SL₂(K) 自然表示张量幂的不可分解因子计数 b_k 的渐近行为，证明了 b_k ~ ω(k) k^{-δ_p} 2^k，并给出 ω 的解析来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定 SL₂(K)（奇特征代数闭域）自然表示张量幂分解为不可分解模的因子数目随 k 增长的渐近规律，并揭示其解析结构。

Method: 对 V^{⊗k} 的 G-不可分解因子（计重数）计数 b_k 进行渐近分析，引入乘性 p²-周期函数 ω(t) 与指数 δ_p=1-log_{p²}((p+1)/2)；并借助 Dirichlet 热核在 (0,p) 上的边界热流所对应的权 3/2 theta 函数的重整化卷积极限密度刻画 t^{-δ}ω(t)。

Result: 存在光滑、严格正、乘性 p²-周期的 ω(t)，使 b_k ~ ω(k) k^{-δ_p} 2^k；且 t^{-δ}ω(t) 是重整化缩放 theta 函数卷积的极限密度。

Conclusion: SL₂(K) 张量幂的不可分解因子数具有精确的渐近展开，其周期涨落函数可由热核/theta 函数的极限密度实现，联系了表示论与解析数论/热核方法。

Abstract: Let $K$ be an algebraically closed field of odd characteristic $p$, let $G= {\rm SL}_2(K)$, and let $V$ be the natural representation of $G$. Let $b_k$ denote the number of $G$-indecomposable factors of $V^{\otimes k}$, counted with multiplicity, and let $δ_p=1-\log_{p^2}\!\bigl(\tfrac{p+1}{2}\bigr)$. Then there exists a smooth, strictly positive, multiplicatively $p^2$-periodic function $ω(t)$ such that $b_k$ is asymptotic to $ω(k)k^{-δ_p}2^k$. We also show that $t^{-δ}ω(t)$ arises as the limiting density of renormalized convolutions of rescaled copies of a positive weight $3/2$ theta function, obtained from the boundary heat flux of the Dirichlet heat kernel on $(0,p)$.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Failure of the fittest: limits on asexual adaptation to changing environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35933)
*Rebekah Hall,Raphael Forien,Matthew M Osmond,Vincent Calvez*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究指出，以往关于无性种群在渐变环境中适应的理论预测假设种群无限大，因而过于乐观；通过推导有限无性种群的平衡表型分布与祖先谱系轨迹，作者发现有限种群因"最适个体难以被找到"而产生额外适应度负荷，显著削弱其在快速环境变化下的续存能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生殖方式（有性/无性）影响种群在变化环境中的续存与灭绝，经典理论仅用遗传方差解释这一差异。近期理论进展虽揭示了无性适应机制，但均假设种群无限大，可能高估了无性种群在快速环境变化下的续存前景。作者旨在刻画有限种群规模对无性适应的影响。

Method: 作者推导了有限无性种群的平衡表型分布，假设不存在超出某个未知性状界限的个体；并进一步推导了祖先谱系的典型轨迹，用以解释所出现的额外适应度负荷。

Result: 当表型分布仍包含最适性状时，种群命运与既有理论预测一致；而当最适性状丢失时，种群会承受额外的适应度负荷。祖先谱系分析表明，所有个体均源自携带最适（但可能已非最优）性状的共同祖先，因为无限无性种群中每个个体都源自一个曾经最优的祖先，而这一个体在种群中被找到的概率随种群规模增大而迅速下降。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种无性适应特有的有限种群效应，它区别于遗传漂变，显著阻碍小型无性种群在变化环境中的续存能力。该效应对无性种群适应预测具有重要修正意义。

Abstract: The dynamics of adaptation in a population are informed by its mode of reproduction. Whether individuals reproduce sexually or asexually, then, can have large effects on population persistence and extinction in changing environments. Classical theory explains this dependence via genetic variance without further accounting for a fundamentally different mode of inheritance. Recent theoretical advances capture these mechanisms, highlighting how they dramatically change the nature of adaptation in gradually changing environments. Here we show that these recent predictions, which assume infinite-population sizes, are overly optimistic about the fate of finite asexual populations when environmental change is rapid. This is because every individual in an infinite asexual population descends from a once optimal ancestor and the likelihood of finding this optimal individual in the population declines rapidly with population size. To predict the fate of finite asexual populations we derive the equilibrium phenotypic distribution assuming there exist no individuals beyond some unknown trait. As expected, we find that when the phenotypic distribution contains the optimal trait, the population's fate is consistent with previous predictions. When the optimal trait is lost, however, an additional fitness load is incurred. We further derive a prediction for the typical trajectory of an ancestral lineage, which explains the additional load: all individuals descend from a common ancestor carrying the fittest trait, which may be suboptimal. Our work uncovers a finite-population effect that is specific to asexual adaptation and distinct from genetic drift, which significantly hinders the ability of small asexual populations to persist in changing environments.

</details>


### [249] [Stochastic gradient descent on the epigenetic landscape: a unified framework for cellular plasticity, tumor heterogeneity, and the asymptotic irrelevance of fitness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37703)
*Artur C. Fassoni*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个统一框架，将分室ODE模型、表型结构PDE与单细胞随机方程联系起来，揭示表型可塑性的长期分布仅由转换率决定，并将其诠释为表型景观上的随机梯度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表型可塑性在发育、分化和治疗耐药中至关重要，现有模型涵盖分室ODE、表型结构PDE和单细胞随机方程等多个尺度，但缺乏连接这些描述的统一框架。

Method: 从含非线性增长和表型间线性转换的一般n分室ODE模型出发，给出近期定理的新初等且可推广证明；将转换限制为近邻态得到表型结构反应-扩散-平流PDE的离散化，从转换率识别扩散与平流；将平流速度解释为有效表观遗传势的负梯度，转化为Fokker-Planck方程，并把单细胞轨迹转为表型景观上的Langevin意义SGD；通过积分-微分项纳入非局部转换（如突变）。

Result: 在均匀竞争下，长期种群分布仅由转换率决定；饱和时所有表型选择性中性，生长阶段适应度差异的印记以显式速率消退；连续模型中适应度渐近无关；状态依赖噪声重塑有效景观而不改变潜在势；癌症可通过提高可塑性获得与改变景观无法区分的效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为Waddington景观提供了物理解释，即细胞执行SGD，癌症对应于被破坏的景观，并统一了反应、梯度流、扩散与跳跃过程。

Abstract: Phenotypic plasticity, the ability of cells to switch between states, is central to development, differentiation, and therapy resistance. Although it is modeled at several scales, from compartmental ODEs to phenotype-structured PDEs and single-cell stochastic equations, a framework connecting these descriptions is missing. We present such a framework. Starting from a general $n$-compartment ODE model encompassing nonlinear growth and linear transitions between phenotypes, we show, with a new, elementary and generalizable proof of a recent theorem, that under uniform competition, the long-term population distribution is solely governed by transition rates. All phenotypes become selectively neutral at saturation, and the imprint of fitness differences during growth fades at an explicit rate. Restricting transitions to neighboring states transforms the model into a discretization of a phenotype-structured reaction-diffusion-advection PDE. In this continuum model, diffusion and advection are identified from switching rates, and fitness remains asymptotically irrelevant. Interpreting the advection velocity as the negative gradient of an effective epigenetic potential transforms the PDE into a Fokker--Planck equation and converts single-cell trajectories into stochastic gradient descent (SGD) in the Langevin sense on the phenotypic landscape. Non-local transitions, such as mutations, are incorporated via an integro-differential term, yielding a unified model with reaction, gradient flow, diffusion, and jumps. State-dependent noise reshapes the effective landscape without altering the underlying potential. This gives cancer a route to elevated plasticity that static, single-cell snapshots cannot distinguish from a changed landscape. This framework provides a physical interpretation of Waddington's landscape, where cells perform SGD, and cancer corresponds to a corrupted landscape.

</details>


### [250] [Nutrient Competition as a General Mechanism of Regulation in Photosymbiosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37826)
*Jordan A. Gault,Nils Rädecker,Iliana B. Baums,Thilo Gross*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究用广义建模方法证明，氮限制足以通过种内与种间竞争的相互作用稳定刺胞动物-藻类共生，其稳定机制随宿主无机氮同化增强而发生转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氮限制被认为是调控光合共生刺胞动物与其藻类共生体互作的关键机制：藻类光合产物促进宿主同化无机氮（尤其是铵），由此引发的双方对无机氮的竞争形成负反馈环路，通过"扶助竞争者"来控制共生体密度。但尚不清楚在缺乏其他调控形式时，该反馈环路是否足以稳定这一互作。

Method: 采用广义建模方法（generalized modeling approach），构建刺胞动物-藻类对氮竞争的动力学模型，并推导使共生关系实现动态稳定的条件。

Result: 明确了氮限制下共生稳定的条件：氮限制通过种内竞争（共生体-共生体）与种间竞争（宿主-共生体）的相互作用足以稳定该互作。随着宿主对无机氮同化的增强，系统稳定机制由共生体种内竞争主导转向宿主与共生体的种间竞争主导；此时需要共生体密度与宿主氮同化之间通过光合产物转运形成正耦合，才能维持动态稳定。

Conclusion: 氮限制无需其他调控形式即可稳定刺胞动物-藻类共生，其稳定依赖于种内与种间竞争的平衡；稳定机制的转换要求共生体密度与宿主氮同化经光合产物转运正耦合。

Abstract: Nitrogen limitation is a key mechanism regulating the mutualism between photosymbiotic cnidarians and their algal symbionts. Algal-derived photosynthate facilitates host assimilation of inorganic nitrogen, specifically ammonium. The resulting competition between symbiotic partners for inorganic nitrogen leads to a negative feedback loop wherein symbiont density is effectively controlled through facilitation of their competitor. However, it is unclear whether this feedback loop is sufficient to stabilize the interaction absent other forms of regulation. Using a generalized modeling approach, we identify the conditions under which cnidarian-algal competition for nitrogen yields dynamically stable symbioses. We show that nitrogen limitation is sufficient to stabilize the interaction through the interplay of intraspecific (symbiont-symbiont) and interspecific (host-symbiont) competition. As host assimilation of inorganic nitrogen increases, the system shifts from being stabilized by intraspecific competition between symbionts to interspecific competition between host and symbionts, necessitating a positive coupling of symbiont density to host nitrogen assimilation via photosynthate translocation for dynamic stability.

</details>


### [251] [Boids of a Feather Flock Together - Evolving Prey Behaviours Under Different Predator Attack Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37885)
*Augusta van Haren,Hanna Hoogen,Luca Pattavina*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究扩展了Ojo等(2023)的捕食者-猎物boids模型，将六种猎物运动倾向整合为单一加权加速度更新，并用进化策略优化行为系数，以考察不同捕食策略下集群猎物的最优反捕食行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集群与群游行为部分被认为是抵御捕食的进化防御，但猎物如何在"社交倾向"与"逃逸倾向"之间取得平衡，可能取决于捕食者的捕猎策略；作者希望量化这种依赖关系。

Method: 在Reynolds boids基础上扩展Ojo等(2023)的捕食者-猎物模型：将六种猎物运动倾向（对齐alignment、凝聚cohesion、分离separation、闪避dodge、排斥repel、摆动wiggle）合并为单一加权加速度更新，并将wiggle重构为正弦式机动；随后用进化策略针对四种捕食者策略（攻击质心attack-centroid、攻击最近attack-nearest、攻击随机attack-random、攻击外围attack-peripheral）优化六个行为系数，每种策略进行五次独立试验。

Result: 各试验内系数均收敛，平均适应度保持稳定或上升，但不同试验常收敛到不同局部最优。猎物存活率在attack-centroid下最高、在attack-nearest下最低，与假设一致。面对attack-centroid时，猎物进化出个体化的捕食者规避行为（高逃逸系数）；面对其余三种策略时则大体维持集群队形。所有策略下，进化都倾向于低repel系数以及相对较高的dodge和wiggle系数。

Conclusion: 最优反捕食行为取决于逃逸倾向与捕食者捕猎策略之间的相互作用；据此，集群/群游的适应性意义需结合具体捕食情境来理解。

Abstract: Flocking and schooling are thought to have evolved partly as defences against predation, but how prey should balance social and escape tendencies may depend on the predator's hunting strategy. We extend the predator-prey boids model of Ojo et al. (2023), itself based on Reynolds' boids, by combining six prey movement tendencies (alignment, cohesion, separation, dodge, repel and wiggle) into a single weighted acceleration update, and by reformulating wiggle as a sinusoidal manoeuvre. We then use an evolutionary strategy to optimise the six behaviour coefficients for collective prey survival against four predator hunting strategies: attack-centroid, attack-nearest, attack-random and attack-peripheral. Across five independent trials per strategy, coefficients converged within trials and mean fitness remained stable or increased, although trials often settled in different local optima. Prey survival was highest under attack-centroid and lowest under attack-nearest, in line with our hypotheses. Against attack-centroid, prey evolved individualistic predator avoidance with high escape coefficients, whereas against the other three strategies they largely kept their flock formation. Across all strategies, evolution favoured a low repel coefficient and relatively high dodge and wiggle coefficients. Our results suggest that optimal anti-predator behaviour depends on the interplay between escape tendencies and the predator's hunting strategy.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Proofs of some conjectures by Bringmann, Han, Heim, and Kane on periodic sign changes in eta-quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35893)
*Jing Jin,Olivia X. M. Yao,Shiwen Zhou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 利用经典theta函数恒等式、剖分与系数正性，证明了Bringmann等人提出的72个弱全纯模形式Fourier系数周期符号模式猜想，相关eta-商周期为2、3、4、5、6、8、12。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bringmann、Han、Heim与Kane近期研究了由eta-商产生的弱全纯模形式的Fourier系数中的周期符号模式，并在其工作附录中提出了大量猜想，这些猜想尚未得到证明。

Method: 采用经典theta函数恒等式、q-级数剖分（dissections）以及逐项系数正性（coefficientwise positivity）等初等q-级数方法。

Result: 证明了72个相关猜想的符号模式；涉及的eta-商周期为2、3、4、5、6、8和12；对每个相关剩余类给出了系数的显式生成函数。

Conclusion: 证明在q-级数层面是初等的，并明确给出了各剩余类系数的生成函数，从而验证了Bringmann等人的大量符号模式猜想。

Abstract: Bringmann, Han, Heim, and Kane recently investigated periodic sign patterns in the Fourier coefficients of weakly holomorphic modular forms arising from eta-quotients and posed numerous conjectures in the appendix to their work. Using classical theta-function identities, dissections, and coefficientwise positivity, we prove 72 of these conjectural sign patterns. The eta-quotients considered here have periods 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, and 12. Our proofs are elementary at the level of $q$-series and yield explicit generating functions for the coefficients in each relevant residue class.

</details>


### [253] [Primes in arithmetic progressions and Siegel zeroes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35950)
*Thomas Wright*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在假设L函数存在Siegel零点的条件下，证明了算术级数中素数分布渐近式在模$q<x^{30/59-\nu}$范围内成立，平均情形下可改进到$q<x^{16/31-\nu}$，优于Friedlander-Iwaniec (2003)的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在改进Friedlander和Iwaniec于2003年关于算术级数中素数计数函数在Siegel零点存在情形下的分布范围结果（原要求$q<x^{233/462}$），并在此基础上推进Sachpazis的近期工作。

Method: 设$\chi$为模$D$的本原Dirichlet特征，$L(s,\chi)$在$s=\beta=1-\frac{1}{\eta\log D}$处有Siegel零点（$\eta>\eta_0(\nu)$足够大）。在此假设下分析Chebyshev素数计数函数$\psi(x,q,a)$的渐近行为，推导对模$q$的分布范围；并对$q$在$q\sim Q$范围内取平均，得到Elliott-Halberstam型结果。

Result: 证明当$q<x^{30/59-\nu}$时，渐近式$\psi(x,q,a)=\frac{\psi(x)}{\varphi(q)}\left[1+O(\varepsilon_{\eta_0})-\chi\left(\frac{aD}{(q,D)}\right)\right]$成立；在对$q$取平均的情形下，得到$Q<x^{16/31-\nu}$范围内的Elliott-Halberstam型结果。

Conclusion: 该结果改进了Friedlander-Iwaniec (2003) 要求的$q<x^{233/462}$，将模$q$的适用范围显著扩大，并在平均情形下进一步优化了界，推进了Sachpazis的相关工作。

Abstract: Let $χ$ be a Dirichlet character mod $D$ with $L(s,χ)$ its associated $L$-function, and let $ψ(x,q,a)$ be, as usual, Chebyshev's prime-counting function for the primes of the arithmetic progression $a$ (mod $q$) with $(a,q)=1$. Let $χ$ be a primitive character modulo $D$, and let $ν>0$ be small. We prove that if $L(s,χ)$ has a Siegel zero at $s=β=1-\frac{1}{η\log D}$ with $η>η_0(ν)$ for some large $η_0(ν)$, there exists a range of $x$ for which the asymptotic $$ψ(x,q,a)=\frac{ψ(x)}{φ(q)}\left[1+O(\varepsilon_{η_0})-χ\left(\frac{aD}{(q,D)}\right)\right]$$ holds for $q<x^{\frac{30}{59}-ν}$. We also show slightly better bounds for $q$ if we take an average over a range of $q$, finding an Elliott-Halberstam-type result for $q\sim Q$ on the range $Q<x^{\frac{16}{31}-ν}$. This improves on a 2003 result of Friedlander and Iwaniec that requires $q<x^{\frac{233}{462}}$ and builds on recent work of Sachpazis.

</details>


### [254] [p-adic Rigidity and Supercongruences at Rank Two Attractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36045)
*Yu Fu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究由秩二吸引子（rank two attractors）暗示的 p 进刚性现象，给出了任意维数下的上同调判据，并将其应用于 Hulek--Verrill A_4 族与镜像四次 K3 铅笔的 CM 纤维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Calabi--Yau 族的普通特殊纤维附近，Beukers--Vlasenko 的 excellent Frobenius 作用在参数圆盘上；Achinger--Zdanowicz 构造了 Frobenius 扭转的典范 W_2-提升，Brantner--Taelman 构造了典范形式提升。作者希望统一这两类提升，并刻画 excellent Frobenius 的不动点与超同余现象。

Method: 提出猜想：Dwork 与 Achinger--Zdanowicz 的一阶障碍映射一致，且 excellent Frobenius 的不动点恰为 Brantner--Taelman 典范形式提升的参数。证明一个任意维数的上同调判据：若 p 进上同调中存在含全纯类的 Frobenius-稳定秩二因子，则该类张成 Cartier-稳定直线，并导出模 p^{2s} 的超同余（对所有 s≥1）。在 Beukers--Vlasenko excellent 提升定理假设下，证明 excellent Frobenius 固定相应参数。应用于 Hulek--Verrill A_4 族在 t_*=-1/7 处（假设 Dummigan 猜想 1.1），以及镜像四次 K3 铅笔的 CM 纤维。另证明分裂形式环面上 q-幂映射在单位剩余圆盘内的唯一周期点是单位截面。

Result: 得到任意维数的上同调判据及其推论：秩二因子迫使全纯类张成 Cartier-稳定直线并产生模 p^{2s} 超同余；在 excellent 提升定理假设下，excellent Frobenius 固定对应参数。对 Hulek--Verrill A_4 族在 t_*=-1/7，假设 Dummigan 猜想 1.1，证明 excellent Frobenius 固定 t_*、超同余成立，且参数 t_* mod p^2 的纤维在所有满足 Dwork 普通性条件的素数处是 Achinger--Zdanowicz 典范提升。镜像四次 K3 铅笔的 CM 纤维给出第二应用。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Dwork 障碍、Achinger--Zdanowicz 提升与 Brantner--Taelman 形式提升统一在一个 p 进刚性框架下，提供了可验证的上同调判据与超同余结论，并在两个具体 Calabi--Yau 族中确认了秩二吸引子参数的刚性行为。

Abstract: We study a $p$-adic rigidity phenomenon suggested by rank two attractors. Near an ordinary special fiber of a Calabi--Yau family, the excellent Frobenius of Beukers and Vlasenko acts on the parameter disk. Achinger--Zdanowicz construct a canonical $W_2$-lift of the Frobenius twist, while Brantner--Taelman construct a canonical formal lift. We conjecture that the Dwork and Achinger--Zdanowicz first-order obstruction maps agree and that the fixed points of the excellent Frobenius are precisely the parameters of the Brantner--Taelman canonical formal lifts.
  Our main theorem gives a cohomological criterion in arbitrary dimension. Under its hypotheses, a Frobenius-stable rank two factor of the $p$-adic cohomology containing the holomorphic class forces that class to span a Cartier-stable line and gives supercongruences modulo $p^{2s}$ for every $s\geq1$. Moreover, if the hypotheses of the Beukers--Vlasenko excellent lift theorem hold, then the excellent Frobenius fixes the corresponding parameter.
  We apply the criterion to the Hulek--Verrill $A_4$ family at $t_*=-1/7$, a parameter predicted to be a rank two attractor. Assuming Dummigan's Conjecture~1.1, we prove that the excellent Frobenius fixes $t_*$, the supercongruences hold, and the fiber with parameter $t_*\bmod p^2$ is the Achinger--Zdanowicz canonical lift at every prime where the required Dwork ordinarity conditions are satisfied. A CM fiber of the mirror quartic K3 pencil gives a second application. We also prove that the $q$-power map on a split formal torus has the unit section as its unique periodic point in the identity residue disk over every finite extension of $W(\mathbb{F}_q)[1/p]$, where $q$ is the cardinality of the residue field.

</details>


### [255] [Strongly Pseudoperfect Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36068)
*Audrey Wang,Joshua Zelinsky*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究强伪完全数（strongly pseudoperfect numbers）：若正整数 n 的正因子子集 S 满足 d∈S 当且仅当 n/d∈S，且 ∑_{d∈S} d = 2n，则称 n 为强伪完全数。论文构造了新的无限族，并给出了 n 成为强伪完全数的新限制条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强伪完全数是完全数与伪完全数概念的变体，其因子子集需满足关于 n/d 的对称性条件。此前此类数的已知例子与结构有限，作者希望扩展对强伪完全数集合的认识，既通过构造新的无限族来丰富例子，又通过证明新的限制条件来刻画其不可能情形或约束其形式。

Method: 基于正整数因子集合的组合与数论分析。定义因子子集 S 满足对称条件（d∈S 当且仅当 n/d∈S）且因子和等于 2n。通过构造满足该对称性和求和条件的因子子集，建立新的无限族；同时通过分析因子结构、因子和约束及对称性，推导 n 为强伪完全数时必须满足的新必要条件或限制。

Result: 构造出新的强伪完全数无限族；并证明了关于正整数 n 何时能成为强伪完全数的新限制条件。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了强伪完全数的例子集合，并加深了对其存在性与结构约束的理解，为后续研究强伪完全数的分类与刻画提供了新的构造工具与限制性结论。

Abstract: A positive integer $n$ is said to be strongly pseudoperfect if there is a subset $S$ of the positive divisors of $n$ such that $d \in S$ if and only if $n/d \in S$, and $\sum_{d \in S} d = 2n$. We construct new infinite families of strongly pseudoperfect numbers, and also prove new restrictions on when a positive integer $n$ can be strongly pseudoperfect.

</details>


### [256] [A $q$-analogue of a binomial supercongruence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36109)
*Oscar Gonzalez*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了 arXiv:2503.20727 中二项式系数同余式的 $q$-模拟，并据此加强和简化了原文主要结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ichino 与 Prasanna 近期证明了一个与 $p$-进环上 $\mathrm{GL}_2$ 表示相关的二项式系数超同余式；本文旨在给出其 $q$-模拟，并由此加强、简化原结果。

Method: 利用 $q$-Lucas 定理证明该超同余式的 $q$-模拟。

Result: 得到原超同余式的 $q$-模拟，并由此直接推出对 arXiv:2503.20727 主要结果的加强；证明也显著简化了原论证。

Conclusion: $q$-Lucas 定理可用于统一处理此类超同余式，既能加强结论又能简化证明。

Abstract: In a recent paper arXiv:2503.20727, Ichino and Prasanna proved a supercongruence for binomial coefficients and related it to representations of $\mathrm{GL}_2$ over $p$-adic rings. We prove a $q$-analog of this supercongruence using the $q$-Lucas theorem, from which a strengthening of the main result of loc. cit. can easily be deduced. Our proof also substantially simplifies the original argument of arXiv:2503.20727.

</details>


### [257] [On closed-form expressions for weighted mean square of the Riemann zeta function and Dirichlet $L-$functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36200)
*Sébastien Darses,Erwan Hillion*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文综述了黎曼zeta函数与Dirichlet L-函数在临界线上的加权均方所导出的闭式恒等式，并证明了模4本原特征情形下Lewis–Zagier公式的指数权重对偶新恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为面向学生、研究者及AI提供关于黎曼zeta函数与Dirichlet L-函数临界线上加权均方闭式恒等式及其相关联系的简明而全面的综述资源。

Method: 以统一、一致的书写方式梳理各类公式（包括互反公式），并在模4本原特征情形下，推导并证明与Lewis–Zagier公式相对应的带指数权重的互反恒等式。

Result: 汇集了黎曼zeta函数与Dirichlet L-函数临界线加权均方的多种闭式恒等式与互反公式；获得并证明了模4本原特征下指数权重形式的互反对偶新恒等式。

Conclusion: 论文为相关主题提供了统一、简洁的参考综述，并通过新互反恒等式扩展了Lewis–Zagier的已有结果。

Abstract: This short note is a review intended to serve as a comprehensive and concise resource for students, researchers, and AIs, on the following topic and its various connections: closed form identities that can be obtained from weighted mean squares of the Riemann zeta function and Dirichlet $L-$functions on the critical line. The various formulas, including reciprocity formulas, are all written in a consistent way.
  In addition, we prove a new reciprocity identity, which is the counterpart with an exponential weight of a formula by Lewis and Zagier in the case of the primitive character of modulus $4$.

</details>


### [258] [On Vojta's harder implication, height inequalities for admissible pairs, points of bounded degree and Deligne-Mumford stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36300)
*Nathan Grieve*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了"容许对"概念,将其与Vojta预测的带判别式项及有界次数点的Diophantine算术不等式联系起来,证明了Vojta abc猜想思路下的"更难蕴含关系",并在特定一般型非奇异Deligne-Mumford栈上推导出有界次数(D_0,S)-整点的Bombieri-Lang猜想形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将Levin提出的"容许对"概念与Vojta预测的Diophantine算术不等式(含判别式项、有界次数点)建立联系,并借助这一视角统一处理Bombieri-Lang猜想相关问题。

Method: 方法上,作者扩展容许对概念,证明其与Vojta型不等式之间的"更难蕴含关系";关键工具包括Abramovich-Várilly-Alvarado针对Deligne-Mumford栈的切片定理(基于Kresch-Vistoli的早期工作),以及Silverman关于射影空间中点判别式由高度控制的不等式。

Result: 主要结果:对具有射影粗模空间的一般型非奇异Deligne-Mumford栈,导出了有界次数(D_0,S)-整点上的Bombieri-Lang猜想形式;同时证明了若干新结果,包括受Vojta abc猜想方法启发的"更难蕴含关系"。

Conclusion: 容许对视角为Vojta不等式与Bombieri-Lang型猜想提供了统一框架,相关结果可在Abramovich-Harris等人工作的背景下加以说明,展示了该方法的适用性。

Abstract: We expand on the concept of \emph{admissible pairs} from \cite{Levin:GCD} and explore its relation with the main Diophantine arithmetic inequalities, with discriminant term and for points of bounded degree, that have been predicted by Vojta \cite{Vojta:1998}. In this context, among other new results, we prove a \emph{harder implication} which is in the spirit of Vojta's approach to the abc Conjecture (from \cite{Vojta:1998}). As our main result, and application of our viewpoint here, we deduce for the case of certain general type nonsingular Deligne-Mumford stacks, with projective course moduli space, a form of the Bombieri-Lang Conjecture for $(D_0,S)$-integral points of bounded degree. A key input for this is a slicing theorem, for Deligne-Mumford stacks, that was obtained by Abramovich and Várilly-Alvarado, \cite{Abramovich:VarillyAlvarado:Pera:2017}, and building on earlier work of Kresch and Vistoli \cite{Kresch:Vistoli:2004}. Another important ingredient is an inequality of Silverman, from \cite{Silverman:1984}, which bounds the discriminant of points in projective space in terms of their heights. As an illustration of our results, we discuss them within the context of the interesting work of Abramovich and Harris \cite{Abramovich:Harris:1991} and others.

</details>


### [259] [Twisted Diophantine Approximation I: Asymptotic Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36411)
*Taehyeong Kim,Vasiliy Neckrasov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对带任意固定实矩阵的扭曲丢番图逼近建立了精确的零一律：其判据为某一 dyadic 级数的收敛或发散，该级数项由 Khintchine-Groshev 块体积除以齐次格点计数构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Kurzweil 与 Fuchs-Kim 判据各自适用于特定情形，缺乏在一般 dyadic 正则逼近函数下统一且精确的刻画，尤其是扭曲（twisted）情形的零一律尚待建立。

Method: 在满足 dyadic 正则性的正非增逼近函数类中，构造以 Khintchine-Groshev 块体积除以齐次格点计数为项的 dyadic 级数；并用对角格轨道 trajectory 的全部 successive minima 给出等价表述，以刻画各个方向上的齐次聚簇。

Result: 得到了精确零一律，并给出非齐次 ψ-Lagrange 常数的几乎必然零-无穷律、分别的 Hausdorff 测度判据及维数结果；在该正则类内可回收 Kurzweil 与 Fuchs-Kim 判据；在临界指数处该级数决定坏逼近移位集是零测还是满 Lebesgue 测度。

Conclusion: 所提出的级数判据为扭曲丢番图逼近提供了统一而精确的收敛-发散刻画，并推广与统一了已有经典结果。

Abstract: We establish an exact zero-one law for twisted Diophantine approximation with an arbitrary fixed real matrix and a positive non-increasing approximation function satisfying dyadic regularity. The criterion is the convergence or divergence of a dyadic series whose summands are Khintchine-Groshev block volumes divided by homogeneous lattice-point counts. An equivalent formulation is using all successive minima of the associated diagonal lattice trajectory, capturing homogeneous clustering in every direction. We also obtain an almost-sure zero-infinity law for inhomogeneous $ψ$-Lagrange constants, separate Hausdorff-measure criteria, and dimension results. Within this regularity class, our theorem recovers the Kurzweil and Fuchs-Kim criteria. At the critical exponent, this series determines whether the set of badly approximable shifts has zero or full Lebesgue measure.

</details>


### [260] [Twisted Diophantine Approximation II: Uniform Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36412)
*Taehyeong Kim,Vasiliy Neckrasov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了带任意固定实矩阵的一致扭曲度量丢番图逼近的一般方法，利用对角格轨迹的全部 successive minima 估计格点计数并处理各方向聚类，从而给出扭曲ψ-Dirichlet向量集的确切Hausdorff维数、端点集维数公式、一致丢番图指数水平集维数，以及ψ-Dirichlet集的零-全律，并应用于恢复与推广若干已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究扭曲度量丢番图逼近中Dirichlet向量集的Hausdorff维数，需要一个能处理任意固定实矩阵、且能精确刻画格点分布聚类的统一框架，以统一和推广已有的一维及高维结果（如Kim-Liao公式、Aggarwal逃逸质量上界、Moshchevitin等的度量准则）。

Method: 发展带任意固定实矩阵的一致扭曲度量丢番图逼近一般方法：使用关联对角格轨迹的全部successive minima来估计格点计数，并在每个方向上计入聚类效应；以这些计数的比值推导Hausdorff维数；对端点集（交与并）及一致丢番图指数水平集分别计算维数；并证明一般Hausdorff测度下ψ-Dirichlet集的零-全律。

Result: 对一大类函数ψ（除有限多个幂函数T^{-τ}外全部包含）得到扭曲ψ-Dirichlet向量集的确切Hausdorff维数；给出对c>0取交与并所得端点集的Hausdorff维数公式，并证明取交不导致维数下降；得到一致丢番图指数水平集的Hausdorff维数；证明ψ-Dirichlet集的一般Hausdorff测度零-全律。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一且精确的扭曲度量丢番图逼近维度理论；应用上恢复了一维Kim-Liao公式，加强了Aggarwal的逃逸质量上界，再现了Moshchevitin与第二作者的度量准则，并给出上述若干结论的推广。

Abstract: We develop a general method for uniform twisted metric Diophantine approximation with an arbitrary fixed real matrix. Using all successive minima of the associated diagonal lattice trajectory, we estimate lattice-point counts, accounting for clustering in every direction. Ratios of these counts give exact Hausdorff dimensions of sets of twisted $ψ$-Dirichlet vectors for a large class of functions $ψ$, including all except finitely many power functions {$T^{-τ}$, $τ>0$}. We obtain Hausdorff dimension formulae for the endpoint sets obtained by taking the intersection and union of the sets of twisted $cψ$-Dirichlet vectors over $c>0$, and prove that taking the intersections causes no dimension drop. Furthermore, we obtain Hausdorff dimensions of level sets of the uniform Diophantine exponent.
  In addition, we prove zero-full laws for general Hausdorff measures of $ψ$-Dirichlet sets. Applications recover the one-dimensional Kim-Liao formulae, strengthen Aggarwal's escape-of-mass upper bound, reproduce the metric criteria of Moshchevitin and the second author and give certain extensions of the above statements.

</details>


### [261] [Reduction of Weil-Deligne Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36467)
*Imin Chen,Deniz Suozer*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了两个2维半单连续Weil群表示在模λ约化后同构的条件，并给出了可分解或不可约的判定描述，最后给出在广义Fermat方程模方法中的应用例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部Langlands对应给出了GL_n(F)的不可约光滑表示与Weil--Deligne表示之间的双射。对于W_F的两个2维半单连续表示，其模λ约化何时同构是一个自然问题，且该问题在广义Fermat方程的模方法中有实际意义。

Method: 利用W_F的连续ℓ进表示与ℓ进Weil--Deligne表示之间的对应关系，先刻画约化表示可分解或不可约的判定条件，进而给出两个约化表示同构的完整刻画。

Result: 给出了两个2维半单连续Weil群表示模λ约化同构的完整刻画，并描述了其约化为可分解或不可约表示的条件。

Conclusion: 论文完整解决了两个2维半单表示约化同构的判定问题，并通过广义Fermat方程模方法中的例子展示了结果的应用。

Abstract: Let $p$ and $\ell$ be distinct odd primes. For a finite extension $F/\mathbb{Q}_p$, the local Langlands correspondence states that there is a canonical bijection between irreducible, smooth representations of $\text{GL}_n(F)$ and $n$-dimensional, $Φ$-semisimple Weil--Deligne representations of the Weil group $W_F$. Given two $2$-dimensional, semisimple, continuous representations $ρ_1, ρ_2$ of $W_F$ with images in $\text{GL}_2(\mathcal{O}_K)$, where $K/\mathbb{Q}_\ell$ is a finite extension with maximal ideal $λ\subset \mathcal{O}_K$, a natural question to ask is when their mod $λ$ reductions $\overlineρ_1$ and $\overlineρ_2$ are isomorphic. In this paper, we give a complete characterization of when the reductions are isomorphic. For this, we first give a description of when the reductions of these representations are decomposable or irreducible, utilizing the correspondence between continuous $\ell$-adic representations of $W_F$ and $\ell$-adic Weil--Deligne representations. We conclude with some examples which arise in the modular method for solving generalized Fermat equations.

</details>


### [262] [Farey Symbols for the Picard Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36629)
*Devendra Tiwari,Helena Verrill*

Main category: math.NT
