TL;DR: 本文引入 Picard Farey 符号，用高斯 Farey 理想八面体剖分编码 \(\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbb{Z}[i])\) 的有限指数子群，并给出八面体计数、侧配对界、Steinberg 展示以及 Bianchi 模符号有限展示的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对 \(\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbb{Z}[i])\)（Picard 群）的有限指数子群，已有 Farey 符号类工具主要用于 \(\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbb{Z})\)，在高斯情形缺乏能够同时记录局部稳定子、面配对与组合结构的有限符号系统；同时希望将子群的几何基本域与 Bianchi 模符号的有限展示直接联系起来。

Method: 使用 \(\mathbb{H}^3\) 的高斯 Farey 理想八面体剖分定义 Picard Farey 符号：记录有限多个高斯有理八面体出现、其局部稳定子以及有序共定向面配对。证明有效标记符号可重构有限 Picard-胞腔作用，从而重构子群。对无挠指数 \(n\) 子群，用完全 Farey 八面体构造基本域，并采用生成树构造控制侧配对变换数量。进一步将高斯八面体边—面复形与 \(\mathbb{Q}(i)\) 的整秩二 Steinberg 展示等同。

Result: 有效标记符号可重构有限 Picard-胞腔作用及对应子群，并给出若干精确例子。无挠指数 \(n\) 子群的任意由完全 Farey 八面体组成的基本域含 \(n/12\) 个八面体；生成树构造给出至多 \(n/4+1\) 个侧配对变换的基本域。高斯八面体边—面复形与 \(\mathbb{Q}(i)\) 的整秩二 Steinberg 展示等同，因此该装饰几何直接决定系数取值 Bianchi 模符号的有限展示；对 \(\Gamma_1(2+i)\) 给出完整的 \(3\times4\) 群环边界矩阵。

Conclusion: Picard Farey 符号为 Picard 群有限指数子群提供了有限组合编码，并连通道具化八面体几何、基本域计数、Steinberg 展示与 Bianchi 模符号有限展示。规范约化以及有效的 Hecke 相容约化仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We introduce Picard Farey symbols for finite-index subgroups of \(G=\PSL_2(\ZZ[i])\), using the Gaussian Farey tessellation of \(\HH^3\) by ideal octahedra. A symbol records finitely many Gaussian-rational octahedral occurrences, their local stabilizers, and ordered cooriented face pairings. We prove that a valid marked symbol reconstructs the finite Picard-cell action and hence the subgroup, and we work out a range of exact examples. For a torsion-free subgroup of index \(n\), every fundamental domain formed from complete Farey octahedra contains \(n/12\) octahedra; a spanning-tree construction gives one with at most \(n/4+1\) side-pairing transformations. We also identify the Gaussian octahedral edge--face complex with the integral rank-two Steinberg presentation for \(\QQ(i)\). Thus the same decorated geometry directly determines a finite presentation of coefficient-valued Bianchi modular symbols; for \(Γ_1(2+i)\) we give the complete \(3\times4\) group-ring boundary matrix. Canonical reduction and an effective Hecke-compatible reduction remain open.

</details>


### [263] [A Local Approach to Monogenity with an Application to Lenny Jones' Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36811)
*Michail Karatarakis,Sumandeep Kaur*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了判别一般首一不可约多项式根生成序的 p-极大性的局部准则，并应用于 $f(X)=X^n+A(BX+1)^m$ 型多项式，证明其单基因性等价于 $A$ 与 $n^n+(-1)^{n+m}B^n(n-m)^{n-m}m^mA$ 均无平方因子，从而给出 KK 主定理的新证明、验证 Lenny Jones 猜想并获得无限多个非单基因不可约多项式族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定多项式是否单基因通常依赖判别式计算，并结合 Dedekind 准则、Newton 多边形或赋值论方法；这些方法往往繁琐且缺乏统一性。作者希望建立更一般的局部准则以简化判定，并借此解决 Lenny Jones 提出的猜想，同时构造显式的非单基因多项式族。

Method: 作者发展了判定任意首一不可约多项式之根所生成序的 p-极大性的局部准则，并将其应用于形如 $f(X)=X^n+A(BX+1)^m$ 的不可约多项式，其中 $1\le m<n$、$\gcd(n,mB)=1$、$A,B\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\{0\}$。

Result: 证明了 $f$ 为单基因当且仅当 $A$ 与 $n^n+(-1)^{n+m}B^n(n-m)^{n-m}m^mA$ 均为无平方因子。由此给出 \cite{KK} 主定理的新证明，进而证明了 Lenny Jones 的猜想 \cite[Conjecture 4.1]{LJ}，并得到显式的无限非单基因不可约多项式族，包括三项式、四项式与幂复合族。

Conclusion: 该局部 p-极大性准则为单基因性研究提供了统一工具，成功验证 Lenny Jones 猜想，并揭示了若干无限非单基因多项式族，拓展了代数数论中单基因多项式的经典结果。

Abstract: The study of monogenic polynomials is a classical problem in algebraic number theory. Existing criteria for deciding whether a polynomial is monogenic typically rely on discriminant computations together with methods such as Dedekind's criterion, Newton polygons, or valuation-theoretic techniques. In this paper, we develop a general local criterion for the $p$-maximality of orders generated by roots of arbitrary monic irreducible polynomials. As an application, we apply this to irreducible polynomials of the type $f(X)=X^n+A(BX+1)^m,$ where $1\le m<n$, $\gcd(n,mB)=1$, and $A,B\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\{0\}$. We show that $f$ is monogenic if and only if both $A~\text{and}~n^n+(-1)^{n+m}B^n(n-m)^{\,n-m}m^mA$ are square-free. This provides a new proof of the main theorem of \cite{KK}, thereby proving Lenny Jones' conjecture \cite[Conjecture 4.1]{LJ}. Furthermore, we obtain explicit infinite families of irreducible non-monogenic polynomials, including trinomial, quadrinomial, and power-compositional families.

</details>


### [264] [The Index of Composition of polynomials and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36877)
*Surender Kumar,Sumandeep Kaur*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了多项式 f(x) 的指数整除复合多项式 f(g(x)) 的指数，并给出了精确的幂次刻画，进而研究了迭代复合多项式的单项性与数域塔中单项数域的数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究整系数多项式环中指数在复合运算下的整除关系，并借此刻画迭代多项式 f^n(x) 的单项性及其生成的数域塔中单项数域的个数，推广已有的单项性结果。

Method: 利用代数数论中导子（index）与判别式的性质，通过分析 Z[θ] 在代数整数环中的指数，将 f(x) 的指数与 f(g(x)) 的指数联系起来，精确确定 f(x) 的指数在 f(g(x)) 指数中的幂次，并导出单项性的必要条件；再将该结果应用于迭代复合 f^n(x)。

Result: 证明了 index(f(x)) 总是整除 index(f(g(x)))，并给出了 index(f(x)) 在 index(f(g(x))) 中的精确幂次；给出 f(g(x)) 单项性的一个必要条件。由此推出：f^n(x) 对所有 n∈N 均单项当且仅当指数序列 ⟨I_n⟩ 收敛。此外，若对给定 n，I_n 为 m 次幂自由整数，则 n 次数域塔 Q⊆K_1⊆…⊆K_n 中至少包含 max{⌊n−log_{°f} m⌋,0} 个单项数域。

Conclusion: 指数在复合运算下具有整除与幂次可控的性质，这为判定迭代多项式 f^n(x) 的单项性提供了序列收敛判据，并给出了数域塔中单项数域数量的下界估计。

Abstract: The index of a given monic irreducible polynomial $f(x)\in\mathbb Z[x]$ having a root $θ$ is the index of $\mathbb Z[θ]$ in the ring of algebraic integers of $\mathbb Q(θ).$ Further, $f(x)$ is monogenic if its index is $1$. In this article, we prove that the index of $f(x)$ always divides the index of $f(g(x)),$ where $f(x), g(x)\in \mathbb Z[x]$. Additionally, we give the precise power of the index of $f(x)$ dividing the index of $f(g(x))$. Also, we provide a necessary condition for the monogenity of $f(g(x))$. As an application of our results, we prove that the $n$-fold composition $f^n(x)=(f\circ f\cdots\circ f)(x)$ of a polynomial $f(x)$ is monogenic for all $n\in\mathbb{N}$ if and only if the sequence $\langle I_n\rangle$ is convergent, where $I_n$ is the index of $f^n(x)$. Moreover, we show that for a given $n\in\mathbb{N}$, if $I_n$ is $m^{\text{th}}$ power free integer, then the $n$-tower $\mathbb Q\subseteq K_1 \subseteq\cdots \subseteq K_{n-1} \subseteq K_n,$ defined by $n$ iterates of a polynomial $f(x)\in\mathbb Z[x]$ contains at least $\max\{\lfloor n-\log_{°f}m \rfloor,0\}$ monogenic number fields.

</details>


### [265] [Collective Contraction in the de Bruijn--Newman Heat Flow: Off-Zero Logarithmic Derivatives and Certified Barrier Refinements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37164)
*Michel Planat*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究 de Bruijn–Newman 族 H_τ(z)（热形变参数 τ，H_0(z)=⅛ξ(½+iz/2)）的零点动力学，证明最大虚部零点 y² 的单调收缩不等式，并用投影相互作用和的对数导数下界与 Polymath 有效逼近 H_τ=B_τF_τ 相接，最终将非实零点最大高度收缩到 0.08。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是推进 de Bruijn–Newman 常数 Λ 的上界。经典方法只在零点层面作粗略估计，忽略零点间集体相互作用；本文希望保留该相互作用项，并与已知的 Polymath 有效逼近框架对接，从而获得更强、可审计的收缩界。

Method: 在 H_τ 的零点动力学中保留集体相互作用项，对最大虚部非实零点 z=x+iy 证明 y² 对 τ 的导数上界 d/dτ y² ≤ -2-4y² 𝒢_τ(z)，其中 𝒢_τ≥0 为投影相互作用和；再用第二个精确不等式把 𝒢_τ 下方界为离零点对数导数 -Im(H_τ'/H_τ)(x+iη)，与 H_τ=B_τF_τ 对接。在边界 X=6000000185827、τ₀=129/800、y₀²=87677/2500000 处，用定向 Cauchy 证书证明 τ₀≤τ≤0.178、x≥X、|y|≤y₀ 时 𝒢_τ>3/2。结合该收缩与已认证的无零点上下屏障，用双包络论证把最大非实高度在时间 0.1747532546428610755… 降到 0.08，再由经典 de Bruijn 收缩完成落地。

Result: 在边界处得到 𝒢_τ>3/2（τ₀≤τ≤0.178，x≥X，|y|≤y₀）；双包络论证在 τ=0.1747532546428610755… 把最大非实高度降至 0.08，随后经典 de Bruijn 收缩完成。相对公开可复现但未经同行评议的 0.1787854 基准证书，得到审计相对界 Λ≤0.1779532546428610755…。

Conclusion: 集体收缩、对数导数桥接与新高 x 证书为三项独立贡献；所得审计相对上界为 Λ≤0.1779532546428610755…。

Abstract: We study the de Bruijn--Newman family $H_τ(z)$, with heat-deformation parameter $τ$ and $H_0(z)=\tfrac18,ξ(\tfrac12+\tfrac{iz}{2})$. The moving zeros considered here are therefore zeros of $H_τ$, not directly of $ζ(s)$. Retaining the collective interaction term in the zero dynamics, we prove that for a simple nonreal zero $z=x+iy$ of maximal imaginary height, [ \frac{d}{dτ}y^2\le -2-4y^2\mathcal G_τ(z), ] where $\mathcal G_τ\ge0$ is a projected interaction sum. A second exact inequality bounds $\mathcal G_τ$ below by an off-zero logarithmic derivative $-\operatorname{Im}(H_τ'/H_τ)(x+iη)$, providing a direct interface with the effective Polymath approximation $H_τ=B_τF_τ$. At the frontier $X=6000000185827$, $τ_0=129/800$, and $y_0^2=87677/2500000$, a directed Cauchy certificate gives $\mathcal G_τ>3/2$ for $τ_0\leτ\le0.178$, $x\ge X$, and $|y|\le y_0$. Combining this contraction with certified upper and lower zero-free barriers, a two-envelope argument reduces the maximal nonreal height to $0.08$ by time $0.1747532546428610755\ldots$; the classical de Bruijn contraction then completes the landing. Relative to the publicly replayable but not yet peer-reviewed $0.1787854$ base certificate, this yields the audit-relative bound [ Λ\le0.1779532546428610755\ldots . ] The collective contraction, logarithmic-derivative bridge, and new high-$x$ certificates are independent contributions.

</details>


### [266] [On Boston's Unramified Conjecture for $\GL_2$ and McLeman's $(3,3)$-Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37252)
*Yufan Luo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文证明了Boston关于无分叉Fontaine–Mazur猜想加强版在Q上的二维奇表示情形，并对虚二次域K给出共轭自对偶二维p进表示的有限像结果，进而解决McLeman (3,3)-猜想在p>3时的充分性方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于Boston对无分叉Fontaine–Mazur猜想的加强形式（预测此类Galois表示像有限），以及McLeman关于p-类域塔的(3,3)-猜想。已有结果主要停留在一般框架或部分情形，二维奇表示及虚二次域共轭自对偶情形尚缺完整证明。

Method: 基于Zhang的近期工作，对有限素集合S（含∞不含p），研究连续奇表示G_{Q,S}→GL_2(A)（A为剩余域特征p有限的完备Noetherian局部环）；并对虚二次域K，在S满足温和条件下研究其pro-p Galois群G_{K,S}(p)的连续共轭自对偶二维p进表示。通过证明像有限，导出Zassenhaus型(3,3)情形下p-类域塔的有限性。

Result: 1) 对任意奇素数p，任何连续奇表示G_{Q,S}→GL_2(A)像有限，从而证明Boston加强版无分叉Fontaine–Mazur猜想在Q上的二维奇情形；2) 对虚二次域K，在S满足温和条件时，G_{K,S}(p)的任意连续共轭自对偶二维p进表示像有限；3) 当p>3且K为p-类秩为2、G_{K,∅}(p)为Zassenhaus型(3,3)的虚二次域时，K的p-类域塔有限（解决McLeman (3,3)-猜想充分性方向）；对p=3，Ahlqvist与Pink的结果在第四Zassenhaus商的13种可能情形中给出其中10种的有限性结论。

Conclusion: 论文推进了无分叉Fontaine–Mazur猜想加强版的研究，确立二维奇表示有限像，并将结果应用于p-类域塔问题，基本解决McLeman (3,3)-猜想（p>3全部、p=3大部分情形），为后续完全刻画剩余情形提供了框架。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime number. Based on recent work of Zhang, we prove that for a finite set $S$ of primes of $\Q$ containing $\infty$ but not $p$, any continuous odd representation $G_{\Q,S} \to \GL_2(A)$ over a complete Noetherian local ring $A$ with finite residue field of characteristic $p$ has finite image, where $G_{\Q,S}$ denotes the Galois group of the maximal extension of $\Q$ unramified outside $S$. This proves the two-dimensional odd case of Boston's strengthening of the unramified Fontaine--Mazur conjecture over $\Q$. Furthermore, we show that for an imaginary quadratic field $K$, any continuous conjugate self-dual two-dimensional $p$-adic representation of its pro-$p$ Galois group $G_{K,S}(p)$ has finite image, provided the primes in $S$ satisfy a modest condition. As an application, we resolve the sufficiency direction of McLeman's $(3,3)$-conjecture on $p$-class field towers for $p>3$. Namely, we prove that if $K$ is an imaginary quadratic field with $p$-class rank two and the Galois group $G_{K,\varnothing}(p)$ of the maximal unramified $p$-extension of $K$ has Zassenhaus type $(3,3)$, then the $p$-class field tower of $K$ is finite. For $p=3$, the results of Ahlqvist and Pink give the same finiteness conclusion in ten of the thirteen possible cases for the fourth Zassenhaus quotient.

</details>


### [267] [The Algebra of Parity-Twisted Crank Moments and a Prime-Detecting Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37301)
*Soon-Yi Kang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了由归一化宇称扭结crank矩所生成的代数，将其显式刻画为Γ₀(2)上准模形式的一个在D=q·d/dq下封闭的真子代数，并构造出仅涉及归一化扭结二阶矩及其乘积与导数的素数判别表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 素数判别函数的构造与模形式/准模形式代数结构之间存在深刻联系。本文旨在通过crank矩所生成的代数来刻画素数判别元素，并确定其最小权重的判别表达式，同时将该代数与MacMahon函数B_k所生成的代数联系起来。

Method: 通过分析归一化宇称扭结crank矩生成的代数，将其显式描述为Γ₀(2)上准模形式在微分算子D=q d/dq下封闭的真子代数；从微分恒等式推导同余式，并构造仅含归一化扭结二阶矩及其乘积与导数的素数判别表达式；通过权分析证明其最高权为8且在此类判别元素中最小。

Result: 所构造的表达式在q^n的系数当且仅当n为素数时消失（对所有n≥2成立）。证明了该元素的最高权8在此代数素数判别元素中是最小的。此外，识别出该代数与MacMahon函数B_k所生成的代数为同一代数，并在B_1中得到了相应的素数判别表达式。

Conclusion: 归一化宇称扭结crank矩生成的代数与MacMahon函数生成的代数相同，其中存在以最高权8为最小的素数判别元素，并可在B_1中给出相应的素数判别表达式。

Abstract: We determine the algebra generated by the normalized parity-twisted crank moments and describe it explicitly as a proper subalgebra of the quasi-modular forms on $Γ_0(2)$ closed under $D=q\frac{d}{dq}$. We derive congruences from differential identities and construct a prime-detecting expression involving only the normalized twisted second moment, its products, and its derivatives. Its coefficient of $q^n$ vanishes if and only if $n$ is prime, for every $n\ge2$. We prove that its highest weight, eight, is minimal among prime-detecting elements of this algebra. Finally, we identify the same algebra as the one generated by MacMahon's functions $B_k$, obtaining a corresponding prime-detecting expression in $B_1$.

</details>


### [268] [Truncated pretentious distances of multiplicative arithmetic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37332)
*Thomas Renard*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究非伪装乘性函数的自相关平均收敛性，将局部伪装行为与素数上的偏差联系起来，并给出一类满足Elliott猜想原始形式的多函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画非伪装乘性函数的自相关平均的收敛性，并理解其局部伪装行为与在素数上偏离1之间的关系，进而推进Elliott猜想原始形式的研究。

Method: 在Granville–Soundararajan意义下考察非伪装乘性函数f: ℕ→𝕌，分析其自相关平均的收敛性，重点研究f在区间[x^ε, x]上的局部伪装行为与其在素数上偏离1的关系。

Result: 建立了函数在短区间上的局部伪装行为与素数上偏差之间的联系，并据此给出了一类非伪装乘性函数，使其满足Elliott猜想的原始表述。

Conclusion: 在Klurman–Mangerel–Teräväinen结果的基础上，证明了Elliott猜想原始形式在一类非伪装乘性函数上成立，深化了对自相关平均收敛性的理解。

Abstract: Let $f\colon\mathbb{N}\to\mathbb{U}$ be a non-pretentious multiplicative function in the sense of Granville and Soundararajan. We study the convergence of the autocorrelation averages of $f$. In particular, we investigate the relationship between the local pretentious behaviour of $f$ on intervals of the form $[x^{\varepsilon},x]$ and its deviation from $1$ on the primes. As an application, we exhibit a class of non-pretentious multiplicative functions for which Elliott's conjecture in its original formulation holds, following up on a result by Klurman, Mangerel and Teräväinen.

</details>


### [269] [On an infinite family of cubic fields with explicit fundamental units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37389)
*Iwao Kimura,Hikaru Umemoto*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究三次域 $K_b=\mathbb{Q}(\theta)$（$\theta$ 为 $x^3-3bx-b^3$ 的唯一实根）中元素 $\eta_b=-1/(\theta-(b+1))$ 何时为基本单位，给出正密度 $b$ 的显式集合，并用 Pell 方程刻画 $\eta_b$ 为单位平方的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定纯三次域家族中特定单位 $\eta_b$ 的基本单位性质，并理解 $\eta_b$ 非基本单位的例外现象（源于极大序），进而应用于构造 $3$-类域塔长度大于 $1$ 的双二次域。

Method: 以 $f_b(x)=x^3-3bx-b^3$ 定义三次域 $K_b$，考察 $\eta_b=-1/(\theta-(b+1))$；构造正密度的 $b$ 集合证明其为基本单位；通过 Pell 方程 $D^2-3E^2=1$ 参数化 $\eta_b$ 为单位平方的 $b$；在序 $\mathbb{Z}[\eta_b]$ 中验证 $\eta_b$ 恒为基本单位；结合判别式的温和条件与例外 $b$（$|b|\le3000$ 中仅四个，$b=3$ 结论失效）分析极大序情形。

Result: 找到了使 $\eta_b$ 为 $K_b$ 基本单位的正密度 $b$ 集合；$\eta_b$ 为单位平方的 $b$ 由 Pell 方程 $D^2-3E^2=1$ 参数化，故为无限集；在温和判别式条件下，这完整刻画了 $\eta_b$ 非基本单位的所有 $b$；条件仅在 $|b|\le3000$ 中四个 $b$ 处失效，其中 $b=3$ 时结论本身不成立；在序 $\mathbb{Z}[\eta_b]$ 中 $\eta_b$ 对所有 $b$ 都是基本单位。作为应用，构造出无穷多个 $3$-类域塔长度大于 $1$ 的双二次域。

Conclusion: $\eta_b$ 在极大序中通常为基本单位，例外稀少且可由 Pell 方程完整分类，并依赖于序的选择；该结果为类域塔理论提供了新的无限族构造。

Abstract: For an integer $b\neq0,1$ let $θ$ be the unique real root of $f_b(x)=x^3-3bx-b^3$ and let $K_b=\mathbb{Q}(θ)$. We exhibit an explicit set of $b$ of positive density for which $η_b=-1/(θ-(b+1))$ is the fundamental unit of $K_b$, and we determine the set of $b$ for which $η_b$ is the square of a unit: it is parametrized by the Pell equation $D^2-3E^2=1$, hence infinite, and under an explicit mild condition on the field discriminant it accounts for all $b$ for which $η_b$ is not the fundamental unit. That condition fails for only four $b$ with $|b|\le3000$, and at one of them, $b=3$, the conclusion itself fails. In the order $\mathbb{Z}[η_b]$ generated by $η_b$, by contrast, $η_b$ is the fundamental unit for every $b$, so these exceptions are a phenomenon of the maximal order. As an application we construct infinitely many biquadratic fields whose $3$-class field tower has length greater than $1$.

</details>


### [270] [On minima of theta and Epstein zeta functions in dimension four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37615)
*Senping Luo,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在四维协体积为1的格中，根格 $\mathcal{D}_4$ 同时极小化 theta 函数 $Θ(α,L)$（$α>0$）与 Epstein zeta 函数 $E(L,s)$（$s>2$），从而解决了四维 Rankin-Sobolev 问题并验证了 Sarnak-Strömbergsson 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sarnak-Strömbergsson（2006）证明了 $\mathcal{D}_4$ 的局部极小性并猜想其为全局极小；四维的 Rankin-Sobolev 问题可追溯至 Éndibaev（1978）与 Shushbaev（1978）。本文旨在证明该全局极小性，解决这一长期悬而未决的问题。

Method: 在协体积为1的全秩格 $L\subset\mathbb R^4$ 空间中，考虑重新标度为协体积1的根格 $\mathcal{D}_4=2^{-1/4}D_4$。通过证明对任意 $α>0$，$Θ(α,L)$ 在正交变换意义下的极小元为 $\mathcal{D}_4$，并由此推出 $s>2$ 时 $E(L,s)$ 的极小元同样为 $\mathcal{D}_4$。

Result: 证明了 $\arg\min_{\mathrm{covol}(L)=1}Θ(α,L)=\mathcal{D}_4$（$α>0$）以及 $\arg\min_{\mathrm{covol}(L)=1}E(L,s)=\mathcal{D}_4$（$s>2$），即 $\mathcal{D}_4$ 在四维中极小化 theta 与 Epstein zeta 函数。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了四维中根格 $\mathcal{D}_4$ 对 theta 与 Epstein zeta 函数的全局极小性，验证了 Sarnak-Strömbergsson 的猜想，并解决了维度四的 Rankin-Sobolev 问题。

Abstract: Let the theta and Epstein zeta functions be $Θ(α,L)=\sum_{v\in L}e^{-πα|v|^2}$ for $α>0$ and $E(L,s)=\sum_{v\in L\setminus\{0\}}{|v|^{-2s}}$ for $s>2$, respectively. We consider full-rank lattices $L\subset\mathbb R^4$. Let the covolume of $L$ be one, and let $\mathcal D_4=2^{-1/4}D_4$ be the root lattice $D_4$ rescaled to covolume one. We prove that, up to orthogonal transformations, \begin{equation}\nonumber arg\,min_{covol(L)=1}Θ(α,L)=\mathcal D_4 \qquad\text{if}\quad α>0, \end{equation} and this implies that \begin{equation}\nonumber arg\,min_{covol(L)=1}E(L,s)=\mathcal D_4 \qquad\text{if}\quad s>2. \end{equation} This proves that the root lattice minimizes the theta and Epstein zeta functions among all lattices in dimension four, as conjectured by Sarnak-Strömbergsson (\cite{SS}, 2006), who established the local minimality of $\mathcal D_4$. Thereby, this resolves the Rankin-Sobolev problem in dimension four dating back to Éndibaev (\cite{End1978}, 1978) and Shushbaev (\cite{Shu1978}, 1978).

</details>


### [271] [Products of two elements of Granville's random model in residue classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37434)
*William D. Banks*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在Granville随机素数模型中，无条件证明每个约化剩余类模q可由两个不超过q^Θ(Θ>3/4)的素数之积表示，且表示数渐近于期望阶；而在Banks–Ford–Tao模型下该陈述以正概率失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős、Odlyzko与Sárközy猜想：每个模q的约化剩余类中都含有两个不超过q的素数之积；该猜想即使假设GRH仍未解决。本文旨在研究其类似命题在随机素数模型中的成立性，并考察模型选择对结论的决定性作用。

Method: 在Granville的随机素数模型中，证明该猜想的analogue无条件成立，并推广到q^Θ（任意固定Θ>3/4）的更大范围；通过分析Granville模型的"discard step"来刻画其与Banks–Ford–Tao模型的差异。

Result: 在Granville模型中，几乎必然地，对每个充分大的模数的每个约化类都可表示，且表示个数渐近于其期望阶。相比之下，在Banks–Ford–Tao的随机素数模型中相应陈述以正概率失败，该失败源于一个初等的局部缺陷，而Granville的discard step恰好消除该缺陷。

Conclusion: 该猜想的类似命题在Granville随机素数模型中成立且范围扩展至q^Θ(Θ>3/4)，模型选择是决定性的：Granville的discard step消除了Banks–Ford–Tao模型中导致结论失败的局部缺陷。

Abstract: Erdős, Odlyzko and Sárközy conjectured that every reduced residue class modulo $q$ contains a product of two primes not exceeding $q$; their conjecture remains open even under the GRH. We prove that its analogue holds, unconditionally and in the wider range $q^Θ$ for any fixed $Θ> 3/4$, in Granville's random model of the primes. Almost surely, every reduced class modulo every sufficiently large modulus is representable, and the number of representations is asymptotic to its expected order. The choice of the model is decisive; the corresponding statement in the random prime model of Banks, Ford and Tao fails with positive probability, an elementary local defect that Granville's discard step removes.

</details>


### [272] [Multiple Zeta Values in Positive Characteristic: A Kaneko-Zagier-Type Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37450)
*Hung-Chun Tsui*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文比较了正特征下的∞-adic与有限多重zeta值，构造了一个从∞-adic多重zeta值代数到有限多重zeta值代数之商的全射比较映射，并证明其相容性及对Shi维数猜想上界的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正特征下∞-adic多重zeta值与有限多重zeta值之间的联系，验证Kaneko-Zagier型猜想映射的良定义性。

Method: 构造从∞-adic多重zeta值代数到有限多重zeta值代数之商的全射比较映射，并证明该映射经过由ζ_A(q-1)生成的商因子分解；验证其与Carlitz多重多对数特殊值及相应⋆-逆值的相容性。

Result: 建立了Kaneko-Zagier型猜想映射的良定义性；证明了比较映射与Carlitz多重多对数特殊值及⋆-逆值的相容性；作为应用，证明了Shi维数猜想对有限多重zeta值所预测的上界。

Conclusion: 该比较映射为理解正特征下两类多重zeta值的关系提供了统一框架，并给出了有限多重zeta值维数上界的证明。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a comparison between $\infty$-adic and finite multiple zeta values in positive characteristic. We construct a surjective comparison map from the algebra of $\infty$-adic multiple zeta values to a quotient of the algebra of finite multiple zeta values. This map factors through the quotient by $ζ_A(q-1)$, thereby establishing the well-definedness of a Kaneko-Zagier-type conjectural map. We further show that the comparison map is compatible with special values of Carlitz multiple polylogarithms, as well as with the corresponding $\star$-inverse values. As an application, we prove the upper bound predicted by Shi's dimension conjecture for finite multiple zeta values.

</details>


### [273] [A closed-form law for the Salikhov-Zeilberger-Zudilin-Bai family, and computational evidence that Bai's point is optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37459)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文用统一闭式表达 Salikhov、Zeilberger-Zudilin 与 Bai 的 π 无理性测度界，证明窗口测度齐次性使三参数格塌缩为二维，并给出 Bai 点为该族全局最小值的计算证据与猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Salikhov (2008)、Zeilberger-Zudilin (2020)、Bai (2026) 用同一形状的复围道积分给出 π 的无理性测度界，仅三个指数不同；作者希望给出统一闭式表达式，使算术引理无需随指数重新推导，并探讨该界族的全局最优性。

Method: 将界写成三指数的闭式函数；鞍点数据由显式三次式给出；算术因子（2 的幂、lcm 范围、推广 Z-Z 引理 2 的素数消去窗口）对任意指数写出。证明窗口测度精确齐次 W = a1·Jhat(a0/a1, b/a1)，使三参数格塌缩为二维；π 系数为单系数抽取；j = 0 时整除判据精确，出现超 Catalan 数并由 Kummer 定理得到 ord_p 等式。在二维搜索中做三组有限计算。

Result: 闭式律在 (2,2,3) 给出 7.1032053341370017，与 Zeilberger-Zudilin 符合至 17 位；在 Bai 点 (1857,1857,2785) 给出 7.1018628323563507，与其发表的 7.101862832357 一致；在 Salikhov 两分支窗口复现 7.606308。在 a0 = a1 切片上，界仅为 Q = 2a0/(3a0-2b) 的函数，Q = 3714 处有尖锐顶点，仅由 (1857,1857,2785) 本原实现。附带 Python 脚本（仅标准库）约半分钟重推所有断言。

Conclusion: 窗口测度齐次性使三参数族约化为二维对象，统一闭式框架免去重复算术引理。计算证据表明 Bai 点为该族全局最小值，由此得到楼层值 7.101862832356 作为猜想；该结论基于三组有限计算，标记为 verified 而非 proved。

Abstract: Salikhov (2008), Zeilberger-Zudilin (2020) and Bai (2026) all bound the irrationality measure of pi with the same shape of complex contour integral, differing only in three exponents. We give one closed-form expression for the resulting bound as a function of those exponents. The saddle-point data come from an explicit cubic; the arithmetic factor -- the power of 2, the range of the lcm, and the prime-elimination window generalising Zeilberger-Zudilin's Lemma 2 -- is written down for arbitrary exponents, so no arithmetic lemma has to be redone for a new choice. The law returns 7.1032053341370017 at (2,2,3), agreeing with Zeilberger-Zudilin to 17 digits, and 7.1018628323563507 at Bai's point (1857,1857,2785), agreeing with his published 7.101862832357; it reproduces Salikhov's 7.606308 from his own two-branch window.
  We prove that the window measure is exactly homogeneous, W = a1.Jhat(a0/a1, b/a1), so the bound depends only on two ratios and the three-parameter lattice collapses to a two-dimensional object; that the pi-coefficient is a single coefficient extraction; and that at j = 0 the divisibility criterion is exact, a super-Catalan number appearing and Kummer's theorem then giving ord_p with equality rather than inequality.
  On that two-dimensional search we give computational evidence that Bai's point is the global minimum of the family: on the slice a0 = a1 the bound is a function of Q = 2a0/(3a0-2b) alone, with a sharp vertex at Q = 3714, realised primitively only by (1857,1857,2785). This rests on three finite computations and is labelled verified, not proved; the resulting floor 7.101862832356 is stated as a conjecture.
  An ancillary Python script (standard library only) re-derives every claim in about half a minute.

</details>


### [274] [Weighted equidistribution of factorial prime valuations in fixed power bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37460)
*Ma Yicen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文研究固定模h与剩余类c下，素数在n!中指数的对数质量，证明在幂次区间n^a < p ≤ n^b内满足指数≡c (mod h)的素数渐近为((b-a)/h + o(1)) log(n!)，并给出多项式-阶乘方程P(x)=n!无穷解族的容量必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画n!中素数指数（即Legendre指数v_p(n!)）在给定模h下的剩余类分布，量化落在幂次区间内的素数所贡献的对数质量，并将其作为工具应用于多项式-阶乘方程的解的结构限制。

Method: 方法结合三项技术：固定阶导数估计、Vaughan恒等式（用于处理素数上的指数和/素变量分解）、以及对不连续赋值函数（指数取模的函数）的有限Fourier逼近。

Result: 主要结果是渐近式((b-a)/h + o(1)) log(n!)，适用于n^a < p ≤ n^b（0 ≤ a < b ≤ 1）且v_p(n!) ≡ c (mod h)的素数；此外得到P(x)=n!存在无穷多解所需的容量必要条件。

Conclusion: 该工作给出n!素数指数模h分布的定量渐近，说明各剩余类在幂次区间内近似均匀（密度1/h），并由此导出多项式-阶乘方程无穷解族的容量型必要条件。

Abstract: For a fixed modulus h and residue class c, we study the logarithmic mass of primes p in a fixed power band for which the exponent of p in n! is congruent to c modulo h. We establish the asymptotic ((b-a)/h + o(1)) log(n!) for primes satisfying n^a < p <= n^b, where 0 <= a < b <= 1. The argument combines a fixed-order derivative estimate, Vaughan's identity, and finite Fourier approximation of a discontinuous valuation function. We also derive a necessary capacity condition for infinite families of solutions to polynomial-factorial equations P(x) = n!.

</details>


### [275] [An infinite family of counterexamples to Goss's conjecture on L-functions of cyclotomic function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37466)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文否证了Goss关于函数域Artin L-函数"模p余一"部分次数的猜想（Vandiver猜想的函数域类比、Angles提出的公开问题），通过构造具体反例证明该次数无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goss猜想 deg_X g(X, omega_P^i) <= 1 对所有 q-magic指标i成立，这是Vandiver猜想在函数域中的类比，且被Angles列为公开问题。作者旨在检验并否证该猜想。

Method: 设 A = F_p[T]，取 P_a = T^p - T - a（a ∈ F_p^*），其为p次不可约多项式。取 q-magic指标 i = p^n - 1（0 <= n <= p-1），通过精确同余计算证明 L-多项式模 P_a 约化为 (1-X)^n。

Result: 由L-多项式模P_a约化为(1-X)^n，推出 deg_X g = n-1。当 p >= 5 且 3 <= n <= p-1 时，deg_X g >= 2，与Goss猜想矛盾；且 deg_X g = n-1 无界，最大取到 p-2。

Conclusion: Goss猜想被否证，其对应次数可以无界增长，最高达到 p-2，从而解决了Angles提出的公开问题。

Abstract: Let p be a prime, q = p, A = F_p[T], and let P be a monic irreducible of degree d with cyclotomic function field K_P and mod-P Teichmuller character omega_P. For a character chi = omega_P^i, Goss defined g(X, chi), the "congruent to one modulo p" part of the Artin L-function L(X, chi), and conjectured that deg_X g(X, omega_P^i) <= 1 for every q-magic index i; this is an analogue for function fields of Vandiver's conjecture, and was raised as an open problem by Angles. We disprove it. For every a in F_p^*, put P_a = T^p - T - a, irreducible of degree p, and let i = p^n - 1 with 0 <= n <= p-1, a q-magic index. We prove an exact congruence showing that the L-polynomial reduces to (1-X)^n modulo P_a, whence deg_X g = n-1. For p >= 5 and 3 <= n <= p-1 this gives deg_X g >= 2, contradicting Goss's conjecture; moreover deg_X g = n-1 is unbounded, attaining p-2.

</details>


### [276] [A negative answer to Erdős Problem #786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37471)
*Shisheng Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文否定了Erdős关于"可容许集"(admissible sets)的两个经典问题：证明了可容许集A的倒数和有上界∑1/a ≤ (1/2)log N + (log log N+2)²，并证明存在常数η>0使每个可容许集A⊆{1,…,N}满足|A|<(1-η)N。两个问题因此均得到否定回答。证明是初等的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős提出两个问题：(1)可容许集能否具有密度1-ε(对任意ε>0)；(2){1,…,N}是否总包含大小约为(1-o(1))N的可容许子集。允许重复的变体已被Erdős-Ruzsa-Sárközy及Granville-Soundararajan否定；对"相异元素之积"情形，第一问近期仅得到7/8的密度上界，第二问一直开放。本文旨在彻底解决这两个问题。

Method: 初等方法。核心技巧是构造一种"耦合"(coupling)，用由小素数构成的整数的最大除数替换，从而避免沿乘法表计数商时固有的除数函数损失。两个否定结论均在Lean 4中依据Formal Conjectures项目的陈述进行了形式化验证。

Result: 证明每个可容许集A⊆{1,…,N}满足∑_{a∈A}1/a ≤ (1/2)log N + (log log N+2)²；并证明存在绝对常数η>0，使得对所有充分大的N，每个可容许集A⊆{1,…,N}都满足|A|<(1-η)N。

Conclusion: 两个Erdős问题(关于可容许集能否达到密度1-ε、以及{1,…,N}是否含大小(1-o(1))N的可容许子集)均得到否定回答，且结论经过Lean 4形式化验证。

Abstract: Call a set $A$ of positive integers admissible if, whenever $a_1\cdots a_r=b_1\cdots b_s$ with $a_1,\dots,a_r$ distinct elements of $A$ and $b_1,\dots,b_s$ distinct elements of $A$, necessarily $r=s$. Erdős asked whether admissible sets can have density $1-\varepsilon$ for every $\varepsilon>0$, and whether $\{1,\dots,N\}$ always contains an admissible subset of size $(1-o(1))N$. For the variant in which repetitions are allowed both questions were answered negatively by Erdős, Ruzsa and Sárközy and by Granville and Soundararajan; for products of distinct elements, the first question was answered only recently (with density bound $7/8$), and the second has remained open. We show that every admissible $A\subseteq\{1,\dots,N\}$ satisfies $\sum_{a\in A}1/a\le\tfrac12\log N+(\log\log N+2)^2$, and that there is an absolute constant $η>0$ such that every admissible $A\subseteq\{1,\dots,N\}$ has $|A|<(1-η)N$ for all large $N$. Both questions therefore have negative answers. The proofs are elementary; the second rests on a coupling that replaces the largest divisor of an integer composed of small primes, which avoids the divisor-function losses inherent in counting quotients along a multiplication table. Both negative answers are formally verified in Lean 4 against the statements of the Formal Conjectures project.

</details>


### [277] [Uniform Estimates for Integers with a Large Smooth Part](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37507)
*Xiangyu Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究整数的"大光滑部分"分布，在 log z ≤ y ≤ z ≤ x/2 范围内给出光滑部分超过阈值 z 的整数计数的一致估计，结果呈 Dickman 函数控制的指数阶，证明结合光滑数、粗糙数的经典估计与光滑计数函数局部行为的一致估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑数（y-smooth）与粗糙数的计数是解析数论的核心对象，但此前结果多关注整数本身是否光滑。本文关注每个整数的"光滑部分"（即其最大的 y-光滑因子）这一更精细的统计量，并希望在较宽参数范围内获得对光滑部分超过给定阈值 z 的整数个数的一致估计，弥补现有估计在该范围内非一致的空白。

Method: 证明方法结合两类工具：一是光滑数与粗糙数的经典估计；二是光滑数计数函数（Ψ(x,y)）在局部行为上的一致估计。将整数按其光滑部分与粗糙部分分解，借助这些一致估计对各分段求和，从而得到整体计数的一致渐近式。

Result: 得到 x 以内 y-光滑部分超过阈值 z 的整数个数的一致估计，适用于 log z ≤ y ≤ z ≤ x/2 范围。该估计具有由 Dickman 函数 ρ 决定的期望指数阶，且在整个范围内保持一致（即误差项一致可控），不依赖于 y、z 的相对位置。

Conclusion: 本文给出了大光滑部分整数分布的一个统一描述，将光滑数计数理论从"整数的光滑性"推广到"整数的光滑部分的精细分布"，并为后续在更宽范围或更强一致性要求下的光滑数相关问题提供了方法与工具。

Abstract: We study the distribution of integers with a large smooth part. We obtain a uniform estimate for the number of integers up to \(x\) whose \(y\)-smooth part exceeds a given threshold \(z\), in the range \(\log z\le y\le z\le x/2\). Our estimate has the expected exponential order governed by the Dickman function and holds uniformly throughout this range. The proof combines classical estimates for smooth and rough numbers with uniform estimates for the local behavior of the smooth-number counting function.

</details>


### [278] [A Short Proof of a Theorem of Pan and Zhang](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37508)
*Xiangyu Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对潘与张关于正密度整数可表示为 $a^2+b^2+2^{c^2}$ 的结论给出了一个简短的新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潘与张已证明正比例的正整数可写成 $a^2+b^2+2^{c^2}$（$a,b,c$ 为非负整数）。作者希望给出一个更简短的证明，以简化原论证。

Method: 采用 Romanoff 方法，并结合初等的除数（divisor）论证来控制相关的二阶矩。

Result: 给出了原结果的一个简短证明，即正比例的正整数可表示为 $a^2+b^2+2^{c^2}$ 的形式。

Conclusion: 通过 Romanoff 方法与初等除数论证控制二阶矩，可以简洁地重新证明潘与张的密度结果。

Abstract: Pan and Zhang proved that a positive proportion of positive integers can be represented in the form $a^2+b^2+2^{c^2}$, where $(a,b,c)$ are nonnegative integers. In this note, we give a short proof of their result. The proof uses Romanoff's method and an elementary divisor argument to control the relevant second moment.

</details>


### [279] [Ax-Schanuel for the Drinfeld $j$-function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37595)
*Gal Binyamini,Dmitry Novikov,Francesco Maria Saettone*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了奇数特征下 Drinfeld $j$-函数的 Ax-Schanuel 定理类比，并给出 Blázquez-Sanz、Casale、Freitag 与 Nagloo 的微分 Galois 理论 Ax-Schanuel 定理的正特征类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ax-Schanuel 定理刻画函数图与代数簇的非典型交，已有结果多集中于特征零或指数函数等情形；本文旨在将这一框架推广到正特征下的 Drinfeld $j$-函数及其微分 Galois 理论版本。

Method: 首先证明正特征下 Blázquez-Sanz 等人的微分 Galois 理论 Ax-Schanuel 定理的类比；随后将主要定理应用于合适的叶层（foliation）以处理 Drinfeld $j$-函数；借助 Pink 的定理，将 Blázquez-Sanz 等人意义下的特殊簇等同于 Drinfeld 意义下的经典弱特殊子簇。

Result: 若 $\boldsymbol{j}\colonΩ^n\rightarrow\mathbb{A}^n_{\mathbb{C}_\infty}$ 的图及其导数与代数簇有非典型交 $\mathcal{V}$，则 $\mathcal{V}$ 投影为 $\mathbb{A}^n_{\mathbb{C}_\infty}$ 中的弱特殊子簇；正特征微分 Galois 理论 Ax-Schanuel 类比成立；且该语境下的特殊簇与 Drinfeld 弱特殊子簇一致。

Conclusion: 本文将 Ax-Schanuel 理论推广至奇数特征下的 Drinfeld $j$-函数，建立了正特征微分 Galois 理论 Ax-Schanuel 定理，并通过 Pink 定理统一了特殊簇的两种刻画。

Abstract: We prove an analog of the Ax-Schanuel theorem for the Drinfeld $j$-function in odd characteristic. Roughly speaking, if the graph of $\boldsymbol{j}\colonΩ^n\rightarrow\mathbb{A}^n_{\mathbb{C}_\infty}$ and its derivatives has an atypical intersection $\mathcal{V}$ with an algebraic variety, then $\mathcal{V}$ projects to a weakly-special subvariety in $\mathbb{A}^n_{\mathbb{C}_\infty}$.
  More generally, we prove a positive characteristic analog of the differential Galois theoretic Ax-Schanuel theorem of Blázquez-Sanz, Casale, Freitag and Nagloo. Our main theorem for $\boldsymbol{j}$ follows by applying this result to a suitable foliation. A theorem of Pink allows us to conclude that the special varieties in the sense of Blázquez-Sanz et al. in this context agree with the classical weakly-special subvarieties in the Drinfeld sense.

</details>


### [280] [Criterion set for diagonal forms of higher degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37736)
*Om Prakash*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 将Bhargava-Hanke关于二次型的290定理推广到全实数域上高次对角形式，证明存在唯一极小有限判别集，并建立相应的渐近局部-整体原理与局部理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bhargava-Hanke的290定理用有限判别集完全刻画了有理整数上的通用二次型。本文希望将这一"通用性可由有限判别集判定"的思想推广到全实数域上的高次对角形式，探究此类形式是否也存在类似的有限判别集。

Method: 研究全实数域上的高次对角形式；证明存在（关于包含关系）极小的唯一有限判别集，用于判定通用高次对角形式；作为证明的一部分，建立此类形式的渐近局部-整体原理的类比，并发展用高次对角形式表示整数的局部理论。

Result: 证明了全实数域上通用高次对角形式存在唯一且极小的有限判别集；建立了相应的渐近局部-整体原理类比；发展了高次对角形式表示整数的局部理论。

Conclusion: 该工作将290定理的有限判别集现象从有理整数上的二次型推广到全实数域上的高次对角形式，为高次形式的通用性判定提供了理论框架。

Abstract: The 290-Theorem of Bhargava-Hanke completely classifies all universal quadratic forms over the rational integers in terms of a finite criterion set: a positive definite quadratic form is universal if and only if it represents every integer in this finite set. In this article, we study diagonal forms of higher degree over a totally real number field and prove the existence of a unique finite criterion set, minimal with respect to inclusion, for universal diagonal forms of higher degree. As part of the proof, we establish an analog of the asymptotic local-global principle for such forms and develop a local theory for the representation of integers by diagonal forms of higher degree.

</details>


### [281] [Restricted Binomial GCDs at Primes Congruent to -1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37754)
*John Fairfax-Ball*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文给出了 $G(N;m)=\gcd\{\binom{N}{k}:0<k<N,\ m\mid k\}$ 在素数 $p\equiv -1\pmod m$ 处完整的 $p$-进赋值公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二项式系数子集的最大公约数的 $p$-进赋值，填补 $p\equiv -1\pmod m$ 情形下完整分类的空白；已有文献（如 McTague 修正的同余类扩展）仅覆盖单奇偶性 $p>m$ 的部分情形，未涉及混合奇偶分支与 $p=m-1$ 情形。

Method: 对 $m\geq 3$、$m\mid N$、$m<N$ 且 $p\equiv -1\pmod m$ 的素数，记 $N=\sum_i d_i p^i$，$A=\sum_{i\text{ even}}d_i$，$B=\sum_{i\text{ odd}}d_i$；利用 Kummer 定理将问题转化为逐位借位计数，并进行极小带符号零和分类。相关定理、其 plus-one 伴随版本、缩放归约及 $m=3,4,6$ 特例已在 Lean 4 / Mathlib 中形式化。

Result: $v_p(G(N;m))$ 的取值为：在 $(A,B)=(1,1)$ 且 $p=m-1$ 的例外混合情形为 $2$；在 $(A,B)=(1,1)$ 且 $m<p$ 的混合情形为 $1$；在 $(A,B)=(m,0)$ 与 $(A,B)=(0,m)$ 的单奇偶性情形为 $1$；其余情形均为 $0$。文献审查（截至 2026 年 9 月 25 日）未发现对完整 minus-one 分类的等价先验定理。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了 $p\equiv -1\pmod m$ 时 $G(N;m)$ 的 $p$-进赋值，且已通过 Lean 4 形式化验证，兼具理论完整性、原创性与机器可验证性。

Abstract: For integers $m\geq 2$ and $m\mid N$, let $G(N;m)=\gcd\{\binom{N}{k}:0<k<N,\ m\mid k\}$. We prove a complete $p$-adic valuation formula for $G(N;m)$ at primes $p\equiv -1\pmod m$, under the hypotheses $m\geq 3$, $m\mid N$, and $m<N$. Writing $N=\sum_i d_i p^i$, put $A=\sum_{i\text{ even}}d_i$ and $B=\sum_{i\text{ odd}}d_i$. Then $v_p(G(N;m))$ is $2$ in the exceptional mixed case $(A,B)=(1,1)$ with $p=m-1$, is $1$ in the mixed case $(A,B)=(1,1)$ with $m<p$, is $1$ in the one-parity cases $(A,B)=(m,0)$ and $(A,B)=(0,m)$, and is $0$ otherwise. The proof uses Kummer's theorem to translate the problem into digitwise borrow counts and a minimal signed zero-sum classification. A source-level literature audit through 25 September 2026 located no equivalent prior theorem for the full minus-one classification: McTague's corrected same-residue extension covers the one-parity $p>m$ subcases, but not the mixed-parity branch or the $p=m-1$ regime. The theorem, its plus-one companion, a scaling reduction, and the $m=3,4,6$ specializations have been formalized in Lean 4 / Mathlib.

</details>


### [282] [Smyth's Conjecture on the Mahler Measure of non-reciprocal trinomials of height $1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37886)
*Paul M Voutier*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Smyth关于高度为1的非互反三项式Mahler测度的猜想，精确刻画了其Mahler测度大于或小于$M(x+y+1)=\rho=1.381356\ldots$的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smyth猜想$M(x+y+1)=\rho$是非互反多项式Mahler测度$M(P)$的最小聚点，但此前缺少对具体族（如高度1的非互反三项式）Mahler测度与$\rho$大小关系的精确判定。

Method: 通过证明Smyth关于高度为1的非互反三项式的猜想，建立其Mahler测度与$\rho$比较的精确判据。

Result: 完全确定了高度1的非互反三项式Mahler测度何时大于、何时小于$\rho=1.381356\ldots$。

Conclusion: 猜想性地，该结果给出了所有非互反多项式（不仅是三项式）满足$M(P)<\rho$的完整谱。

Abstract: We prove a conjecture of Smyth on the Mahler measure of non-reciprocal trinomials of height $1$, determining exactly when their Mahler measures are greater than or less than $M(x+y+1)=ρ=1.381356\ldots$, conjectured to be the smallest limit point of $M(P)$ for non-reciprocal polynomials. Conjecturally, our result gives the full spectrum of $M(P)<ρ$ for all non-reciprocal polynomials, not just for trinomials.

</details>


### [283] [Congruences for the Andrews--Uncu partition function $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37920)
*Nayandeep Deka Baruah,Hirakjyoti Das,Manjil P. Saikia,Abhishek Sarma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了无重复偶数部分且每个偶数部分小于每个奇数部分的整数分拆函数 $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$，证明了模 $2$ 的幂的同余式、无穷族同余式以及递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Andrews 于 2018 年定义了分拆函数 $\mathcal{EO}(n)$（每个偶数部分小于每个奇数部分）。随后 Uncu 考察了其中无重复偶数部分的子集，记为 $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$。本文旨在深入研究 $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$ 的同余性质与递推结构。

Method: 研究方法结合了初等方法、算法方法以及模形式（modular form）技术。

Result: 证明了 $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$ 模 $2$ 的幂的若干同余式，以及一些无穷族同余式，并给出了该分拆函数的一个递推关系。

Conclusion: 该工作推广并深化了对 $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$ 这一分拆函数算术性质的理解，丰富了分拆函数同余式的研究。

Abstract: In 2018, Andrews defined and studied the partition function $\mathcal{EO}(n)$, which counts the number of partitions of $n$ where each even part is less than each odd part. Thereafter, Uncu considered a different subset of such partitions, namely those partitions counted by $\mathcal{EO}(n)$ where there are no repeated even parts (we denote the number of such partitions by $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$). In this paper, we prove several congruences modulo powers of $2$ for $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$. We also prove some infinite families of congruences as well as a recurrence for the number of this class of partitions. Our methods involve elementary, algorithmic, and modular form techniques.

</details>


### [284] [Computing residual representations of abelian threefolds with imaginary multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37975)
*Shiva Chidambaram,Pip Goodman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究虚二次域上具有复乘的极化阿贝尔三维簇的剩余伽罗瓦表示，给出像"尽可能大"的判据与高效算法，并将 ΓU₃(ℓ) 实现为 ℚ 上的伽罗瓦群，同时发现若干 Picard 曲线有理族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设 M 为虚二次域，A 为定义在 M 上、几何自同态代数同构于 M 的极化阿贝尔三维簇。作者希望理解其附着的剩余伽罗瓦表示的结构（自同态域、由自同态施加的限制），并判定表示像何时"尽可能大"，从而在数论（如逆伽罗瓦问题）与大规模计算中获得可用的工具。

Method: 1) 刻画自同态域及自同态对剩余表示的自然限制；2) 设计判定剩余伽罗瓦表示像为"大"的判据，并据此开发适合大规模计算的高效算法；3) 将算法应用于多族数百万条曲线（其 Jacobian 为具虚乘的阿贝尔三维簇）以及 Sutherland 的 7-smooth Picard 曲线数据集。

Result: 在计算范围内，除一条 Picard 曲线外，所有自同态代数为 M 的曲线其 Jacobian 的 mod-ℓ 像对任意 ℓ>7 都尽可能大；由此对全部 ℓ ≢ 1, 25, 121 (mod 168) 将酉半相似群 ΓU₃(ℓ) 实现为 ℚ 上的伽罗瓦群。另发现一条 Picard 曲线似在 ℚ(ζ₃) 上有 13 次同源。还发现若干 Picard 曲线的有理族，其一般成员的自同态代数为 6 维且非 CM 域，这些曲线位于 Picard 模曲面上且看似为 Shimura 曲线；其中一些似乎参数化具四元数乘的非主极化阿贝尔曲面。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了虚乘阿贝尔三维簇剩余伽罗瓦表示像的判据与可扩展算法，实现了 ΓU₃(ℓ) 作为 ℚ 上伽罗瓦群的新情形，并通过大规模计算发现具有非 CM 6 维自同态代数的 Picard 曲线族及疑似的 Shimura 曲线与四元数乘阿贝尔曲面参数化。

Abstract: Let $M$ be an imaginary quadratic field. Let $A$ be a polarised abelian threefold defined over $M$ with geometric endomorphism algebra isomorphic to $M$. We study residual Galois representations attached to $A$. In particular, we describe the endomorphism field and the natural restrictions placed on the residual representations by their endomorphisms. We then devise criteria for the image of residual Galois representations to be large, and provide efficient algorithms suitable for large scale calculations.
  Subsequently, we apply this algorithm to millions of curves in several families, whose Jacobians are abelian threefolds with imaginary multiplication, and to Sutherland's dataset of $7$-smooth Picard curves. We produce an explicit example of a Picard curve which appears to have an isogeny of degree $13$ defined over $\mathbb{Q}(ζ_3)$. The Jacobians of all other curves, in the range of our computation with endomorphism algebra $M$, have mod-$\ell$ image as large as possible for any prime $\ell>7$. This allows us to realise the group $Γ\mathrm{U}_3(\ell)$ of unitary semisimilitudes as a Galois group over $\mathbb{Q}$ for all $\ell \not\equiv 1, 25, 121 \pmod{168}$.
  Our algorithms also led us to the discovery of several interesting rational families of Picard curves whose generic members appear to have endomorphism algebra of dimension $6$ which is not a CM field. These represent curves on the Picard modular surface and appear to be Shimura curves. Some of them appear to parametrise non-principally polarised abelian surfaces with quaternionic multiplication.

</details>


### [285] [Improved exponent bounds for Erdős--Selfridge curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38076)
*Pranabesh Das,Kyle Pratt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了Erdős–Selfridge曲线上有理点坐标指数ℓ的上界，在k充分大时将Bennett–Siksek给出的双指数上界改进为更优结果，方法结合组合论证、筛法以及广义Fermat方程解的相关结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bennett与Siksek证明：对素数ℓ，曲线 y^ℓ = x(x+1)⋯(x+k−1) 上满足 y≠0 的有理点，其ℓ可由k以双指数方式界定。但该上界过于宽松，作者希望在k充分大时给出更紧的ℓ上界。

Method: 采用组合论证与筛法理论，并结合广义Fermat方程解的结果进行估计。

Result: 在k充分大的条件下，作者改进了ℓ的上界，使其优于原有的双指数界。

Conclusion: 通过组合、筛法与广义Fermat方程工具，可在k较大时显著改善Erdős–Selfridge曲线上有理点指数的界。

Abstract: Given integers $k,\ell \geq 2$, define the Erdős--Selfridge curve \begin{align*} \mathcal{C}_{k,\ell} : y^\ell = x(x+1)\cdots (x+k-1). \end{align*} Bennett and Siksek proved that if $(x,y)$ is a rational point on $\mathcal{C}_{k,\ell}$ with $\ell$ a prime and $y \neq 0$, then $\ell$ is bounded (doubly-exponentially) in terms of $k$. We improve this bound on $\ell$ when $k$ is sufficiently large. Our method uses combinatorial and sieve-theoretic arguments, as well as results on solutions to generalized Fermat equations.

</details>
